import {HISTORY_SQL} from '@/lib/lag-compensation';
import { database, hash, mutate, roomGate, roomLocks, type RoomRow } from '@/lib/store';
import { actor, view, type Room } from '@/lib/game';
import { fieldAct, fieldSnapshot, poseFor, stopCameras, type Pose } from '@/lib/field';
import {proposeMotion,MOVE_CHECKPOINT_SQL,HTTP_MOVE_ALLOWED,LIVE_LEASE_MS} from '@/lib/field-transport';
import { consumeInputs } from '@/lib/motion';
import { moveTo, WORLD, locationAt } from '@/lib/map';
const reply=(v:unknown,status=200)=>Response.json(v,{status,headers:{'Cache-Control':'no-store, private','Vary':'Authorization'}});
// HTTP is the fallback transport: the client uses it before its WebSocket is ready, while
// it is reconnecting, and for actions when the socket is unhealthy. A live connection's
// movement lease (see lib/field-transport.ts) always takes precedence over HTTP writes.
const UPSERT_HTTP_POSE='INSERT INTO field_positions (code,player,x,y,at,seq,ack,clock,warp,previous,place,owner,owner_at) SELECT ?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,\'http\',?';
const ON_CONFLICT_HTTP=' ON CONFLICT(code,player) DO UPDATE SET history=' + HISTORY_SQL + ',x=excluded.x,y=excluded.y,at=excluded.at,seq=excluded.seq,ack=excluded.ack,clock=excluded.clock,warp=excluded.warp,previous=excluded.previous,place=excluded.place,owner=\'http\',owner_at=excluded.owner_at';
export async function POST(req:Request){try{
 const origin=req.headers.get('origin');if(origin&&origin!==new URL(req.url).origin)return reply({error:'잘못된 요청입니다.'},403);
 const raw=await req.text();if(raw.length>18000)return reply({error:'요청이 너무 큽니다.'},413);const b=JSON.parse(raw);
 const token=(req.headers.get('authorization')||'').replace(/^Bearer /,'');if(!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(token)||!(/^[A-Z2-9]{6}$/.test(String(b.code))))throw Error('입장 정보를 확인해 주세요.');
 const key=await hash(token),db=database();
 const positions=()=>db.prepare('SELECT player AS id, x, y, at, seq, ack, clock, warp, previous, place, history, owner, owner_at FROM field_positions WHERE code = ?').bind(b.code);
 // Verified checkpoint movement + fresh room + all positions share one round trip.
 const now=Date.now(),lease=now-LIVE_LEASE_MS,base=b.base as Pose|undefined,count=Number(b.count);
 const proposal=b.action==='frame'&&b.protocol===2&&Number.isInteger(count)&&count>=6&&count<=29&&b.seq===base?.seq&&(b.warp||0)===(base?.warp||0)?proposeMotion(base,b.inputs,now,{count,mapId:b.mapId==='nereid'?'nereid':'aurora'}):null;
 const writes=proposal&&base?[db.prepare(MOVE_CHECKPOINT_SQL).bind(proposal.x,proposal.y,proposal.at,proposal.ack,proposal.clock,proposal.place,proposal.place,now,b.code,base.id,base.x,base.y,base.at,base.seq,base.ack,base.clock,base.warp,now,key,b.seq,count,b.mapId==='nereid'?'nereid':'aurora',lease)]:[];
 const initial=await db.batch([...writes,db.prepare('SELECT state, version, expires FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code = ?').bind(b.code),positions()]);
 const moved=!!writes.length&&initial[0].meta.changes===1,offset=writes.length;
 const row=initial[offset].results[0] as RoomRow|undefined;if(!row)throw Error('방을 찾을 수 없거나 만료되었습니다. 코드를 확인해 주세요.');
 let r=await mutate(b.code,key,undefined,{row,db}),poses=initial[offset+1].results as (Pose&{owner?:string;owner_at?:number})[];
 const seed=()=>({row:{state:JSON.stringify(r),version:r.revision||0,expires:row.expires},db});
 const {p}=actor(r,key);
 // Does a live connection currently own this player's movement?
 const leased=(id:string)=>{const saved=poses.find(x=>x.id===id);return !!saved&&!!saved.owner&&saved.owner!=='http'&&(saved.owner_at||0)>=lease;};
 if(b.action==='frame'&&p&&r.phase==='field'&&r.field?.watching?.[p.id]&&!r.paused){const w=r.field.watching[p.id];if(b.camera!==true||w.until-Date.now()<6000)r=await mutate(b.code,key,room=>{const current=room.field?.watching?.[p.id];if(!current)return;if(b.camera===true)current.until=Date.now()+12000;else stopCameras(room,p.id,Date.now());},seed());}

 if(b.action!=='frame'&&b.protocol===2&&p){
  for(let attempt=0;attempt<24;attempt++){
   const current=r.players.find(x=>x.key===key)!;const now=Date.now(),old=poseFor(r,current,poses,now),saved=poses.find(x=>x.id===current.id),ownedLive=leased(current.id);
   let next={...old,ack:old.ack||0,clock:old.clock||old.at};
   if(r.phase==='field'&&!r.paused&&b.seq===r.seq&&!r.field?.venting?.[current.id]&&!r.field?.watching?.[current.id]&&(b.warp||0)===(old.warp||0))next={...next,...consumeInputs(next,b.inputs,now,r.config)};
   const place=locationAt(next,r.config),candidate:Pose={...next,id:current.id,at:Math.max(now,(saved?.at||0)+1),seq:r.field?.epoch||0,warp:old.warp||0,place,previous:place!==old.place?old.place:old.previous};
   const actionPoses=poses.filter(x=>x.id!==current.id).concat(candidate),draft:Room=structuredClone(r);
   fieldAct(draft,key,b,actionPoses,now);draft.revision=(r.revision||0)+1;
   // While a live connection owns movement the action uses its last checkpoint plus the
   // sent inputs but must not overwrite the row (no stale-position retry loop either).
   const update=db.prepare('UPDATE mafia_rooms SET state=?,version=version+1,live_gate=?,live_locks=? WHERE code=? AND version=? AND (COALESCE((SELECT at FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?),0)=? OR ?=1)').bind(JSON.stringify(draft),roomGate(draft),roomLocks(draft),r.code,r.revision||0,r.code,current.id,saved?.at||0,ownedLive?1:0);
   // changes() belongs to the preceding guarded update in this atomic batch.
   const move=db.prepare(UPSERT_HTTP_POSE+' WHERE changes()=1'+ON_CONFLICT_HTTP+' WHERE '+HTTP_MOVE_ALLOWED).bind(r.code,current.id,candidate.x,candidate.y,candidate.at,candidate.seq,candidate.ack,candidate.clock,candidate.warp,candidate.previous,candidate.place,now,lease);
   const committed=await db.batch([update,move]);
   if(committed[0].meta.changes===1)return reply({room:view(draft,key),revision:draft.revision,frame:fieldSnapshot(draft,key,actionPoses)});
   // Same randomised back-off as mutate(): without it simultaneous actions (29 players
   // pressing at once) retry in lock-step and can exhaust the attempts. The window grows
   // with each failed attempt so an uncontended retry stays immediate.
   await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,Math.random()*Math.min(45,4*attempt)));
   const refreshed=await db.batch([db.prepare('SELECT state,version,expires FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=?').bind(b.code),positions()]);
   const fresh=refreshed[0].results[0] as RoomRow|undefined;if(!fresh)throw Error('방을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
   r=await mutate(b.code,key,undefined,{row:fresh,db});poses=refreshed[1].results as Pose[];
  }
  throw Error('요청이 몰리고 있습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
 }
 // Actions carry the last unacknowledged movement with them; no separate flush request.
 if(!moved&&p&&!leased(p.id)&&(b.action==='frame'||b.protocol===2))for(let attempt=0;attempt<2;attempt++){
  if(r.phase!=='field'||!r.field||r.paused||b.seq!==r.seq||r.field.venting?.[p.id]||r.field.watching?.[p.id])break;
  const now=Date.now(),old=poseFor(r,p,poses,now),saved=poses.find(x=>x.id===p.id);let next={...old,ack:old.ack||0,clock:old.clock||old.at};
  if(b.protocol===2){if((b.warp||0)===(old.warp||0))next={...next,...consumeInputs(next,b.inputs,now,r.config)};}
  else {const elapsed=Math.max(0,Math.min(.8,(now-old.at)/1000));if(Number.isFinite(b.x)&&Number.isFinite(b.y))next={...next,...moveTo(old,{x:b.x,y:b.y},WORLD.speed*1.15*elapsed,r.config)};}
  if(saved&&saved.seq===r.field.epoch&&saved.warp===(old.warp||0)&&next.ack===(saved.ack||0)&&next.x===saved.x&&next.y===saved.y)break;
  const place=locationAt(next,r.config),previous=place!==old.place?old.place:old.previous;
  const write=db.prepare(UPSERT_HTTP_POSE+' WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=? AND version=?)'+ON_CONFLICT_HTTP+' WHERE field_positions.at = ? AND '+HTTP_MOVE_ALLOWED).bind(r.code,p.id,next.x,next.y,Math.max(now,(saved?.at||0)+1),r.field.epoch,next.ack,next.clock,old.warp||0,previous,place,now,r.code,r.revision||0,saved?.at||0,lease);
  // The version predicate prevents an old frame from moving after a meeting or pause.
  const updated=await db.batch([write,positions()]);poses=updated[1].results as Pose[];
  if(updated[0].meta.changes===1)break;
  r=await mutate(b.code,key);
 }
 if(b.action!=='frame')r=await mutate(b.code,key,room=>fieldAct(room,key,b,poses,Date.now()),seed());
 const frame=fieldSnapshot(r,key,poses);
 // Player roles, chat and task lists only travel when their authoritative revision changes.
 return reply({...(b.action!=='frame'||b.revision!==r.revision?{room:view(r,key)}:{}),revision:r.revision,transport:moved?'checkpoint':'sync',frame});
 }catch(e){return reply({error:e instanceof Error?e.message:'현장 연결을 다시 시도해 주세요.'},400);}}

import { act, addPlayer, configOf, newRoom, view } from '@/lib/game';
import { database, hash, mutate, roomGate, roomLocks } from '@/lib/store';
const headers = { 'Cache-Control':'no-store, private', 'Vary':'Authorization', 'X-Content-Type-Options':'nosniff' };
const reply = (body:unknown,status=200) => Response.json(body,{status,headers});
function tokenOf(req:Request) { const t = (req.headers.get('authorization') || '').replace(/^Bearer /,''); if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(t)) throw new Error('입장 정보가 없습니다. 다시 접속해 주세요.'); return t; }
function roomCode(s:unknown) { const c = String(s || '').toUpperCase(); if (!/^[A-Z2-9]{6}$/.test(c)) throw new Error('6자리 방 코드를 확인해 주세요.'); return c; }
export async function GET(req:Request) { try { const code = roomCode(new URL(req.url).searchParams.get('code')); const key = await hash(tokenOf(req)); return reply(view(await mutate(code,key),key)); } catch(e) { return reply({error:e instanceof Error ? e.message : '연결 오류가 발생했습니다.'},400); } }
export async function POST(req:Request) {
  try {
    const origin=req.headers.get('origin'); if(origin && origin!==new URL(req.url).origin) return reply({error:'잘못된 요청입니다.'},403);
    const raw = await req.text(); if (raw.length > 4000) return reply({error:'요청이 너무 큽니다.'},413);
    const b = JSON.parse(raw); const key = await hash(tokenOf(req));
    if (b.action === 'create') {
      const alphabet='ABCDEFGHJKLMNPQRSTUVWXYZ23456789'; const code=Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(6)),x=>alphabet[x%alphabet.length]).join('');
      const r = newRoom(code,key,configOf(b.config || {}));
      await database().prepare('INSERT INTO mafia_rooms (code,state,version,expires,live_gate,live_locks) VALUES (?,?,0,?,?,?)').bind(code,JSON.stringify(r),Date.now()+86400000,roomGate(r),roomLocks(r)).run();
      return reply(view(r,key));
    }
    const code=roomCode(b.code);
    const r= b.action==='join' ? await mutate(code,null,r=>addPlayer(r,key,String(b.name||''),Number(b.avatar))) : await mutate(code,key,r=>act(r,key,b));
    return reply(view(r,key));
  } catch(e) { console.error('Game request:',e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'unknown'); return reply({error:e instanceof Error ? e.message : '요청을 처리하지 못했습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.'},400); }
}

import {acceptLive} from '@/lib/live-server';
export const dynamic='force-dynamic';
export async function GET(req:Request){
 if(req.headers.get('upgrade')?.toLowerCase()!=='websocket')return Response.json({error:'실시간 연결이 필요합니다.'},{status:426});
 const origin=req.headers.get('origin');if(origin&&origin!==new URL(req.url).origin)return new Response('Forbidden',{status:403});
 const [client,server]=Object.values(new WebSocketPair());acceptLive(server);
 return new Response(null,{status:101,webSocket:client});
}


======================================================================
FILE: app/chatgpt-auth.ts
======================================================================
import { headers } from "next/headers";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

export type ChatGPTUser = {
  userId: string;
  displayName: string;
  email: string;
  fullName: string | null;
};

const USER_ID_HEADER = "oai-authenticated-user-id";
const USER_EMAIL_HEADER = "oai-authenticated-user-email";
const USER_FULL_NAME_HEADER = "oai-authenticated-user-full-name";
const USER_FULL_NAME_ENCODING_HEADER =
  "oai-authenticated-user-full-name-encoding";
const PERCENT_ENCODED_UTF8 = "percent-encoded-utf-8";
const SIGN_IN_PATH = "/signin-with-chatgpt";
const SIGN_OUT_PATH = "/signout-with-chatgpt";
const CALLBACK_PATH = "/callback";

export async function getChatGPTUser(): Promise<ChatGPTUser | null> {
  const requestHeaders = await headers();
  const userId = requestHeaders.get(USER_ID_HEADER);
  const email = requestHeaders.get(USER_EMAIL_HEADER);
  if (!userId || !email) return null;

  const encodedFullName = requestHeaders.get(USER_FULL_NAME_HEADER);
  const fullName =
    encodedFullName &&
    requestHeaders.get(USER_FULL_NAME_ENCODING_HEADER) === PERCENT_ENCODED_UTF8
      ? safeDecodeURIComponent(encodedFullName)
      : null;

  return {
    userId,
    displayName: fullName ?? email,
    email,
    fullName,
  };
}

export async function requireChatGPTUser(
  returnTo: string,
): Promise<ChatGPTUser> {
  const user = await getChatGPTUser();
  if (user) return user;

  redirect(chatGPTSignInPath(returnTo));
}

export function chatGPTSignInPath(returnTo: string): string {
  const safeReturnTo = safeRelativeReturnPath(returnTo);
  return `${SIGN_IN_PATH}?return_to=${encodeURIComponent(safeReturnTo)}`;
}

export function chatGPTSignOutPath(returnTo = "/"): string {
  const safeReturnTo = safeRelativeReturnPath(returnTo);
  return `${SIGN_OUT_PATH}?return_to=${encodeURIComponent(safeReturnTo)}`;
}

function safeRelativeReturnPath(value: string): string {
  if (!value.startsWith("/") || value.startsWith("//")) return "/";

  let url: URL;
  try {
    url = new URL(value, "https://app.local");
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
  if (url.origin !== "https://app.local") return "/";
  if (isReservedAuthPath(url.pathname)) return "/";

  return `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
}

function isReservedAuthPath(pathname: string): boolean {
  return (
    pathname === SIGN_IN_PATH ||
    pathname === SIGN_OUT_PATH ||
    pathname === CALLBACK_PATH
  );
}

function safeDecodeURIComponent(value: string): string | null {
  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(value);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

복구합니다.'}</small></label>}{config.mode==='field'&&config.mapId==='nereid'&&<p className="map-config-summary">1인칭 3D · PC: WASD + 마우스 · 모바일: 이동 스틱 + 화면 드래그</p>}<div className="fields two"><NumberSelect label="참가 인원" value={config.count} values={Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>i+6)} onChange={count=>setConfig({...config,count})} suffix="명"/><NumberSelect label="토론 시간" value={config.discussion} 공개"/></label>}<div className="mini-title">{config.count}명 자동 배분 <span>진행자는 인원에서 제외</span></div><Distribution count={config.count} mode={config.mode}/>{config.mode==='field'&&<p const makeToken=()=>Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
function PhaseIcon({phase}:{phase:Phase}){return phase==='night'||phase==='reveal'?<Moon/>:phase==='vote'||phase==='trial'||phase==='defense'?<Gavel/>:phase==='finished'?<Crown/>:<Sun/>;}
export default function Home(){
 const [session,setSession]=useState<Session|null>(null),[resume,setResume]=useState<Session|null>(null),[room,setRoom]=useState<View|null>(null);
 const [name,setName]=useState(''),[code,setCode]=useState(''),[avatar,setAvatar]=useState(0),[config,setConfig]=useState<Config>(defaults),[busy,setBusy]=useState(false),[error,setError]=useState('');
 const [sound,setSound]=useState(false),[modal,setModal]=useState<'rules'|'roles'|'identity'|'settings'|null>(null),[detailRole,setDetailRole]=useState<Role>('crewmate');
 const [now,setNow]=useState(Date.now()),[connection,setConnection]=useState(''),[transition,setTransition]=useState(''),[confirm,setConfirm]=useState<{text:string;action:string;target?:string}|null>(null),[text,setText]=useState('');
 useEffect(()=>{const field=(room?.config.mode||config.mode)==='field';if(field!==FIELD_ROLE_KEYS.includes(detailRole as never))setDetailRole(field?'crewmate':'mafia');},[room?.config.mode,config.mode,detailRole]);
 const liveRoom=useRef(room);liveRoom.current=room;
 const offset=useRef(0),lastSeq=useRef(-1),audio=useRef<AudioContext|null>(null),soundRef=useRef(false),chatEnd=useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null),currentSession=useRef<Session|null>(null);
 const cue=(low=false)=>{if(!soundRef.current||!audio.current)return;const ctx=audio.current;void ctx.resume();[low?146:523,low?174:659,low?220:784].forEach((f,i)=>{const o=ctx.createOscillator(),g=ctx.createGain();o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=f;g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,ctx.currentTime+i*.1);g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(.06,ctx.currentTime+i*.1+.03);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,ctx.currentTime+i*.1+.8);o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);o.start(ctx.currentTime+i*.1);o.stop(ctx.currentTime+i*.1+.85);});};
 const toggleSound=()=>{if(!audio.current){const Ctx=window.AudioContext||(window as unknown as {webkitAudioContext:typeof AudioContext}).webkitAudioContext;audio.current=new Ctx();}soundRef.current=!sound;setSound(!sound);cue();};
 const accept=(v:View)=>{offset.current=v.serverNow-Date.now();setRoom(prev=>prev&&prev.code===v.code&&(prev.revision>v.revision||prev.revision===v.revision&&prev.serverNow>v.serverNow)?prev:v);setConnection('');};
 const request=async(body:Record<string,unknown>,s:Session)=>{const res=await fetch('/api/game',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json',Authorization:`Bearer ${s.token}`},body:JSON.stringify({...body,code:s.code})});const v=await res.json() as View & {error?:string};if(!res.ok)throw new Error(v.error||'연결을 확인해 주세요.');return v as View;};
 useEffect(()=>{try{const raw=sessionStorage.getItem('nightfall-session')||localStorage.getItem('nightfall-session');if(raw)setResume(JSON.parse(raw));}catch{}const c=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('room');if(c)setCode(c.toUpperCase().slice(0,6));const t=setInterval(()=>{if(liveRoom.current?.phase!=='field')setNow(Date.now());},250);return()=>clearInterval(t);},[]);
 useEffect(()=>{if(!session)return;currentSession.current=session;let cancelled=false;let timer:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;const poll=async()=>{if(liveRoom.current?.phase==='field'){timer=setTimeout(poll,1200);return;}const started=performance.now();try{const res=await fetch(`/api/game?code=${session.code}`,{headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${session.token}`},cache:'no-store'});const v=await res.json() as View & {error?:string};if(!cancelled){if(!res.ok)throw new Error(v.error||'연결을 확인해 주세요.');accept(v);}}catch(e){if(!cancelled)setConnection(e instanceof Error?e.message:'연결을 다시 시도하고 있습니다.');}finally{if(!cancelled)timer=setTimeout(poll,document.hidden?4000:Math.max(0,350-(performance.now()-started)));}};void poll();return()=>{cancelled=true;clearTimeout(timer);};},[session]);
 useEffect(()=>{if(!room)return;if(lastSeq.current!==room.seq){if(lastSeq.current>=0&&room.phase!=='lobby'){setTransition(room.phase==='night'?`${room.day}번째 밤`:room.phase==='dawn'?'아침이 밝았습니다':PHASE[room.phase].name);cue(room.phase==='night');}lastSeq.current=room.seq;setText('');if(room.phase==='lobby')setModal(null);}const t=setTimeout(()=>setTransition(''),2200);return()=>clearTimeout(t);},[room?.seq]);
 useEffect(()=>{chatEnd.current?.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});},[room?.messages.length]);
 const saveSession=(s:Session)=>{setSession(s);setResume(s);currentSession.current=s;try{sessionStorage.setItem('nightfall-session',JSON.stringify(s));localStorage.setItem('nightfall-session',JSON.stringify(s));}catch{}};
 const enter=async(kind:'create'|'join')=>{setBusy(true);setError('');try{const s={code:kind==='join'?code.trim().toUpperCase():'',token:makeToken()};const v=await request({action:kind,name,avatar,config},s);saveSession({...s,code:v.code});accept(v);}catch(e){setError((e as Error).message);}finally{setBusy(false);}};
 const send=async(action:string,extra:Record<string,unknown>={})=>{if(!session||!room)return false;setBusy(true);try{const v=await request({action,seq:room.seq,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),...extra},session);accept(v);if(['vote','night','trial'].includes(action))cue();return true;}catch(e){toast.error((e as Error).message);return false;}finally{setBusy(false);}};
 const copy=async(value:string)=>{try{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(value);toast.success('복사했습니다.');}catch{toast('복사할 내용',{description:value,duration:12000});}};
 const leave=()=>{setRoom(null);setSession(null);currentSession.current=null;lastSeq.current=-1;setConnection('');};
 const teamMarked=(p:{role:Role|null})=>!!room&&room.config.mode==='field'&&isImpostor(room.myRole)&&isImpostor(p.role)&&['discussion','vote','verdict'].includes(room.phase);
 const me=room?.players.find(p=>p.id===room.me),alive=room?.players.filter(p=>p.alive)||[],candidate=room?.players.find(p=>p.id===room.candidate);
 const seconds=room?Math.max(0,Math.ceil((room.paused?room.remaining:room.deadline-now-offset.current)/1000)):0;
 const nightActive=room&&me?canNight({...me,role:room.myRole,used:room.used},room.day):false;
 const actionable=!!room&&!!me?.alive&&!room.paused&&(room.phase==='vote'||(room.phase==='night'&&nightActive));
 const eligible=(p:View['players'][number])=>{if(!room||!me||!actionable)return false;if(room.phase==='vote')return p.alive&&p.id!==me.id;const dead=room.myRole==='medium'||room.myRole==='priest';return p.alive!==dead&&(room.myRole==='doctor'||p.id!==me.id)&&!(room.myRole==='mafia'&&(p.role==='mafia'||p.role==='spy'));};
 const activeChannel=room&&me&&!me.alive&&room.phase!=='finished'?'ghost':room?.phase==='night'?(room.myRole==='medium'?'ghost':room.myRole==='mafia'||(room.myRole==='spy'&&room.connected)?'mafia':'none'):'town';
 const chats=room?.messages.filter(m=>m.channel===activeChannel)||[];
 const canChat=!!room&&((!!me&&!me.alive&&room.phase!=='finished')||(room.phase==='night'&&activeChannel!=='none')||(['lobby','discussion','vote','trial','finished'].includes(room.phase))||(room.phase==='defense'&&(room.host||me?.id===room.candidate)));
 const shareUrl=typeof window==='undefined'?'':`${location.origin}/?room=${room?.code||''}`;
 return <main className={`app ${room?.phase==='night'?'is-night':''} ${room?.phase==='field'?'field-session':''}`}>
  <div className="city-backdrop"/><div className="ambient"/>

  <header className="topbar"><button className="brand" onClick={()=>room?setModal('rules'):undefined}><span className="brand-mark"><Moon size={22}/></span><span>밤의 시민<small>NIGHTFALL</small></span></button><nav><button className="text-button" onClick={()=>setModal('roles')}><Fingerprint size={17}/><span>직업 도감</span></button><button className="text-button" onClick={()=>setModal('rules')}><BookOpen size={17}/><span>게임 규칙</span></button><button className={`icon-button ${sound?'active':''}`} aria-label={sound?'효과음 끄기':'효과음 켜기'} title={sound?'효과음 켜짐':'효과음 꺼짐'} onClick={toggleSound}>{sound?<Volume2 size={19}/>:<VolumeX size={19}/>}</button>{room&&<button className="icon-button" aria-label="첫 화면으로" title="첫 화면으로 · 게임은 유지됩니다" onClick={leave}><LogOut size={18}/></button>}</nav></header>

    {room.myRole==='judge'&&['discussion','vote'].includes(room.phase)&&<JudgePanel room={room} send={send} busy={busy}/>}

    <div className="players-grid">{room.players.map((p,i)=><div className={`player-wrap ${!p.alive?'dead':''}`} key={p.id}><button disabled={!eligible(p)||busy} onClick={()=>send(room.phase==='vote'?'vote':'night',{target:p.id})} className={`player-card ${teamMarked(p)?'impostor-ally':''} ${p.id===room.me?'is-me':''} ${room.myAction===p.id?'selected':''} ${room.candidate===p.id?'candidate':''} ${eligible(p)?'can-select':''}`} style={{'--role':p.role?ROLE[p.role].color:'#e4d3ad'} as CSSProperties}><div className="portrait"><Avatar index={p.avatar}/><span className="player-number">{String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</span>{p.id===room.me&&<span className="me-badge">나</span>}{!p.alive&&<span className="death-cover"><Skull size={27}/></span>}{room.phase==='lobby'&&p.ready&&<span className="ready-check"><Check size={16}/></span>}{room.myAction===p.id&&<span className="target-badge"><Fingerprint size={18}/></span>}</div><div className="player-caption"><strong>{p.name}</strong>{teamMarked(p)&&<span className="ally-label">임포스터 팀</span>}<span className={p.role?'known-role':''}>{room.phase==='lobby'?(p.ready?'준비 완료':'대기 중'):p.role?ROLE[p.role].name:p.alive?'정체 미상':'탈락'}</span></div>{['vote','defense','trial','verdict'].includes(room.phase)&&<div className="vote-count"><span>{room.voted.includes(p.id)?'투표함':'·'}</span><b>{room.votes[p.id]||0}표</b></div>}</button>{room.host&&room.phase==='lobby'&&<button className="kick" aria-label={`${p.name} 내보내기`} onClick={()=>setConfirm({text:`${p.name} 님을 대기실에서 내보낼까요?`,action:'kick',target:p.id})}><X size={13}/></button>}</div>)}{room.phase==='lobby'&&Array.from({length:room.config.count-room.players.length},(_,i)=><div className="empty-seat" key={`empty${i}`}><Fingerprint size={24}/><span>입장 대기</span><small>{String(room.players.length+i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</small></div>)}</div>
    {room.phase==='vote'&&<div className="action-footer"><p>{room.myAction?`선택 완료 · ${room.myAction==='skip'?'기권':room.players.find(p=>p.id===room.myAction)?.name}`:'의심되는 생존자를 선택하세요.'}</p><button className={`secondary ${room.myAction==='skip'?'chosen':''}`} disabled={!me?.alive||room.paused||busy} onClick={()=>send('vote',{target:'skip'})}>이번 투표 기권 {room.votes.skip?`(${room.votes.skip}표)`:''}</button></div>}
    {room.phase==='night'&&me&&<div className="action-footer"><p>{!me.alive?'탈락자는 사망자 대화에 참여할 수 있습니다.':nightActive?(room.myAction?`${room.players.find(p=>p.id===room.myAction)?.name} 선택 완료 · 마감 전 변경 가능`:'능력 대상을 선택하거나 이번 밤은 보류하세요.'):(room.myRole==='reporter'&&room.day<2?'특종은 두 번째 밤부터 사용할 수 있습니다.':room.used?'1회 능력을 이미 사용했습니다.':'선택할 밤 능력이 없습니다. 아침을 기다려 주세요.')}</p>{nightActive&&<button className="secondary" disabled={busy||room.paused} onClick={()=>send('night',{target:'skip'})}>능력 보류</button>}</div>}
    {room.phase==='lobby'&&<div className="lobby-bottom"><div><span className="mini-title">이번 게임의 직업</span><Distribution mode={room.config.mode} count={room.config.count} compact/></div>{!room.host&&me&&<button className={me.ready?'secondary ready-button':'primary ready-button'} disabled={busy} onClick={()=>send('ready',{ready:!me.ready})}>{me.ready?<><Check size={18}/> 준비 완료 · 취소</>:<><Check size={18}/> 준비 완료하기</>}</button>}{room.host&&room.config.mode==='field'&&room.players.length===0&&<button className="secondary ready-button" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>send('soloStart')}><Play size={17}/> 진행자 혼자 입장</button>}{room.host&&<button className="primary ready-button" disabled={busy||room.players.length!==room.config.count||!room.players.every(p=>p.ready)} onClick={()=>send('start')}><Play size={17}/> 게임 시작</button>}</div>}
    {room.host&&room.phase!=='lobby'&&room.phase!=='finished'&&<div className="host-controls"><span><Crown size={15}/> 진행 제어</span><button className="secondary" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>send('pause')}>{room.paused?<Play size={15}/>:<Pause size={15}/>} {room.paused?'다시 진행':'일시정지'}</button><button className="secondary" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>setConfirm({text:'현재 단계를 마감하고 다음 단계로 넘어갈까요? 제출된 선택으로 즉시 판정합니다.',action:'next'})}><SkipForward size={15}/> 다음 단계</button></div>}
    {room.phase==='finished'&&<div className="end-card"><Crown/><p>{room.field?.rules===2?(room.winner==='neutral'?`${room.players.filter(p=>room.winnerIds.includes(p.id)).map(p=>p.name).join(', ')} 님이 혼자 승리했습니다.`:room.winner==='mafia'?'임포스터 팀이 승리했습니다.':'크루 팀이 승리했습니다.'):(room.winner==='mafia'?'마피아와 스파이가 승리했습니다.':'경찰·의사·특수 직업·시민이 승리했습니다.')}<small>{room.winner==='neutral'?'중립은 같은 중립 역할과도 승리를 공유하지 않습니다.':'승리한 팀은 탈락 여부와 관계없이 함께 승리합니다. 중립은 제외됩니다.'}</small></p>{room.host&&<button className="primary" disabled={busy} onClick={()=>send('reset')}><RotateCcw size={17}/> 다시 한 판</button>}</div>}
   </section><aside className="sidebar">
    {room.host?<div className="panel host-card"><div className="eyebrow"><Crown size={15}/> MODERATOR</div><h3>교실 진행 화면</h3><p>이 화면을 함께 보세요.<br/>비밀 직업과 밤 행동은 표시되지 않습니다.</p>{room.phase==='lobby'&&<button className="secondary wide" onClick={()=>copy(shareUrl)}><LinkIcon size={16}/> 학생 입장 링크 복사</button>}</div>:room.myRole?<button className="identity-card panel" onClick={()=>setModal('identity')}><div className="card-seal"><Fingerprint size={29}/></div><div><span className="eyebrow">CONFIDENTIAL</span><h3>나의 비밀 직업</h3><p>눌러서 정체와 능력 확인</p></div><Eye size={19}/></button>:<div className="panel identity-card"><Avatar index={me?.avatar||0}/><div><span className="eyebrow">CITIZEN FILE</span><h3>{me?.name}</h3><p>게임 시작 후 직업이 배정됩니다.</p></div></div>}
    {room.myRole==='spy'&&room.connected&&<div className="private-note"><Radio size={16}/> 접선 완료 · 밤에 마피아 대화 가능</div>}
    {room.phase==='night'&&Object.keys(room.mafiaVotes).length>0&&<div className="private-note mafia-plans"><b>동료의 공격 지목</b>{Object.entries(room.mafiaVotes).map(([id,target])=><span key={id}>{room.players.find(p=>p.id===id)?.name} → {room.players.find(p=>p.id===target)?.name}</span>)}</div>}
    <Tabs defaultValue="chat" className="panel comms"><TabsList className="tabbar"><TabsTrigger value="chat"><MessageCircle size={15}/>대화</TabsTrigger><TabsTrigger value="intel"><Search size={15}/>단서{room.intel.length>0&&<b className="count-badge">{room.intel.length}</b>}</TabsTrigger><TabsTrigger value="log">기록</TabsTrigger></TabsList><TabsContent value="chat"><div className={`channel-title channel-${activeChannel}`}><span/>{activeChannel==='mafia'?'마피아 비밀 대화':activeChannel==='ghost'?'사망자 · 영매 대화':activeChannel==='none'?'고요한 밤':'시민 광장'}<small>{activeChannel==='mafia'||activeChannel==='ghost'?'비공개':'LIVE'}</small></div><div className="chat-messages" aria-live="polite" aria-relevant="additions">{chats.length?chats.map(m=><div className={`message ${m.player===room.me?'mine':''}`} key={m.id}><span>{m.name}</span><p>{m.text}</p></div>):<div className="chat-empty"><MessageCircle size={30}/><p>{activeChannel==='none'?'밤에는 대화를 멈추고\n자신의 능력을 사용하세요.':activeChannel==='ghost'?'탈락한 친구들과 단서를 나누세요.\n밤에는 영매가 들을 수 있습니다.':'첫 이야기를 시작해 보세요.\n교실에서 직접 대화해도 좋습니다.'}</p></div>}<div ref={chatEnd}/></div><form className="chat-form" onSubmit={async e=>{e.preventDefault();if(await send('chat',{text}))setText('');}}><input aria-label="대화 메시지" placeholder={canChat?'메시지 입력…':'지금은 읽기만 가능합니다.'} disabled={!canChat} value={text} maxLength={200} onChange={e=>setText(e.target.value)}/><button aria-label="메시지 전송" disabled={!canChat||!text.trim()||busy}><Send size={18}/></button></form></TabsContent><TabsContent value="intel"><div className="channel-title"><EyeOff size={14}/>나만 볼 수 있는 조사 결과</div><div className="chat-messages clues">{room.intel.length?room.intel.map((x,i)=><div className="clue" key={i}><span>NIGHT {String(x.day).padStart(2,'0')}</span><p>{x.text}</p></div>):<div className="chat-empty"><Search size={30}/><p>아직 수집한 정보가 없습니다.\n조사 결과는 아침에 도착합니다.</p></div>}</div></TabsContent><TabsContent value="log"><div className="channel-title">도시의 사건 기록</div><div className="chat-messages 이야기가 곧 시작됩니다.</p></div>}</div></TabsContent></Tabs>
    <div className="connection-status"><Radio size={13}/>{connection?'다시 연결 중…':'실시간 연결'}<span>방은 생성 후 24시간 유지</span></div>
   </aside></div>}
  </div>}
  <footer className="footer"><span>NIGHTFALL <i/> 밤의 시민</span><span>끝까지, 당신의 정체를 숨기세요.</span></footer>
  {transition&&<div className={`phase-transition ${room?.field&&room.phase==='discussion'?'meeting-cinematic':''}`} key={room?.seq}><PhaseIcon phase={room?.phase||'night'}/><p>{transition}</p></div>}
  <Dialog open={modal!==null} onOpenChange={v=>{if(!v)setModal(null);}}><DialogContent className={`game-dialog ${modal==='roles'?'role-dialog':''}`}><DialogTitle>{modal==='roles'?'직업 도감':modal==='rules'?'게임 규칙':modal==='settings'?'방 설정':'당신의 비밀 직업'}</DialogTitle><DialogDescription>{modal==='identity'?'다른 사람에게 화면을 보여주지 마세요.':modal==='roles'?'각자의 능력을 이해하고, 서로의 주장을 의심하세요.':modal==='settings'?'진행자는 참가 인원에 포함되지 않습니다.':'밤의 시민에 적용되는 규칙입니다.'}</DialogDescription>
   {modal==='identity'&&room?.myRole&&<div className="role-reveal" style={{'--role':ROLE[room.myRole].color} as CSSProperties}><>{room.field?<DollAvatar className="role-art identity-portrait" index={me?.avatar||0}/>:<RoleArt role={room.myRole}/>}</><span>{ROLE[room.myRole].en}</span><h2>{ROLE[room.myRole].name}</h2><h4>{room.field?.rules===2?(isNeutral(room.myRole)?'중립 · 독립 승리':isImpostor(room.myRole)?'임포스터 팀':'크루 팀'):['mafia','spy'].includes(room.myRole)?'마피아 팀':'시민 팀'}</h4><p>{room.config.mode==='field'?FIELD_ROLE[room.myRole].mission:ROLE[room.myRole].mission}</p>{room.field?.guardian&&<b>수호천사: 가까운 생존자에게 8초 보호막 · 35초 재사용</b>}{['reporter','priest'].includes(room.myRole)&&<b>{room.used?'1회 능력 사용 완료':'1회 능력 사용 가능'}</b>}{room.myRole==='soldier'&&<b>{room.shield?'방탄 1회 남음':'방탄 소모됨'}</b>}<button className="secondary wide" onClick={()=>setModal(null)}><EyeOff size={17}/> 정체 가리기</button></div>}
   {modal==='roles'&&<div className="codex"><div className="role-list">{Object.entries(ROLE).filter(([k])=>(room?.config.mode||config.mode)==='field'?FIELD_ROLE_KEYS.includes(k as never):!FIELD_ROLE_KEYS.includes(k as never)).map(([k,r])=><button className={detailRole===k?'chosen':''} key={k} onClick={()=>setDetailRole(k as Role)} style={{'--role':r.color} as CSSProperties}><i/>{r.name}</button>)}</div><div className="codex-detail" style={{'--role':ROLE[detailRole].color} as CSSProperties}><RoleArt role={detailRole}/><span className="eyebrow">{ROLE[detailRole].en}</span><h2>{ROLE[detailRole].name}</h2><h4>{ROLE[detailRole].description}</h4><p>{(room?.config.mode||config.mode)==='field'?FIELD_ROLE[detailRole].mission:ROLE[detailRole].mission}</p></div></div>}
   {modal==='settings'&&<><ConfigFields config={config} setConfig={setConfig}/><button className="primary" disabled={busy} onClick={async()=>{if(await send('settings',{config}))setModal(null);}}>설정 저장</button></>}
   {modal==='rules'&&<div className="rules-content"><h3>네레이드 · 해양 과학기지</h3><p>방 설정에서 네레이드를 선택하면 바다 위 연구동과 순환 다리로 연결된 새 맵에서 탐색합니다. 회로, 기체 압력, 부력, 밀도, 혼합물 분리, 열, 빛의 반사, 광합성, 소화 효소, 상태 변화, 질량 보존, 태양계 미션 12종을 해결하세요. 장치의 연구 노트에서 개념 힌트를 볼 수 있습니다. 중학교 1~3학년 과학 개념을 함께 다루며 학년별 진도는 교과서에 따라 다릅니다.</p><h3>현장 수사 모드 · 이동과 미션</h3><p>WASD·방향키 또는 화면의 터치 조이스틱으로 인원에 따라 12~20개 장소를 이동합니다. 내 미션 장치에 가까이 가서 <b>미션(E)</b>을 누르고 배선·암호·정렬·기억·보정·카드 스캔·잔해 제거·데이터 전송·연료 주입·필터 청소·항로 조정·차단기 복구 12종 미니게임을 완료하세요. 36~60개 장치 중 1인당 6~12개가 배정됩니다. 크루 전체 미션을 완료하면 즉시 크루가 승리하며 임포스터와 중립의 위장 미션은 합산되지 않습니다. 탈락자는 유령으로 미션을 계속할 수 있습니다.</p><p>임포스터는 가까운 크루를 <b>공격(Q)</b>하며 공격 간격은 30초입니다. 정전은 35초 동안 시민의 시야를 좁힙니다. 전력실에서 복구할 수 있습니다. 쓰러진 사람에게 가까이 가서 <b>신고(R)</b>하면 회의가 열립니다. 중앙 홀에서는 1인 1회의 긴급회의를 부를 수 있습니다. 회의 후에는 모두 중앙 홀에서 탐색을 재개합니다.</p><p>현장 모드는 크루원·엔지니어·과학자·추적자·경보원·탐정·판사, 임포스터·변신술사·팬텀·바이퍼, 중립 광대·수집가·고독한 사냥꾼·처형인·생존주의자·방화범·도둑·탈출 전문가·검시관으로 구성됩니다. 먼저 탈락한 크루에게 수호천사 능력이 주어집니다. 특수 능력은 F, 벤트 진입은 V를 누르세요. 임포스터와 엔지니어는 네 개 연결망의 벤트로 우회하고 숨을 수 있습니다. 안에서는 공격할 수 없고, 임포스터는 30초·엔지니어는 10초 후 자동으로 나옵니다. 퇴장 뒤 재사용 대기는 각각 8초·25초입니다. 토론 뒤 한 번 투표해 최다 득표자를 추방하며, 동률이나 건너뛰기가 최다면 추방하지 않습니다. 직업을 직접 알아내는 능력과 2표 능력은 없습니다. 이동 중에는 근처의 플레이어만 보이고, 멀리 떨어진 사람의 탈락은 회의 전까지 표시되지 않습니다. 특수 직업의 현장 능력은 직업 도감에서 확인하세요. 진행자는 전체 지도를 볼 수 있습니다.</p><p><b>CCTV(C)</b>: 보안실 모니터(소규모 맵은 관제실)에서 최대 8개 고정 카메라를 실시간으로 확인합니다. 4분할·확대 화면을 전환할 수 있습니다. 감시 중에는 이동·공격·신고가 불가능하며 몸은 현장에 남아 공격받을 수 있습니다. 카메라에는 빨간 작동등이 켜집니다. 정전 중에도 카메라는 작동하지만, 통신 방해 중에는 CCTV·생체 신호·추적이 중단됩니다. 통신실 장치에서 복구하거나 45초 뒤 자동 복구됩니다.</p><h3>중립 · 독립 승리</h3><p>크루는 전체 인원의 절반(홀수는 올림), 임포스터는 6~8명일 때 1명·9~12명 2명·13~18명 3명·19~24명 4명·25~29명 5명이며, 나머지는 중립으로 배정됩니다. 16명은 크루 8명·임포스터 3명·중립 5명(광대·수집가·고독한 사냥꾼·처형인·생존주의자 각 1명)입니다. 광대는 항상 1명이며 모든 중립 직업은 중복 없이 배정됩니다. 인원이 늘면 방화범·도둑·탈출 전문가·검시관 순서로 추가되어 최대 9명이 모두 다른 직업을 맡습니다. 고독한 사냥꾼은 13명 이상에서 배정합니다. 중립끼리도 팀이 아닙니다. 생존주의자와 탈출 전문가의 미션은 개인 승리 조건이고, 나머지 중립의 미션은 위장입니다. 모두 크루 미션 게이지에는 포함되지 않습니다. 광대는 일반 투표로 추방되면 즉시 승리합니다. 수집가는 15명 이하 2구, 16~22명 3구, 23~29명 4구를 수집하면 즉시 승리합니다. 수집한 시체는 신고할 수 없으며 15초마다 수집합니다. 고독한 사냥꾼은 누구든 30초마다 공격할 수 있고 최후의 1인이 되면 승리합니다. 살아 있는 동안 두 팀의 인원수·적 전멸 승리를 막지만 크루 미션 완료는 막지 못합니다. 처형인은 비밀 목표의 일반 투표 추방, 생존주의자는 개인 미션 3개 완료와 마지막 4명 안에 생존, 방화범은 모든 생존자에게 기름을 묻힌 뒤 점화, 도둑은 서로 다른 4명의 전리품을 중앙 홀에서 반출, 탈출 전문가는 개인 미션 전체 완료 후 하역장에서 탈출, 검시관은 서로 다른 시체 3구의 증거를 기록실에 제출하면 승리합니다. 처형인은 자신도 생존해야 하며 목표가 공격·판결로 죽으면 새 목표를 받습니다. 크루 전체 미션 완료는 생존주의자의 승리 조건보다 우선합니다. 방화범이 살아 있으면 사냥꾼과 마찬가지로 적 전멸·인원수 승리가 보류됩니다. 세부 거리·재사용 시간은 직업 도감에서 확인하세요. 중립은 벤트·방해 공작·임포스터 비밀 대화를 쓸 수 없습니다.</p><p>일반 투표 추방 후 임포스터 여부를 기본으로 공개합니다. 진행자는 설정에서 끌 수 있습니다. 이는 전체 직업 공개와 별도이며 중립의 구체적인 직업은 알리지 않습니다.</p><p>중립 승리 방식 참고: <a href="https://goose-goose-duck.fandom.com/wiki/Neutrals" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Goose Goose Duck 중립 역할 자료</a>. 역할 이름·능력·승리 조건·배분은 이 게임에 맞춰 별도로 구성했습니다.</p><p>역할 참고: <a href="https://www.innersloth.com/new-roles-cosmicubes-out-now-emergency-meeting-33/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">Innersloth 공식 역할 소개</a>, <a href="https://www.innersloth.com/new-roles-enter-the-fray-v2024-6-18-emergency-meeting-38/" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">추적자·경보원·팬텀 소개</a>. 인원·시간·배분은 이 교실용 게임에 맞게 조정했습니다.</p><h3>아래는 클래식 마피아 모드 규칙입니다</h3><div className="rules-flow"><span>직업 확인</span><ArrowRight/><span>밤 행동</span><ArrowRight/><span>토론</span><ArrowRight/><span>지목·변론·심판</span></div><h3>승리 조건</h3><p><b>시민 팀</b>은 마피아를 모두 탈락시키면 승리합니다. 스파이만 남아도 시민 팀 승리입니다. <b>마피아 팀</b>은 생존 마피아 수가 나머지 생존자 수 이상이면 승리합니다. 이 인원 계산에서 스파이는 나머지 생존자에 포함되지만, 승패는 마피아 팀과 함께합니다.</p><h3>투표와 최후 변론</h3><p>지목 투표의 단독 최다 득표자가 25초간 변론합니다. 동률이거나 기권이 최다이면 추방 없이 밤으로 넘어갑니다. 이후 20초간 찬반 투표를 하며, 후보자를 제외한 생존자 전체의 과반수가 찬성하면 추방됩니다. 정치인의 지목은 2표, 찬반은 1표입니다. 미참여는 찬성으로 세지 않습니다.</p><h3>밤 능력과 비밀</h3><p>마피아의 단독 최다 지목 대상만 공격하며 동률이면 공격하지 않습니다. 의사의 보호 → 군인의 방탄 → 탈락 → 취재·부활 → 승리 판정 순으로 처리합니다. 조사·추적은 밤에 제출한 마지막 선택을 기준으로 처리합니다. 기자와 성직자는 그 밤에 본인이 탈락하면 능력이 실패합니다.</p><p>스파이는 접선 전까지 마피아가 누구인지 모릅니다. 접선 후 비밀 대화에 참여하며 마피아의 공격 대상에서 제외됩니다. 모든 선택은 마감 전까지 변경할 수 있습니다.</p><h3>탈락해도 참여할 수 있습니다</h3><p>탈락자는 투표와 능력 사용이 중단되며 사망자 대화만 가능합니다. 영매는 밤에 이 대화를 볼 수 있습니다. 부활하면 원래 직업·진영으로 돌아오고 이미 소모한 능력은 회복되지 않습니다.</p><h3>교실에서 시작하기</h3><p>교사가 방을 만들고 입장 링크나 6자리 코드를 공유합니다. 학생은 각자 기기에서 이름과 캐릭터를 고른 후 준비를 누릅니다. 모든 인원이 준비되면 교사가 시작합니다. 새로고침 후 이전 방으로 돌아갈 수 있습니다. 브라우저 데이터를 지우면 입장 정보가 사라집니다.</p><div className="rules-source">직업 설계 참고: <a href="https://mafia42.com/artwork/job_info" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">마피아42 공식 직업 소개</a>, <a href="https://namu.moe/w/마피아42/스킬" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">직업 능력 자료</a>. 이 게임은 별도 제작한 게임이며, 세부 판정은 위 규칙을 따릅니다.</div></div>}
  </DialogContent></Dialog>
  <AlertDialog open={!!confirm} onOpenChange={v=>{if(!v)setConfirm(null);}}><AlertDialogContent className="game-dialog"><AlertDialogTitle>진행 확인</AlertDialogTitle><AlertDialogDescription>{confirm?.text}</AlertDialogDescription><AlertDialogFooter><AlertDialogCancel>취소</AlertDialogCancel><AlertDialogAction onClick={()=>{if(confirm)void send(confirm.action,{target:confirm.target});setConfirm(null);}}>진행</AlertDialogAction></AlertDialogFooter></AlertDialogContent></AlertDialog>

FILE: build/sites-vite-plugin.ts
======================================================================
// Vendored from @openai/sites-vite-plugin 0.2.0 (openai/sites#9).
// See sites-vite-plugin.LICENSE for the upstream MIT license.
import { access, cp, mkdir, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import type { Plugin } from "vite";

const localUserId = "local_seedy";
const localEmail = "seedy@sites.test";
const localFullName = "Seedy";
const localCookieName = "__sites_local_auth";
const localHosts = new Set(["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "::1"]);
const localAddresses = new Set(["127.0.0.1", "::1", "::ffff:127.0.0.1"]);
const authPaths = new Set([
  "/signin-with-chatgpt",
  "/signout-with-chatgpt",
  "/callback",
]);

async function exists(path: string): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await access(path);
    return true;
  } catch (error) {
    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code === "ENOENT") {
      return false;
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

export function sites({ mockAuth = true } = {}): Plugin {
  let root = process.cwd();
  let command: "build" | "serve" = "build";

  return {
    name: "sites",
    configResolved(config) {
      root = config.root;
      command = config.command;
    },
    configureServer(server) {
      if (!mockAuth) return;
      const secure = Boolean(server.config.server.https);

      server.config.logger.info(`Sites local sign-in: ${localEmail}`);
      server.middlewares.use((request, response, next) => {
        for (const name of Object.keys(request.headers)) {
          if (name.startsWith("oai-authenticated-user-")) {
            removeHeader(request, name);
          }
        }

        let authority: URL;
        let url: URL;
        try {
          authority = new URL(
            `${secure ? "https" : "http"}://${request.headers.host}`,
          );
          url = new URL(request.url ?? "/", authority);
        } catch {
          if (authPaths.has((request.url ?? "/").split("?")[0])) {
            respond(response, 403);
          } else {
            next();
          }
          return;
        }

        const hostname = authority.hostname
          .replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "")
          .toLowerCase();
        if (
          !localHosts.has(hostname) ||
          !localAddresses.has(request.socket.remoteAddress ?? "") ||
          url.origin !== authority.origin
        ) {
          if (authPaths.has(url.pathname)) respond(response, 403);
          else next();
          return;
        }

        const cookies = (request.headers.cookie ?? "")
          .split(";")
          .map((cookie) => cookie.trim())
          .filter(Boolean);
        const signInCookies = cookies
          .filter((cookie) => cookie.startsWith(`${localCookieName}=`))
          .map((cookie) => cookie.slice(localCookieName.length + 1));
        const applicationCookies = cookies.filter(
          (cookie) => !cookie.startsWith(`${localCookieName}=`),
        );
        if (applicationCookies.length !== cookies.length) {
          removeHeader(request, "cookie");
          if (applicationCookies.length) {
            setHeader(request, "cookie", applicationCookies.join("; "));
          }
        }

        if (url.pathname === "/callback") {
          respond(response, 501);
          return;
        }

        const signIn = url.pathname === "/signin-with-chatgpt";
        const signOut = url.pathname === "/signout-with-chatgpt";
        if (!signIn && !signOut) {
          if (signInCookies.length === 1 && signInCookies[0] === "1") {
            setHeader(request, "oai-authenticated-user-id", localUserId);
            setHeader(request, "oai-authenticated-user-email", localEmail);
            setHeader(
              request,
              "oai-authenticated-user-full-name",
              localFullName,
            );
            setHeader(
              request,
              "oai-authenticated-user-full-name-encoding",
              "percent-encoded-utf-8",
            );
          }
          next();
          return;
        }

        if (
          (request.headers.origin && request.headers.origin !== url.origin) ||
          request.headers["sec-fetch-site"] === "cross-site"
        ) {
          respond(response, 403);
          return;
        }

        if (
          request.headers["next-router-prefetch"] !== undefined ||
          request.headers["x-middleware-prefetch"] === "1" ||
          [request.headers.purpose, request.headers["sec-purpose"]].some(
            (value) =>
              typeof value === "string" &&
              value
                .split(/[;,]/)
                .some((part) => part.trim().toLowerCase() === "prefetch"),
          )
        ) {
          respond(response, 204);
          return;
        }

        if (
          request.method !== "GET" &&
          (!signOut || request.method !== "POST")
        ) {
          response.setHeader("Allow", signIn ? "GET" : "GET, POST");
          respond(response, 405);
          return;
        }

        response.statusCode = request.method === "POST" ? 303 : 302;
        response.setHeader("Cache-Control", "private, no-store");
        response.setHeader(
          "Location",
          safeReturn(url.searchParams.get("return_to")),
        );
        response.setHeader(
          "Set-Cookie",
          `${localCookieName}=${signIn ? "1" : ""}; Path=/; ${
            signOut ? "Max-Age=0; " : ""
          }HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax${secure ? "; Secure" : ""}`,
        );
        response.end();
      });
    },
    async closeBundle() {
      if (command !== "build") return;

      const outputDirectory = resolve(root, "dist", ".openai");
      const hostingConfig = resolve(root, ".openai", "hosting.json");
      const drizzleSource = resolve(root, "drizzle");

      await rm(outputDirectory, { recursive: true, force: true });
      await mkdir(outputDirectory, { recursive: true });

      await cp(hostingConfig, resolve(outputDirectory, "hosting.json"));
      if (await exists(drizzleSource)) {
        await cp(drizzleSource, resolve(outputDirectory, "drizzle"), {
          recursive: true,
        });
      }
    },
  };
}

function removeHeader(request: IncomingMessage, name: string): void {
  delete request.headers[name];
  for (let index = request.rawHeaders.length - 2; index >= 0; index -= 2) {
    if (request.rawHeaders[index]?.toLowerCase() === name) {
      request.rawHeaders.splice(index, 2);
    }
  }
}

function setHeader(
  request: IncomingMessage,
  name: string,
  value: string,
): void {
  removeHeader(request, name);
  request.headers[name] = value;
  request.rawHeaders.push(name, value);
}

function respond(response: ServerResponse, status: number): void {
  response.statusCode = status;
  response.setHeader("Cache-Control", "private, no-store");
  response.end();
}

function safeReturn(value: string | null): string {
  if (!value?.startsWith("/") || value.startsWith("//")) return "/";

  try {
    const url = new URL(value, "http://localhost");
    if (url.origin !== "http://localhost" || authPaths.has(url.pathname)) {
      return "/";
    }
    return `${url.pathname}${url.search}${url.hash}`;
  } catch {
    return "/";
  }
}

'use client';
import {useState} from 'react';
import {Search,Save} from 'lucide-react';
import {Select,SelectContent,SelectItem,SelectTrigger,SelectValue} from '@/components/ui/select';
import type {View} from '@/lib/game';
type Case=NonNullable<View['field']>['cases'][number];
function CasePage({file,readOnly,send}:{file:Case;readOnly:boolean;send:(a:string,e?:Record<string,unknown>)=>Promise<boolean>}){
 const [memo,setMemo]=useState(file.memo),[saving,setSaving]=useState(false),[saved,setSaved]=useState(file.memo);
 return <div className="case-page"><div className="case-stamp">탐색 {file.day} · {file.victim} 사건 <span>검문 {file.answers.length} / 3</span></div><p className="case-help">검문은 다음 탐색에서 대상 가까이 이동한 뒤 사용하세요. 위치는 사건 당시의 기록이며 직업을 판정하지 않습니다.</p><div className="case-answers">{file.answers.length?file.answers.map(a=><div key={a.id}><strong>{a.name}</strong><span>{a.place}</span></div>):<p>아직 검문한 사람이 없습니다.</p>}</div><label className="field"><span>내 추리 메모 · 현장 / 용의자 / 임포스터 유형</span><textarea maxLength={400} rows={4} value={memo} disabled={readOnly} onChange={e=>setMemo(e.target.value)} placeholder="회의에서 들은 주장과 목격 내용을 기록하세요. 이 메모는 나만 볼 수 있습니다."/></label><button className="secondary" disabled={readOnly||saving||memo===saved} onClick={async()=>{setSaving(true);try{if(await send('caseNote',{caseId:file.id,memo}))setSaved(memo);}finally{setSaving(false);}}}><Save size={16}/>{saving?'저장 중…':memo===saved?'저장됨':'메모 저장'}</button></div>;
}
export default function CaseNotebook({room,send,selected,onSelect}:{room:View;send:(a:string,e?:Record<string,unknown>)=>Promise<boolean>;selected?:string;onSelect?:(id:string)=>void}){
 const [current,setCurrent]=useState('');const files=room.field?.cases||[],file=files.find(c=>c.id===(selected??current))||files.at(-1),me=room.players.find(p=>p.id===room.me);
 return <section className="case-notebook"><h3><Search size={20}/> 탐정 사건 수첩</h3>{file?<><Select value={file.id} onValueChange={id=>{setCurrent(id);onSelect?.(id);}}><SelectTrigger className="select-box" aria-label="조사할 사건"><SelectValue/></SelectTrigger><SelectContent>{files.map(c=><SelectItem key={c.id} value={c.id}>탐색 {c.day} · {c.victim} 사건</SelectItem>)}</SelectContent></Select><CasePage key={file.id} file={file} readOnly={!me?.alive||room.paused||!['field','discussion','vote'].includes(room.phase)} send={send}/></>:<p className="case-help">회의가 열리면 탈락한 크루의 사건 파일이 생깁니다. 현재는 미션과 목격 정보에 집중하세요.</p>}</section>;
}

'use client';
import {useEffect,useRef,useState} from 'react';
import {Check,ScanLine,Upload,Fuel,Crosshair,Trash2,SlidersHorizontal,Power} from 'lucide-react';
import {STATIONS} from '@/lib/map';
import {puzzleDuration} from '@/lib/puzzles';
import type {Challenge} from '@/lib/field';
export default function ExtendedPuzzle({challenge,busy,finish}:{challenge:Challenge;busy:boolean;finish:(a:number[])=>Promise<void>}){
 const kind=STATIONS.find(s=>s.id===challenge.station)!.kind;
 const [removed,setRemoved]=useState<number[]>([]),[levels,setLevels]=useState([0,0,0]),[switches,setSwitches]=useState(challenge.solution.map(n=>1-n)),[amount,setAmount]=useState(0),[swipe,setSwipe]=useState(0),[swipeMs,setSwipeMs]=useState(0),[message,setMessage]=useState(''),[elapsed,setElapsed]=useState(0);
 const held=useRef(false),began=useRef(0),track=useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null),born=useRef(Date.now()),last=useRef(Date.now());
 const duration=puzzleDuration(kind);
 useEffect(()=>{const stop=()=>{held.current=false;};window.addEventListener('blur',stop);window.addEventListener('pointerup',stop);window.addEventListener('pointercancel',stop);const t=setInterval(()=>{const now=Date.now(),dt=Math.min(100,now-last.current);last.current=now;setElapsed(now-born.current);if(held.current)setAmount(v=>Math.min(100,v+dt/duration*100));},40);return()=>{clearInterval(t);window.removeEventListener('blur',stop);window.removeEventListener('pointerup',stop);window.removeEventListener('pointercancel',stop);};},[duration]);
 const done=kind==='swipe'?swipeMs>0:kind==='asteroids'?removed.length===8:kind==='filter'?removed.length===6:kind==='align'?levels.every((v,i)=>Math.abs(v-challenge.solution[i])<=3):kind==='switches'?switches.every((v,i)=>v===challenge.solution[i]):amount>=100;
 const answer=kind==='swipe'?[swipeMs]:kind==='asteroids'||kind==='filter'?[...removed].sort((a,b)=>a-b):kind==='align'?levels:kind==='switches'?switches:[100];
 const remove=(i:number)=>setRemoved(a=>a.includes(i)?a:[...a,i]);
 return <div className="task-puzzle expanded-puzzle">
  {kind==='swipe'&&<><p className="task-instruction">카드를 왼쪽 끝에서 오른쪽 끝까지 0.45~1.5초 동안 밀어 주세요.</p><div className="card-reader" ref={track}><div className="reader-slot"/><button aria-label="출입 카드 끌기" className="swipe-card" style={{left:`${swipe*70}%`}} disabled={done} onPointerDown={e=>{e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);began.current=performance.now();setSwipe(0);setMessage('오른쪽으로 천천히 밀기');}} onPointerMove={e=>{if(!e.currentTarget.hasPointerCapture(e.pointerId)||!track.current)return;const b=track.current.getBoundingClientRect();setSwipe(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(e.clientX-b.left-36)/(b.width-80))));}} onPointerUp={e=>{const ms=Math.round(performance.now()-began.current);if(swipe>.92&&ms>=450&&ms<=1500){setSwipeMs(ms);setMessage('인증 완료');}else{setSwipe(0);setMessage(swipe<.92?'오른쪽 끝까지 밀어 주세요.':ms<450?'너무 빨라요. 다시 시도하세요.':'너무 느려요. 다시 시도하세요.');}e.currentTarget.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}} onPointerCancel={()=>{setSwipe(0);setMessage('다시 밀어 주세요.');}}><ScanLine size={30}/><strong>CREW</strong><small>ID / AURORA</small></button></div><p className="task-hint" aria-live="polite">{message||'카드를 잡고 오른쪽으로 밀어 주세요.'}</p></>}
  {kind==='asteroids'&&<><p className="task-instruction">움직이는 잔해 8개를 눌러 항로를 확보하세요.</p><div className="debris-field">{Array.from({length:8},(_,i)=><button key={i} aria-label={`잔해 ${i+1}`} disabled={removed.includes(i)} className={removed.includes(i)?'cleared':''} style={{left:`${12+(i%4)*24}%`,top:`${22+Math.floor(i/4)*48}%`,animationDelay:`${-i*.41}s`}} onClick={()=>remove(i)}>{removed.includes(i)?<Check size={20}/>:<Crosshair size={30}/>}</button>)}</div><p className="task-hint">{removed.length} / 8개 제거</p></>}
  {kind==='filter'&&<><p className="task-instruction">필터에 붙은 이물질을 모두 눌러 제거하세요.</p><div className="filter-board">{Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=><button key={i} aria-label={`필터 이물질 ${i+1}`} disabled={removed.includes(i)} className={removed.includes(i)?'clean':''} onClick={()=>remove(i)}>{removed.includes(i)?<Check/>:<Trash2/>}</button>)}</div><p className="task-hint">정화율 {Math.round(removed.length/6*100)}%</p></>}
  {(kind==='upload'||kind==='fuel')&&<><p className="task-instruction">{kind==='upload'?'전송 버튼을 누른 채 연결을 유지하세요.':'밸브를 누른 채 연료통을 채우세요.'} 손을 떼면 멈춥니다.</p><div className={`device-tank ${kind}`}><div style={{height:`${amount}%`}}/>{kind==='fuel'?<Fuel size={44}/>:<Upload size={44}/>}<strong>{Math.floor(amount)}%</strong></div><button className="primary wide hold-control" disabled={done} onPointerDown={e=>{e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);held.current=true;}} onPointerUp={()=>held.current=false} onPointerCancel={()=>held.current=false} onKeyDown={e=>{if(e.key===' '||e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();held.current=true;}}} onKeyUp={()=>held.current=false} onBlur={()=>held.current=false}>{done?'처리 완료':kind==='fuel'?'누르고 연료 주입':'누르고 데이터 전송'}</button></>}
  {kind==='align'&&<><p className="task-instruction">세 축의 값을 목표에 맞춰 항로를 정렬하세요. 허용 오차 ±3.</p><div className="alignment-panel">{levels.map((v,i)=><label key={i}><span><SlidersHorizontal size={17}/> {'XYZ'[i]}축 <b>목표 {challenge.solution[i]}</b><em>{v}</em></span><input aria-label={`${'XYZ'[i]}축 정렬`} type="range" min="0" max="100" value={v} onChange={e=>setLevels(a=>a.map((n,j)=>j===i?Number(e.target.value):n))}/></label>)}</div></>}
  {kind==='switches'&&<><p className="task-instruction">각 차단기를 위에 표시된 목표 ON / OFF 상태로 맞추세요.</p><div className="breaker-grid">{switches.map((v,i)=><button key={i} aria-label={`${i+1}번 차단기`} aria-pressed={!!v} className={v?'on':''} onClick={()=>setSwitches(a=>a.map((n,j)=>j===i?1-n:n))}><small>목표 {challenge.solution[i]?'ON':'OFF'}</small><Power/><strong>{v?'ON':'OFF'}</strong></button>)}</div></>}
  {done&&<p className="task-hint success" role="status"><Check size={16}/> 장치 작동 완료</p>}
  <button className="primary wide finish-task" disabled={!done||busy||elapsed<duration} onClick={()=>finish(answer)}>{busy?'처리 중…':done&&elapsed<duration?'장치 확인 중…':'미션 완료'}</button>
 </div>;
}

'use client';

const COLORS=['#78d6ef','#ee986f','#c399ef','#a0d97f'];
const QUICK_SPEECH=['같이 가요!','어디서 왔어요?','미션 하는 중!','잠깐 기다려요','누가 지나갔어요?'];
type Session={code:string;token:string};
type Props={sound:boolean;room:View;session:Session;accept:(v:View)=>void;send:(action:string,extra?:Record<string,unknown>)=>Promise<boolean>;identity:()=>void};
export default function FieldGame({sound,room,session,accept,send,identity}:Props){
 const reach=useRef<Reach|null>(null);const fps=room.config.mapId==='nereid'&&!room.host,fpsRef=useRef(fps);fpsRef.current=fps;const look=useRef<Look>({yaw:0,pitch:0}),sceneFrame=useRef<SceneFrame|null>(null);const [follow,setFollow]=useState('');const followRef=useRef(follow);followRef.current=follow;const spectator=useRef<Point>(emergencyFor(room.config));
 const map=useMemo(()=>mapFor(room.config),[room.config.count,room.config.mapId]),LOCATIONS=map.locations,CORRIDORS=map.corridors,STATIONS=map.stations,EMERGENCY=emergencyFor(room.config),REPAIR=repairFor(room.config);
 const [speechOpen,setSpeechOpen]=useState(false),[speechText,setSpeechText]=useState('');const speechInput=useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null),composing=useRef(false),speechClock=useRef(0);
 const [caseId,setCaseId]=useState('');const activeCase=room.field?.cases.find(c=>c.id===caseId)||room.field?.cases.at(-1);
 const [hostRoom,setHostRoom]=useState('');const hostFocus=useRef<string|null>(null),viewTransform=useRef({x:0,y:0,scale:1,width:1,height:1}),zoom=useRef<number|null>(null);
 const focusRoom=(id:string)=>{hostFocus.current=id||null;setHostRoom(id);};
 const [liveStatus,setLiveStatus]=useState<LiveStatus>({connected:false,rtt:0,checkpointAge:0}),[netDebug,setNetDebug]=useState(false);
 useEffect(()=>{if(new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('netdebug')==='1')setNetDebug(true);},[]);
 const pendingStrike=useRef<(Effect&{seenAt:number})|null>(null);
 const movementLock=useRef(false),lastUiFrame=useRef(0);
 const busyRef=useRef(false),effects=useRef<(Effect&{seenAt:number})[]>([]),motion=useRef<Record<string,{phase:number;stride:number;face:number;x:number;y:number}>>({}),camera=useRef<Point|null>(null),soundRef=useRef(sound),audioRef=useRef<AudioContext|null>(null);soundRef.current=sound;
 const canvas=useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null),wrapper=useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null),keys=useRef(new Set<string>()),stick=useRef<Point>({x:0,y:0}),thumb=useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null),joystick=useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null),latest=useRef(room),halt=useRef(false),acceptRef=useRef(accept);
 // All field networking (prediction, live socket, HTTP fallback, interpolation) lives in
 // FieldNet; this component only draws what it holds and forwards input and actions.
 const cueRef=useRef<(kind:string)=>void>(()=>{}),netRef=useRef<FieldNet|null>(null),netKey=useRef('');
 if(!netRef.current||netKey.current!==session.code+session.token){netKey.current=session.code+session.token;netRef.current=new FieldNet({code:session.code,token:session.token,room,spawn:spawn(Math.max(0,room.players.findIndex(p=>p.id===room.me)),room.config),
  liveUrl:()=>`${location.protocol==='https:'?'wss:':'ws:'}//${location.host}/api/live`,
  http:async body=>{const controller=new AbortController(),timeout=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),body.action==='frame'?4000:6000);try{const res=await fetch('/api/field',{method:'POST',signal:controller.signal,headers:{Authorization:`Bearer ${session.token}`,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});const data=await res.json() as FrameReply;if(!res.ok)throw Error(data.error||(body.action==='frame'?'연결을 확인해 주세요.':'다시 시도해 주세요.'));return data;}finally{clearTimeout(timeout);}},
  onRoom:v=>{latest.current=v;acceptRef.current(v);},
  onFrame:(f,data)=>{if(performance.now()-lastUiFrame.current>110||data.room){setFrame(f);lastUiFrame.current=performance.now();}setFault('');},
  onReset:p=>{camera.current={...p};},
  onEffect:e=>{effects.current.push({...e,seenAt:performance.now()});cueRef.current(e.kind);},
  onFault:message=>setFault(message),
  onLiveStatus:state=>setLiveStatus(old=>old.connected===state.connected&&old.rtt===state.rtt&&Math.abs(old.checkpointAge-state.checkpointAge)<100?old:state)});}
 const net=netRef.current;
 const [frame,setFrame]=useState<FieldSnapshot|null>(null),[hud,setHud]=useState<Point>(()=>({...netRef.current!.visual})),[busy,setBusy]=useState(false),[mapOpen,setMapOpen]=useState(false),[taskOpen,setTaskOpen]=useState(false),[notesOpen,setNotesOpen]=useState(false),[chatOpen,setChatOpen]=useState(false),[chat,setChat]=useState(''),[fault,setFault]=useState(''),[selected,setSelected]=useState(''),[mission,setMission]=useState(''),[clock,setClock]=useState(Date.now()),[hit,setHit]=useState(false),[ventOpen,setVentOpen]=useState(false),[vitalsOpen,setVitalsOpen]=useState(false);
 latest.current=room;net.syncRoom(room);acceptRef.current=accept;halt.current=!!room.field?.watching||!!room.field?.vent||taskOpen||mapOpen||notesOpen||chatOpen||ventOpen||vitalsOpen||movementLock.current||room.paused;
 const me=room.players.find(p=>p.id===room.me),role=room.myRole,red=isImpostor(role)||role==='mafia'||role==='spy',f=room.field!,progress=f.total?Math.floor(f.done/f.total*100):0;
 const previousAnnouncement=useRef(room.announcement),previousAlive=useRef(me?.alive);
 useEffect(()=>{if(previousAnnouncement.current!==room.announcement&&room.announcement)toast(room.announcement,{duration:7000});previousAnnouncement.current=room.announcement;},[room.announcement]);
 useEffect(()=>{let t:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>|undefined;if(previousAlive.current&&me&&!me.alive){setHit(true);t=setTimeout(()=>setHit(false),1800);}previousAlive.current=me?.alive;return()=>clearTimeout(t);},[me?.alive]);
 const serverNow=clock+net.serverOffset;
 useEffect(()=>{if(taskOpen&&!room.field?.challenge)setTaskOpen(false);},[room.field?.challenge,taskOpen]);
 useEffect(()=>{const t=setInterval(()=>{if(document.hidden)return;const v=netRef.current!.visual;setHud(old=>distance(old,v)>1?{...v}:old);setClock(Date.now());},120);return()=>clearInterval(t);},[]);
 cueRef.current=(kind:string)=>{if(!soundRef.current)return;try{const Ctx=window.AudioContext||(window as unknown as {webkitAudioContext:typeof AudioContext}).webkitAudioContext;const ctx=audioRef.current||new Ctx();audioRef.current=ctx;void ctx.resume();const o=ctx.createOscillator(),g=ctx.createGain();const hit=kind==='attack'||kind==='blocked';o.type=hit?'triangle':'sine';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(hit?180:kind==='task'?740:430,ctx.currentTime);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(hit?45:kind==='task'?1200:210,ctx.currentTime+.2);g.gain.setValueAtTime(hit?.13:.055,ctx.currentTime);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,ctx.currentTime+.3);o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);o.start();o.stop(ctx.currentTime+.32);}catch{}};
 // Spare figure sheets are not rendered while the game screen is open (see doll-sprites).
 useEffect(()=>{let paused=false,gone=false;void import('@/lib/doll-sprites').then(m=>{if(gone)return;m.pauseSheetWarmup(true);paused=true;});return()=>{gone=true;if(paused)void import('@/lib/doll-sprites').then(m=>m.pauseSheetWarmup(false));};},[]);
 useEffect(()=>{const n=net;n.start();if(new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('netdebug')==='1')Reflect.set(window,'__fieldNet',n);/* for measurement scripts (tests/browser) */const wake=()=>n.wake();window.addEventListener('focus',wake);return()=>{window.removeEventListener('focus',wake);n.stop();};},[net]);
 const act=async(action:string,extra:Record<string,unknown>={})=>{if(busyRef.current)return false;busyRef.current=true;movementLock.current=['taskStart','cameraStart','ventEnter','ventMove','ventExit','emergency','report','repair','commsRepair'].includes(action);setBusy(true);try{const current=latest.current;let contact:Promise<unknown>|null=null;if(fpsRef.current&&['taskStart','emergency','cameraStart','repair','commsRepair'].includes(action)){
 const station=mapFor(current.config).stations.find(s=>s.id===extra.station),point=action==='taskStart'?station:action==='emergency'?emergencyFor(current.config):action==='cameraStart'?securityConsole(current.config):action==='repair'?repairFor(current.config):communicationsConsole(current.config);
 if(point){reach.current={started:performance.now(),target:{action:action as DeviceTarget['action'],station:station?.id,point,height:action==='taskStart'?1.13:1.01,label:''}};keys.current.clear();stick.current={x:0,y:0};contact=new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,CONTACT_MS));}
 }const before=latest.current.intel.length;
  // The request leaves together with the reach animation; the panel opens when both the hand
  // has touched the device and the server has answered (the longer of the two, not their sum).
  const [data]=await Promise.all([netRef.current!.act(action,extra),contact]);if(!data)return false;if(data.room&&data.room.intel.length>before)toast(data.room.intel.at(-1)?.text);return true;}catch(e){toast.error((e as Error).name==='AbortError'?'응답이 늦어지고 있습니다. 연결 상태를 확인해 주세요.':(e as Error).message);return false;}finally{movementLock.current=false;busyRef.current=false;setBusy(false);}};
 const speechUnavailable=room.host||room.paused||!!f.vent||!!f.watching||!!f.challenge||f.invisibleUntil>serverNow;
 const openSpeech=()=>{if(speechUnavailable)return;setSpeechOpen(true);requestAnimationFrame(()=>speechInput.current?.focus({preventScroll:true}));};
 const speak=async(text:string,typed=false)=>{if(speechUnavailable||busyRef.current||!text.trim()||Date.now()<speechClock.current)return;const ok=await act('say',{text});if(ok){speechClock.current=Date.now()+1500;if(typed){setSpeechText('');setSpeechOpen(false);speechInput.current?.blur();canvas.current?.focus({preventScroll:true});}}};
 const speechAction=useRef(openSpeech);speechAction.current=openSpeech;
 const tasks=f.tasks.map(t=>({...t,...STATIONS.find(s=>s.id===t.station)!}));
 const nearestTask=tasks.filter(t=>!t.done).sort((a,b)=>distance(hud,a)-distance(hud,b))[0];
 const nearbyTask=nearestTask&&distance(hud,nearestTask)<100?nearestTask:null;
 const bodies=(frame?.bodies||[]).filter(b=>distance(hud,b)<120).sort((a,b)=>distance(hud,a)-distance(hud,b));
 const body=bodies[0];const cooldown=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((f.cooldown-serverNow)/1000));
 const field2=f.rules===2,guardian=f.guardian;
 const nearby=(frame?.people||[]).map(p=>({...p,...net.interpolated[p.id]})).filter(p=>p.alive&&p.id!==room.me&&distance(hud,p)<150&&(!fps||inFront(hud,p,look.current.yaw))).sort((a,b)=>distance(hud,a)-distance(hud,b));
 const target=nearby.find(p=>p.id===selected)||nearby[0];const killTarget=nearby.find(p=>p.id===selected&&distance(hud,p)<93&&(role==='lonewolf'||!isImpostor(room.players.find(x=>x.id===p.id)?.role)))||nearby.find(p=>distance(hud,p)<93&&(role==='lonewolf'||!isImpostor(room.players.find(x=>x.id===p.id)?.role)))||null;
 const skillCooldown=Math.max(0,Math.ceil((f.skillCooldown-serverNow)/1000)),invisible=f.invisibleUntil>serverNow;
 const currentVent=ventsFor(room.config).find(v=>f.vent?v.id===f.vent.vent:distance(hud,v)<82),canVent=!!me?.alive&&(red||role==='engineer');
 const skillReady=(guardian||['scientist','tracker','shapeshifter','phantom','investigator','survivor','arsonist','thief'].includes(role||''))&&!skillCooldown;
 const passive=!guardian&&['crewmate','noisemaker','impostor','engineer','viper','judge','jester','scavenger','lonewolf','executioner','escapeartist','coroner'].includes(role||'');
 const deadAbility=role==='medium'||role==='priest';const abilityTarget=deadAbility?body:target;const spent=['reporter','priest'].includes(role||'')&&room.used;
 const terminal=securityConsole(room.config),commsConsole=communicationsConsole(room.config),nearSecurity=atSecurity(hud,room.config),commsDown=f.commsUntil>serverNow;
 const openCameras=async()=>{if(me?.alive&&nearSecurity&&await act('cameraStart')){keys.current.clear();stick.current={x:0,y:0};}};
 const closeCameras=async()=>{await act('cameraStop');keys.current.clear();stick.current={x:0,y:0};};
 const openTask=async()=>{if(nearbyTask&&await act('taskStart',{station:nearbyTask.id})){setTaskOpen(true);keys.current.clear();stick.current={x:0,y:0};}};
 const enterVent=async()=>{if(f.vent){setVentOpen(true);return;}if(currentVent&&canVent&&!invisible&&await act('ventEnter')){keys.current.clear();stick.current={x:0,y:0};setVentOpen(true);}};
 const leaveVent=async()=>{if(!latest.current.field?.vent||await act('ventExit'))setVentOpen(false);};
 const skill=async()=>{if(!skillReady)return;const ok=await act('skill',{target:target?.id,caseId:activeCase?.id});if(ok&&role==='scientist')setVitalsOpen(true);if(ok&&role==='tracker')setMapOpen(true);};
 const kill=()=>{const own=net.visual,live=net.snapshot?.people||[],eligible=live.filter(p=>p.alive&&!p.vented&&!p.invisible&&p.id!==room.me&&(role==='lonewolf'||!isImpostor(room.players.find(x=>x.id===p.id)?.role))).map(p=>({...p,...net.interpolated[p.id]})).filter(p=>distance(own,p)<93&&(!fpsRef.current||inFront(own,p,look.current.yaw))).sort((a,b)=>distance(own,a)-distance(own,b));const killTarget=eligible.find(p=>p.id===selected)||eligible[0];if(!killTarget||!me?.alive||cooldown||invisible||busyRef.current||room.paused||(!red&&role!=='lonewolf'))return;
  // Immediate wind-up acknowledges the press; death/protection remain server-authoritative.
  pendingStrike.current={id:'pending',kind:'attack',actor:room.me!,target:killTarget.id,avatar:me.avatar,x:own.x,y:own.y,tx:killTarget.x,ty:killTarget.y,at:Date.now(),seenAt:performance.now(),acid:role==='viper'};
  void act('kill',{target:killTarget.id,targetAt:killTarget.at}).finally(()=>{pendingStrike.current=null;});
 };

 useEffect(()=>{
  const editable=(target:EventTarget|null)=>target instanceof HTMLElement&&!!target.closest('input,textarea,[contenteditable="true"],[role="dialog"],[role="listbox"],[role="combobox"][aria-expanded="true"]');
  const clear=()=>{keys.current.clear();stick.current={x:0,y:0};if(netRef.current)netRef.current.inputElapsed=0;};
  const down=(e:KeyboardEvent)=>{const code=e.code;if(code==='F3'&&!e.repeat){e.preventDefault();setNetDebug(v=>!v);return;}if(code==='KeyM'&&!e.repeat&&!e.isComposing&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey&&!(e.target instanceof HTMLElement&&e.target.closest('input,textarea,[contenteditable="true"]'))){e.preventDefault();mapShortcut.current();return;}if(editable(e.target))return;if(movementKey(code)){e.preventDefault();keys.current.add(code);return;}if(e.repeat||halt.current)return;if(code==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();speechAction.current();return;}if(code==='KeyE')void actionRef.current.openTask();if(code==='KeyR')actionRef.current.report();if(code==='KeyQ')actionRef.current.ability();if(code==='KeyF')actionRef.current.skill();if(code==='KeyV')actionRef.current.vent();if(code==='KeyC')actionRef.current.cameras();};
  const up=(e:KeyboardEvent)=>{if(movementKey(e.code))e.preventDefault();keys.current.delete(e.code);};

  window.addEventListener('keydown',down,{passive:false});window.addEventListener('keyup',up,{passive:false});window.addEventListener('blur',clear);document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',visibility);document.addEventListener('focusin',focus);
  return()=>{window.removeEventListener('keydown',down);window.removeEventListener('keyup',up);window.removeEventListener('blur',clear);document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange',visibility);document.removeEventListener('focusin',focus);};

  <Dialog open={notesOpen} onOpenChange={setNotesOpen}><DialogContent className="game-dialog"><DialogTitle>나의 단서 수첩</DialogTitle><DialogDescription>공개 사건 기록과 나에게만 보이는 조사 단서입니다.</DialogDescription>{role==='investigator'&&<CaseNotebook room={room} send={send} selected={activeCase?.id} onSelect={setCaseId}/>}<div className="field-notes"><h4>공개 사건</h4>{room.events.slice(-6).reverse().map(n=><div key={n.id}><small>탐색 {n.day}</small><p>{n.text}</p></div>)}<h4>나만의 단서</h4>{room.intel.length?room.intel.slice().reverse().map((n,i)=><div key={i}><small>탐색 {n.day}</small><p>{n.text}</p></div>):<p>아직 조사 기록이 없습니다. 이동하며 단서를 모으세요.</p>}</div></DialogContent></Dialog>
  <Dialog open={chatOpen} onOpenChange={setChatOpen}><DialogContent className="game-dialog"><DialogTitle>{privateChannel==='ghost'?'유령 대화':'임포스터 비밀 대화'}</DialogTitle><DialogDescription>이 대화는 같은 채널의 참가자에게만 보입니다.</DialogDescription><div className="field-chat">{room.messages.filter(m=>m.channel===privateChannel).map(m=><div className="message" key={m.id}><span>{m.name}</span><p>{m.text}</p></div>)}</div><form className="chat-form" onSubmit={async e=>{e.preventDefault();if(await send('chat',{text:chat}))setChat('');}}><input value={chat} maxLength={200} onChange={e=>setChat(e.target.value)} aria-label="비밀 메시지" placeholder="메시지 입력"/><button disabled={!chat.trim()} aria-label="전송"><Send size={18}/></button></form></DialogContent></Dialog>
 </section>;
}
// Network diagnostics (F3 or ?netdebug=1): measured on this device, not estimated.
function NetPanel({net,close}:{net:FieldNet;close:()=>void}){
 const [s,setS]=useState(()=>net.stats.summary());const [,tick]=useState(0);
 useEffect(()=>{const t=setInterval(()=>{setS(net.stats.summary());tick(n=>n+1);},500);return()=>clearInterval(t);},[net]);
 const ms=(v:number|null|undefined)=>v===null||v===undefined?'–':`${Math.round(v)}ms`,sv=s.server;
 const rows:[string,string][]=[['전송',net.healthy()?'WebSocket (실시간)':'HTTP 대체'],['RTT',ms(s.rttMs)],['프레임 간격 p95',ms(s.frameGapP95)],['원격 샘플 나이 p50/p95',`${ms(s.sampleAgeP50)} / ${ms(s.sampleAgeP95)}`],['원격 표시 지연 p50/p95',`${ms(s.renderDelayP50)} / ${ms(s.renderDelayP95)}`],['입력 확인 p50/p95',`${ms(s.ackP50)} / ${ms(s.ackP95)}`],['보정 오차 최대',s.correctionMax===null?'–':`${s.correctionMax}px`],['행동 응답 p50',ms(s.actionP50)],['마지막 공격 요청→확정',ms(s.killLast)],['프레임 작업 p95 / 간격 p95',`${ms(s.frameWorkP95)} / ${ms(s.frameDtP95)}`],...(net.stats.view3d?[['3D 그리기 · 해상도',`${ms(net.stats.render3d.q(.95))} · ${Math.round(net.stats.view3d.quality*100)}% · ${net.stats.view3d.fps}fps`] as [string,string]]:[]),['미확인 입력',String(net.pending.length)],['재전송·재기준·재연결',`${s.counts.resend||0} · ${s.counts.rebase||0} · ${net.live?.reconnects||0}`]];
 if(sv)rows.push(['서버 인스턴스',`${sv.instance} · 이 인스턴스 ${sv.peersHere}명 / 전체 ${sv.instances}개`],['D1 RTT · 초당 문장',`${ms(sv.dbRttMs)} · ${sv.dbStatementsPerSec}`],['D1 쿼리(이 연결)',`${sv.queries} / ${sv.budget}`],['D1 오류 · 재활용',`${sv.dbErrors} · ${sv.recycles}`],['체크포인트 나이',ms(sv.checkpointAge)]);
 return <div className="net-debug" role="status" aria-label="네트워크 진단"><div><strong>네트워크 진단</strong><button onClick={close} aria-label="진단 닫기"><X size={14}/></button></div><dl>{rows.map(([k,v])=><div key={k}><dt>{k}</dt><dd>{v}</dd></div>)}</dl><small>F3으로 열고 닫기</small></div>;
}
function BasicTaskPuzzle({challenge,busy,finish}:{challenge:Challenge;busy:boolean;finish:(a:number[])=>Promise<void>}){
 const kind=STATIONS.find(s=>s.id===challenge.station)!.kind;
 const [left,setLeft]=useState<number|null>(null),[pairs,setPairs]=useState<number[]>([]),[digits,setDigits]=useState<number[]>([]),[pattern,setPattern]=useState<number[]>([]),[step,setStep]=useState(1),[show,setShow]=useState(true),[hits,setHits]=useState<number[]>([]),[needle,setNeedle]=useState(0),[mistake,setMistake]=useState(false);
 const start=useRef(Date.now());const needleRef=useRef(0);
 useEffect(()=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>setShow(false),2300);let raf=0;const draw=()=>{const n=(Math.sin((Date.now()-start.current)/650)+1)*50;needleRef.current=n;setNeedle(n);raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);};if(kind==='calibrate')raf=requestAnimationFrame(draw);return()=>{clearTimeout(timer);cancelAnimationFrame(raf);};},[kind]);
 const mismatch=()=>{setMistake(true);setTimeout(()=>setMistake(false),450);};
 const done=kind==='wires'?pairs.length===4:kind==='code'?digits.length===4:kind==='sequence'?step===7:kind==='pattern'?pattern.length===4:hits.length===3;
 const answer=kind==='wires'?challenge.solution:kind==='code'?digits:kind==='sequence'?[1,2,3,4,5,6]:kind==='pattern'?[...pattern].sort((a,b)=>a-b):hits;
 return <div className={`task-puzzle ${mistake?'mistake':''}`}>
  <div className="task-instruction">{kind==='wires'?'왼쪽과 오른쪽에서 같은 기호를 연결하세요.':kind==='code'?'표시된 4자리 코드를 기억하고 입력하세요.':kind==='sequence'?'흩어진 숫자를 1부터 6까지 순서대로 누르세요.':kind==='pattern'?'켜진 4개의 칸을 기억했다가 똑같이 선택하세요.':'포인터가 초록 영역에 들어왔을 때 멈추세요. 3회 성공하면 완료됩니다.'}</div>
  {kind==='wires'&&<div className="wires-puzzle"><div>{[0,1,2,3].map(i=><button key={i} disabled={pairs.includes(i)} className={left===i?'armed':''} style={{'--wire':COLORS[i]} as CSSProperties} onClick={()=>setLeft(i)}>{['◆','●','▲','■'][i]} {pairs.includes(i)?'연결 완료':['A','B','C','D'][i]}</button>)}</div><div className="wire-gaps">{[0,1,2,3].map(i=><span key={i} style={{background:pairs.includes(i)?COLORS[i]:'#344658'}}/>)}</div><div>{[0,1,2,3].map(i=>{const symbol=challenge.solution.indexOf(i);return<button key={i} disabled={pairs.includes(symbol)} style={{'--wire':COLORS[symbol]} as CSSProperties} onClick={()=>{if(left!==null&&challenge.solution[left]===i){setPairs([...pairs,left]);setLeft(null);}else mismatch();}}>{['◆','●','▲','■'][symbol]} {['A','B','C','D'][symbol]}</button>;})}</div></div>}
  {kind==='code'&&<><div className="code-display">{show?challenge.solution.join(' '):digits.length?digits.join(' '):'— — — —'}</div><p className="task-hint">{show?'코드를 기억하세요…':'기억한 숫자를 입력하세요.'}</p><div className="number-pad">{[1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8,9].map(n=><button disabled={show||digits.length>=4} key={n} onClick={()=>setDigits([...digits,n])}>{n}</button>)}<button onClick={()=>{setDigits([]);setShow(true);setTimeout(()=>setShow(false),2300);}}>다시 보기</button><button disabled={show||digits.length>=4} onClick={()=>setDigits([...digits,0])}>0</button><button onClick={()=>setDigits(digits.slice(0,-1))}>지우기</button></div></>}
  {kind==='sequence'&&<><div className="sequence-status">다음 숫자 <b>{step<=6?step:'완료'}</b></div><div className="sequence-grid">{[4,1,6,3,5,2].map(n=><button key={n} disabled={n<step} onClick={()=>{if(n===step)setStep(step+1);else{setStep(1);mismatch();}}}>{n<step?<Check/>:n}</button>)}</div></>}
  {kind==='pattern'&&<><p className="task-hint">{show?'켜진 칸을 기억하세요…':`${pattern.length} / 4개 선택`}</p><div className="pattern-grid">{Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=><button key={i} aria-label={`${i+1}번 칸`} aria-pressed={pattern.includes(i)} className={(show?challenge.solution.includes(i):pattern.includes(i))?'on':''} disabled={show} onClick={()=>setPattern(pattern.includes(i)?pattern.filter(n=>n!==i):[...pattern,i])}/>)}</div><button className="secondary wide" onClick={()=>{setPattern([]);setShow(true);setTimeout(()=>setShow(false),2300);}}>패턴 다시 보기</button></>}
  {kind==='calibrate'&&<><div className="calibration-meter"><div className="good-zone"/><i style={{left:`${needle}%`}}/></div><div className="calibration-hits">{[0,1,2].map(i=><span key={i} className={hits.length>i?'on':''}>{hits.length>i?<Check size={21}/>:i+1}</span>)}</div><button className="primary wide" disabled={hits.length===3} onClick={()=>{const n=needleRef.current;if(n>=43&&n<=57)setHits([...hits,Math.round(n*10)/10]);else mismatch();}}>포인터 멈추기</button></>}
  <button className="primary wide finish-task" disabled={!done||busy} onClick={()=>finish(answer)}>{busy?'처리 중…':'미션 완료'}</button>
 </div>;
}

function TaskPuzzle(props:{mapConfig:import('@/lib/game').Config;challenge:Challenge;busy:boolean;finish:(a:number[])=>Promise<void>}){const kind=mapFor(props.mapConfig).stations.find(s=>s.id===props.challenge.station)!.kind;if(kind.startsWith('science_'))return <SciencePuzzle {...props} kind={kind as import('@/lib/science').ScienceKind}/>;return ['wires','code','sequence','pattern','calibrate'].includes(kind)?<BasicTaskPuzzle {...props}/>:<ExtendedPuzzle {...props}/>;}
