export const DOLL_NAMES=['루카','세린','테오','로제','미아','리안','엘리','레온','카이','유나','노아','루비','하린','이안','루나','시온'];


======================================================================
FILE: lib/doll-portraits.ts
======================================================================
import * as T from 'three';
import {createDoll,DOLL} from './doll-character';
import {stageLights} from './doll-stage';
import {withDollRenderer,DOLL_CANVAS} from './doll-render';
const cache=new Map<number,Promise<string>>(),wanted=new Map<number,number>();
// The card shows the figure exactly as other players see it in the 3D map: same model, same
// light rig, tone mapping and floor, seen from first-person eye height at a few steps.
// A portrait is only rendered while some card still waits for it: cards pass a signal that
// aborts when they unmount, and a queued job nobody waits for is dropped (the avatar picker
// of a player who rejoins straight into a game used to keep rendering 16 portraits in play).
export function dollPortrait(index:number,signal?:AbortSignal):Promise<string>{const id=((index%16)+16)%16;wanted.set(id,(wanted.get(id)||0)+1);signal?.addEventListener('abort',()=>wanted.set(id,(wanted.get(id)||1)-1),{once:true});const cached=cache.get(id);if(cached)return cached;
 const result=withDollRenderer(async renderer=>{if(!wanted.get(id))throw new DOMException('portrait no longer shown','AbortError');
  const scene=new T.Scene();scene.background=new T.Color('#102c40');const camera=new T.PerspectiveCamera(34,DOLL_CANVAS.width/DOLL_CANVAS.height,.1,30);camera.position.set(1.3,1.43,-3.2);camera.lookAt(0,DOLL.height*.53,0);
  stageLights(scene);
  const doll=createDoll(id);doll.head.rotation.y=-.08;scene.add(doll.root);
  const floor=new T.Mesh(new T.CircleGeometry(.62,40),new T.MeshLambertMaterial({color:'#485d64'}));floor.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;floor.position.y=-.002;scene.add(floor);
  // Encoded off the main thread (toBlob) rather than with the blocking toDataURL.
  renderer.render(scene,camera);const blob=await new Promise<Blob|null>(resolve=>renderer.domElement.toBlob(resolve,'image/webp',.93));doll.dispose();floor.geometry.dispose();floor.material.dispose();if(!blob)throw Error('portrait');return URL.createObjectURL(blob);});
 cache.set(id,result);result.catch(()=>cache.delete(id));return result;}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/doll-render.ts
======================================================================
import * as T from 'three';
// One WebGL renderer for every offscreen doll render (selection portraits and the top-view
// sprite sheets), never one context per card. Jobs run one per frame as early as possible:
// most of them then finish while the lobby or the game is still loading (deferring them to
// idle time moved the same work into the first seconds of play, measured). The canvas
// keeps one size (DOLL_CANVAS; sprite frames use a corner of it): resizing it for every job
// reallocated the drawing buffer each time. Shaders compile without blocking error checks. The context
// is kept while jobs keep coming and released after a long quiet spell or when the page is
// hidden; it is never force-lost (WEBGL_lose_context blocked the main thread for 120-150 ms
// in the middle of play, measured in headless Chromium).
// The canvas is kept, so a renderer made again later reuses the same WebGL context instead of
// piling up contexts that only garbage collection would free.
let renderer:T.WebGLRenderer|undefined,canvas:HTMLCanvasElement|undefined,idle:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>|undefined,queue:Promise<unknown>=Promise.resolve();
const release=()=>{renderer?.dispose();renderer=undefined;};
if(typeof document!=='undefined')document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden){clearTimeout(idle);queue=queue.then(release,release);}});
export const DOLL_CANVAS={width:384,height:448};
export function withDollRenderer<R>(job:(r:T.WebGLRenderer)=>R|Promise<R>):Promise<R>{
 const run=queue.then(async()=>{
  await new Promise<void>(resolve=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>resolve()));clearTimeout(idle);
  canvas||=document.createElement('canvas');if(!renderer){renderer=new T.WebGLRenderer({canvas,antialias:true,alpha:true,preserveDrawingBuffer:true});renderer.debug.checkShaderErrors=false;renderer.setPixelRatio(1);renderer.setSize(DOLL_CANVAS.width,DOLL_CANVAS.height,false);}
  renderer.setViewport(0,0,DOLL_CANVAS.width,DOLL_CANVAS.height);renderer.outputColorSpace=T.SRGBColorSpace;renderer.toneMapping=T.ACESFilmicToneMapping;renderer.toneMappingExposure=1.0;
  try{return await job(renderer);}finally{idle=setTimeout(release,30000);}
 });
 queue=run.then(()=>{},()=>{});return run;
}

import * as T from 'three';
import {createDoll,DOLL} from './doll-character';
import {stageLights} from './doll-stage';
import {withDollRenderer,DOLL_CANVAS} from './doll-render';
import {DOLL_FRAMES,type DollSheet} from './characters';
// Top-view (Aurora) character frames rendered from the same 3D figure as the 3D map and the
// selection portraits, with the same lights. Frames face right; the 2D renderer mirrors them.
const W=170,H=190,UNIT=80,ELEVATION=.38,TURN=.78;
const cache=new Map<number,Promise<DollSheet>>(),needed=new Set<number>();
// optional: a background warm-up, dropped when its turn comes while a game screen is open
// (unless the top view has asked for the same figure in the meantime).
export function dollSheet(index:number,optional=false):Promise<DollSheet>{const id=((index%16)+16)%16;if(!optional)needed.add(id);const cached=cache.get(id);if(cached)return cached;
 const result=withDollRenderer(async renderer=>{if(!needed.has(id)&&warmupPaused)throw new DOMException('sheet not needed now','AbortError');renderer.setClearColor(0x000000,0);
  const scene=new T.Scene();stageLights(scene);
  const camera=new T.OrthographicCamera(-W/2/UNIT,W/2/UNIT,H/2/UNIT,-H/2/UNIT,.1,30),target=new T.Vector3(0,.93,0);
  camera.position.copy(target).add(new T.Vector3(0,Math.sin(ELEVATION),-Math.cos(ELEVATION)).multiplyScalar(8));camera.lookAt(target);camera.updateMatrixWorld();
  const foot=new T.Vector3(0,0,0).project(camera),footX=(foot.x+1)/2*W,footY=(1-foot.y)/2*H;
  const doll=createDoll(id);doll.shadow.visible=false;scene.add(doll.root);
  // Frames are drawn in the bottom-left W x H of the shared canvas (WebGL viewport origin).
  renderer.setViewport(0,0,W,H);const top=DOLL_CANVAS.height-H;
  const sheet=document.createElement('canvas');sheet.width=W*DOLL_FRAMES;sheet.height=H;const ctx=sheet.getContext('2d')!;
  // 0 idle · 1-4 walk (same limb swing as the 3D map) · 5 knife strike · 6 strike without knife
  const strides=[0,.5,.18,-.5,-.18];
  for(let f=0;f<DOLL_FRAMES;f++){
   const stride=f<5?strides[f]:0,strike=f>=5;
   doll.root.rotation.y=strike?TURN+.45:TURN;doll.legs[0].rotation.x=stride;doll.legs[1].rotation.x=-stride;doll.arms[0].rotation.x=-stride*.65;doll.arms[1].rotation.x=strike?2.0:stride*.65;doll.knife.visible=f===5;
   renderer.clear();renderer.render(scene,camera);ctx.drawImage(renderer.domElement,0,top,W,H,f*W,0,W,H);
  }
  doll.dispose();
  return{image:sheet,frameW:W,frameH:H,footX,footY,scale:94/(DOLL.height*Math.cos(ELEVATION)*UNIT)};
 });
 cache.set(id,result);result.catch(()=>cache.delete(id));return result;}
// Background warm-up of a sheet (portraits warm the sheet of the figure they show). Paused
// while a game screen is open: the top view asks for its own figures, the 3D view never draws
// sheets, and rendering spare ones beside the game only cost frames.
let warmupPaused=0;
export function pauseSheetWarmup(paused:boolean){warmupPaused=Math.max(0,warmupPaused+(paused?1:-1));}
export function warmDollSheet(index:number){if(!warmupPaused)void dollSheet(index,true).catch(()=>{});}

import * as T from 'three';
// The 3D map's light rig (lib/scene3d.ts). Portraits and the top-view sprites use the same
// lights so a character looks the same on the selection card and in both maps.
export function stageLights(scene:T.Scene){
 scene.add(new T.HemisphereLight('#c5ebff','#314044',2.4));
 const sun=new T.DirectionalLight('#ffdfb3',2);sun.position.set(-25,50,12);scene.add(sun);
 const rim=new T.DirectionalLight('#93c8ef',1.1);rim.position.set(12,15,-15);scene.add(rim);
}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/field-net.ts

// Client-side networking for the field mode: input prediction and reconciliation, the
// live socket with HTTP fallback, remote-player interpolation and network diagnostics.
// FieldGame renders from this object; tests/sim drives exactly the same code.
import {LiveClient,LiveActionError,type LiveStatus} from './live-client.ts';
import {stepInput,reconcileMotion,type MoveInput} from './motion.ts';
import {inputSteps,INPUT_STEP_MS} from './input.ts';
import {updatePresence,retainedPeers,presenceOpacity,type Presence} from './peer-presence.ts';
import {pushRemote,sampleRemote,type RemoteTrack} from './remote-motion.ts';
import {moveTo,WORLD,type Point} from './map.ts';
import type {View} from './game';
import type {FieldSnapshot,Effect} from './field';

export type ServerDiag={instance:string;conn:string;peersHere:number;ownedHere:number;instances:number;dbRttMs:number;dbStatementsPerSec:number;queries:number;budget:number;dbErrors:number;recycles:number;checkpointAge:number};
export type FrameReply={room?:View;revision:number;frame:FieldSnapshot;error?:string;transport?:string;diag?:ServerDiag};
export type Person=FieldSnapshot['people'][number];
export type RenderedPerson=Person&{mine:boolean;opacity:number};
type Options={code:string;token:string;room:View;spawn:Point;liveUrl:string|null|(()=>string|null);http:(body:Record<string,unknown>)=>Promise<FrameReply>;
 onRoom?:(v:View)=>void;onReset?:(p:Point)=>void;onFrame?:(f:FieldSnapshot,data:FrameReply)=>void;onEffect?:(e:Effect)=>void;onFault?:(message:string)=>void;onLiveStatus?:(s:LiveStatus)=>void};

const RECENT_KEEP_MS=4000,HTTP_GRACE_MS=700,CAMERA_KEEPALIVE_MS=2000;
class Window{values:number[]=[];size:number;constructor(size=120){this.size=size;}push(v:number){if(!Number.isFinite(v))return;this.values.push(v);if(this.values.length>this.size)this.values.shift();}
 q(p:number){if(!this.values.length)return null;const s=[...this.values].sort((a,b)=>a-b);return Math.round(s[Math.min(s.length-1,Math.floor(p*s.length))]*10)/10;}
 get last(){return this.values.at(-1)??null;}}
// Rolling measurements shown by the in-game network panel (F3 / ?netdebug=1).
export class NetStats{
 ack=new Window();correction=new Window(60);sampleAge=new Window(400);frameGap=new Window();action=new Window(30);kill=new Window(20);frameWork=new Window(240);frameDt=new Window(240);renderDelay=new Window(400);render3d=new Window(240);view3d:{quality:number;fps:number}|null=null;
 counts:Record<string,number>={};server:ServerDiag|null=null;rtt=0;lastFrameAt=0;
 count(k:string){this.counts[k]=(this.counts[k]||0)+1;}
 // 3D view: script+draw time per frame, drawing resolution and frame-rate target (governor).
 record3d(ms:number,quality:number,fps:number){this.render3d.push(ms);this.view3d={quality,fps};}
 summary(){return{rttMs:this.rtt,frameGapP95:this.frameGap.q(.95),sampleAgeP50:this.sampleAge.q(.5),sampleAgeP95:this.sampleAge.q(.95),renderDelayP50:this.renderDelay.q(.5),renderDelayP95:this.renderDelay.q(.95),ackP50:this.ack.q(.5),ackP95:this.ack.q(.95),correctionMax:this.correction.q(1),actionP50:this.action.q(.5),killLast:this.kill.last,frameWorkP95:this.frameWork.q(.95),frameDtP95:this.frameDt.q(.95),counts:{...this.counts},server:this.server};}
}

export class FieldNet{
 room:View;snapshot:FieldSnapshot|null=null;
 local:Point;visual:Point;correction={x:0,y:0};
 pending:MoveInput[]=[];recent:(MoveInput&{t:number})[]=[];inputNumber=1;lastAck=0;warp=0;inputElapsed=0;wasMoving=false;
 bootstrapped=false;connected=false;busy=false;holdLive=false;resendFrom:number|null=null;
 tracks:Record<string,RemoteTrack>={};presence:Record<string,Presence<Person>>={};interpolated:Record<string,Point&{at?:number}>={};epoch=-1;
 live:LiveClient|null=null;stats=new NetStats();serverOffset=0;
 private sentAt=new Map<number,number>();private seenEffects=new Set<string>();private stopped=true;private pollTimer?:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;private polling=false;private lastCameraPoll=0;private boot?:ReturnType<typeof setInterval>;private unhealthySince=0;private hidden=()=>typeof document!=='undefined'&&document.hidden;
 private o:Options;
 constructor(o:Options){this.o=o;this.room=o.room;this.local={...o.spawn};this.visual={...o.spawn};}
 setHiddenProbe(fn:()=>boolean){this.hidden=fn;}
 // Room views also arrive from the page (host controls); keep the newest.
 syncRoom(v:View){if(v.revision>=this.room.revision)this.room=v;}
 healthy(){return !!this.live?.healthy();}
 // ---- frames -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 apply(data:FrameReply,source:'http'|'live'|'action'){
  const f=data.frame;
  if(data.revision<this.room.revision||f.own.warp!==undefined&&(f.own.warp||0)<this.warp||this.snapshot&&data.revision<=this.room.revision&&f.serverNow<this.snapshot.serverNow){this.stats.count('frame.stale');return false;}
  this.stats.count('frame.'+source);const received=performance.now();
  if(source==='live'){if(this.stats.lastFrameAt)this.stats.frameGap.push(received-this.stats.lastFrameAt);this.stats.lastFrameAt=received;}
  if(this.epoch!==f.seq){this.tracks={};this.presence={};this.interpolated={};this.epoch=f.seq;}
  const removed=new Set<string>();
  for(const body of f.bodies)removed.add(body.id);
  for(const e of f.effects||[]){if(e.kind==='attack'&&e.target)removed.add(e.target);if(e.kind==='vanish'||e.kind==='vent')removed.add(e.actor);}
  updatePresence(this.presence,f.people,received,removed);
  for(const id of Object.keys(this.tracks))if(!this.presence[id]){delete this.tracks[id];delete this.interpolated[id];}
  const me=data.room?.me??this.room.me;
  for(const person of f.people){
   // Sample age only means something for an actor that moved (an idle actor keeps its old motion time).
   const prev=this.tracks[person.id]?.samples.at(-1);if(person.id!==me&&prev&&(person.at||0)>prev.at&&Math.hypot(person.x-prev.x,person.y-prev.y)>.5)this.stats.sampleAge.push(f.serverNow-(person.at||f.serverNow)+this.stats.rtt/2);
   this.tracks[person.id]=pushRemote(this.tracks[person.id],{...person,at:person.at||f.serverNow,warp:person.warp||0},received);}
  if(data.room){this.room=data.room;this.o.onRoom?.(data.room);}
  if(data.diag)this.stats.server=data.diag;
  this.connected=true;this.serverOffset=f.serverNow-Date.now();this.snapshot=f;
  const own=f.own,ack=own.ack||0;
  if(!this.bootstrapped||this.warp!==(own.warp||0)){
   this.pending=[];this.recent=[];this.resendFrom=null;this.inputNumber=ack+1;this.lastAck=ack;this.warp=own.warp||0;this.local={x:own.x,y:own.y};this.visual={...this.local};this.bootstrapped=true;this.correction={x:0,y:0};this.sentAt.clear();this.o.onReset?.(this.local);
  }else if(ack>=this.lastAck){
   const t=performance.now();for(const [n,s] of this.sentAt)if(n<=ack){this.stats.ack.push(t-s);this.sentAt.delete(n);}
   const result=reconcileMotion(own,this.pending,this.room.config);
   for(const i of this.pending)if(i.n<=ack)this.recent.push({...i,t});
   const keep=t-RECENT_KEEP_MS;while(this.recent.length&&(this.recent[0].t<keep||this.recent.length>400))this.recent.shift();
   this.pending=result.pending;const dx=this.local.x-result.position.x,dy=this.local.y-result.position.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>.01){this.stats.correction.push(Math.hypot(dx,dy));this.stats.count('correction');}
   this.correction={x:this.correction.x+dx,y:this.correction.y+dy};this.local=result.position;this.lastAck=ack;this.resendFrom=null;
  }
  for(const e of f.effects||[])if(!this.seenEffects.has(e.id)){this.seenEffects.add(e.id);this.o.onEffect?.(e);}
  if(this.seenEffects.size>250)this.seenEffects=new Set(Array.from(this.seenEffects).slice(-100));
  this.o.onFrame?.(f,data);return true;
 }
 // The server lost (or never had) inputs after `after`: send them again, or, if we no
 // longer have them, continue from the server's pose.
 private resend(after:number,warp:number){
  if(warp!==this.warp||after>=this.lastAck&&this.pending.every(i=>i.n>after))return;
  const all=[...this.recent,...this.pending];if(after>=this.inputNumber-1)return;
  if(all.some(i=>i.n===after+1)){this.resendFrom=after;this.stats.count('resend');return;}
  const own=this.snapshot?.own;if(own&&(own.ack||0)===after)this.rebase(own);
 }
 private rebase(own:{x:number;y:number;ack?:number;warp?:number}){
  this.stats.count('rebase');this.correction={x:this.correction.x+this.local.x-own.x,y:this.correction.y+this.local.y-own.y};
  this.local={x:own.x,y:own.y};this.pending=[];this.recent=[];this.resendFrom=null;this.lastAck=own.ack||0;this.inputNumber=this.lastAck+1;this.sentAt.clear();
 }
 private outgoing(){if(this.resendFrom===null)return this.pending;const from=this.resendFrom;const seen=new Set<number>();const out:MoveInput[]=[];for(const i of [...this.recent,...this.pending])if(i.n>from&&!seen.has(i.n)){seen.add(i.n);out.push({n:i.n,dx:i.dx,dy:i.dy,ms:i.ms});}return out;}
 // ---- transports ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 start(){
  if(!this.stopped)return;this.stopped=false;
  void this.poll();
  const url=typeof this.o.liveUrl==='function'?this.o.liveUrl():this.o.liveUrl;
  if(url)this.boot=setInterval(()=>{if(!this.snapshot||this.live||this.stopped)return;const live=new LiveClient({url,code:this.o.code,token:this.o.token,
   input:()=>({seq:this.room.seq,warp:this.warp,hold:this.holdLive,inputs:this.holdLive?[]:this.outgoing()}),
   frame:data=>{this.apply(data,'live');},
   status:s=>{this.stats.rtt=s.rtt;this.o.onLiveStatus?.(s);},
   ready:m=>{this.stats.count('live.ready');this.resend(m.ack,m.warp);},
   resend:m=>this.resend(m.after,m.warp),
   rebase:m=>{if(m.own&&(m.own.warp||0)===this.warp)this.rebase(m.own);}});this.live=live;live.start();},100);
 }
 // One HTTP poll loop; wake() runs it early (tab focus) without starting a second loop.
 private async poll(){
  if(this.stopped||this.polling)return;this.polling=true;clearTimeout(this.pollTimer);const started=performance.now();let failed=false;
  try{await this.frameOnce();}catch(e){if(!this.stopped&&!this.busy){failed=true;this.connected=false;this.o.onFault?.(e instanceof Error?(e.name==='AbortError'?'응답을 기다리고 있습니다. 다시 연결합니다.':e.message):'연결을 확인해 주세요.');}}
  finally{this.polling=false;if(!this.stopped)this.pollTimer=setTimeout(()=>void this.poll(),this.hidden()?1000:failed?350:Math.max(12,(this.room.host?120:100)-(performance.now()-started)));}
 }
 stop(){this.stopped=true;clearTimeout(this.pollTimer);clearInterval(this.boot);this.live?.stop();this.live=null;}
 wake(){if(this.stopped||this.busy||this.polling||this.hidden())return;void this.poll();}
 // HTTP frame polling only while the live socket is unavailable for a while.
 async frameOnce(){
  const now=performance.now(),watching=!!this.room.field?.watching;
  // CCTV views arrive over the socket too; HTTP only renews the watch lease every 2 s.
  if(this.live?.healthy()){this.unhealthySince=0;if(!watching||now-this.lastCameraPoll<CAMERA_KEEPALIVE_MS)return;}
  else if(this.live&&!watching){this.unhealthySince||=now;if(now-this.unhealthySince<HTTP_GRACE_MS)return;}
  if(watching)this.lastCameraPoll=now;
  const r=this.room,data=await this.o.http({action:'frame',base:this.snapshot?.own,count:r.config.count,mapId:r.config.mapId,revision:r.revision,camera:!!r.field?.watching&&!this.hidden(),protocol:2,inputs:this.busy?[]:this.pending,warp:this.warp,code:this.o.code,seq:r.seq});
  if(data.error)throw Error(data.error);if(!this.stopped)this.apply(data,'http');
 }
 // Field action: over the live socket when healthy (same isolate, ordered after the
 // inputs), otherwise HTTP. A transport failure retries once over HTTP with the same
 // requestId, which the server de-duplicates.
 async act(action:string,extra:Record<string,unknown>={}):Promise<FrameReply|null>{
  if(this.busy)return null;this.busy=true;const started=performance.now();
  const body={action,protocol:2,inputs:this.outgoing(),warp:this.warp,code:this.o.code,seq:this.room.seq,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),...extra};
  try{
   let data:FrameReply|null=null;
   if(this.live?.healthy()){try{data=await this.live.act(body) as unknown as FrameReply;this.stats.count('act.live');}catch(e){if(e instanceof LiveActionError)throw e;this.stats.count('act.liveFailed');}}
   if(!data){this.holdLive=true;data=await this.o.http({...body,inputs:this.pending});this.stats.count('act.http');if(data.error)throw Error(data.error);}
   this.apply(data,'action');const ms=performance.now()-started;this.stats.action.push(ms);if(action==='kill')this.stats.kill.push(ms);return data;
  }finally{this.busy=false;this.holdLive=false;}
 }
 // ---- per rendered frame ---------------------------------------------------------------
 // Turns held keys/stick into fixed 20 ms inputs, predicts locally and eases corrections.
 step(dt:number,direction:Point,halt:boolean){
  const r=this.room,moving=Math.hypot(direction.x,direction.y)>.05&&!halt&&this.connected&&!r.host&&this.pending.length<180;
  const inputDirection=moving?direction:{x:0,y:0};
  if(this.bootstrapped&&this.connected&&!r.host&&!halt&&this.pending.length<180&&(moving||this.wasMoving)){
   const batch=moving?inputSteps(this.inputElapsed,dt*1000):{steps:1,remainder:0};this.inputElapsed=batch.remainder;
   const t=performance.now();
   for(let i=0;i<batch.steps&&this.pending.length<180;i++){const input={n:this.inputNumber++,dx:inputDirection.x,dy:inputDirection.y,ms:INPUT_STEP_MS};this.pending.push(input);this.sentAt.set(input.n,t);this.local=stepInput(this.local,input,r.config);}
  }else this.inputElapsed=0;this.wasMoving=moving;
  const predicted=stepInput(this.local,{n:0,dx:inputDirection.x,dy:inputDirection.y,ms:this.inputElapsed},r.config);
  const decay=Math.exp(-dt*18);this.correction.x*=decay;this.correction.y*=decay;
  this.visual=moveTo(predicted,{x:predicted.x+this.correction.x,y:predicted.y+this.correction.y},Math.hypot(this.correction.x,this.correction.y),r.config);
  return moving;
 }
 // Everybody to draw this frame: live snapshot plus briefly retained peers, each at its
 // interpolated position. Retained peers are never attack targets (they are not in the
 // snapshot people list used for targeting).
 render(time:number,opts:{retain:boolean}):RenderedPerson[]{
  const s=this.snapshot,r=this.room;if(!s)return[];
  const live=s.people,ids=new Set(live.map(p=>p.id));
  const held=opts.retain?retainedPeers(this.presence,time,this.visual,s.radius||WORLD.vision,r.config).filter(p=>!ids.has(p.id)):[];
  const out:RenderedPerson[]=[];const serverNow=Date.now()+this.serverOffset;
  for(const p of [...live,...held]){const mine=p.id===r.me;let pos:Point&{at?:number}=p;
   if(mine)pos=this.visual;else{const track=this.tracks[p.id];if(track){pos=sampleRemote(track,r.paused?(track.painted??time):time,r.config);const a=track.samples;if(pos.at&&a.length>1&&Math.hypot(a[a.length-1].x-a[a.length-2].x,a[a.length-1].y-a[a.length-2].y)>.5)this.stats.renderDelay.push(serverNow-pos.at);}}
   this.interpolated[p.id]=pos;out.push({...p,x:pos.x,y:pos.y,at:pos.at??p.at,mine,opacity:mine?1:presenceOpacity(this.presence[p.id],time)});}
  return out;
 }
}


======================================================================

export const FIELD_ROLES = {
 executioner:{name:'처형인',en:'EXECUTIONER',color:'#d7a6ce',description:'비밀 목표를 투표로 추방시키는 중립',mission:'나에게만 보이는 목표를 일반 투표로 추방시키고 내가 살아 있으면 혼자 승리합니다. 판사의 판결이나 공격으로는 승리하지 않습니다. 목표가 다른 이유로 탈락하면 새 목표를 받습니다. 공격 능력은 없습니다.',index:4,art:'special' as const},

export type FieldRole=keyof typeof FIELD_ROLES;
export const FIELD_ROLE_KEYS=Object.keys(FIELD_ROLES) as FieldRole[];
export function isImpostor(role:string|null|undefined){return ['impostor','shapeshifter','phantom','viper'].includes(role||'');}
export function isNeutral(role:string|null|undefined){return ['jester','scavenger','lonewolf','executioner','survivor','arsonist','thief','escapeartist','coroner'].includes(role||'');}
export function isCrew(role:string|null|undefined){return !!role&&!isImpostor(role)&&!isNeutral(role);}
export function fieldDistribution(n:number){
 const d=Object.fromEntries(FIELD_ROLE_KEYS.map(k=>[k,0])) as Record<FieldRole,number>;
 const bad=n<=8?1:n<=12?2:n<=18?3:n<=24?4:5;
 d.shapeshifter=n>=9?1:0;d.phantom=n>=13?1:0;d.viper=n>=14?1:0;d.impostor=bad-d.shapeshifter-d.phantom-d.viper;
 d.engineer=n>=25?4:n>=20?3:n>=14?2:1;
 d.scientist=n>=25?3:n>=15?2:1;
 d.tracker=n>=26?3:n>=16?2:n>=9?1:0;
 d.noisemaker=n>=27?3:n>=18?2:n>=10?1:0;
 d.investigator=n>=23?2:n>=11?1:0;d.judge=n>=12?1:0;
 // Crew keep roughly half the seats; the remaining seats go to independent neutrals.
 const crew=Math.ceil(n/2),slots=n-crew-bad;
 // Every neutral role appears at most once, including the single Jester.
 const neutrals:FieldRole[]=['jester','scavenger',...(n>=13?['lonewolf' as const]:[]),'executioner','survivor','arsonist','thief','escapeartist','coroner'];
 for(const role of neutrals.slice(0,slots))d[role]=1;
 const crewRoles=['engineer','scientist','tracker','noisemaker','investigator','judge'] as const;
 const reserve=Math.max(1,Math.floor(crew*.2));
 let used=crewRoles.reduce((total,key)=>total+d[key],0);
 while(used>crew-reserve){const key=[...crewRoles].sort((a,b)=>d[b]-d[a]).find(k=>d[k]>1);if(!key)break;d[key]--;used--;}
 for(const key of [...crewRoles].reverse())if(used>crew-reserve&&d[key]){d[key]--;used--;}
 d.crewmate=crew-used;
 return d;
}

import type {MapContext} from './map.ts';
import {HISTORY_SQL} from './lag-compensation.ts';
import {consumeInputs} from './motion.ts';
import {locationAt} from './map.ts';
import type {Pose} from './field.ts';
// A client checkpoint is only a proposal. The database must match every field before writing.
export function proposeMotion(raw:unknown,inputs:unknown,now:number,count:MapContext):Pose|null{
 const b=raw as Pose;if(!b||typeof b.id!=='string'||b.id.length>80||!Array.isArray(inputs)||!inputs.length||inputs.length>200)return null;
 for(const k of ['x','y','at','seq','ack','clock','warp'] as const)if(!Number.isFinite(b[k]))return null;
 if(!Number.isSafeInteger(b.ack)||!Number.isSafeInteger(b.seq)||b.at>now+100||b.clock!>now+100||Math.abs(b.x)>6000||Math.abs(b.y)>4000)return null;
 const next=consumeInputs({...b,ack:b.ack!,clock:b.clock!},inputs,now,count);if(next.ack===b.ack)return null;
 const place=locationAt(next,count);return{...next,id:b.id,at:Math.max(now,b.at+1),seq:b.seq,warp:b.warp||0,place,previous:place!==b.place?b.place:b.previous};
}
// A live WebSocket connection holds a lease on its player's movement (owner/owner_at,
// refreshed at least every second). HTTP may only move a player whose lease has lapsed,
// so the two transports never fight over the same row.
export const LIVE_LEASE_MS=2500;
export const HTTP_MOVE_ALLOWED=`(field_positions.owner IN ('','http') OR field_positions.owner_at<?)`;
// D1 checks identity, phase, live map size, movement locks, warp and exact saved checkpoint atomically.
// Bind: x,y,at,ack,clock,place,place,now, code,player,bx,by,bat,bseq,back,bclock,bwarp, now,key,seq,count,map, leaseCutoff
export const MOVE_CHECKPOINT_SQL=`UPDATE field_positions SET history=${HISTORY_SQL},x=?,y=?,at=?,ack=?,clock=?,previous=CASE WHEN place<>? THEN place ELSE previous END,place=?,owner='http',owner_at=?
 WHERE code=? AND player=? AND x=? AND y=? AND at=? AND seq=? AND ack=? AND clock=? AND warp=?
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM mafia_rooms r,json_each(r.state,'$.players') p
 WHERE r.code=field_positions.code AND r.expires>? AND json_extract(p.value,'$.id')=field_positions.player AND json_extract(p.value,'$.key')=?
 AND json_extract(r.state,'$.phase')='field' AND json_extract(r.state,'$.paused')=0
 AND json_extract(r.state,'$.seq')=? AND json_extract(r.state,'$.field.epoch')=field_positions.seq AND json_extract(r.state,'$.config.count')=? AND COALESCE(json_extract(r.state,'$.config.mapId'),'aurora')=?
 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM json_each(r.state,'$.field.venting') v WHERE v.key=field_positions.player)
 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM json_each(r.state,'$.field.watching') w WHERE w.key=field_positions.player)
 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM json_each(r.state,'$.field.warps') w WHERE w.key=field_positions.player AND json_extract(w.value,'$.at')>field_positions.warp))
 AND ${HTTP_MOVE_ALLOWED}`;

function shuffled<T>(a:T[]){for(let i=a.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0]%(i+1);[a[i],a[j]]=[a[j],a[i]];}return a;}
const roleNames:Record<string,string>={mafia:'마피아',spy:'스파이',police:'경찰',doctor:'의사',soldier:'군인',reporter:'기자',detective:'사립탐정',medium:'영매',politician:'정치인',priest:'성직자',citizen:'시민'};
export function red(p:Player){return isImpostor(p.role)||p.role==='mafia'||p.role==='spy';}
function note(p:Player,r:Room,text:string){p.intel.push({day:r.day,text});p.intel=p.intel.slice(-60);}
function announce(r:Room,text:string){r.announcement=text;r.events.push({id:crypto.randomUUID(),day:r.day,text});r.events=r.events.slice(-60);}
function phase(r:Room,p:Room['phase'],seconds:number,now:number){r.phase=p;r.seq++;r.deadline=seconds?now+seconds*1000:0;r.paused=false;r.remaining=0;}
export function initField(r:Room,now:number){r.field={mapVersion:3,watching:{},commsUntil:0,venting:{},rules:2,effects:[],skillCooldowns:{},ventCooldowns:{},vitals:{},tracking:{},disguises:{},invisible:{},guardians:[],alerts:[],warps:{},tasks:{},challenges:{},bodies:[],cooldowns:{},protects:{},meetings:{},knownDead:[],lightsUntil:0,sabotageAt:now+30000,meetingAt:now+40000,epoch:0};for(const p of r.players){const map=mapFor(r.config),pool=shuffled([...map.stations]),used=new Set<string>(),kinds=shuffled([...new Set(map.stations.map(s=>s.kind))]);r.field.tasks[p.id]=kinds.slice(0,map.tasksPerPlayer).map(kind=>{const s=pool.find(s=>s.kind===kind&&!used.has(s.room))||pool.find(s=>s.kind===kind)!;used.add(s.room);return{station:s.id,done:false};});}refreshNeutralTargets(r);}
export function resumeField(r:Room,now:number){const f=r.field!;r.day++;phase(r,'field',0,now);f.epoch=r.seq;f.speech={};f.judgments=[];for(const p of r.players)if(p.role==='investigator'){f.skillCooldowns||={};f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=Math.max(f.skillCooldowns[p.id]||0,now+15000);}f.watching={};f.commsUntil=0;f.venting={};f.effects=[];f.warps={};f.tracking={};f.disguises={};f.invisible={};f.vitals={};f.alerts=[];f.challenges={};f.bodies=[];f.knownDead=r.players.filter(p=>!p.alive).map(p=>p.id);f.lightsUntil=0;f.protects={};f.meetingAt=now+20000;f.sabotageAt=Math.max(f.sabotageAt,now+18000);for(const p of r.players)f.cooldowns[p.id]=Math.max(f.cooldowns[p.id]||0,now+20000);announce(r,'현장 탐색을 시작합니다. 미션을 수행하고 수상한 움직임을 기억하세요.');}
export function progress(r:Room){const f=r.field;if(!f)return{done:0,total:0};const tasks=r.players.filter(p=>!red(p)&&!isNeutral(p.role)).flatMap(p=>f.tasks[p.id]||[]);return{done:tasks.filter(t=>t.done).length,total:tasks.length};}
export function fieldSummary(r:Room,p?:Player){if(!r.field)return null;const f=r.field;return{...progress(r),neutral:neutralSummary(r,p),cases:p?.role==='investigator'?(f.cases||[]).filter(c=>c.released).map(c=>({id:c.id,victim:r.players.find(x=>x.id===c.victim)?.name||'',day:c.day,memo:f.caseNotes?.[p.id]?.[c.id]?.memo||'',answers:Object.entries(f.caseNotes?.[p.id]?.[c.id]?.answers||{}).map(([id,place])=>({id,name:r.players.find(x=>x.id===id)?.name||'',place}))})):[],judge:p?.role==='judge'?{done:(f.tasks[p.id]||[]).filter(t=>t.done).length,required:3,used:p.used,pending:f.judgments?.find(j=>j.judge===p.id)?.target||null}:null,watching:!!p&&!!f.watching?.[p.id],commsUntil:f.commsUntil||0,vent:p?f.venting?.[p.id]||null:null,rules:f.rules||1,guardian:!!p&&!!f.guardians?.includes(p.id),skillCooldown:p?f.skillCooldowns?.[p.id]||0:0,ventCooldown:p?f.ventCooldowns?.[p.id]||0:0,invisibleUntil:p?f.invisible?.[p.id]||0:0,disguisedUntil:p?f.disguises?.[p.id]?.until||0:0,tasks:p?f.tasks[p.id]||[]:[],challenge:p?f.challenges[p.id]||null:null,cooldown:p?f.watching?.[p.id]?Date.now()+f.watching[p.id].killRemaining:f.cooldowns[p.id]||0:0,protectUntil:p?f.protects[p.id]||0:0,emergenciesLeft:p?Math.max(0,1-(f.meetings[p.id]||0)):0,lightsUntil:f.lightsUntil,sabotageAt:red(p||{role:null} as Player)?f.sabotageAt:0,meetingAt:f.meetingAt,epoch:f.epoch};}
export function neutralWin(r:Room,p:Player,now:number,reason:string){r.winner='neutral';r.winnerIds=[p.id];phase(r,'finished',0,now);announce(r,`${p.name} 님이 ${reason}`);return true;}
export function fieldWin(r:Room,now:number){
 refreshNeutralTargets(r);
 const alive=r.players.filter(p=>p.alive),m=alive.filter(p=>r.field?.rules===2?isImpostor(p.role):p.role==='mafia').length,hunter=alive.find(p=>['lonewolf','arsonist'].includes(p.role||'')),pr=progress(r);
 if(hunter&&alive.length===1)return neutralWin(r,hunter,now,'최후의 생존자가 되어 독립 승리했습니다.');
 if(pr.total>0&&pr.done===pr.total){r.winner='town';phase(r,'finished',0,now);announce(r,'모든 크루 미션이 완료되었습니다. 크루 팀 승리!');return true;}
 const survivor=survivalWinner(r);if(survivor)return neutralWin(r,survivor,now,'개인 미션 3개를 완료하고 마지막 4명 안에 살아남아 독립 승리했습니다.');
 if(r.solo)return false;
 if(!m&&!hunter){r.winner='town';phase(r,'finished',0,now);announce(r,'모든 임포스터와 중립 사냥꾼·방화범이 탈락했습니다. 크루 팀 승리!');return true;}
 if(!hunter&&m>=alive.length-m){r.winner='mafia';phase(r,'finished',0,now);announce(r,'임포스터가 시설을 장악했습니다. 임포스터 팀 승리!');return true;}return false;
}
export function startMeeting(r:Room,text:string,now:number){const f=r.field!;f.speech={};f.judgments=[];for(const c of f.cases||[])c.released=true;for(const id of Object.keys(f.watching||{}))stopCameras(r,id,now);f.commsUntil=0;f.knownDead=r.players.filter(p=>!p.alive).map(p=>p.id);f.challenges={};f.bodies=[];f.lightsUntil=0;r.votes={};r.trial={};r.candidate=null;phase(r,'discussion',r.config.discussion,now);announce(r,text);}
export function fieldTick(r:Room,now:number){if(r.phase!=='field'||!r.field||r.paused)return false;const f=r.field;let changed=false;if(f.mapVersion!==3){f.mapVersion=3;f.watching={};f.venting={};f.challenges={};f.bodies=[];f.warps=Object.fromEntries(r.players.map((p,i)=>[p.id,{...spawn(i,r.config),at:now}]));r.seq++;f.epoch=r.seq;changed=true;}const bodies=f.bodies.filter(b=>!b.dissolveAt||b.dissolveAt>now);if(bodies.length!==f.bodies.length){f.bodies=bodies;changed=true;}for(const [id,w] of Object.entries(f.watching||{}))if(now>=w.until||!r.players.find(p=>p.id===id)?.alive){stopCameras(r,id,now);changed=true;}if(f.commsUntil&&now>=f.commsUntil){f.commsUntil=0;changed=true;}if(f.lightsUntil&&now>=f.lightsUntil){f.lightsUntil=0;changed=true;}for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(f.venting||{}))if(now>=v.until){delete f.venting![id];const p=r.players.find(p=>p.id===id)!;f.ventCooldowns||={};f.ventCooldowns[id]=now+(p.role==='engineer'?25000:8000);const location=ventsFor(r.config).find(x=>x.id===v.vent);if(location){f.warps||={};f.warps[id]={x:location.x,y:location.y,at:now};effect(r,p,'vent',location,location,undefined,now);}changed=true;}return changed;}
export function stopCameras(r:Room,id:string,now:number){const f=r.field!,w=f.watching?.[id];if(!w)return;f.cooldowns[id]=Math.min(now,w.until)+w.killRemaining;f.warps||={};f.warps[id]={x:w.x,y:w.y,at:now};delete f.watching![id];}
export function poseFor(r:Room,p:Player,poses:Pose[],now:number):Pose{const saved=poses.find(x=>x.id===p.id&&x.seq===r.field?.epoch),warp=r.field?.warps?.[p.id];const pose=saved||{id:p.id,...spawn(r.players.indexOf(p),r.config),at:now,seq:r.field?.epoch||0,ack:0,clock:now,warp:0,previous:'중앙 홀',place:'중앙 홀'};if(warp&&warp.at>(pose.warp||0))return{...pose,x:warp.x,y:warp.y,at:warp.at,clock:warp.at,warp:warp.at,place:locationAt(warp,r.config)};return pose;}
function effect(r:Room,p:Player,kind:Effect['kind'],a:Point,b:Point=a,target?:Player,now=Date.now()){const f=r.field!,d=f.disguises?.[p.id],appearance=d&&d.until>now?r.players.find(x=>x.id===d.target):p;f.effects=[...(f.effects||[]).filter(e=>now-e.at<3500),{id:crypto.randomUUID(),kind,actor:p.id,target:target?.id,acid:kind==='attack'&&p.role==='viper',avatar:appearance?.avatar??p.avatar,victimAvatar:target?.avatar,x:a.x,y:a.y,tx:b.x,ty:b.y,at:now}].slice(-32);}
export type FieldBody={targetAt?:number;action:string;seq?:number;target?:string;station?:string;nonce?:string;answer?:number[];requestId?:string;caseId?:string;text?:string};
export function fieldAct(r:Room,key:string,b:FieldBody,poses:Pose[],now=Date.now()){
 const f=r.field,p=r.players.find(x=>x.key===key);if(!f||r.phase!=='field')throw Error('지금은 현장 탐색 시간이 아닙니다.');if(r.paused)throw Error('진행자가 게임을 일시정지했습니다.');if(b.seq!==r.seq)throw Error('회의가 시작되었거나 탐색 단계가 바뀌었습니다.');if(!p)throw Error('참가자만 행동할 수 있습니다.');
 const dedup=`${key}:${b.requestId||''}`;if(b.requestId&&r.processed.includes(dedup))return;
 const near=(a:Point,b:Point,radius=105)=>distance(a,b)<=radius&&sight(a,b,r.config);
 const pos=poseFor(r,p,poses,now),target=r.players.find(x=>x.id===b.target),tp=target?poseFor(r,target,poses,now):null;
 if(f.venting?.[p.id]&&!['ventMove','ventExit'].includes(b.action))throw Error('환기구에서 나온 뒤 행동할 수 있습니다.');
 if(target&&f.venting?.[target.id])throw Error('환기구 안의 대상에게 행동할 수 없습니다.');
 if(f.watching?.[p.id]&&b.action!=='cameraStop')throw Error('CCTV를 닫은 뒤 행동할 수 있습니다.');
 const station=mapFor(r.config).stations.find(s=>s.id===b.station),task=f.tasks[p.id]?.find(t=>t.station===b.station);
 if(b.action==='cameraStop'){stopCameras(r,p.id,now);}
 else if(b.action==='say'){
  if((f.invisible?.[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('은신이 끝난 뒤 주변 대화를 사용할 수 있습니다.');
  if(f.challenges[p.id])throw Error('미션 장치를 닫은 뒤 말할 수 있습니다.');
  if(typeof b.text!=='string')throw Error('메시지를 입력하세요.');
  const text=Array.from(b.text.replace(/[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/g,' ').trim()).slice(0,60).join('');if(!text)throw Error('메시지를 입력하세요.');
  f.speech||={};if(f.speech[p.id]&&now-f.speech[p.id].at<1500)throw Error('1.5초 후 다시 말할 수 있습니다.');
  f.speech[p.id]={text,at:now,until:now+6500,alive:p.alive};
 }
 else if(b.action==='taskStart'){
  if(!station||!task||task.done||!near(pos,station,105))throw Error('내 미션 장치 가까이 이동해 주세요.');
  const solution=puzzleSolution(station.kind);
  f.challenges[p.id]={station:station.id,nonce:crypto.randomUUID(),started:now,solution};
 }else if(b.action==='taskFinish'){
  const c=f.challenges[p.id];if(!c||c.nonce!==b.nonce||!station||!task||task.done||c.station!==station.id||!near(pos,station,105))throw Error('미션을 다시 열어 주세요.');
  if(now-c.started<puzzleDuration(station.kind))throw Error('장치가 처리 중입니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.');const answer=b.answer;if(!Array.isArray(answer)||answer.length>12||answer.some(n=>!Number.isFinite(n)))throw Error('미션 입력을 확인해 주세요.');
  const correct=puzzleCorrect(station.kind,c.solution,answer);
  if(!correct)throw Error('미션 답이 맞지 않습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');task.done=true;delete f.challenges[p.id];if(p.role==='scientist'){f.skillCooldowns||={};f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=0;}effect(r,p,'task',pos,pos,undefined,now);fieldWin(r,now);
 }else if(b.action==='cancelTask'){delete f.challenges[p.id];}
 else {
  const guardian=!!f.guardians?.includes(p.id);if(!p.alive&&!(guardian&&b.action==='skill'))throw Error('유령은 미션과 사망자 대화만 할 수 있습니다.');
  f.skillCooldowns||={};f.ventCooldowns||={};f.invisible||={};f.disguises||={};f.tracking||={};f.vitals||={};
  if((f.invisible[p.id]||0)>now&&['kill','ability','report','emergency','vent','ventEnter'].includes(b.action))throw Error('은신이 끝난 뒤 사용할 수 있습니다.');
  if(['ignite','cashout','escape','examine','submitEvidence'].includes(b.action)||(b.action==='skill'&&['survivor','arsonist','thief'].includes(p.role||''))){
   const result=neutralAction(r,p,b.action,target,tp,pos,now);effect(r,p,result.effect,result.point,result.point,undefined,now);
   if(result.won)neutralWin(r,p,now,result.won);
  }else if(b.action==='collect'){
   if(p.role!=='scavenger')throw Error('수집가만 시체를 수집할 수 있습니다.');
   if((f.skillCooldowns[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('수집 재사용 시간이 남았습니다.');
   const body=f.bodies.find(x=>x.id===b.target);if(!body||(body.dissolveAt&&body.dissolveAt<=now)||!near(pos,body,105))throw Error('수집할 시체 가까이 이동하세요.');
   f.bodies=f.bodies.filter(x=>x.id!==body.id);f.collected||={};f.collected[p.id]=(f.collected[p.id]||0)+1;f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+15000;
   effect(r,p,'scan',pos,body,undefined,now);const required=r.config.count<=15?2:r.config.count<=22?3:4;note(p,r,`시체 수집 ${f.collected[p.id]} / ${required}`);
   if(f.collected[p.id]>=required)neutralWin(r,p,now,'시체 수집 목표를 달성하여 독립 승리했습니다.');
  }else if(b.action==='cameraStart'){
   if(!atSecurity(pos,r.config))throw Error('보안 모니터 가까이 이동하세요.');if((f.invisible[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('은신이 끝난 뒤 이용하세요.');if((f.commsUntil||0)>now)throw Error('통신실에서 통신 방해를 먼저 복구하세요.');if(f.challenges[p.id])throw Error('미션 장치를 닫은 뒤 이용하세요.');f.watching||={};f.watching[p.id]={x:pos.x,y:pos.y,at:now,until:now+12000,killRemaining:Math.max(0,(f.cooldowns[p.id]||0)-now)};f.warps||={};f.warps[p.id]={x:pos.x,y:pos.y,at:now};
  }else if(b.action==='commsSabotage'){
   if(!red(p)||now<f.sabotageAt||f.lightsUntil>now||(f.commsUntil||0)>now)throw Error('아직 통신 방해를 사용할 수 없습니다.');f.commsUntil=now+45000;f.sabotageAt=now+65000;f.vitals={};f.tracking={};
  }else if(b.action==='commsRepair'){
   if(!near(pos,communicationsConsole(r.config),105)||(f.commsUntil||0)<=now)throw Error('통신실의 복구 장치 가까이 이동하세요.');f.commsUntil=0;
  }else if(['ventEnter','ventMove','ventExit'].includes(b.action)){
   if(!isImpostor(p.role)&&p.role!=='engineer')throw Error('환기구를 사용할 수 없는 역할입니다.');
   f.venting||={};f.warps||={};const vents=ventsFor(r.config),inside=f.venting[p.id];
   if(b.action==='ventEnter'){
    if((f.ventCooldowns[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('환기구 재사용 시간이 남았습니다.');const from=vents.find(v=>near(pos,v,85));if(!from)throw Error('환기구 가까이 이동해 주세요.');
    f.venting[p.id]={vent:from.id,until:now+(p.role==='engineer'?10000:30000)};f.warps[p.id]={x:from.x,y:from.y,at:now};effect(r,p,'vent',from,from,undefined,now);
   }else if(b.action==='ventMove'){
    const from=vents.find(v=>v.id===inside?.vent),to=vents.find(v=>v.id===b.target);if(!inside||!from||!to||!from.links.includes(to.id))throw Error('연결된 환기구를 선택하세요.');inside.vent=to.id;f.warps[p.id]={x:to.x,y:to.y,at:Math.max(now,(f.warps[p.id]?.at||0)+1)};
   }else{
    if(!inside)throw Error('환기구 안에 있지 않습니다.');const from=vents.find(v=>v.id===inside.vent)!;delete f.venting[p.id];f.warps[p.id]={x:from.x,y:from.y,at:Math.max(now,(f.warps[p.id]?.at||0)+1)};f.ventCooldowns[p.id]=now+(p.role==='engineer'?25000:8000);effect(r,p,'vent',from,from,undefined,now);
   }
  }else if(b.action==='skill'){
   if((f.commsUntil||0)>now&&['scientist','tracker'].includes(p.role||''))throw Error('통신 방해 중에는 정보 장치를 사용할 수 없습니다.');if((f.skillCooldowns[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('특수 능력 재사용 시간이 남았습니다.');
   if(guardian){if(!target?.alive||!tp||!near(pos,tp,120))throw Error('보호할 생존자 가까이 이동하세요.');f.protects[target.id]=now+8000;f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+35000;effect(r,p,'shield',tp,tp,target,now);}
   else if(p.role==='investigator'){
    const c=f.cases?.find(c=>c.id===b.caseId&&c.released);if(!c)throw Error('수첩에서 회의에 공개된 사건을 선택하세요.');
    if(!target?.alive||!tp||target.id===p.id||!near(pos,tp,150)||(f.invisible[target.id]||0)>now)throw Error('검문할 생존자 가까이 이동하세요.');
    f.caseNotes||={};f.caseNotes[p.id]||={};const n=f.caseNotes[p.id][c.id]||={memo:'',answers:{}};
    if(n.answers[target.id])throw Error('이미 검문한 대상입니다.');if(Object.keys(n.answers).length>=3)throw Error('이 사건의 검문 3회를 모두 사용했습니다.');
    n.answers[target.id]=c.locations[target.id]||'당시 생존 기록 없음';f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+20000;
    note(p,r,`${r.players.find(x=>x.id===c.victim)?.name} 사건 · ${target.name}의 당시 위치: ${n.answers[target.id]}. 직업 판정이 아닙니다.`);
   }
   else if(p.role==='scientist'){f.vitals[p.id]=now+10000;f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+30000;}
   else if(p.role==='tracker'){if(!target?.alive||!tp||target.id===p.id||!near(pos,tp,150))throw Error('추적할 생존자 가까이 이동하세요.');f.tracking[p.id]={target:target.id,until:now+15000};f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+40000;}
   else if(p.role==='shapeshifter'){if(!target?.alive||!tp||target.id===p.id||!near(pos,tp,150))throw Error('외형을 복제할 생존자 가까이 이동하세요.');f.disguises[p.id]={target:target.id,until:now+15000};f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+45000;effect(r,p,'shift',pos,pos,undefined,now);}
   else if(p.role==='phantom'){f.invisible[p.id]=now+8000;f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+45000;f.cooldowns[p.id]=Math.max(f.cooldowns[p.id]||0,now+10000);effect(r,p,'vanish',pos,pos,undefined,now);}
   else throw Error('직접 사용하는 특수 능력이 없습니다.');
  }else if(b.action==='kill'&&(isImpostor(p.role)||p.role==='lonewolf')){
   // What the attacker saw is what counts: the target as rendered on the attacker's screen (rewound up to
   // REWIND_MS, in range and in sight there), even if it has since stepped behind a wall. Pressing on a
   // visible, in-range target and seeing nothing happen is the failure this genre cannot afford.
   const historical=tp?rewindTarget(tp,b.targetAt,now,r.config):null;
   if(now<(f.cooldowns[p.id]||0))throw Error('공격 재사용 시간이 남았습니다.');if(!target?.alive||!tp||(isImpostor(p.role)&&isImpostor(target.role))||((f.invisible[target.id]||0)>now)||!(near(pos,tp,95)||(historical&&near(pos,historical,95)))||p.id===target.id)throw Error('공격할 대상 가까이 이동하세요.');f.cooldowns[p.id]=now+30000;
   if((f.protects[target.id]||0)>now){delete f.protects[target.id];effect(r,p,'blocked',pos,tp,target,now);note(p,r,'보호막이 공격을 막았습니다.');}
   else{target.alive=false;stopCameras(r,target.id,now);delete f.challenges[target.id];f.bodies.push({id:target.id,x:tp.x,y:tp.y,at:now,...(p.role==='viper'?{dissolveAt:now+12000}:{})});
    f.cases||=[];f.cases.push({id:crypto.randomUUID(),victim:target.id,day:r.day,at:now,released:false,locations:Object.fromEntries(r.players.filter(x=>x.alive).map(x=>[x.id,locationAt(poseFor(r,x,poses,now),r.config)]))});f.guardians||=[];if(isCrew(target.role)&&f.guardians.length<(r.config.count>=23?2:1)){f.guardians.push(target.id);note(target,r,'수호천사 능력이 생겼습니다. 가까운 생존자를 보호할 수 있습니다.');}if(target.role==='noisemaker'){f.alerts||=[];f.alerts.push({id:target.id,x:tp.x,y:tp.y,until:now+12000});}effect(r,p,'attack',pos,tp,target,now);note(target,r,'공격받아 탈락했습니다. 유령으로 미션을 계속하세요.');fieldWin(r,now);}
  }else
 if(b.action==='report'){
   const body=f.bodies.find(x=>x.id===b.target);if(!body||(body.dissolveAt&&body.dissolveAt<=now)||!near(pos,body,125))throw Error('신고할 수 있는 현장이 가까이에 없습니다.');const victim=r.players.find(x=>x.id===body.id);startMeeting(r,`${p.name} 님이 ${locationAt(body,r.config)}에서 ${victim?.name} 님을 발견하고 신고했습니다.`,now);
  }else if(b.action==='emergency'){
   if(!near(pos,emergencyFor(r.config),100))throw Error('중앙 홀의 회의 버튼으로 이동해 주세요.');if(now<f.meetingAt)throw Error('긴급회의 대기 시간이 남아 있습니다.');if((f.meetings[p.id]||0)>=1)throw Error('긴급회의는 각자 한 번만 부를 수 있습니다.');if(f.lightsUntil>now||(f.commsUntil||0)>now)throw Error('방해 장치를 먼저 복구해 주세요. 현장 신고는 가능합니다.');f.meetings[p.id]=(f.meetings[p.id]||0)+1;startMeeting(r,`${p.name} 님이 긴급회의를 소집했습니다.`,now);
  }else if(b.action==='sabotage'){
   if(!red(p)||now<f.sabotageAt||f.lightsUntil>now||(f.commsUntil||0)>now)throw Error('아직 정전을 일으킬 수 없습니다.');f.lightsUntil=now+35000;f.sabotageAt=now+65000;
  }else if(b.action==='repair'){
   if(!near(pos,repairFor(r.config),105)||f.lightsUntil<=now)throw Error('전력실의 복구 장치 가까이 이동해 주세요.');f.lightsUntil=0;
  }else if(b.action==='ability'){
   if(now<(f.cooldowns[p.id]||0))throw Error('능력 재사용 시간이 남아 있습니다.');
   if(['soldier','politician','citizen'].includes(p.role||''))throw Error('직접 사용하는 능력이 없습니다.');
   if(['medium','priest'].includes(p.role||'')){
    const body=f.bodies.find(x=>x.id===b.target);if(!body||!target||target.alive||!near(pos,body,105))throw Error('쓰러진 사람 가까이 이동해 주세요.');
    if(p.role==='medium'){note(p,r,`${target.name}의 직업은 ${roleNames[target.role!]}입니다.`);f.cooldowns[p.id]=now+30000;}
    else{if(p.used)throw Error('부활 기회를 이미 사용했습니다.');p.used=true;target.alive=true;f.bodies=f.bodies.filter(x=>x.id!==target.id);f.knownDead=f.knownDead.filter(id=>id!==target.id);f.cooldowns[target.id]=now+20000;announce(r,`${target.name} 님이 성직자의 능력으로 부활했습니다.`);}
   }else{
    if(!target?.alive||!tp||!near(pos,tp,p.role==='mafia'?90:110)||(p.role!=='doctor'&&p.id===target.id))throw Error('대상 가까이 이동해 주세요.');
    if(p.role==='mafia'){
     if(target.role==='mafia'||(target.role==='spy'&&target.connected))throw Error('동료를 공격할 수 없습니다.');f.cooldowns[p.id]=now+35000;
     if((f.protects[target.id]||0)>now){delete f.protects[target.id];note(target,r,'보호막이 공격을 막았습니다.');note(p,r,'보호막으로 공격이 막혔습니다.');}
     else if(target.role==='soldier'&&target.shield){target.shield=false;note(target,r,'방탄이 공격을 막았습니다. 방탄이 소모되었습니다.');note(p,r,'방탄으로 공격이 막혔습니다.');}
     else{target.alive=false;stopCameras(r,target.id,now);delete f.challenges[target.id];f.bodies.push({id:target.id,x:tp.x,y:tp.y,at:now});note(target,r,'현장에서 탈락했습니다. 유령으로 미션을 계속 수행하세요.');fieldWin(r,now);}
    }else if(p.role==='doctor'){f.protects[target.id]=now+20000;f.cooldowns[p.id]=now+40000;note(p,r,`${target.name} 님에게 20초간 보호막을 부여했습니다.`);}
    else if(p.role==='police'){note(p,r,`${target.name}: ${target.role==='mafia'?'마피아입니다.':'마피아가 아닙니다.'}`);f.cooldowns[p.id]=now+45000;}
    else if(p.role==='spy'){note(p,r,`${target.name}의 직업은 ${roleNames[target.role!]}입니다.`);if(target.role==='mafia'){p.connected=true;note(p,r,'마피아와 접선했습니다.');}f.cooldowns[p.id]=now+45000;}
    else if(p.role==='detective'){note(p,r,`${target.name}의 직전 방문 장소: ${tp.previous}. (현재 ${locationAt(tp,r.config)})`);f.cooldowns[p.id]=now+35000;}
    else if(p.role==='reporter'){if(p.used)throw Error('취재 기회를 이미 사용했습니다.');p.used=true;target.publicRole=true;announce(r,`[특종] ${target.name}의 직업은 ${roleNames[target.role!]}입니다.`);}
   }
  }else throw Error('알 수 없는 현장 행동입니다.');
 }
 if(b.requestId){r.processed.push(dedup);r.processed=r.processed.slice(-160);}
}
// memory (optional, per live connection): players this viewer saw recently. They stay
// listed for a short grace period near the edge of vision so that position updates
// arriving at slightly different times do not make them blink in and out of view.
const VISIBILITY_GRACE_MS=450,VISIBILITY_GRACE_RANGE=1.15;
// `predict` (live server only): where an actor whose pose came from another server
// instance probably is now. Their stored pose is a few hundred ms old; near a doorway that
// old pose can sit behind the wall while the actor is already in plain view.
export function fieldSnapshot(r:Room,key:string,poses:Pose[],now=Date.now(),memory?:Map<string,number>,predict?:(id:string)=>Point|undefined){
 const p=r.players.find(p=>p.key===key),host=r.host===key&&!r.solo,f=r.field;if(!p&&!host)throw Error('입장 정보를 확인해 주세요.');const own=p?poseFor(r,p,poses,now):{...emergencyFor(r.config),id:'host',at:now,seq:f?.epoch||0,ack:0,clock:now,warp:0,previous:'중앙 홀',place:'중앙 홀'};
 const speechTime=r.paused?(f?.pausedAt||now):now;
 const radius=p?.alive&&!red(p)&&((f?.lightsUntil||0)>now)?145:WORLD.vision;
 const canSee=(point:Point)=>host||(distance(own,point)<=radius&&sight(own,point,r.config));
 const seePerson=(x:Player)=>{const at=poseFor(r,x,poses,now);let seen=canSee(at);if(!seen&&predict&&distance(own,at)<=radius+110){const guess=predict(x.id);seen=!!guess&&canSee(guess);}if(seen){memory?.set(x.id,now);return true;}const last=memory?.get(x.id);return last!==undefined&&now-last<=VISIBILITY_GRACE_MS&&distance(own,at)<=radius*VISIBILITY_GRACE_RANGE;};
 if(memory)for(const [id,at] of memory)if(now-at>VISIBILITY_GRACE_MS*4)memory.delete(id);
 const people=r.players.filter(x=>x.id===p?.id||((x.alive||!p?.alive||host)&&(!f?.venting?.[x.id]||host)&&(!(f?.invisible?.[x.id]&&f.invisible[x.id]>now)||host||!p?.alive)&&seePerson(x))).map(x=>{const loc=poseFor(r,x,poses,now),d=f?.disguises?.[x.id],disguise=d&&d.until>now?r.players.find(t=>t.id===d.target):null;const bubble=f?.speech?.[x.id],speech=bubble&&bubble.until>speechTime&&bubble.alive===x.alive&&!f?.venting?.[x.id]&&!((f?.invisible?.[x.id]||0)>now)&&(x.alive||!!p&&!p.alive)?{text:bubble.text,at:bubble.at,until:bubble.until}:null;return{speech,at:loc.at,warp:loc.warp||0,id:x.id,x:loc.x,y:loc.y,avatar:disguise?.avatar??x.avatar,name:disguise?.name??x.name,alive:x.alive,vented:!!f?.venting?.[x.id],protected:(f?.protects[x.id]||0)>now,invisible:(f?.invisible?.[x.id]||0)>now};});
 const tr=p?f?.tracking?.[p.id]:null,tracked=tr&&tr.until>now?r.players.find(x=>x.id===tr.target&&x.alive):null;
 const watching=!!p&&p.alive&&r.phase==='field'&&!!f?.watching?.[p.id]&&f.watching[p.id].until>now&&atSecurity(own,r.config),comms=(f?.commsUntil||0)>now;
 const activeCameras=r.phase==='field'&&!comms&&r.players.some(x=>x.alive&&f?.watching?.[x.id]&&f.watching[x.id].until>now&&atSecurity(poseFor(r,x,poses,now),r.config));
 const cameras=watching?camerasFor(r.config).map(camera=>{const visible=(q:Point)=>cameraSees(camera,q,r.config);return{...camera,offline:comms,people:comms?[]:r.players.filter(x=>x.alive&&!f?.venting?.[x.id]&&!((f?.invisible?.[x.id]||0)>now)&&visible(poseFor(r,x,poses,now))).map(x=>{const loc=poseFor(r,x,poses,now),d=f?.disguises?.[x.id],appearance=d&&d.until>now?r.players.find(t=>t.id===d.target):x;return{id:x.id,x:loc.x,y:loc.y,name:appearance?.name||x.name,avatar:appearance?.avatar??x.avatar};}),bodies:comms?[]:(f?.bodies||[]).filter(b=>(!b.dissolveAt||b.dissolveAt>now)&&visible(b)).map(b=>({...b,avatar:r.players.find(x=>x.id===b.id)?.avatar||0})),effects:comms?[]:(f?.effects||[]).filter(e=>e.at>=f!.watching![p!.id].at&&now-e.at<1500&&visible({x:e.tx,y:e.ty}))};}):null;

 return{own:{...own,history:undefined},people:watching?[]:people,cameras,activeCameras,bodies:(watching?[]:f?.bodies||[]).filter(b=>(!b.dissolveAt||b.dissolveAt>now)&&canSee(b)).map(b=>({...b,name:r.players.find(p=>p.id===b.id)?.name||'',avatar:r.players.find(p=>p.id===b.id)?.avatar||0})),effects:(f?.effects||[]).filter(e=>now-e.at<2800&&(e.actor===p?.id||e.target===p?.id||!watching&&canSee(e))).map(e=>({...e})),alerts:p&&!red(p)?(f?.alerts||[]).filter(a=>a.until>now):[],vitals:p&&(f?.vitals?.[p.id]||0)>now?r.players.map(x=>({id:x.id,name:x.name,alive:x.alive})):null,tracked:tracked?{name:tracked.name,...poseFor(r,tracked,poses,now),history:undefined,until:tr!.until}:null,radius,serverNow:now,seq:r.seq};
}
export type FieldSnapshot=ReturnType<typeof fieldSnapshot>;

import type {Point} from './map.ts';
// Positive yaw turns right; the existing server still receives world-space input.
export function cameraMovement(p:Point,yaw:number):Point{const c=Math.cos(yaw),s=Math.sin(yaw);return{x:p.x*c-p.y*s,y:p.x*s+p.y*c};}
export function inFront(own:Point,target:Point,yaw:number,halfAngle=Math.PI*.42){const x=target.x-own.x,y=target.y-own.y,d=Math.hypot(x,y);return d<12||(x*Math.sin(yaw)-y*Math.cos(yaw))/d>=Math.cos(halfAngle);}

};
export const PHASE: Record<Phase,{ name: string; sub: string }> = {
  field:{name:'도시의 비밀을 추적하세요',sub:'이동하며 미션을 수행하고, 발견한 현장을 신고하세요.'},
  lobby:{name:'시민들이 모이고 있습니다',sub:'캐릭터를 선택하고 준비를 완료하세요.'},
  reveal:{name:'당신의 정체를 확인하세요',sub:'직업은 자신만 볼 수 있습니다. 화면을 조심하세요.'},
  night:{name:'도시에 밤이 찾아왔습니다',sub:'모두 조용히, 각자의 비밀 행동을 선택하세요.'},
  dawn:{name:'새로운 아침이 밝았습니다',sub:'지난밤 도시에서 일어난 일을 확인하세요.'},
  discussion:{name:'누구의 말을 믿겠습니까?',sub:'친구들과 대화하며 마피아를 찾아내세요.'},
  vote:{name:'도시의 운명을 결정하세요',sub:'의심되는 한 사람을 지목하세요. 마감 전 변경할 수 있습니다.'},
  defense:{name:'마지막 변론을 듣겠습니다',sub:'후보자의 이야기를 듣고 추방 여부를 판단하세요.'},
  trial:{name:'이 사람을 추방하겠습니까?',sub:'후보자를 제외한 생존자 과반수가 찬성해야 추방됩니다.'},
  verdict:{name:'시민들의 선택',sub:'최종 투표 결과를 공개합니다.'},
  finished:{name:'모든 비밀이 밝혀졌습니다',sub:'각자의 정체를 확인하세요.'},
};
export const defaults: Config = { mode:'field', count:16, discussion:120, night:45, vote:35, revealDead:false,confirmEject:true };
export function distribution(n:number,mode?:Config['mode']):Record<Role,number> {
  const empty=Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(ROLE).map(k=>[k,0])) as Record<Role,number>;if(mode==='field')return {...empty,...fieldDistribution(n)};
  const d:Record<Role,number> = { ...empty,mafia:Math.min(5,Math.max(1,Math.floor((n-1)/5))), spy:n>=8?1:0, police:n>=26?2:1, doctor:n>=22?2:1, soldier:n>=24?2:n>=8?1:0, detective:n>=28?2:n>=10?1:0, reporter:n>=12?1:0, medium:n>=13?1:0, politician:n>=14?1:0, priest:n>=16?1:0, citizen:0 };
  d.citizen=n-Object.values(d).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0); return d;
}
export function configOf(v:Partial<Config>):Config { const range=(n:unknown,lo:number,hi:number,fallback:number)=>typeof n==='number'&&Number.isInteger(n)?Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,n)):fallback; return { viewMode:v.mapId==='nereid'?'fps':'topdown', mapId:v.mapId==='nereid'?'nereid':'aurora', mode:v.mode==='classic'?'classic':'field', count:range(v.count,6,29,16),discussion:range(v.discussion,30,300,120),night:range(v.night,20,120,45),vote:range(v.vote,20,120,35),revealDead:v.revealDead===true,confirmEject:v.confirmEject!==false }; }
export function newRoom(code:string,host:string,config:Config,now=Date.now()):Room { return {code,host,config,players:[],phase:'lobby',seq:0,day:0,deadline:0,paused:false,remaining:0,votes:{},trial:{},candidate:null,actions:{},messages:[],events:[],announcement:'',winner:null,created:now,processed:[]}; }
function event(r:Room,text:string) { r.announcement=text; r.events.push({id:crypto.randomUUID(),day:r.day,text}); r.events=r.events.slice(-60); }
function intel(p:Player,day:number,text:string) { p.intel.push({day,text}); p.intel=p.intel.slice(-60); }
function enter(r:Room,phase:Phase,seconds:number,now:number) { r.phase=phase;r.seq++;r.deadline=seconds?now+seconds*1000:0;r.paused=false;r.remaining=0; }
function checkWin(r:Room,now:number) { if(r.field?.rules===2)return fieldWin(r,now);const alive=r.players.filter(p=>p.alive),m=alive.filter(p=>r.field?.rules===2?isImpostor(p.role):p.role==='mafia').length; if(!m||m>=alive.length-m) { r.winner=m?'mafia':'town';enter(r,'finished',0,now);event(r,r.field?.rules===2?(m?'임포스터가 시설을 장악했습니다.':'모든 임포스터를 찾아냈습니다. 크루 팀 승리!'):(m?'마피아가 도시를 장악했습니다.':'시민들이 도시를 되찾았습니다.'));return true; } return false; }
export function tally(votes:Record<string,string>,players?:Player[]) { const counts:Record<string,number>={};for(const [id,target] of Object.entries(votes)) counts[target]=(counts[target]||0)+(players?.find(p=>p.id===id)?.role==='politician'?2:1);return counts; }
function uniqueTop(votes:Record<string,string>,players?:Player[]) { const e=Object.entries(tally(votes,players)).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);return e.length&&(e.length===1||e[0][1]>e[1][1])?e[0][0]:null; }
export function next(r:Room,now=Date.now()) {
  if(r.field&&['reveal','verdict'].includes(r.phase)){resumeField(r,now);return;}
  if(r.field&&r.phase==='field'){startMeeting(r,'진행자가 회의를 소집했습니다.',now);return;}
  if(r.phase==='reveal'||r.phase==='verdict') {r.day++;r.actions={};r.votes={};r.trial={};r.candidate=null;enter(r,'night',r.config.night,now);event(r,`${r.day}번째 밤, 도시가 잠들었습니다.`);}
  else if(r.phase==='night') {
    const active=r.players.filter(p=>p.alive),oldEvents=r.events.length;
    const mafiaVotes:Record<string,string>={};
    for(const p of active) {
      const target=r.players.find(t=>t.id===r.actions[p.id]); if(!target)continue;
      if(p.role==='mafia'&&target.alive&&target.role!=='mafia')mafiaVotes[p.id]=target.id;
      if(p.role==='police'&&target.alive)intel(p,r.day,`${target.name}: ${target.role==='mafia'?'마피아입니다.':'마피아가 아닙니다.'}`);
      if(p.role==='spy'&&target.alive) {intel(p,r.day,`${target.name}의 직업은 ${ROLE[target.role!].name}입니다.`);if(target.role==='mafia'){p.connected=true;intel(p,r.day,'마피아와 접선했습니다. 다음 밤부터 비밀 대화에 합류합니다.');}}
      if(p.role==='detective'&&target.alive) {const selected=r.players.find(t=>t.id===r.actions[target.id]);intel(p,r.day,`${target.name}: ${selected?`${selected.name}을(를) 선택했습니다.`:'선택한 행동 대상이 없습니다.'}`);}
      if(p.role==='medium'&&!target.alive)intel(p,r.day,`${target.name}의 직업은 ${ROLE[target.role!].name}입니다.`);
    }
    const victim=r.players.find(p=>p.id===uniqueTop(mafiaVotes));
    const protects=active.filter(p=>p.role==='doctor').map(p=>r.actions[p.id]);
    if(victim&&!protects.includes(victim.id)) {
      if(victim.role==='soldier'&&victim.shield){victim.shield=false;intel(victim,r.day,'방탄이 발동해 공격을 막았습니다. 이제 방탄은 없습니다.');event(r,'지난밤에는 아무도 탈락하지 않았습니다.');}
      else {victim.alive=false;event(r,`${victim.name} 님이 지난밤 탈락했습니다.${r.config.revealDead?` 정체는 ${ROLE[victim.role!].name}였습니다.`:''}`);}
    } else event(r,'지난밤에는 아무도 탈락하지 않았습니다.');
    for(const p of active) {
      const target=r.players.find(t=>t.id===r.actions[p.id]);if(!target)continue;
      if(p.role==='reporter'&&!p.used&&r.day>=2){p.used=true;if(p.alive&&target.alive){target.publicRole=true;event(r,`[특종] ${target.name}의 직업은 ${ROLE[target.role!].name}입니다.`);}else intel(p,r.day,'취재가 실패했습니다. 특종 기회가 소모되었습니다.');}
      if(p.role==='priest'&&!p.used&&!target.alive){p.used=true;if(p.alive){target.alive=true;event(r,`${target.name} 님이 성직자의 능력으로 돌아왔습니다.`);}else intel(p,r.day,'탈락하여 부활이 실패했습니다.');}
    }
    r.announcement=r.events.slice(oldEvents).map(e=>e.text).join(' ');enter(r,'dawn',12,now);checkWin(r,now);
  } else if(r.phase==='dawn')enter(r,'discussion',r.config.discussion,now);
  else if(r.phase==='discussion'){r.votes={};enter(r,'vote',r.config.vote,now);}
  else if(r.phase==='vote') {
    if(r.field?.rules===2){
      const j=r.field.judgments?.find(j=>r.players.some(p=>p.id===j.judge&&p.alive&&p.role==='judge'&&!p.used)&&r.players.some(p=>p.id===j.target&&p.alive));
      if(j){const judge=r.players.find(p=>p.id===j.judge)!,target=r.players.find(p=>p.id===j.target)!,victim=isImpostor(target.role)?target:judge;judge.used=true;victim.alive=false;r.candidate=victim.id;r.field.judgments=[];r.field.guardians||=[];if(isCrew(victim.role)&&r.field.guardians.length<(r.config.count>=23?2:1))r.field.guardians.push(victim.id);event(r,victim===target?`[판결] ${judge.name} 님이 지목한 ${target.name} 님은 임포스터입니다. 투표 결과 대신 추방합니다.`:`[오판] ${target.name} 님은 임포스터가 아닙니다. 판사 ${judge.name} 님이 대신 추방됩니다.`);enter(r,'verdict',8,now);checkWin(r,now);return;}
      const top=uniqueTop(r.votes),p=r.players.find(p=>p.id===top&&p.alive);r.candidate=p?.id||null;if(p){p.alive=false;r.field.guardians||=[];if(isCrew(p.role)&&r.field.guardians.length<(r.config.count>=23?2:1))r.field.guardians.push(p.id);event(r,`${p.name} 님이 최다 득표로 추방되었습니다.${r.config.confirmEject!==false?` ${p.name} 님은 임포스터${isImpostor(p.role)?'였습니다':'가 아니었습니다'}.`:''}${r.config.revealDead?` 정체: ${ROLE[p.role!].name}`:''}`);if(p.role==='jester'){neutralWin(r,p,now,'일반 투표로 추방되어 독립 승리했습니다.');return;}const executioner=executionWinner(r,p.id);if(executioner){neutralWin(r,executioner,now,'목표를 일반 투표로 추방시켜 독립 승리했습니다.');return;}}else event(r,'동률 또는 건너뛰기로 아무도 추방되지 않았습니다.');enter(r,'verdict',8,now);checkWin(r,now);return;}
    const top=uniqueTop(r.votes,r.players),candidate=r.players.find(p=>p.id===top&&p.alive);
    if(candidate){r.candidate=candidate.id;event(r,`${candidate.name} 님이 추방 후보로 지목되었습니다. 최후 변론을 시작합니다.`);enter(r,'defense',25,now);}
    else {event(r,'동률 또는 기권으로 추방 후보가 결정되지 않았습니다.');enter(r,'verdict',10,now);}
  } else if(r.phase==='defense'){r.trial={};enter(r,'trial',20,now);}
  else if(r.phase==='trial') {
    const p=r.players.find(p=>p.id===r.candidate&&p.alive),eligible=r.players.filter(p=>p.alive&&p.id!==r.candidate).length,yes=Object.values(r.trial).filter(v=>v==='yes').length;
    if(p&&yes>eligible/2){if(p.role==='politician'){p.publicRole=true;event(r,`${p.name} 님은 정치인입니다. 추방을 피하고 살아남았습니다.`);}else{p.alive=false;event(r,`${p.name} 님이 찬성 ${yes}표로 추방되었습니다.${r.config.revealDead?` 정체는 ${ROLE[p.role!].name}였습니다.`:''}`);}}
    else event(r,'추방 찬성이 과반수에 미치지 않아 부결되었습니다.');
    enter(r,'verdict',10,now);checkWin(r,now);
  }
}
export function tick(r:Room,now=Date.now()) { if(fieldTick(r,now))return true; if(!r.paused&&r.deadline&&now>=r.deadline){next(r,now);return true;}return false; }
export function actor(r:Room,key:string) {const host=key===r.host,p=r.players.find(p=>p.key===key);if(!host&&!p)throw new Error('입장 정보가 만료되었습니다. 다시 입장해 주세요.');return{host,p};}
export function addPlayer(r:Room,key:string,name:string,avatar:number) {if(r.players.some(p=>p.key===key))return;if(r.phase!=='lobby')throw new Error('이미 게임이 시작된 방입니다.');if(r.players.length>=r.config.count)throw new Error('방의 정원이 모두 찼습니다.');name=name.trim().slice(0,12);if(!name)throw new Error('이름을 입력해 주세요.');if(r.players.some(p=>p.name===name))throw new Error('같은 이름이 있습니다. 이름 뒤에 번호를 붙여 주세요.');r.players.push({id:crypto.randomUUID(),key,name,avatar:Math.max(0,Math.min(15,Math.floor(avatar)||0)),role:null,alive:true,ready:false,connected:false,used:false,shield:true,publicRole:false,intel:[]});}
export function canNight(p:{role:Role|null;alive:boolean;used?:boolean},day:number) {return p.alive&&!!p.role&&!['citizen','politician','soldier'].includes(p.role)&&!(['reporter','priest'].includes(p.role)&&p.used)&&!(p.role==='reporter'&&day<2);}
export function act(r:Room,key:string,body:{action:string;seq?:number;target?:string;text?:string;channel?:string;ready?:boolean;config?:Partial<Config>;requestId?:string;caseId?:string;memo?:string},now=Date.now()) {
  const{host,p}=actor(r,key),dedup=`${key}:${body.requestId||''}`;if(body.requestId&&r.processed.includes(dedup))return;const a=body.action;
  if(['start','next','pause','settings','reset','kick','soloStart','soloStop'].includes(a)&&!host)throw new Error('진행자만 사용할 수 있습니다.');
  if(['vote','trial','night','next','pause','start','reset','judge','caseNote','soloStart','soloStop'].includes(a)&&body.seq!==r.seq)throw new Error('단계가 바뀌었습니다. 현재 화면에서 다시 선택해 주세요.');
  if(a==='soloStart'){
    if(r.phase!=='lobby'||r.config.mode!=='field'||r.players.length)throw Error('참가자가 없는 현장 수사 대기실에서만 혼자 입장할 수 있습니다.');
    addPlayer(r,key,'진행자',0);const solo=r.players[0];solo.role='engineer';solo.ready=true;r.solo=true;r.day=0;r.winner=null;r.winnerIds=[];r.paused=false;r.messages=[];r.events=[];r.actions={};r.votes={};r.trial={};r.candidate=null;initField(r,now);resumeField(r,now);event(r,'진행자 단독 입장 · 멀티플레이와 같은 맵과 미션입니다. 엔지니어로 벤트도 확인할 수 있습니다.');
  } else if(a==='soloStop'){
    if(!r.solo)throw Error('단독 입장 중이 아닙니다.');
    r.players=[];delete r.solo;delete r.field;r.paused=false;r.winner=null;r.winnerIds=[];r.messages=[];r.events=[];r.actions={};r.votes={};r.trial={};r.candidate=null;r.day=0;r.announcement='';enter(r,'lobby',0,now);
  } else if(a==='settings'){if(r.phase!=='lobby')throw new Error('대기실에서만 변경할 수 있습니다.');const c=configOf(body.config||{});if(c.count<r.players.length)throw new Error('현재 입장 인원보다 적게 설정할 수 없습니다.');r.config=c;}
  else if(a==='caseNote'){
    if(!p?.alive||p.role!=='investigator'||!r.field||r.paused||!['field','discussion','vote'].includes(r.phase))throw Error('지금 사건 수첩을 수정할 수 없습니다.');
    const c=r.field.cases?.find(c=>c.id===body.caseId&&c.released);if(!c)throw Error('공개된 사건을 선택하세요.');r.field.caseNotes||={};r.field.caseNotes[p.id]||={};const n=r.field.caseNotes[p.id][c.id]||={memo:'',answers:{}};n.memo=(body.memo||'').slice(0,400);
  }
  else if(a==='judge'){
    if(!p?.alive||p.role!=='judge'||!r.field||r.field.rules!==2||r.phase!=='vote'||r.paused||p.used)throw Error('지금 판결할 수 없습니다.');
    if((r.field.tasks[p.id]||[]).filter(t=>t.done).length<3)throw Error('미션 3개를 먼저 완료하세요.');const t=r.players.find(x=>x.id===body.target&&x.alive&&x.id!==p.id);if(!t)throw Error('다른 생존자를 선택하세요.');r.field.judgments||=[];if(r.field.judgments.some(j=>j.judge===p.id))throw Error('이미 판결을 예약했습니다.');r.field.judgments.push({judge:p.id,target:t.id,at:now});
  }
  else if(a==='ready'){if(!p||r.phase!=='lobby')throw new Error('대기실에서만 준비할 수 있습니다.');p.ready=body.ready===true;}
  else if(a==='kick'){if(r.phase!=='lobby')throw new Error('대기실에서만 내보낼 수 있습니다.');r.players=r.players.filter(p=>p.id!==body.target);}
  else if(a==='start') {
    if(r.phase!=='lobby'||r.players.length!==r.config.count||!r.players.every(p=>p.ready))throw new Error('설정한 인원이 모두 입장하고 준비를 완료해야 합니다.');
    const deck=Object.entries(distribution(r.players.length,r.config.mode)).flatMap(([role,n])=>Array(n).fill(role)) as Role[];
    const roles=drawRoles(deck,r.players.map(p=>p.role??p.previousRole));

    r.players.forEach((p,i)=>{p.role=roles[i];p.alive=true;p.connected=false;p.used=false;p.shield=true;p.publicRole=false;p.intel=[];});r.day=0;r.winner=null;r.winnerIds=[];r.messages=[];r.events=[];r.actions={};r.votes={};r.trial={};r.candidate=null;if(r.config.mode==='field')initField(r,now);else delete r.field;enter(r,'reveal',20,now);event(r,'직업이 배정되었습니다. 자신의 정체를 확인하세요.');
  } else if(a==='next'){if(r.phase==='lobby'||r.phase==='finished')throw new Error('지금은 단계를 넘길 수 없습니다.');next(r,now);}
  else if(a==='pause'){if(r.phase==='field'&&r.field){r.paused=!r.paused;if(r.paused)r.field.pausedAt=now;else{const delta=now-(r.field.pausedAt||now);for(const id of Object.keys(r.field.cooldowns))r.field.cooldowns[id]+=delta;for(const id of Object.keys(r.field.protects))r.field.protects[id]+=delta;for(const record of [r.field.skillCooldowns,r.field.ventCooldowns,r.field.vitals,r.field.invisible])if(record)for(const id of Object.keys(record))record[id]+=delta;for(const record of [r.field.tracking,r.field.disguises,r.field.venting])if(record)for(const value of Object.values(record))value.until+=delta;for(const watcher of Object.values(r.field.watching||{})){watcher.at+=delta;watcher.until+=delta;}for(const speech of Object.values(r.field.speech||{})){speech.at+=delta;speech.until+=delta;}for(const body of r.field.bodies)if(body.dissolveAt)body.dissolveAt+=delta;if(r.field.commsUntil)r.field.commsUntil+=delta;for(const alert of r.field.alerts||[])alert.until+=delta;if(r.field.lightsUntil)r.field.lightsUntil+=delta;r.field.sabotageAt+=delta;r.field.meetingAt+=delta;delete r.field.pausedAt;}return;}if(!r.deadline)throw new Error('진행 중인 게임이 아닙니다.');r.paused=!r.paused;if(r.paused)r.remaining=Math.max(0,r.deadline-now);else r.deadline=now+r.remaining;}
  else if(a==='reset'){if(r.phase!=='finished')throw new Error('게임이 끝난 뒤 다시 시작할 수 있습니다.');r.players.forEach(p=>{p.previousRole=p.role??p.previousRole;p.role=null;p.alive=true;p.ready=false;p.connected=false;p.used=false;p.shield=true;p.publicRole=false;p.intel=[];});r.winner=null;r.winnerIds=[];r.messages=[];r.events=[];r.actions={};r.votes={};r.trial={};r.candidate=null;r.day=0;r.announcement='';delete r.field;enter(r,'lobby',0,now);}
  else if(a==='vote'||a==='night'||a==='trial') {
    if(!p?.alive||r.paused)throw new Error('지금은 선택할 수 없습니다.');if(r.phase!==a)throw new Error('선택 시간이 아닙니다.');
    const t=r.players.find(t=>t.id===body.target);
    if(a==='vote'){if(body.target!=='skip'&&(!t?.alive||t.id===p.id))throw new Error('자신 이외의 생존자를 선택해 주세요.');r.votes[p.id]=body.target!;}
    else if(a==='trial'){if(p.id===r.candidate||!['yes','no'].includes(body.target||''))throw new Error('지금은 찬반 투표를 할 수 없습니다.');r.trial[p.id]=body.target!;}
    else {
      if(!canNight(p,r.day))throw new Error('지금 사용할 수 있는 밤 능력이 없습니다.');
      if(body.target==='skip')delete r.actions[p.id];
      else {const deadTarget=p.role==='medium'||p.role==='priest';if(!t||t.alive===deadTarget||(p.role!=='doctor'&&t.id===p.id)||(p.role==='mafia'&&(t.role==='mafia'||(t.role==='spy'&&t.connected))))throw new Error('이 대상에게는 능력을 사용할 수 없습니다.');r.actions[p.id]=t.id;}
    }
  } else if(a==='chat') {
    const text=(body.text||'').trim().slice(0,200);if(!text)throw new Error('메시지를 입력해 주세요.');let channel:Message['channel']='town';
    if(p&&!p.alive&&r.phase!=='finished')channel='ghost';
    else if(r.phase==='night'||r.phase==='field') {if(p?.role==='medium')channel='ghost';else if(isImpostor(p?.role)||p?.role==='mafia'||(p?.role==='spy'&&p.connected))channel='mafia';else throw new Error('밤에는 자신의 비밀 대화만 사용할 수 있습니다.');}
    else if(!['lobby','discussion','vote','defense','trial','finished'].includes(r.phase))throw new Error('지금은 대화 시간이 아닙니다.');
    if(r.phase==='defense'&&channel==='town'&&!host&&p?.id!==r.candidate)throw new Error('최후 변론 중에는 후보자만 말할 수 있습니다.');
    const last=r.messages.findLast(m=>m.player===(p?.id||'host'));if(last&&now-last.at<900)throw new Error('잠시 후 전송해 주세요.');r.messages.push({id:crypto.randomUUID(),player:p?.id||'host',name:p?.name||'진행자',text,channel,at:now});r.messages=r.messages.slice(-120);
  } else throw new Error('지원하지 않는 요청입니다.');
  if(body.requestId){r.processed.push(dedup);r.processed=r.processed.slice(-160);}
}
export function view(r:Room,key:string,now=Date.now()) {
  const{host,p}=actor(r,key),mafia=isImpostor(p?.role)||p?.role==='mafia'||(p?.role==='spy'&&p.connected),finished=r.phase==='finished';const mafiaVotes:Record<string,string>={};if(mafia&&p?.alive&&r.phase==='night')for(const m of r.players.filter(x=>x.role==='mafia'&&x.alive))if(r.actions[m.id])mafiaVotes[m.id]=r.actions[m.id];
  return {field:fieldSummary(r,p),revision:r.revision||0,code:r.code,config:r.config,phase:r.phase,seq:r.seq,day:r.day,deadline:r.deadline,paused:r.paused,remaining:r.remaining,serverNow:now,host:host&&!r.solo,solo:!!r.solo&&host,me:p?.id||null,myRole:p?.role||null,connected:p?.connected||false,used:p?.used||false,shield:p?.shield||false,
    players:r.players.map(x=>({id:x.id,name:x.name,avatar:x.avatar,alive:r.phase==='field'&&x.id!==p?.id&&!host?!(r.field?.knownDead.includes(x.id)):x.alive,ready:x.ready,role:finished||x.publicRole||(r.config.revealDead&&!x.alive&&(r.phase!=='field'||r.field?.knownDead.includes(x.id)))||x.id===p?.id||(mafia&&(isImpostor(x.role)||x.role==='mafia'||(x.role==='spy'&&x.connected)))?x.role:null})),
    intel:p?.intel||[],myAction:p?(r.phase==='vote'?r.votes[p.id]:r.phase==='night'?r.actions[p.id]:r.phase==='trial'?r.trial[p.id]:null)||null:null,
    votes:['vote','defense','trial','verdict','finished'].includes(r.phase)?tally(r.votes,r.field?.rules===2?undefined:r.players):{},voted:['vote','defense','trial','verdict'].includes(r.phase)?Object.keys(r.votes):[],trial:tally(r.trial),candidate:r.candidate,mafiaVotes,
    messages:r.messages.filter(m=>m.channel==='town'||(m.channel==='mafia'&&mafia&&p?.alive&&['night','field'].includes(r.phase))||(m.channel==='ghost'&&p&&(!p.alive||(p.role==='medium'&&['night','field'].includes(r.phase))))),events:r.events,announcement:r.announcement,winner:r.winner,winnerIds:r.winnerIds||[]};
}
export type View=ReturnType<typeof view>;

import type {Point} from './map.ts';
export const INPUT_STEP_MS=20;
const directions:Record<string,Point>={KeyW:{x:0,y:-1},ArrowUp:{x:0,y:-1},KeyS:{x:0,y:1},ArrowDown:{x:0,y:1},KeyA:{x:-1,y:0},ArrowLeft:{x:-1,y:0},KeyD:{x:1,y:0},ArrowRight:{x:1,y:0}};
export function movementKey(code:string){return Object.hasOwn(directions,code);}
export function movementVector(held:Set<string>,stick:Point={x:0,y:0}):Point{let x=stick.x,y=stick.y;const up=held.has('KeyW')||held.has('ArrowUp'),down=held.has('KeyS')||held.has('ArrowDown'),left=held.has('KeyA')||held.has('ArrowLeft'),right=held.has('KeyD')||held.has('ArrowRight');x+=Number(right)-Number(left);y+=Number(down)-Number(up);const scale=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(x,y));return{x:x/scale,y:y/scale};}
export function inputSteps(remainder:number,delta:number){const total=remainder+Math.max(0,Math.min(100,delta)),steps=Math.floor((total+1e-7)/INPUT_STEP_MS);return{steps,remainder:Math.max(0,total-steps*INPUT_STEP_MS)};}

export function reachAmount(elapsed:number){if(elapsed<0||elapsed>=620)return 0;const t=elapsed<260?elapsed/260:elapsed<360?1:1-(elapsed-360)/260;return t*t*(3-2*t);}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/lag-compensation.ts
