import type {MapContext} from './map.ts';
import {distance,sight,type Point} from './map.ts';
export type HistoricPose=Point&{at:number;warp?:number;seq:number;history?:string|HistoricPose[]};
// How far back an attacker's rendered view may be honoured. It must cover the remote
// sample age plus the observer's interpolation delay (see lib/remote-motion.ts). With D1
// 170 ms away and players on different isolates the simulator measured a p95 render age of
// ~810 ms. A longer window only means more presses on a visible target succeed; it never delays a kill.
export const REWIND_MS=1100;
export const HISTORY_SQL="json_insert(CASE WHEN json_array_length(field_positions.history)>=12 THEN json_remove(field_positions.history,'$[0]') ELSE field_positions.history END,'$[#]',json_object('x',field_positions.x,'y',field_positions.y,'at',field_positions.at,'warp',field_positions.warp,'seq',field_positions.seq))";
export function rewindTarget(target:HistoricPose,at:unknown,now:number,count:MapContext):Point|null{
 if(typeof at!=='number'||!Number.isFinite(at)||at<now-REWIND_MS||at>now+50)return null;
 let history:HistoricPose[]=[];try{history=Array.isArray(target.history)?target.history:JSON.parse(target.history||'[]');}catch{return null;}
 if(!Array.isArray(history))return null;
 const samples=[...history,target].filter(p=>p.seq===target.seq&&(p.warp||0)===(target.warp||0)&&p.at>=now-REWIND_MS-600).sort((a,b)=>a.at-b.at);
 if(!samples.length||at<samples[0].at||at>target.at+50)return null;
 if(at>=target.at)return target;
 for(let i=1;i<samples.length;i++){const a=samples[i-1],b=samples[i];if(at>b.at)continue;if(!sight(a,b,count)||distance(a,b)>205*1.5*(b.at-a.at)/1000+12)return null;const u=(at-a.at)/(b.at-a.at);return{x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*u,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*u};}return null;
}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/live-client.ts
======================================================================
export type LiveStatus={connected:boolean;rtt:number;checkpointAge:number;instance?:string;reconnects?:number};
type ActWaiter={resolve:(v:Record<string,unknown>)=>void;reject:(e:Error)=>void;timer:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>};
export class LiveActionError extends Error{rejected=true;}
type Options={url:string;code:string;token:string;input:()=>Record<string,unknown>|null;frame:(data:any)=>void;status:(s:LiveStatus)=>void;
 ready?:(m:{ack:number;warp:number;seq:number;instance?:string})=>void;resend?:(m:{after:number;warp:number;seq:number})=>void;rebase?:(m:{own:any})=>void};
// Independent send/receive: a slow acknowledgement never prevents newer input being sent.
// Field actions travel on the same socket (request/response by id) so they are applied by
// the same server isolate, in order, right after the inputs that preceded them.
export class LiveClient{
 socket:WebSocket|null=null;lastFrame=0;ready=false;rtt=0;checkpointAge=0;resync=false;closed=false;instance='';reconnects=0;
 retry=0;timer?:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>;attempt=0;nextPing=0;next:WebSocket|null=null;waiters=new Map<string,ActWaiter>();
 private options:Options;
 constructor(options:Options){this.options=options;}
 healthy(){return this.ready&&!this.resync&&performance.now()-this.lastFrame<1000&&this.checkpointAge<1000;}
 start(){this.closed=false;this.connect();const send=()=>{if(this.closed)return;const ws=this.socket,now=performance.now();if(ws?.readyState===WebSocket.OPEN&&this.ready&&ws.bufferedAmount<32000){const input=this.options.input();if(input)ws.send(JSON.stringify({type:'input',...input}));if(now>=this.nextPing){ws.send(JSON.stringify({type:'ping',sent:now}));this.nextPing=now+2000;}}if(this.ready&&now-this.lastFrame>2500)ws?.close();this.timer=setTimeout(send,50);};this.timer=setTimeout(send,50);}
 status(){this.options.status({connected:this.healthy(),rtt:this.rtt,checkpointAge:this.checkpointAge,instance:this.instance,reconnects:this.reconnects});}
 // handover=true opens a replacement socket while the current one keeps working; the
 // swap happens when the new one is authenticated (server-requested recycling).
 connect(handover=false){if(this.closed)return;const ws=new WebSocket(this.options.url);if(handover)this.next=ws;else this.socket=ws;let authed=false;const timeout=setTimeout(()=>{if(!authed)ws.close();},5000);
  ws.onopen=()=>ws.send(JSON.stringify({type:'auth',code:this.options.code,token:this.options.token}));
  ws.onmessage=event=>{try{const data=JSON.parse(event.data);
   if(data.type==='ready'){authed=true;clearTimeout(timeout);
    if(ws===this.next){const old=this.socket;this.socket=ws;this.next=null;this.reconnects++;setTimeout(()=>{try{old?.close();}catch{}},400);}
    else if(ws!==this.socket)return;
    // The stall watchdog (no frame for 2.5 s) starts counting from authentication, not page load.
    this.ready=true;this.attempt=0;this.lastFrame=performance.now();this.instance=String(data.instance||'');this.options.ready?.(data);}
   else if(data.type==='frame'){this.lastFrame=performance.now();this.checkpointAge=Number(data.checkpointAge)||0;this.resync=data.resync===true;this.options.frame(data);this.status();}
   else if(data.type==='actResult'){const w=this.waiters.get(String(data.id));if(w){this.waiters.delete(String(data.id));clearTimeout(w.timer);if(data.ok)w.resolve(data);else w.reject(new LiveActionError(String(data.error||'요청을 처리하지 못했습니다.')));}}
   else if(data.type==='resend')this.options.resend?.(data);
   else if(data.type==='rebase')this.options.rebase?.(data);
   else if(data.type==='recycle'){if(ws===this.socket&&!this.next)this.connect(true);}
   else if(data.type==='pong'&&typeof data.sent==='number'){this.rtt=Math.max(0,Math.round(performance.now()-data.sent));this.status();}
   else if(data.type==='error')ws.close();}catch{ws.close();}};
  ws.onerror=()=>{};ws.onclose=event=>{clearTimeout(timeout);if(ws===this.next){this.next=null;return;}if(this.socket!==ws)return;this.ready=false;this.failWaiters();this.status();if(this.closed)return;
   // A recycle that could not hand over reconnects at once; real failures back off.
   const delay=(event as CloseEvent)?.code===4000?0:Math.min(8000,400*2**this.attempt++);this.reconnects++;this.retry=window.setTimeout(()=>this.connect(),delay);};
 }
 // Resolves with the server reply; rejects with LiveActionError when the server refused the
 // action, or a plain Error when the transport failed (caller may retry over HTTP: the
 // server de-duplicates by requestId).
 act(body:Record<string,unknown>,timeoutMs=3500):Promise<Record<string,unknown>>{
  const ws=this.socket;if(!ws||ws.readyState!==WebSocket.OPEN||!this.ready)return Promise.reject(new Error('live socket not ready'));
  const id=Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)+Date.now().toString(36);
  return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const timer=setTimeout(()=>{this.waiters.delete(id);reject(new Error('live action timeout'));},timeoutMs);this.waiters.set(id,{resolve,reject,timer});try{ws.send(JSON.stringify({type:'act',id,body}));}catch(e){clearTimeout(timer);this.waiters.delete(id);reject(e instanceof Error?e:new Error('send failed'));}});
 }
 failWaiters(){for(const [id,w] of this.waiters){clearTimeout(w.timer);w.reject(new Error('live socket closed'));this.waiters.delete(id);}}
 stop(){this.closed=true;this.ready=false;clearTimeout(this.timer);clearTimeout(this.retry);this.failWaiters();this.socket?.close();this.next?.close();this.socket=null;this.next=null;}
}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/live-server.ts

import {locationAt,moveTo,distance,WORLD,type Point} from './map.ts';
import {database,hash,roomGate,roomWrite,liveQueryBudget,type RoomRow} from './store';
import {actor,view,tick,type Room} from './game';
import {fieldAct,fieldSnapshot,poseFor,type Pose,type FieldBody} from './field';
import {consumeInputs,type MoveInput} from './motion';
import {HISTORY_SQL,REWIND_MS,type HistoricPose} from './lag-compensation';

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Realtime field server.
//
// A Cloudflare Worker has no per-room singleton: every WebSocket may land in a different
// isolate. Earlier versions therefore relayed everybody's position through D1 on every
// tick, which made other players lag 0.5-2 s, flicker out of view and made attacks miss.
//
// Now each isolate is the single authority ("owner") for the players whose sockets it
// holds: their inputs are applied in memory the moment they arrive and are broadcast to
// every peer in the same isolate immediately. D1 is used only to (a) persist owned poses
// for other isolates and for reconnects and (b) learn about players owned elsewhere.
// One D1 batch per cycle carries both, and the large room JSON is only transferred when
// its version changed. Ownership is a lease in field_positions (owner/owner_at): a newer
// connection for the same player takes it over and the older one is closed.
//
// Field actions (attack, report, missions...) are also sent over the socket and run in
// the actor's isolate against exactly the positions that actor was shown, so "what you
// saw is what you hit" even for targets owned by another isolate.
//
// D1 limits queries per Worker invocation; a WebSocket is one invocation. Every D1
// statement is charged to the peer whose context issued it, the cycle is driven by the
// peer with the most budget left, and a peer close to its budget is asked to reconnect
// (seamless: the client re-sends unconfirmed inputs).
//
// Workers forbid I/O on behalf of another request: a socket may only be written from the
// request (connection) that accepted it, and a D1 call belongs to the connection that made
// it. Shared room state is therefore never pushed to other sockets directly: work done in one
// connection's context marks peers (force/outbox) and each peer's own 50 ms pump delivers.
// A cycle whose connection ended mid-call is abandoned after CYCLE_STALE_MS.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

type Row=Pose&{owner:string;owner_at:number};
type Peer={socket:WebSocket;key:string;id:string|null;conn:string;owner:string;claimedAt:number;revision:number;seen:number;rateAt:number;rateCount:number;
 replaced?:boolean;closed?:boolean;retiringSince?:number;signature?:string;lastBroadcast:number;lastDiag:number;queries:number;visible:Map<string,number>;wantResend?:number;
 /** messages for this socket queued by other request contexts; only its own pump sends them */ outbox:unknown[];force?:boolean;closeWith?:[number,string]};
type Owned={peer:Peer;pose:Pose;queue:Map<number,MoveInput>;dirty:boolean;written:number;heartbeat:number;gapSince:number|null;resendAsked:number};

const CYCLE_STALE_MS=6000,MOTION_JITTER_MS=100,MAX_INFLIGHT=2,CYCLE_MS=70,QUIET_CYCLE_MS=220,BROADCAST_MS=50,TICK_MS=300,HISTORY_KEEP=REWIND_MS+700;
const rooms=new Map<string,LiveRoom>();
let INSTANCE='';
const send=(socket:WebSocket,data:unknown)=>{try{if(socket.readyState===1)socket.send(JSON.stringify(data));}catch{/* closed underneath us */}};
// Safe from any context: delivered by the peer's own pump within one broadcast interval.
const post=(peer:Peer,data:unknown)=>{if(!peer.closed&&peer.outbox.length<64)peer.outbox.push(data);};
const READ_SQL=`SELECT r.version AS version,r.expires AS expires,r.live_gate AS gate,CASE WHEN r.version<>?1 THEN r.state END AS state,
 (SELECT json_group_array(json_array(p.player,p.x,p.y,p.at,p.seq,p.ack,p.clock,p.warp,p.previous,p.place,p.owner,p.owner_at)) FROM field_positions p WHERE p.code=r.code) AS poses
 FROM mafia_rooms r WHERE r.code=?2`;
// One statement persists every dirty owned pose. Rows are only touched by their lease
// holder, only while the room is in the same unpaused field phase and the player is not
// locked in a vent/CCTV, and never move backwards in input order.
const FLUSH_SQL=`INSERT INTO field_positions (code,player,x,y,at,seq,ack,clock,warp,previous,place,owner,owner_at)
 SELECT ?1,json_extract(j.value,'$[0]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[1]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[2]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[3]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[4]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[5]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[6]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[7]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[8]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[9]'),json_extract(j.value,'$[10]'),?4
 FROM json_each(?2) j WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM mafia_rooms r WHERE r.code=?1 AND r.expires>?4 AND r.live_gate=?3 AND instr(r.live_locks,'|'||json_extract(j.value,'$[0]')||'|')=0)
 ON CONFLICT(code,player) DO UPDATE SET history=CASE WHEN field_positions.ack<>excluded.ack OR field_positions.seq<>excluded.seq THEN ${HISTORY_SQL} ELSE field_positions.history END,
 x=excluded.x,y=excluded.y,at=excluded.at,seq=excluded.seq,ack=excluded.ack,clock=excluded.clock,warp=excluded.warp,previous=excluded.previous,place=excluded.place,owner_at=excluded.owner_at
 WHERE field_positions.owner=excluded.owner AND (field_positions.seq<>excluded.seq OR field_positions.warp<>excluded.warp OR field_positions.ack<excluded.ack OR field_positions.owner_at<excluded.owner_at-900)`;
// Taking over a player always succeeds; it also creates the row for a new epoch.
const CLAIM_SQL=`INSERT INTO field_positions (code,player,x,y,at,seq,ack,clock,warp,previous,place,owner,owner_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
 ON CONFLICT(code,player) DO UPDATE SET owner=excluded.owner,owner_at=excluded.owner_at`;
// Extrapolate remote (other-isolate) actors for visibility only; never for positions or hits.
const PREDICT_MS=500;
const parsePoses=(json:string|null):Row[]=>{if(!json)return[];try{return(JSON.parse(json) as unknown[][]).map(a=>({id:String(a[0]),x:Number(a[1]),y:Number(a[2]),at:Number(a[3]),seq:Number(a[4]),ack:Number(a[5]),clock:Number(a[6]),warp:Number(a[7]),previous:String(a[8]),place:String(a[9]),owner:String(a[10]||''),owner_at:Number(a[11]||0)}));}catch{return[];}};
const isQuotaError=(e:unknown)=>/too many|limit|exceed|subrequest/i.test(e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e));
// The host's per-connection D1 query cap is learned from its own error message, so nothing
// has to be configured whatever plan the hosting runs on: after the first "too many
// requests/subrequests" error, every connection in this isolate hands over a few queries
// before that point. LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET (default 800) only sets an upper bound.
const isCapError=(e:unknown)=>/too many (api )?(sub)?requests|subrequest/i.test(e instanceof Error?e.message:String(e));
let learnedBudget=Infinity;
const queryBudget=()=>Math.min(liveQueryBudget(),learnedBudget);

class LiveRoom{
 peers=new Set<Peer>();owned=new Map<string,Owned>();rows=new Map<string,Row>();history=new Map<string,HistoricPose[]>();
 r:Room;version:number;fresh=Date.now();closed=false;cycles=new Map<number,number>();tickingSince=0;nextCycle=0;nextTick=0;cycleSeq=0;applied=0;
 stats={rtt:[] as number[],statements:[] as number[],errors:0,recycles:0};
 constructor(r:Room,version:number,rows:Row[]){this.r=r;this.version=version;this.r.revision=version;this.ingestRows(rows,Date.now());}
 // ---- helpers -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 player(id:string|null){return id?this.r.players.find(p=>p.id===id):undefined;}
 remember(id:string,p:HistoricPose){const h=this.history.get(id)||[];const last=h.at(-1);if(last&&last.at===p.at&&last.x===p.x&&last.y===p.y)return;h.push({x:p.x,y:p.y,at:p.at,warp:p.warp||0,seq:p.seq});const from=Date.now()-HISTORY_KEEP;while(h.length>2&&h[0].at<from)h.shift();this.history.set(id,h);}
 // Everybody's best known pose: owned players from memory, the rest from D1.
 poses(now:number,withHistory=false):Pose[]{
  const out:Pose[]=[];
  for(const p of this.r.players){const o=this.owned.get(p.id);const pose=o?o.pose:this.rows.get(p.id);if(!pose)continue;out.push(withHistory?{...pose,history:(this.history.get(p.id)||[]).filter(h=>h.at<pose.at) as never}:pose);}
  void now;return out;
 }
 // Lazily evaluated (only for actors near the edge of someone's view) and memoised per 50 ms.
 private guesses=new Map<string,{bucket:number;point:Point|undefined}>();
 predict(id:string,now:number):Point|undefined{
  const bucket=Math.floor(now/50),hit=this.guesses.get(id);if(hit&&hit.bucket===bucket)return hit.point;
  let point:Point|undefined;const row=this.rows.get(id),h=this.history.get(id);
  if(row&&!this.owned.has(id)&&now-row.at<=1500&&h&&h.length>=2){const b=h[h.length-1],a=h[h.length-2],dt=b.at-a.at,d=distance(a,b);
   if(dt>0&&dt<=600&&b.at===row.at&&(b.warp||0)===(row.warp||0)&&(a.warp||0)===(b.warp||0)&&a.seq===b.seq&&d>=.5){const v=Math.min(WORLD.speed,d/(dt/1000)),e=Math.min(PREDICT_MS,now-b.at)/1000;point=moveTo(b,{x:b.x+(b.x-a.x)/d*v*e,y:b.y+(b.y-a.y)/d*v*e},v*e,this.r.config);}}
  this.guesses.set(id,{bucket,point});return point;
 }
 // Re-derive an owned pose after the room state changed (warps, new epoch after a meeting).
 normalize(o:Owned,now:number){const p=this.player(o.peer.id);if(!p)return;const next=poseFor(this.r,p,[o.pose],now);if(next!==o.pose){const epochChanged=next.seq!==o.pose.seq,warped=(next.warp||0)!==(o.pose.warp||0);o.pose={...next,ack:epochChanged?0:next.ack||0,clock:next.clock??now,warp:next.warp||0};o.dirty=true;if(epochChanged||warped)o.queue.clear();this.remember(p.id,o.pose);}}
 ingestRows(rows:Row[],now:number){
  for(const row of rows){this.rows.set(row.id,row);if(!this.owned.has(row.id))this.remember(row.id,row);}
  void now;
 }
 // ---- peers ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 add(peer:Peer,row:Row|undefined,now:number){
  for(const old of this.peers)if(old.key===peer.key){old.replaced=true;}
  this.peers.add(peer);
  const p=this.player(peer.id);if(!p)return;
  const prev=this.owned.get(p.id);if(prev&&prev.peer!==peer)prev.peer.replaced=true;
  const pose=poseFor(this.r,p,row?[row]:[],now);
  this.owned.set(p.id,{peer,pose:{...pose,ack:pose.ack||0,clock:pose.clock??now,warp:pose.warp||0},queue:new Map(),dirty:false,written:pose.ack||0,heartbeat:now,gapSince:null,resendAsked:0});
  this.remember(p.id,pose);
 }
 remove(peer:Peer){
  peer.closed=true;this.peers.delete(peer);
  for(const [id,o] of this.owned)if(o.peer===peer){this.owned.delete(id);const row=this.rows.get(id);if(row)this.rows.set(id,{...row,...o.pose,owner:row.owner,owner_at:row.owner_at});}
  if(!this.peers.size){this.closed=true;if(rooms.get(this.r.code)===this)rooms.delete(this.r.code);}
 }
 // Remaining D1 budget decides which peer pays for the shared cycle.
 driver(){let best:Peer|undefined;for(const p of this.peers)if(!p.replaced&&!p.closed&&!p.retiringSince&&(!best||p.queries<best.queries))best=p;return best;}
 async d1(peer:Peer,stmts:D1PreparedStatement[]){
  const before=peer.queries;peer.queries+=stmts.length;const t=Date.now();
  try{const out=await database().batch(stmts);this.stats.rtt.push(Date.now()-t);if(this.stats.rtt.length>40)this.stats.rtt.shift();this.stats.statements.push(...stmts.map(()=>Date.now()));return out;}
  catch(e){this.stats.errors++;if(isCapError(e))learnedBudget=Math.min(learnedBudget,Math.max(10,before-4));if(isQuotaError(e))peer.queries=Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;throw e;}
  finally{if(peer.queries>=queryBudget())this.recycle(peer);}
 }
 // Ask the client to open a fresh connection (new invocation, new budget). The old one
 // keeps serving frames until the new one has taken over, so there is no visible gap.
 recycle(peer:Peer){if(peer.closed||peer.replaced||peer.retiringSince)return;peer.retiringSince=Date.now();this.stats.recycles++;post(peer,{type:'recycle'});}
 inflight(now:number){for(const [id,start] of this.cycles)if(now-start>CYCLE_STALE_MS)this.cycles.delete(id);return this.cycles.size;}
 pump(peer:Peer){
  if(this.closed||peer.closed)return false;
  if(peer.replaced||Date.now()-peer.seen>12000||peer.retiringSince&&Date.now()-peer.retiringSince>8000){try{peer.socket.close(peer.retiringSince?4000:1001,peer.retiringSince?'recycle':'Reconnect');}catch{}this.remove(peer);return false;}
  if(peer.closeWith){try{peer.socket.close(...peer.closeWith);}catch{}this.remove(peer);return false;}
  for(const m of peer.outbox.splice(0))send(peer.socket,m);
  const now=Date.now();
  for(const o of this.owned.values())if(o.peer===peer)this.advance(o,now);
  this.broadcast(peer,!!peer.force);peer.force=false;
  if(this.driver()===peer&&this.inflight(now)<MAX_INFLIGHT&&now>=this.nextCycle)void this.cycle(peer);
  return true;
 }
 // ---- movement -------------------------------------------------------------------------
 ingest(peer:Peer,b:Record<string,unknown>){
  const now=Date.now();peer.seen=now;
  if(b.type==='ping'){send(peer.socket,{type:'pong',sent:b.sent,serverNow:now});return;}
  if(b.type==='act'){void this.act(peer,b);return;}
  if(b.type!=='input')return;
  if(now-peer.rateAt>=1000){peer.rateAt=now;peer.rateCount=0;}if(++peer.rateCount>45){peer.socket.close(1008,'Too many inputs');return;}
  const o=peer.id?this.owned.get(peer.id):undefined;if(!o||o.peer!==peer)return;
  this.queue(o,b,now);this.advance(o,now);
 }
 queue(o:Owned,b:Record<string,unknown>,now:number){
  if(b.seq!==this.r.seq||!Array.isArray(b.inputs)||b.inputs.length>360||(Number(b.warp)||0)!==(o.pose.warp||0))return;
  const ack=o.pose.ack||0;
  for(const raw of b.inputs){const i=raw as MoveInput;if(!i||!Number.isSafeInteger(i.n)||i.n<=ack||i.n>ack+360||!Number.isFinite(i.ms)||i.ms<=0||i.ms>50||!Number.isFinite(i.dx)||!Number.isFinite(i.dy)||Math.abs(i.dx)>1||Math.abs(i.dy)>1)continue;if(!o.queue.has(i.n))o.queue.set(i.n,{n:i.n,dx:i.dx,dy:i.dy,ms:i.ms});}
  void now;
 }
 advance(o:Owned,now:number){
  const r=this.r,f=r.field,p=this.player(o.peer.id);if(!p||!f||r.phase!=='field'||r.paused||f.venting?.[p.id]||f.watching?.[p.id])return false;
  this.normalize(o,now);
  const base=o.pose,ack=base.ack||0;
  for(const n of o.queue.keys())if(n<=ack)o.queue.delete(n);
  if(!o.queue.size){o.gapSince=null;return false;}
  const ordered=[...o.queue.values()].sort((a,b)=>a.n-b.n);
  if(ordered[0].n!==ack+1){
   // A hole in the sequence (e.g. after a reconnect): ask the client to resend once,
   // then tell it to rebase on our pose if it cannot.
   o.gapSince??=now;
   if(now-o.resendAsked>600){o.resendAsked=now;post(o.peer,{type:'resend',after:ack,warp:base.warp||0,seq:base.seq});}
   if(now-o.gapSince>2500){o.queue.clear();o.gapSince=null;post(o.peer,{type:'rebase',own:{...base,history:undefined}});}
   return false;
  }
  o.gapSince=null;
  const next=consumeInputs({...base,ack,clock:base.clock??base.at},ordered,now,r.config);
  if(next.ack===ack)return false;
  let consumed=0;for(const i of ordered){if(i.n>next.ack)break;consumed+=i.ms;o.queue.delete(i.n);}
  // Stamp with motion time: while moving, consecutive samples are exactly as far apart as
  // the movement they describe (observers interpolate at true speed, not network rhythm).
  // After a pause, or when inputs fall too far behind, re-anchor to arrival time.
  const continuous=base.at+consumed>=now-MOTION_JITTER_MS;
  const at=Math.round(Math.max(base.at+1,continuous?Math.min(now,base.at+consumed):now));
  const place=locationAt(next,r.config);
  o.pose={...base,x:next.x,y:next.y,ack:next.ack,clock:next.clock,at,place,previous:place!==base.place?base.place:base.previous};
  o.dirty=true;this.remember(p.id,o.pose);return true;
 }
 // ---- broadcast ------------------------------------------------------------------------
 broadcast(peer:Peer,force=false){
  if(this.closed||peer.closed)return;const now=Date.now(),poses=this.poses(now);
  const sig=`${this.version}:${this.r.seq}:${this.r.paused}:${poses.map(p=>`${p.id}:${p.ack}:${p.at}:${p.warp}`).join('|')}`;
  if(!force&&sig===peer.signature&&now-peer.lastBroadcast<250)return;peer.signature=sig;peer.lastBroadcast=now;
  try{
   const {p}=actor(this.r,peer.key),frame=fieldSnapshot(this.r,peer.key,poses,now,peer.visible,id=>this.predict(id,now));
   const o=p?this.owned.get(p.id):undefined;if(o)frame.own={...o.pose,history:undefined};
   const changed=peer.revision!==this.version;
   const diag=now-peer.lastDiag>=1000?this.diagnostics(peer,now):undefined;if(diag)peer.lastDiag=now;
   send(peer.socket,{type:'frame',transport:'live',instance:INSTANCE,revision:this.version,...(changed?{room:view(this.r,peer.key)}:{}),frame,checkpointAge:now-this.fresh,resync:false,...(diag?{diag}:{})});peer.revision=this.version;
  }catch{try{peer.socket.close(1008,'Session ended');}catch{}this.remove(peer);}
 }
 diagnostics(peer:Peer,now:number){
  const owners=new Set<string>();for(const row of this.rows.values())if(row.owner&&row.owner.startsWith('live:')&&row.owner_at>now-4000)owners.add(row.owner.split(':')[1]);
  const statements=this.stats.statements.filter(t=>t>now-5000);this.stats.statements=statements;
  const rtt=this.stats.rtt.length?Math.round(this.stats.rtt.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/this.stats.rtt.length):0;
  return{instance:INSTANCE.slice(0,8),conn:peer.conn,peersHere:this.peers.size,ownedHere:this.owned.size,instances:Math.max(1,owners.size),dbRttMs:rtt,dbStatementsPerSec:Math.round(statements.length/5*10)/10,queries:peer.queries,budget:queryBudget(),dbErrors:this.stats.errors,recycles:this.stats.recycles,checkpointAge:now-this.fresh};
 }
 // ---- D1 cycle --------------------------------------------------------------------------
 // Up to MAX_INFLIGHT cycles overlap so the cadence does not depend on the D1 round trip.
 async cycle(driver:Peer){
  const start=Date.now();if(this.closed||this.inflight(start)>=MAX_INFLIGHT)return;const id=++this.cycleSeq;this.cycles.set(id,start);
  {let remote=false;for(const row of this.rows.values())if(!this.owned.has(row.id)&&row.owner&&row.owner_at>start-4000){remote=true;break;}this.nextCycle=start+(remote||this.owned.size===0?CYCLE_MS:QUIET_CYCLE_MS);}
  try{
   const items:unknown[][]=[],flushed:[Owned,number][]=[];
   for(const o of this.owned.values()){if(!o.dirty&&start-o.heartbeat<1000)continue;const q=o.pose;items.push([q.id,q.x,q.y,Math.round(q.at),q.seq,q.ack||0,q.clock??q.at,q.warp||0,q.previous,q.place,o.peer.owner]);flushed.push([o,q.ack||0]);}
   const db=database(),stmts:D1PreparedStatement[]=[];
   if(items.length)stmts.push(db.prepare(FLUSH_SQL).bind(this.r.code,JSON.stringify(items),roomGate(this.r),start));
   stmts.push(db.prepare(READ_SQL).bind(this.version,this.r.code));
   const res=await this.d1(driver,stmts);
   for(const [o] of flushed){o.heartbeat=start;}
   const row=res.at(-1)!.results[0] as {version:number;expires:number;gate:string;state:string|null;poses:string}|undefined;
   if(!row||row.expires<Date.now()){this.closed=true;for(const p of this.peers)p.closeWith=[1001,'Room closed'];return;}
   if(id<this.applied)return;this.applied=id;
   const now=Date.now();
   if(row.state&&row.version>this.version)this.adopt(JSON.parse(row.state) as Room,row.version,now);
   const rows=parsePoses(row.poses);this.ingestRows(rows,now);
   // Persisted owned rows: drop the dirty flag once D1 has our pose; detect takeovers.
   for(const [o,ack] of flushed){const saved=this.rows.get(o.pose.id);if(saved&&saved.owner===o.peer.owner&&(saved.ack||0)>=ack&&saved.seq===o.pose.seq&&(o.pose.ack||0)===ack)o.dirty=false;o.written=Math.max(o.written,saved?.ack||0);}
   for(const o of [...this.owned.values()]){const saved=this.rows.get(o.pose.id);if(!saved||start<o.peer.claimedAt)continue;
    if(saved.owner!==o.peer.owner){
     // Newer live connection elsewhere: step aside. HTTP fallback or a stale row: re-claim.
     if(saved.owner.startsWith('live:')&&saved.owner_at>=o.peer.claimedAt){o.peer.replaced=true;continue;}
     await this.claim(o.peer,o,driver);
    }}
   if(!row.gate&&this.r.phase==='field')void this.d1(driver,[roomWrite(db,this.r,this.r.code,this.version)]).then(()=>{}).catch(()=>{});
   this.fresh=now;
   if(now>=this.nextTick&&now-this.tickingSince>CYCLE_STALE_MS){this.nextTick=now+TICK_MS;this.tickingSince=now;try{await this.tick(driver,now);}finally{this.tickingSince=0;}}
  }catch(error){console.warn('Live cycle unavailable',error instanceof Error?error.message:'unknown');}
  finally{this.cycles.delete(id);}
 }
 async claim(peer:Peer,o:Owned,driver:Peer){
  // Somebody else (HTTP fallback) moved this player: continue from the stored row.
  const saved=this.rows.get(o.pose.id);if(saved&&saved.seq===o.pose.seq&&(saved.ack||0)>(o.pose.ack||0)){o.pose={...o.pose,x:saved.x,y:saved.y,at:Math.max(o.pose.at,saved.at),ack:saved.ack,clock:saved.clock,place:saved.place,previous:saved.previous};o.queue.clear();post(peer,{type:'resend',after:saved.ack||0,warp:o.pose.warp||0,seq:o.pose.seq});}
  const q=o.pose,now=Date.now();peer.claimedAt=now;
  await this.d1(driver,[database().prepare(CLAIM_SQL).bind(this.r.code,q.id,q.x,q.y,Math.round(q.at),q.seq,q.ack||0,q.clock??q.at,q.warp||0,q.previous,q.place,peer.owner,now)]);
  o.dirty=true;
 }
 adopt(r:Room,version:number,now:number){
  if(version<=this.version)return;r.revision=version;this.r=r;this.version=version;
  for(const o of this.owned.values())this.normalize(o,now);
  // Each connection sends the new room on its own next pump (cross-request I/O is not allowed).
  for(const peer of this.peers)peer.force=true;
 }
 async tick(driver:Peer,now:number){
  const draft=structuredClone(this.r);if(!tick(draft,now))return;
  const v=this.version;draft.revision=v+1;
  const res=await this.d1(driver,[roomWrite(database(),draft,this.r.code,v)]);
  if(res[0].meta.changes===1)this.adopt(draft,v+1,Date.now());
 }
 // ---- actions ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 async act(peer:Peer,msg:Record<string,unknown>){
  const id=String(msg.id||''),body=(msg.body||{}) as FieldBody&{inputs?:unknown;warp?:number};
  const reply=(data:Record<string,unknown>)=>send(peer.socket,{type:'actResult',id,...data});
  try{
   if(typeof body.action!=='string'||body.action==='frame')throw Error('알 수 없는 현장 행동입니다.');
   const o=peer.id?this.owned.get(peer.id):undefined;
   // Inputs sent with the action are applied first, in order, by this same owner.
   if(o&&o.peer===peer){this.queue(o,{inputs:body.inputs,seq:body.seq,warp:body.warp},Date.now());this.advance(o,Date.now());}
   for(let attempt=0;attempt<8;attempt++){
    const now=Date.now(),v=this.version,draft:Room=structuredClone(this.r);draft.revision=v;
    const poses=this.poses(now,true);
    fieldAct(draft,peer.key,body,poses,now);draft.revision=v+1;
    const res=await this.d1(peer,[roomWrite(database(),draft,this.r.code,v)]);
    if(res[0].meta.changes===1){
     if(this.version===v)this.adopt(draft,v+1,Date.now());
     const after=Date.now();
     reply({ok:true,revision:this.version,room:view(this.r,peer.key),frame:{...fieldSnapshot(this.r,peer.key,this.poses(after),after,peer.visible),...(o?{own:{...o.pose,history:undefined}}:{})}});
     peer.revision=this.version;return;
    }
    // Someone else changed the room first: back off briefly, load it and try again.
    await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,Math.random()*30));
    const fresh=await this.d1(peer,[database().prepare(READ_SQL).bind(this.version,this.r.code)]);
    const row=fresh[0].results[0] as {version:number;state:string|null;poses:string}|undefined;
    if(!row)throw Error('방을 찾을 수 없습니다.');
    if(row.state&&row.version>this.version)this.adopt(JSON.parse(row.state) as Room,row.version,Date.now());
    this.ingestRows(parsePoses(row.poses),Date.now());
   }
   throw Error('요청이 몰리고 있습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
  }catch(e){reply({ok:false,error:e instanceof Error?e.message:'현장 연결을 다시 시도해 주세요.'});}
 }
}

export function acceptLive(server:WebSocket){
 INSTANCE||=crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll('-','');server.accept();
 let peer:Peer|undefined,room:LiveRoom|undefined,authenticating=false;let pumpTimer:ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>|undefined;
 const authTimeout=setTimeout(()=>{if(!peer)server.close(1008,'Authentication timeout');},5000);
 const close=()=>{clearTimeout(authTimeout);clearTimeout(pumpTimer);if(peer&&room)room.remove(peer);};server.addEventListener('close',close);server.addEventListener('error',close);
 server.addEventListener('message',async event=>{
  try{
   if(typeof event.data!=='string'||event.data.length>24000){server.close(1008,'Invalid message');return;}
   const b=JSON.parse(event.data);if(peer&&room){room.ingest(peer,b);return;}
   if(authenticating)return;if(b.type!=='auth'||!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(b.token)||!/^[A-Z2-9]{6}$/.test(b.code)){server.close(1008,'Authentication required');return;}
   authenticating=true;const key=await hash(b.token),db=database(),conn=crypto.randomUUID().slice(0,8);
   let existing=rooms.get(b.code);if(existing?.closed)existing=undefined;
   const results=await db.batch([db.prepare(READ_SQL).bind(existing?existing.version:-1,b.code)]);
   const row=results[0].results[0] as (RoomRow&{poses:string;gate:string})|undefined;if(!row||row.expires<Date.now())throw Error('Room expired');
   const rows=parsePoses(row.poses);
   let r:Room;if(row.state){r=JSON.parse(row.state);r.revision=row.version;}else r=existing!.r;
   const who=actor(r,key);if(r.phase!=='field'||!r.field)throw Error('Field phase required');
   if(server.readyState!==1)return;
   room=rooms.get(b.code);if(!room||room.closed){if(rooms.size>=64)throw Error('Connection capacity');room=new LiveRoom(r,row.version,rows);rooms.set(b.code,room);}
   else{if(row.state&&row.version>room.version)room.adopt(r,row.version,Date.now());room.ingestRows(rows,Date.now());}
   if(room.peers.size>=64)throw Error('Room capacity');
   const now=Date.now();
   peer={socket:server,key,id:who.p?.id||null,conn,owner:`live:${INSTANCE}:${conn}`,claimedAt:now,revision:-1,seen:now,rateAt:now,rateCount:0,lastBroadcast:0,lastDiag:0,queries:1,visible:new Map(),outbox:[]};
   room.add(peer,who.p?rows.find(x=>x.id===who.p!.id):undefined,now);
   const o=who.p?room.owned.get(who.p.id):undefined;
   if(o)await room.claim(peer,o,peer);
   clearTimeout(authTimeout);
   // The client re-sends every input it has not seen persisted beyond this point.
   send(server,{type:'ready',instance:INSTANCE.slice(0,8),conn,ack:o?.pose.ack||0,warp:o?.pose.warp||0,seq:o?.pose.seq??r.seq});
   const pump=()=>{if(peer&&room&&server.readyState===1&&room.pump(peer))pumpTimer=setTimeout(pump,BROADCAST_MS);};pump();
  }catch{send(server,{type:'error',error:'실시간 연결을 인증하지 못했습니다. 일반 연결로 복구합니다.'});try{server.close(1008,'Live connection rejected');}catch{}close();}
 });
}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/map-render.ts
======================================================================
import {roomFloors} from './map.ts';
import type {Location,Point,Rect} from './map.ts';
export type FacilityMap={id?:string;width:number;height:number;locations:Location[];corridors:Rect[]};
type Edge={x1:number;y1:number;x2:number;y2:number};
function merge(intervals:number[][]){const result:number[][]=[];for(const pair of intervals.sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0])){const last=result.at(-1);if(last&&pair[0]<=last[1])last[1]=Math.max(last[1],pair[1]);else result.push([...pair]);}return result;}
// Only the outside boundary gets a wall: adjoining corridor pieces remain open.
export function floorEdges(rects:Rect[]):Edge[]{const result:Edge[]=[],seen=new Set<string>();for(const r of rects)for(const side of [0,1,2,3]){const vertical=side<2,edge=vertical?r.x+(side?r.w:0):r.y+(side===3?r.h:0),outside=edge+(side===0||side===2?-.1:.1),low=vertical?r.y:r.x,high=low+(vertical?r.h:r.w),covered=merge(rects.filter(n=>n!==r&&(vertical?outside>n.x&&outside<n.x+n.w:outside>n.y&&outside<n.y+n.h)).map(n=>[Math.max(low,vertical?n.y:n.x),Math.min(high,vertical?n.y+n.h:n.x+n.w)]).filter(([a,b])=>b>a));let cursor=low;for(const [a,b] of [...covered,[high,high]]){if(a>cursor){const e=vertical?{x1:edge,y1:cursor,x2:edge,y2:a}:{x1:cursor,y1:edge,x2:a,y2:edge},key=JSON.stringify(e);if(!seen.has(key)){seen.add(key);result.push(e);}}cursor=Math.max(cursor,b);}}return result;}
export function roomAtPoint(map:FacilityMap,p:Point){return map.locations.find(r=>p.x>=r.x&&p.x<=r.x+r.w&&p.y>=r.y&&p.y<=r.y+r.h);}
export function viewForRoom(map:FacilityMap,id:string|null,width:number,height:number){const room=map.locations.find(r=>r.id===id);return room?{x:room.x+room.w/2,y:room.y+room.h/2,scale:Math.min((width-40)/(room.w+180),(height-100)/(room.h+180),1.8)}:{x:map.width/2,y:map.height/2,scale:Math.min((width-30)/map.width,(height-100)/map.height)};}
export function screenToWorld(p:Point,view:Point&{scale:number;width:number;height:number}):Point{return{x:view.x+(p.x-view.width/2)/view.scale,y:view.y+(p.y-view.height/2)/view.scale};}
export function drawFacility(ctx:CanvasRenderingContext2D,map:FacilityMap,rooms:CanvasImageSource|null){
 if(map.id==='nereid'){drawMarineFacility(ctx,map);return;}
 ctx.clearRect(0,0,map.width,map.height);ctx.fillStyle='#080f1a';ctx.fillRect(0,0,map.width,map.height);
 const edges=floorEdges([...map.locations.flatMap(roomFloors),...map.corridors]);ctx.beginPath();for(const e of edges){ctx.moveTo(e.x1,e.y1+9);ctx.lineTo(e.x2,e.y2+9);}ctx.lineWidth=26;ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.strokeStyle='#020711';ctx.stroke();
 for(const r of map.locations){ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();for(const f of roomFloors(r))ctx.rect(f.x,f.y,f.w,f.h);ctx.clip();ctx.fillStyle='#193140';ctx.fillRect(r.x,r.y,r.w,r.h);if(rooms){const ys=[0,390,787,1254],row=Math.floor(r.tile/3);ctx.drawImage(rooms,(r.tile%3)*418,ys[row],418,ys[row+1]-ys[row],r.x,r.y,r.w,r.h);}ctx.fillStyle=r.color+'0c';ctx.fillRect(r.x,r.y,r.w,r.h);ctx.restore();}
 ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();for(const c of map.corridors)ctx.rect(c.x,c.y,c.w,c.h);ctx.fillStyle='#233947';ctx.fill();ctx.clip();ctx.strokeStyle='#69849425';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();for(let x=0;x<map.width;x+=48){ctx.moveTo(x,0);ctx.lineTo(x,map.height);}for(let y=0;y<map.height;y+=48){ctx.moveTo(0,y);ctx.lineTo(map.width,y);}ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle='#9ac6d553';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.setLineDash([12,18]);for(const c of map.corridors)if(Math.max(c.w,c.h)>180){ctx.beginPath();if(c.w>c.h){ctx.moveTo(c.x+20,c.y+c.h/2);ctx.lineTo(c.x+c.w-20,c.y+c.h/2);}else{ctx.moveTo(c.x+c.w/2,c.y+20);ctx.lineTo(c.x+c.w/2,c.y+c.h-20);}ctx.stroke();}ctx.restore();
 // Door thresholds use the same corridor intersections as collision geometry.
 for(const r of map.locations)for(const side of [0,1,2,3]){const vertical=side<2,edge=vertical?r.x+(side?r.w:0):r.y+(side===3?r.h:0),low=vertical?r.y:r.x,high=low+(vertical?r.h:r.w);const openings=merge(map.corridors.filter(c=>vertical?c.x<edge&&c.x+c.w>edge:c.y<edge&&c.y+c.h>edge).map(c=>[Math.max(low,vertical?c.y:c.x),Math.min(high,vertical?c.y+c.h:c.x+c.w)]).filter(([a,b])=>b>a));for(const [a,b] of openings){ctx.fillStyle='#293f4c';if(vertical)ctx.fillRect(edge-26,a,52,b-a);else ctx.fillRect(a,edge-26,b-a,52);ctx.fillStyle=r.color;if(vertical){ctx.fillRect(edge-29,a,58,5);ctx.fillRect(edge-29,b-5,58,5);}else{ctx.fillRect(a,edge-29,5,58);ctx.fillRect(b-5,edge-29,5,58);}ctx.strokeStyle='#aecbd34a';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();if(vertical){ctx.moveTo(edge,a+10);ctx.lineTo(edge,b-10);}else{ctx.moveTo(a+10,edge);ctx.lineTo(b-10,edge);}ctx.stroke();}}
 ctx.beginPath();for(const e of edges){ctx.moveTo(e.x1,e.y1);ctx.lineTo(e.x2,e.y2);}ctx.strokeStyle='#0d1c29';ctx.lineWidth=12;ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle='#637f8b';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.stroke();
}
export function drawRoomLabel(ctx:CanvasRenderingContext2D,r:Location,scale:number,overview=false,count?:number){const size=overview?Math.max(22,12/scale):18;ctx.font=`bold ${size}px Arial,"Malgun Gothic",sans-serif`;const text=r.name+(count===undefined?'':` · ${count}`),w=ctx.measureText(text).width+36,h=size+20,x=r.x+r.w/2-w/2,y=r.y+14;ctx.fillStyle='#07141eed';ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(x,y,w,h,7);ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle=r.color;ctx.fillRect(x,y+7,4,h-14);ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(text,r.x+r.w/2,y+size+4);}
let facilityBuffer:{map:FacilityMap;canvas:HTMLCanvasElement;painted:boolean}|null=null;
// All live camera panels share the same background buffer rather than allocating one per feed.
export function facilityLayer(map:FacilityMap,rooms?:HTMLImageElement){if(!facilityBuffer||facilityBuffer.map!==map){const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=map.width;canvas.height=map.height;drawFacility(canvas.getContext('2d')!,map,null);facilityBuffer={map,canvas,painted:false};}if(rooms?.complete&&rooms.naturalWidth&&!facilityBuffer.painted){drawFacility(facilityBuffer.canvas.getContext('2d')!,map,rooms);facilityBuffer.painted=true;}return facilityBuffer.canvas;}

// Nereid uses open-water research pavilions and timber bridges, never the Aurora room atlas.
function drawMarineFacility(ctx:CanvasRenderingContext2D,map:FacilityMap){
 const sea=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,map.width,map.height);sea.addColorStop(0,'#073b51');sea.addColorStop(.6,'#092b43');sea.addColorStop(1,'#061c33');ctx.fillStyle=sea;ctx.fillRect(0,0,map.width,map.height);
 ctx.strokeStyle='#64d5dc15';ctx.lineWidth=2;for(let y=30;y<map.height;y+=72){ctx.beginPath();for(let x=0;x<map.width;x+=12){const yy=y+Math.sin(x/120+y)*9;x?ctx.lineTo(x,yy):ctx.moveTo(x,yy);}ctx.stroke();}
 const floors=[...map.locations.flatMap(roomFloors),...map.corridors],edges=floorEdges(floors);
 ctx.beginPath();for(const e of edges){ctx.moveTo(e.x1,e.y1+12);ctx.lineTo(e.x2,e.y2+12);}ctx.lineWidth=28;ctx.strokeStyle='#001623a0';ctx.stroke();
 ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();for(const c of map.corridors)ctx.rect(c.x,c.y,c.w,c.h);ctx.fillStyle='#766b55';ctx.fill();ctx.clip();ctx.strokeStyle='#aa997245';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();for(let y=0;y<map.height;y+=20){ctx.moveTo(0,y);ctx.lineTo(map.width,y);}for(let x=0;x<map.width;x+=80){ctx.moveTo(x,0);ctx.lineTo(x,map.height);}ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
 for(const [i,r] of map.locations.entries()){
  ctx.fillStyle=['#284e5b','#304963','#3c465f','#42564c'][i%4];ctx.fillRect(r.x,r.y,r.w,r.h);
  ctx.strokeStyle='#b9eee01c';ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.beginPath();for(let x=r.x+24;x<r.x+r.w;x+=48){ctx.moveTo(x,r.y);ctx.lineTo(x,r.y+r.h);}for(let y=r.y+24;y<r.y+r.h;y+=48){ctx.moveTo(r.x,y);ctx.lineTo(r.x+r.w,y);}ctx.stroke();
  ctx.strokeStyle=r.color+'65';ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeRect(r.x+18,r.y+18,r.w-36,r.h-36);
  ctx.fillStyle=r.color+'12';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(r.x+r.w/2,r.y+r.h/2,Math.min(r.w,r.h)*.3,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle=r.color+'55';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(r.x+r.w/2,r.y+r.h/2,44,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.moveTo(r.x+r.w/2-60,r.y+r.h/2);ctx.lineTo(r.x+r.w/2+60,r.y+r.h/2);ctx.moveTo(r.x+r.w/2,r.y+r.h/2-60);ctx.lineTo(r.x+r.w/2,r.y+r.h/2+60);ctx.stroke();
  // Flush wall displays decorate the pavilion without implying solid obstacles.
  for(let k=0;k<3;k++){const x=r.x+r.w-104,y=r.y+72+k*55;ctx.fillStyle='#102f40';ctx.fillRect(x,y,76,38);ctx.fillStyle=r.color+'90';ctx.fillRect(x+7,y+7,4,24);ctx.fillStyle='#b3e6df55';ctx.fillRect(x+20,y+9,44,3);ctx.fillRect(x+20,y+19,28,3);}
  ctx.font='bold 14px monospace';ctx.fillStyle='#c2e7df66';ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.fillText('NEREID / '+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'),r.x+30,r.y+r.h-26);
 }
 ctx.beginPath();for(const e of edges){ctx.moveTo(e.x1,e.y1);ctx.lineTo(e.x2,e.y2);}ctx.lineWidth=12;ctx.strokeStyle='#153242';ctx.stroke();ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeStyle='#87c9c4';ctx.stroke();
}

export const population=(c:MapContext)=>typeof c==='number'?c:c.count;
export const mapIdentity=(c:MapContext)=>typeof c==='number'?'aurora':c.mapId==='nereid'?'nereid':'aurora';

export type Rect={x:number;y:number;w:number;h:number};

 ['reactor','반응로','REACTOR','#eb8b79',4,0,3],['navigation','항법실','NAVIGATION','#83b1ff',4,1,1],['oxygen','산소실','OXYGEN','#76ddba',4,2,8],['hangar','격납고','HANGAR','#d1b78c',4,3,6],

const roomBounds:Record<string,[number,number,number,number]>={
 lab:[210,160,490,380],control:[1040,140,500,330],medical:[1790,130,520,380],
 power:[150,850,460,360],atrium:[900,690,620,500],archive:[1920,830,520,360],

 cooling:[160,2130,470,320],server:[880,2230,490,300],loading:[1770,2280,580,280],

export const LOCATIONS:Location[]=layouts.map(([id,name,en,color,,,tile])=>{const [x,y,w,h]=roomBounds[id];return{id,name,en,color,tile,x,y,w,h};});
const insetRooms=new Set(['lab','control','medical','storage','security','observatory','reactor','oxygen']);
export function roomFloors(r:Location):Rect[]{return !r.science&&insetRooms.has(r.id)?[{x:r.x,y:r.y,w:r.w*.72,h:r.h},{x:r.x+r.w*.72,y:r.y+r.h*.25,w:r.w*.28,h:r.h*.75}]:[{x:r.x,y:r.y,w:r.w,h:r.h}];}
export function roomOutline(r:Location){return !r.science&&insetRooms.has(r.id)?`M${r.x},${r.y}h${r.w*.72}v${r.h*.25}h${r.w*.28}v${r.h*.75}h${-r.w}Z`:`M${r.x},${r.y}h${r.w}v${r.h}h${-r.w}Z`;}
const titles:Record<Puzzle,string>={...SCIENCE_TITLES,wires:'배선 연결',code:'접근 코드 입력',sequence:'표본 순서 분류',pattern:'기억 회로 복구',calibrate:'주파수 보정',swipe:'출입 카드 스캔',asteroids:'우주 잔해 제거',upload:'데이터 전송',fuel:'연료 주입',filter:'필터 이물질 제거',align:'항로 정렬',switches:'차단기 복구'};

const mapCache=new Map<string,ReturnType<typeof buildMap>>();
export function mapFor(context:MapContext){const count=population(context),key=mapIdentity(context)+':'+count;let map=mapCache.get(key);if(!map){map=mapIdentity(context)==='nereid'?buildScienceMap(count):buildMap(count);mapCache.set(key,map);}return map;}
function buildMap(count:number){
 const size=count<=10?12:count<=20?16:20,locations=LOCATIONS.slice(0,size),corridors:Rect[]=[];
 const route=(points:[number,number][],width=112)=>{for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++){const [ax,ay]=points[i-1],[bx,by]=points[i];if(ax!==bx&&ay!==by)throw Error('Corridors must be orthogonal');corridors.push({x:Math.min(ax,bx)-width/2,y:Math.min(ay,by)-width/2,w:Math.abs(bx-ax)+width,h:Math.abs(by-ay)+width});}};
 // Three interlocking circulation loops, narrow alternate passages and secluded mission rooms.
 route([[680,340],[840,340],[840,260],[1060,260]]);
 route([[1520,300],[1650,300],[1650,395],[1810,395]]);

 route([[2190,1170],[2190,1360],[2390,1360],[2390,1550]]);
 route([[1420,1170],[1420,1480],[1620,1480],[1620,1750]],104);
 route([[1240,1770],[1330,1770],[1330,1990],[1440,1990]]);
 route([[1790,1910],[1970,1910],[1970,1740],[2120,1740]]);
 route([[850,1950],[850,2070],[410,2070],[410,2150]],104);
 route([[1090,1950],[1090,2120],[1140,2120],[1140,2250]]);
 route([[1350,2380],[1540,2380],[1540,2490],[1790,2490]]);
 route([[1640,2130],[1640,2220],[2010,2220],[2010,2300]],104);
 route([[610,2320],[750,2320],[750,2410],[900,2410]]);
 route([[2500,1890],[2500,2170],[2310,2170],[2310,2300]]);
 // Hallway junctions offer bypasses without crossing a room.
 route([[840,340],[840,575],[1080,575]],100);
 route([[745,1110],[745,1320],[1180,1320]],100);
 route([[2190,1360],[1740,1360],[1740,1480],[1420,1480]],100);
 route([[1970,1740],[1970,2220]],100);
 if(size>=16){
  route([[2290,370],[2540,370],[2540,250],[2820,250]]);
  route([[3050,450],[3050,590],[2910,590],[2910,770]],104);
  route([[2420,1030],[2610,1030],[2610,900],[2800,900]]);
  route([[3120,1130],[3120,1300],[2980,1300],[2980,1470]]);
  route([[2620,1750],[2730,1750],[2730,1650],[2860,1650]],104);
  route([[3160,1820],[3160,2000],[2950,2000],[2950,2160]]);
  route([[2330,2450],[2560,2450],[2560,2330],[2810,2330]]);
 }
 if(size>=16)route([[3120,1300],[3420,1300],[3420,1010],[3230,1010]],100);
 if(size===20){
  route([[3330,330],[3550,330],[3550,250],[4000,250]]);
  route([[4210,490],[4210,660],[4090,660],[4090,840]],104);
  route([[3230,1010],[3500,1010],[3500,900],[3900,900]]);
  route([[4280,1160],[4280,1370],[4150,1370],[4150,1550]]);
  route([[3320,1720],[3600,1720],[3600,1620],[3990,1620]],104);
  route([[4250,1880],[4250,2080],[4070,2080],[4070,2270]]);
  route([[3250,2380],[3470,2380],[3470,2470],[3830,2470]]);
 }
 return{id:'aurora' as 'aurora'|'nereid',width:size===20?4650:size===16?3490:2750,height:2760,locations,corridors,floors:locations.flatMap(roomFloors),stations:STATIONS.filter(s=>locations.some(l=>l.id===s.room)),tasksPerPlayer:count<=10?6:count<=16?8:count<=22?10:12,label:size===20?'오로라 기지 · 심층 구역':size===16?'오로라 기지 · 동부 확장':'오로라 기지 · 중앙 시설'};
}
const maps=[mapFor(10),mapFor(20),mapFor(29)];
export const CORRIDORS=maps[1].corridors;
export function locationAt(p:Point,count:MapContext=16){return mapFor(count).locations.find(r=>p.x>=r.x&&p.x<=r.x+r.w&&p.y>=r.y&&p.y<=r.y+r.h)?.name||'연결 통로';}
const FLOOR_CELL=128;
const floorIndexes=new Map<string,Map<string,Rect[]>>();
export function floor(p:Point,count:MapContext=16){const key=mapIdentity(count)+':'+population(count);let index=floorIndexes.get(key);if(!index){index=new Map();const m=mapFor(count);for(const r of [...m.floors,...m.corridors])for(let y=Math.floor(r.y/FLOOR_CELL);y<=Math.floor((r.y+r.h)/FLOOR_CELL);y++)for(let x=Math.floor(r.x/FLOOR_CELL);x<=Math.floor((r.x+r.w)/FLOOR_CELL);x++){const k=x+','+y,cell=index.get(k)||[];cell.push(r);index.set(k,cell);}floorIndexes.set(key,index);}return !!index.get(Math.floor(p.x/FLOOR_CELL)+','+Math.floor(p.y/FLOOR_CELL))?.some(r=>p.x>=r.x&&p.x<=r.x+r.w&&p.y>=r.y&&p.y<=r.y+r.h);}
export function walkable(p:Point,count:MapContext=16){return [{x:p.x-12,y:p.y},{x:p.x+12,y:p.y},{x:p.x,y:p.y-12},{x:p.x,y:p.y+12}].every(q=>floor(q,count));}
export function distance(a:Point,b:Point){return Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y);}
export function sight(a:Point,b:Point,count:MapContext=16){const n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(distance(a,b)/12));for(let i=1;i<n;i++)if(!floor({x:a.x+(b.x-a.x)*i/n,y:a.y+(b.y-a.y)*i/n},count))return false;return true;}
export function moveTo(a:Point,b:Point,budget:number,count:MapContext=16){const d=distance(a,b);if(!d||!Number.isFinite(d))return a;const scale=Math.min(1,budget/d),dx=(b.x-a.x)*scale,dy=(b.y-a.y)*scale,n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(dx,dy)/6));let p={...a};for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x={x:p.x+dx/n,y:p.y};if(walkable(x,count))p=x;const y={x:p.x,y:p.y+dy/n};if(walkable(y,count))p=y;}return{x:Math.round(p.x*10)/10,y:Math.round(p.y*10)/10};}
export function spawn(i:number,count:MapContext=16):Point{return{x:(mapIdentity(count)==='nereid'?1450:1000)+(i%6)*36,y:(mapIdentity(count)==='nereid'?1390:900)+Math.floor(i/6)*38};}

// Separate vent networks cross distant wings; adjacency is explicit, never array-index based.
const networks=[{id:'A',name:'과학 · 의료망',color:'#7cdbdf',rooms:['lab','medical','garden','observatory','oxygen']},{id:'B',name:'동력 · 화물망',color:'#edaf79',rooms:['power','storage','cooling','engine','reactor','hangar']},{id:'C',name:'관제 · 정보망',color:'#ba9cf4',rooms:['control','archive','comms','server','security','navigation']},{id:'D',name:'중앙 · 보급망',color:'#d9cc8a',rooms:['atrium','loading','lounge']}];
function makeVents(count:MapContext){const rooms=mapFor(count).locations;return networks.flatMap(n=>{const members=n.rooms.filter(id=>rooms.some(l=>l.id===id));return members.map((id,i)=>{const l=rooms.find(l=>l.id===id)!;return{id,name:l.name,x:l.x+82,y:l.y+l.h-64,network:n.id,networkName:n.name,color:n.color,links:[...new Set([members[(i+1)%members.length],members[(i+members.length-1)%members.length]])].filter(x=>x!==id)};});});}

const ventCache=new Map<string,ReturnType<typeof makeVents>>();
export function ventsFor(count:MapContext){const key=mapIdentity(count)+':'+population(count);let vents=ventCache.get(key);if(!vents){vents=makeVents(count);ventCache.set(key,vents);}return vents;}
export function emergencyFor(c:MapContext){return mapIdentity(c)==='nereid'?{x:1600,y:1470}:EMERGENCY;}
export function repairFor(c:MapContext){if(mapIdentity(c)==='aurora')return REPAIR;const r=mapFor(c).locations.find(r=>r.id==='power')!;return{x:r.x+100,y:r.y+r.h*.7};}
function buildScienceMap(count:number):ReturnType<typeof buildMap>{

 const kinds=SCIENCE_KINDS,stations=locations.flatMap((r,i)=>[0,1,2].map(j=>{const kind=kinds[(i*3+j)%kinds.length];return{id:`nereid-${r.id}-${kind}`,name:SCIENCE_TITLES[kind],room:r.id,kind,x:r.x+r.w*[.23,.75,.72][j],y:r.y+r.h*[.37,.38,.78][j]};}));

}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/motion.ts

import type {MapContext} from './map.ts';
import {moveTo,WORLD,type Point} from './map.ts';
export type MoveInput={n:number;dx:number;dy:number;ms:number};
export type MotionPose=Point&{ack:number;clock:number};
export function stepInput(p:Point,input:MoveInput,count:MapContext):Point {const magnitude=Math.max(1,Math.hypot(input.dx,input.dy)),seconds=input.ms/1000;return moveTo(p,{x:p.x+input.dx/magnitude*WORLD.speed*seconds,y:p.y+input.dy/magnitude*WORLD.speed*seconds},WORLD.speed*seconds,count);}
export function replayInputs(p:Point,inputs:MoveInput[],count:MapContext):Point {for(const input of inputs)p=stepInput(p,input,count);return p;}
// The acknowledged input number, not the age of an HTTP response, determines what to replay.
export function reconcileMotion(server:Point&{ack?:number},pending:MoveInput[],count:MapContext){const remaining=pending.filter(i=>i.n>(server.ack||0));return {pending:remaining,position:replayInputs(server,remaining,count)};}
export function consumeInputs(old:MotionPose,raw:unknown,now:number,count:MapContext):MotionPose {let pose={...old,clock:Math.max(old.clock,now-3000)};if(!Array.isArray(raw)||raw.length>200)return pose;for(const value of raw){const i=value as MoveInput;if(!i||!Number.isSafeInteger(i.n)||!Number.isFinite(i.ms)||!Number.isFinite(i.dx)||!Number.isFinite(i.dy)||i.ms<=0||i.ms>50||Math.abs(i.dx)>1||Math.abs(i.dy)>1)break;if(i.n<=pose.ack)continue;if(i.n!==pose.ack+1||pose.clock+i.ms>now+100)break;pose={...stepInput(pose,i,count),ack:i.n,clock:pose.clock+i.ms};}return pose;}

import type {MapContext} from './map.ts';
import type {Room,Player} from './game';
import {isCrew,isNeutral} from './field-roles.ts';
import {emergencyFor,mapFor,distance,sight,type Point} from './map.ts';

export type NeutralProgress={target?:string;marks:string[];shields:number};
export function neutralPoint(role:string,count:MapContext):Point{
 const id=role==='escapeartist'?'loading':role==='coroner'?'archive':'atrium';
 if(id==='atrium')return emergencyFor(count);
 const room=mapFor(count).locations.find(r=>r.id===id)!;return{x:room.x+room.w/2,y:room.y+room.h/2};
}
export function refreshNeutralTargets(r:Room){
 const f=r.field!;f.neutralProgress||={};
 for(const p of r.players.filter(p=>p.role==='executioner'&&p.alive)){
  const state=f.neutralProgress[p.id]||={marks:[],shields:0};
  if(r.players.some(t=>t.id===state.target&&t.alive))continue;
  const others=r.players.filter(t=>t.alive&&t.id!==p.id&&t.role!=='jester');
  const crew=others.filter(t=>isCrew(t.role)),pool=crew.length?crew:others;
  state.target=pool.length?pool[crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0]%pool.length].id:undefined;
  if(state.target)p.intel.push({day:r.day,text:`처형 목표: ${r.players.find(t=>t.id===state.target)!.name}. 일반 투표로 추방시켜야 합니다.`});
 }
}
export function executionWinner(r:Room,ejected:string){return r.players.find(p=>p.alive&&p.role==='executioner'&&r.field?.neutralProgress?.[p.id]?.target===ejected);}
export function survivalWinner(r:Room){return r.players.filter(p=>p.alive).length<=4?r.players.find(p=>p.alive&&p.role==='survivor'&&(r.field?.tasks[p.id]||[]).filter(t=>t.done).length>=3):undefined;}
export function neutralSummary(r:Room,p?:Player){
 if(!p||!isNeutral(p.role))return null;
 const f=r.field!,state=f.neutralProgress?.[p.id],marks=state?.marks||[],tasks=f.tasks[p.id]||[],done=tasks.filter(t=>t.done).length;
 const target=r.players.find(t=>t.id===state?.target),required=r.config.count<=15?2:r.config.count<=22?3:4,collected=f.collected?.[p.id]||0;
 const allDoused=r.players.filter(t=>t.alive&&t.id!==p.id).every(t=>marks.includes(t.id));
 const objectives:Record<string,string>={
  jester:'일반 투표로 추방되면 나 혼자 승리',scavenger:`시체 수집 ${collected}/${required} · 시체 가까이에서 수집`,
  lonewolf:'크루와 임포스터를 모두 탈락시키고 최후의 1인으로 생존',
  executioner:target?`처형 목표: ${target.name} · 내가 살아 있는 동안 일반 투표로 추방시키기`:'새 처형 목표를 기다리고 있습니다.',
  survivor:`개인 미션 ${Math.min(done,3)}/3 · 생존자가 4명 이하일 때 살아남기 · 보호막 ${Math.max(0,2-(state?.shields||0))}회 남음`,
  arsonist:`기름을 묻힌 대상 ${marks.length}명 · ${allDoused?'점화 가능':'나를 제외한 모든 생존자에게 기름을 묻히세요'}`,
  thief:`전리품 ${marks.length}/4 · 모두 모으면 중앙 홀 회의 버튼 근처에서 반출`,
  escapeartist:`탈출 준비 ${done}/${tasks.length} · 전부 완료하고 하역장 중앙에서 탈출`,
  coroner:`검시 증거 ${marks.length}/3 · 모두 모으면 기록실 중앙에서 증거 제출`,
 };
 return{role:p.role,collected,required,objective:objectives[p.role!]||'',marks:[...marks],target:target?{id:target.id,name:target.name}:null,ready:p.role==='arsonist'?allDoused:p.role==='thief'?marks.length>=4:p.role==='coroner'?marks.length>=3:p.role==='escapeartist'?tasks.length>0&&done===tasks.length:false,shields:Math.max(0,2-(state?.shields||0))};
}
export function neutralAction(r:Room,p:Player,action:string,target:Player|undefined,tp:Point|null,pos:Point,now:number):{won?:string;effect:'scan'|'shield'|'task';point:Point}{
 const f=r.field!;if(!p.alive)throw Error('살아 있을 때만 중립 능력을 사용할 수 있습니다.');
 const near=(q:Point,range=110)=>distance(pos,q)<=range&&sight(pos,q,r.config);
 f.neutralProgress||={};const state=f.neutralProgress[p.id]||={marks:[],shields:0};
 f.skillCooldowns||={};
 if(action==='skill'&&p.role==='survivor'){
  if(state.shields>=2)throw Error('자기 보호막을 모두 사용했습니다.');
  if((f.skillCooldowns[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('보호막 재사용 시간이 남았습니다.');
  state.shields++;f.protects[p.id]=now+8000;f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+45000;return{effect:'shield',point:pos};
 }
 if(action==='skill'&&['arsonist','thief'].includes(p.role||'')){
  if((f.skillCooldowns[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('특수 능력 재사용 시간이 남았습니다.');
  if(!target?.alive||target.id===p.id||!tp||!near(tp)||(f.invisible?.[target.id]||0)>now)throw Error('다른 생존자 가까이 이동하세요.');
  if(state.marks.includes(target.id))throw Error('이미 능력을 사용한 대상입니다.');
  state.marks.push(target.id);f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+(p.role==='thief'?25000:20000);
  p.intel.push({day:r.day,text:`${target.name}: ${p.role==='thief'?'전리품 확보':'기름 묻히기'} 완료.`});return{effect:'scan',point:tp};
 }
 if(action==='ignite'&&p.role==='arsonist'){
  if(!r.players.filter(t=>t.alive&&t.id!==p.id).every(t=>state.marks.includes(t.id)))throw Error('모든 생존자에게 기름을 먼저 묻히세요.');
  return{effect:'task',point:pos,won:'모든 생존자에게 기름을 묻힌 뒤 점화하여 독립 승리했습니다.'};
 }
 if(action==='examine'&&p.role==='coroner'){
  if((f.skillCooldowns[p.id]||0)>now)throw Error('검시 재사용 시간이 남았습니다.');
  const body=f.bodies.find(b=>b.id===target?.id);if(!body||(body.dissolveAt&&body.dissolveAt<=now)||!near(body,105))throw Error('검시할 시체 가까이 이동하세요.');
  if(state.marks.includes(body.id))throw Error('이미 검시한 시체입니다.');
  state.marks.push(body.id);f.skillCooldowns[p.id]=now+15000;return{effect:'scan',point:body};
 }
 const finishes:Record<string,{role:string;ready:boolean;reason:string}>={
  cashout:{role:'thief',ready:state.marks.length>=4,reason:'전리품을 중앙 홀에서 반출하여 독립 승리했습니다.'},
  escape:{role:'escapeartist',ready:(f.tasks[p.id]||[]).length>0&&(f.tasks[p.id]||[]).every(t=>t.done),reason:'미션을 마치고 하역장에서 탈출하여 독립 승리했습니다.'},
  submitEvidence:{role:'coroner',ready:state.marks.length>=3,reason:'검시 증거를 기록실에 제출하여 독립 승리했습니다.'},
 };
 const finish=finishes[action];if(finish&&p.role===finish.role){const point=neutralPoint(p.role,r.config);if(!finish.ready)throw Error('개인 승리 목표를 먼저 완료하세요.');if(!near(point,105))throw Error('목표에 표시된 제출·탈출 장소 중앙으로 이동하세요.');return{effect:'task',point,won:finish.reason};}
 throw Error('이 직업이 사용할 수 없는 중립 능력입니다.');
}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/peer-presence.ts
======================================================================
import type {MapContext} from './map.ts';
import {distance,sight,type Point} from './map.ts';
export type VisiblePeer=Point&{id:string;alive:boolean;vented?:boolean;invisible?:boolean};
export type Presence<T extends VisiblePeer>={person:T;seen:number;missingAt?:number};
// Only hold an already observed pose briefly. Never make stale actors valid attack targets.
export function updatePresence<T extends VisiblePeer>(cache:Record<string,Presence<T>>,people:T[],now:number,hidden:Iterable<string>=[]){
 const ids=new Set(people.map(p=>p.id)),hide=new Set(hidden);
 for(const [id,entry] of Object.entries(cache)){
  if(hide.has(id)){delete cache[id];continue;}
  if(!ids.has(id)){entry.missingAt??=now;if(now-entry.missingAt>1200)delete cache[id];}
 }
 for(const person of people)cache[person.id]={person,seen:now};
}
export function retainedPeers<T extends VisiblePeer>(cache:Record<string,Presence<T>>,now:number,own:Point,radius:number,count:MapContext):T[]{
 return Object.values(cache).filter(e=>e.missingAt!==undefined&&now-e.missingAt<220&&e.person.alive&&!e.person.invisible&&!e.person.vented&&distance(own,e.person)<=radius&&sight(own,e.person,count)).map(e=>e.person);
}
export function presenceOpacity<T extends VisiblePeer>(entry:Presence<T>|undefined,now:number){return entry?.missingAt===undefined?1:Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(220-(now-entry.missingAt))/100));}

import {scienceProblem,type ScienceKind} from './science.ts';
import type { Puzzle } from './map';
const random=(max:number)=>crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0]%max;
export function puzzleSolution(kind:Puzzle):number[]{
 if(kind.startsWith('science_'))return [random(4)];
 if(kind==='wires')return [0,1,2,3].sort(()=>random(3)-1);
 if(kind==='code')return Array.from({length:4},()=>random(10));
 if(kind==='sequence')return [1,2,3,4,5,6];
 if(kind==='pattern'){const pool=[0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7,8];for(let i=8;i>0;i--){const j=random(i+1);[pool[i],pool[j]]=[pool[j],pool[i]];}return pool.slice(0,4).sort((a,b)=>a-b);}
 if(kind==='calibrate')return [50];
 if(kind==='align')return Array.from({length:3},()=>20+random(61));
 if(kind==='switches')return Array.from({length:6},()=>random(2));
 if(kind==='asteroids')return [0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7];
 if(kind==='filter')return [0,1,2,3,4,5];
 if(kind==='swipe')return [900];
 return [100];
}
export function puzzleDuration(kind:Puzzle){return kind==='upload'?6500:kind==='fuel'?4500:1800;}
export function puzzleCorrect(kind:Puzzle,solution:number[],answer:number[]){
 if(!Array.isArray(answer)||answer.length>12||answer.some(n=>!Number.isFinite(n)))return false;
 if(kind.startsWith('science_'))return JSON.stringify(answer)===JSON.stringify(scienceProblem(kind as ScienceKind,solution[0]).answer);
 if(kind==='calibrate')return answer.length===3&&answer.every(n=>n>=43&&n<=57);
 if(kind==='swipe')return answer.length===1&&answer[0]>=450&&answer[0]<=1500;
 if(kind==='align')return answer.length===3&&answer.every((v,i)=>Math.abs(v-solution[i])<=3);
 return JSON.stringify(answer)===JSON.stringify(solution);
}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/remote-motion.ts
======================================================================
import type {MapContext} from './map.ts';
import {distance,moveTo,WORLD,type Point} from './map.ts';
export type RemoteSample=Point&{at:number;warp:number;alive:boolean};
// samples: server-stamped positions (at = server/motion time).
// lags: recent (local receive time - sample.at). The receive clock and the server clock
// differ by an unknown constant; it cancels out because the render time is derived from
// the same differences, so no clock synchronisation is needed.
export type RemoteTrack={samples:RemoteSample[];lags:number[];gaps:number[];delay:number;target:number;received:number;rendered?:Point&{at:number};painted?:number;cursor?:number;starved?:number;
 /** @deprecated kept for older callers/tests */ interval:number;offset:number;jitter:number};
const KEEP_MS=3000,LAG_WINDOW=48,MIN_MARGIN=24,MAX_DELAY=900;
function quantile(values:number[],q:number){if(!values.length)return 0;const s=[...values].sort((a,b)=>a-b);return s[Math.min(s.length-1,Math.floor(q*s.length))];}
function fresh(s:RemoteSample,received:number):RemoteTrack{return{samples:[s],lags:[received-s.at],gaps:[],delay:0,target:0,received,interval:100,offset:s.at-received,jitter:0};}
// Packet arrivals never directly change the displayed position or reset its clock.
export function pushRemote(old:RemoteTrack|undefined,s:RemoteSample,received=s.at):RemoteTrack{
 const last=old?.samples.at(-1);
 if(!old||!last||last.warp!==s.warp||last.alive!==s.alive)return fresh(s,received);
 if(s.at<=last.at)return old;
 const gap=s.at-last.at,travel=distance(last,s)/WORLD.speed*1000;
 // A sample after a pause must not smear the new movement across the whole pause.
 // Only when the actor was known to be at rest (identical last positions, or a single
 // sample); sparse samples of a moving actor (slow stick, wall sliding) are left alone.
 const prev=old.samples.at(-2),resting=!prev||distance(prev,last)<.5;
 const paused=resting&&gap>travel+160;
 if(paused){const start=s.at-Math.max(40,Math.min(gap,travel*1.05));if(start>last.at)old.samples.push({...last,at:start});}
 old.samples.push(s);
 const keepFrom=s.at-KEEP_MS;while(old.samples.length>3&&old.samples[1].at<keepFrom)old.samples.shift();
 old.lags.push(received-s.at);if(old.lags.length>LAG_WINDOW)old.lags.shift();
 // Sample spacing while moving decides how far ahead of the newest sample we must wait.
 // received - previous sample time = how stale our newest data was just before this
 // arrival; its tail is exactly how far behind the newest sample rendering must stay.
 if(!paused){old.gaps.push(Math.min(received-last.at,old.lags[old.lags.length-1]+1000));if(old.gaps.length>LAG_WINDOW)old.gaps.shift();}
 old.received=received;old.interval=Math.max(0,gap);old.offset=Math.max(old.offset,s.at-received);
 return old;
}
function pointAt(track:RemoteTrack,t:number,count:MapContext):Point&{starved:number}{
 const a=track.samples;if(t<=a[0].at)return{...a[0],starved:0};
 for(let i=1;i<a.length;i++)if(t<=a[i].at){const p=a[i-1],q=a[i],u=(t-p.at)/(q.at-p.at);return{...moveTo(p,{x:p.x+(q.x-p.x)*u,y:p.y+(q.y-p.y)*u},distance(p,q)*u,count),starved:0};}
 const last=a.at(-1)!,over=t-last.at;if(a.length<2||!last.alive)return{...last,starved:over};
 // Short, bounded extrapolation (150 ms) while the next sample is late; then hold.
 const prev=a.at(-2)!,ms=last.at-prev.at,extra=Math.min(150,over);
 if(ms<=0||distance(prev,last)<.01)return{...last,starved:over};
 const v=Math.min(WORLD.speed,distance(prev,last)/(ms/1000));
 return{...moveTo(last,{x:last.x+(last.x-prev.x)/distance(prev,last)*v*extra/1000,y:last.y+(last.y-prev.y)/distance(prev,last)*v*extra/1000},v*extra/1000,count),starved:over};
}
// Jitter buffer: render at (now - delay) where delay covers the late tail of recent
// arrivals plus one sample interval. Delay changes are rate-limited (playback 0.85x-1.25x)
// so jitter never makes an actor jump, freeze or run backwards.
export function sampleRemote(track:RemoteTrack,now:number,count:MapContext):Point&{at:number}{
 // lags include an unknown clock constant; only their spread above the fastest path matters.
 const base=Math.min(...track.lags),need=track.gaps.length>=3?quantile(track.gaps,.85)-base+12:quantile(track.lags,.95)-base+112;
 track.target=base+Math.max(MIN_MARGIN,Math.min(MAX_DELAY,need));
 if(track.painted===undefined){track.delay=track.target;track.painted=now;const t=Math.max(track.samples[0].at,now-track.delay);track.cursor=t;const p=pointAt(track,t,count);track.rendered={x:p.x,y:p.y,at:t};return track.rendered;}
 // Real elapsed time (bounded): at 7-15 fps a 50 ms cap made remote actors walk at half speed
 // and fall further and further behind (measured in headless Chromium at 7.5 fps).
 const dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(250,now-track.painted));track.painted=now;
 // Rendering further behind than needed across a stretch where the actor stood still (an
 // idle actor's first sample carries its old motion time, which inflates the delay until it
 // moves): skip that stretch at once. Nothing moves in it, so nothing visibly jumps; without
 // this the excess drained at 0.15x and observers saw the actor 1-2 s late after it started.
 if(track.delay>track.target){
  const cur=Math.max(track.cursor??-Infinity,now-track.delay),want=now-track.target,here=pointAt(track,cur,count),a=track.samples;
  let still=Infinity;for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++){if(a[i].at<=cur)continue;if(distance(a[i],here)>.5){still=i>0?Math.max(cur,a[i-1].at):cur;break;}}
  const to=Math.min(want,still);if(to>cur)track.delay=now-to;
 }
 const diff=track.target-track.delay;track.delay+=diff>0?Math.min(diff,dt*.25):Math.max(diff,-dt*.15);
 // Never move the render clock backwards.
 const t=Math.max(track.cursor??-Infinity,now-track.delay);track.cursor=t;
 const goal=pointAt(track,t,count);track.starved=goal.starved;
 const pos=moveTo(track.rendered!,goal,WORLD.speed*1.3*dt/1000,count);
 track.rendered={...pos,at:t};return track.rendered;
}


======================================================================
FILE: lib/role-draw.ts
======================================================================
// Randomize both participants and role slots, then maximize assignments that
// differ from the previous round. Counts are preserved, including repeated roles.
function shuffle<T>(items:T[]):T[]{
 for(let i=items.length-1;i>0;i--){const limit=2**32-2**32%(i+1);let n:number;do{n=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0];}while(n>=limit);const j=n%(i+1);[items[i],items[j]]=[items[j],items[i]];}
 return items;
}
export function drawRoles<T extends string>(deck:T[],previous:(T|null|undefined)[]):T[]{
 if(deck.length!==previous.length)throw Error('Role count must match players');
 const slots=shuffle([...deck]),order=shuffle(previous.map((_,i)=>i));
 const owner=slots.map(()=>-1),assigned=previous.map(()=>-1);
 const edges=previous.map(last=>shuffle(slots.map((_,i)=>i).filter(i=>slots[i]!==last)));
 function match(player:number,seen:Set<number>):boolean{
  for(const slot of edges[player]){if(seen.has(slot))continue;seen.add(slot);if(owner[slot]===-1||match(owner[slot],seen)){owner[slot]=player;assigned[player]=slot;return true;}}
  return false;
 }
 for(const player of order)match(player,new Set());
 const spare=shuffle(owner.map((p,i)=>p===-1?i:-1).filter(i=>i>=0));
 for(const player of order)if(assigned[player]===-1)assigned[player]=spare.pop()!;
 return assigned.map(i=>slots[i]);
}

 const enableAudio=()=>{try{audio||=new AudioContext();void audio.resume();}catch{}};
 const footstep=(id:string,p:Point,own:Point,yaw:number,time:number)=>{if(!audio||audio.state!=='running'||time-(stepTimes.get(id)||0)<340)return;stepTimes.set(id,time);const dist=distance(p,own);if(dist>220||!sight(p,own,config))return;const ctx=audio,o=ctx.createOscillator(),g=ctx.createGain(),pan=ctx.createStereoPanner();o.type='triangle';o.frequency.setValueAtTime(95,ctx.currentTime);o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(38,ctx.currentTime+.075);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.035*(1-dist/240),ctx.currentTime);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,ctx.currentTime+.09);pan.pan.value=Math.max(-1,Math.min(1,((p.x-own.x)*Math.cos(yaw)+(p.y-own.y)*Math.sin(yaw))/150));o.connect(g).connect(pan).connect(ctx.destination);o.start();o.stop(ctx.currentTime+.1);};

 function update(data:SceneFrame,look:Look,follow:string){const dt=Math.min(.05,(data.now-last)/1000||.016);last=data.now;latestData=data;const r=data.room,focus=r.host&&follow?data.people.find(p=>p.id===follow):null,own=focus||data.own;camera.position.set(own.x*U,1.43,own.y*U);camera.rotation.set(-look.pitch,-look.yaw,0);camera.updateMatrixWorld();
  const fog=scene.fog as T.Fog;fog.near=Math.max(1,data.radius*U*.65);fog.far=Math.max(2.5,data.radius*U*1.15);if(r.host){fog.near=18;fog.far=65;}
  if(data.sound&&data.moving&&!r.paused)footstep('own',own,own,look.yaw,data.now);step+=data.moving?dt*9:0;hand.visible=!r.host&&!!r.players.find(p=>p.id===r.me)?.alive;const ownAvatar=r.players.find(p=>p.id===r.me)?.avatar||0;dressArm(ownAvatar);
  const attack=data.effects.find(e=>e.actor===r.me&&['attack','blocked'].includes(e.kind)&&data.now-e.seenAt<620);const age=attack?(data.now-attack.seenAt)/620:0;heldKnife.visible=!!attack;hand.position.set(0,data.moving?Math.sin(step)*.012:0,attack?-Math.sin(age*Math.PI)*.42:0);hand.rotation.x=attack?Math.sin(age*Math.PI)*-.8:0;const reach=data.reach,amount=reach?reachAmount(data.now-reach.started):0;if(reach&&amount>0&&!attack){const t=reach.target,contact=camera.worldToLocal(new T.Vector3(t.point.x*U,t.height,t.point.y*U));hand.position.subVectors(contact,VIEW_HAND).multiplyScalar(amount);hand.rotation.x=0;}ownArm?.aim(amount>0&&!attack?armDirection.copy(hand.position).add(VIEW_WRIST).sub(VIEW_SHOULDER).normalize():undefined);redButton.position.y=1.01-(reach?.target.action==='emergency'?.065*amount:0);
  for(const [id,s] of stations){const task=r.field?.tasks.find(t=>t.station===id),near=distance(own,s.pos)<150;s.label.visible=!!task&&!task.done&&near&&sight(own,s.pos,config);s.screen.material=mat(task?.done?'#477267':task?'#6ce6bf':'#446572');}
  const visible=new Set<string>();for(const p of data.people){if(p.id===r.me&&!r.host||p.id===focus?.id||p.vented||p.invisible||!p.alive)continue;visible.add(p.id);let actor=puppets.get(p.id);const caption=p.speech&&p.speech.until>r.serverNow?p.name+' · '+p.speech.text:p.name;
   if(!actor||actor.avatar!==p.avatar){if(actor)removePuppet(actor);actor=puppet(p.avatar,caption);puppets.set(p.id,actor);}if(actor.caption!==caption){const old=actor.label;actor.root.remove(old);disposeLabel(old);actor.label=label(caption,'#e6f6ee',Math.min(3.6,.9+caption.length*.08),.28);actor.label.position.y=DOLL.labelY;actor.root.add(actor.label);actor.caption=caption;}
   actor.root.visible=true;const dx=p.x-actor.last.x,dy=p.y-actor.last.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(Number.isFinite(d)&&d>.04){actor.phase+=Math.min(d,20)/22;actor.root.rotation.y=Math.atan2(-dx,-dy);}if(data.sound&&d>.08&&Number.isFinite(d)&&!r.paused)footstep(p.id,p,own,look.yaw,data.now);actor.last={x:p.x,y:p.y};actor.root.position.set(p.x*U,0,p.y*U);const stride=Number.isFinite(d)&&d>.08?Math.sin(actor.phase*1.8)*.55:0;actor.legs[0].rotation.x=stride;actor.legs[1].rotation.x=-stride;actor.arms[0].rotation.x=-stride*.65;actor.arms[1].rotation.x=stride*.65;
   const strike=data.effects.find(e=>e.actor===p.id&&data.now-e.seenAt<620&&['attack','blocked'].includes(e.kind));actor.knife.visible=!!strike;if(strike){actor.arms[1].rotation.x=1.5+Math.sin((data.now-strike.seenAt)/620*Math.PI)*.5;actor.root.rotation.y=Math.atan2(strike.x-strike.tx,strike.y-strike.ty);}
   actor.label.visible=distance(own,p)<220&&sight(own,p,config);
  }
  for(const b of data.bodies){const id='body:'+b.id;visible.add(id);let actor=puppets.get(id);if(!actor){actor=puppet(b.avatar,'R · '+b.name);puppets.set(id,actor);}actor.root.visible=true;actor.root.position.set(b.x*U,.18,b.y*U);actor.root.rotation.z=Math.PI/2;actor.label.visible=false;}
  for(const [id,p] of puppets)if(!visible.has(id))p.root.visible=false;
  renderer.render(scene,camera);
 }
 function disposeLabel(l:T.Sprite){const material=l.material as T.SpriteMaterial;if(material.map){material.map.dispose();textures.delete(material.map);}material.dispose();}
 function removePuppet(p:Puppet){scene.remove(p.root);disposeLabel(p.label);p.dispose();}
 return{update,pickDevice,enableAudio,resize(w:number,h:number){renderer.setSize(w,h,false);camera.aspect=w/Math.max(1,h);camera.updateProjectionMatrix();},
  setQuality(scale:number){renderer.setPixelRatio(basePixelRatio*scale);},
  dispose(){puppets.forEach(p=>p.dispose());scene.traverse(o=>{if(o instanceof T.Mesh&&!o.geometry.userData.shared)o.geometry.dispose();if(o instanceof T.Sprite)o.material.dispose();});textures.forEach(t=>t.dispose());materials.forEach(m=>m.dispose());renderer.dispose();void audio?.close();},stats:()=>renderer.info.render};
}
