export const SCIENCE_KINDS=['science_circuit','science_pressure','science_buoyancy','science_density','science_separate','science_heat','science_light','science_plant','science_digest','science_state','science_mass','science_planets'] as const;
export type ScienceKind=typeof SCIENCE_KINDS[number];
export const SCIENCE_TITLES:Record<ScienceKind,string>={science_circuit:'비상 전등 회로 복구',science_pressure:'기체 압력 조절',science_buoyancy:'수중 탐사추 보정',science_density:'밀도 표본 정렬',science_separate:'해수 시료 분리',science_heat:'열 이동 경로 설정',science_light:'반사경 각도 조절',science_plant:'온실 광합성 복구',science_digest:'소화 효소 배관 연결',science_state:'흡열 냉각 장치 가동',science_mass:'반응 물질 질량 보정',science_planets:'행성 탐사 경로 설정'};
export type ScienceProblem={title:string;topic:string;instruction:string;hint:string;explanation:string;mode:'select'|'order'|'slider'|'circuit'|'match';options:string[];answer:number[];min?:number;max?:number;unit?:string;readings?:string[];left?:string[];diagram?:string};
export function scienceProblem(kind:ScienceKind,seed:number):ScienceProblem{
 const v=Math.abs(seed|0)%4,base={title:SCIENCE_TITLES[kind],options:[] as string[],answer:[] as number[],topic:'',instruction:'',hint:'',explanation:'',mode:'select' as ScienceProblem['mode']};
 switch(kind){
 case 'science_circuit':return{...base,topic:'전기와 자기',mode:'circuit',instruction:'전류가 전구를 지나도록 S1·S2를 연결하고, 전구를 우회하는 S3는 끊으세요.',hint:'전구 양쪽을 도선으로 직접 연결하면 전류가 도선을 따라 흘러 전구가 켜지지 않아요.',explanation:'S1·S2를 닫고 S3를 열면 전지와 전구가 하나의 닫힌 회로로 연결됩니다.',options:['S1 · 전원','S2 · 귀환','S3 · 우회'],answer:[1,1,0],diagram:'circuit'};
 case 'science_pressure':{const p=[1,2,3,4][v],volume=120,target=p*2;return{...base,topic:'기체의 특성 · 보일 법칙',mode:'slider',instruction:`온도와 기체의 양은 일정합니다. 압력을 ${target}기압으로 만들도록 부피를 조절하세요.`,readings:[`처음 압력 ${p}기압`,`처음 부피 ${volume}mL`,`목표 압력 ${target}기압`],hint:'온도와 기체의 양이 일정할 때 압력 × 부피는 일정해요.',explanation:'압력이 2배가 되면 부피는 절반인 60mL가 됩니다.',min:20,max:160,unit:'mL',answer:[60],diagram:'gas'};}
 case 'science_buoyancy':{const air=10+v*2,water=air-(2+v);return{...base,topic:'힘의 작용 · 부력',mode:'slider',instruction:'같은 추를 물속에 완전히 잠기게 했습니다. 추에 작용하는 부력을 설정하세요.',readings:[`공기 중 저울 ${air}N`,`물속 저울 ${water}N`],hint:'물속에서 줄어든 용수철저울의 값이 부력의 크기예요. 부력은 위쪽으로 작용해요.',explanation:`부력 = ${air} − ${water} = ${air-water}N. 중력 자체가 줄어든 것은 아닙니다.`,min:0,max:15,unit:'N',answer:[air-water],diagram:'buoyancy'};}
 case 'science_density':return{...base,topic:'물질의 성질 · 밀도',mode:'order',instruction:'시료를 밀도가 작은 것부터 큰 순서로 투입하세요.',options:[`A · ${80+v*8}g / ${10+v}cm³`,`B · ${20+v*2}g / ${10+v}cm³`,`C · ${50+v*5}g / ${10+v}cm³`],answer:[1,2,0],hint:'밀도 = 질량 ÷ 부피. 질량만 비교하지 마세요.',explanation:'B: 2g/cm³ → C: 5g/cm³ → A: 8g/cm³ 순서입니다.'};
 case 'science_separate':return{...base,topic:'물질의 성질 · 혼합물 분리',mode:'order',instruction:'소금과 모래가 섞인 시료에서 소금을 회수하세요. 필요한 작업 3개를 순서대로 선택하세요.',options:['거름종이로 모래 거르기','자석으로 분리하기','소량의 물에 소금 녹이기','거른 용액의 물 증발시키기','시료를 냉동시키기'],answer:[2,0,3],hint:'소금은 물에 녹지만 모래는 녹지 않아요. 여과해도 녹은 소금은 물과 함께 통과해요.',explanation:'용해 → 여과 → 증발 순서로 소금을 회수합니다. 실제 가열은 보호 장비와 교사 지도가 필요합니다.'};
 case 'science_heat':{const t=[20+v*5,80+v*5,50+v*5];return{...base,topic:'열 · 열의 이동',mode:'order',instruction:'열이 전달되는 방향에 맞춰 높은 온도에서 낮은 온도 순으로 배관을 연결하세요.',options:t.map((n,i)=>`${'ABC'[i]} 탱크 · ${n}℃`),answer:[1,2,0],hint:'접촉한 물체 사이에서 열은 온도가 높은 쪽에서 낮은 쪽으로 이동해요.',explanation:`${t[1]}℃ → ${t[2]}℃ → ${t[0]}℃ 방향입니다. 충분한 시간이 지나면 열평형에 도달합니다.`};}
 case 'science_light':{const a=[25,35,45,55][v];return{...base,topic:'빛과 파동 · 반사 법칙',mode:'slider',instruction:`법선과 입사 광선 사이의 각은 ${a}°입니다. 반사각을 맞춰 수신기를 조준하세요.`,readings:[`입사각 ${a}°`,'각도 기준: 거울 면이 아닌 법선'],answer:[a],min:0,max:80,unit:'°',hint:'입사각과 반사각은 모두 법선을 기준으로 재며, 서로 같아요.',explanation:`반사각은 입사각과 같은 ${a}°입니다.`,diagram:'light'};}
 case 'science_plant':return{...base,topic:'식물과 에너지 · 광합성',mode:'select',instruction:'온실의 광합성에 공급해야 할 원료와 에너지원 3가지를 켜세요.',options:['물 공급','빛 공급','이산화 탄소 공급','포도당 공급','산소 공급'],answer:[0,1,2],hint:'식물은 빛에너지를 이용해 물과 이산화 탄소로 양분을 만들어요.',explanation:'물과 이산화 탄소는 원료, 빛은 에너지원입니다. 포도당과 산소는 광합성의 생성물입니다.'};
 case 'science_digest':return{...base,topic:'동물과 에너지 · 소화',mode:'match',instruction:'영양소마다 작용하는 소화 효소의 배관을 연결하세요.',left:['녹말','단백질','지방'],options:['라이페이스','아밀레이스','펩신'],answer:[1,2,0],hint:'아밀레이스는 녹말, 펩신은 단백질, 라이페이스는 지방의 소화에 관여해요.',explanation:'녹말–아밀레이스, 단백질–펩신, 지방–라이페이스로 연결됩니다. 펩신이 단백질을 바로 아미노산까지 분해하는 것은 아닙니다.'};
 case 'science_state':return{...base,topic:'물질의 상태 변화 · 열에너지',mode:'select',instruction:'주변에서 열에너지를 흡수하는 상태 변화 3개를 냉각 장치에 연결하세요.',options:['얼음 → 물','수증기 → 물','물 → 수증기','물 → 얼음','드라이아이스 → 기체'],answer:[0,2,4],hint:'융해·기화·승화(고체→기체)는 열에너지를 흡수해요.',explanation:'얼음의 융해, 물의 기화, 드라이아이스의 승화가 주변의 열에너지를 흡수합니다.'};
 case 'science_mass':{const a=12+v*3,b=8+v,other=5+v;return{...base,topic:'화학 반응의 규칙성 · 질량 보존',mode:'slider',instruction:'밀폐 용기에서 A와 B가 남김없이 반응해 C와 D만 생성됩니다. D의 질량을 맞추세요.',readings:[`반응 전 A ${a}g + B ${b}g`,`반응 후 C ${other}g + D ?g`],answer:[a+b-other],min:0,max:40,unit:'g',hint:'밀폐된 계에서는 반응 전후 전체 질량이 같아요.',explanation:`D의 질량 = ${a} + ${b} − ${other} = ${a+b-other}g입니다.`};}
 case 'science_planets':return{...base,topic:'태양계 · 행성의 배열',mode:'order',instruction:'탐사선의 경로를 태양에 가까운 행성부터 먼 행성 순으로 설정하세요.',options:['화성','금성','수성','지구'],answer:[2,1,3,0],hint:'태양에 가까운 네 행성은 수성·금성·지구·화성 순서예요.',explanation:'수성 → 금성 → 지구 → 화성. 이 네 행성은 암석질 표면을 가진 지구형 행성입니다.'};
 }

import { env } from 'cloudflare:workers';
import { type Room, actor, tick } from './game';
// D1 counts queries per Worker invocation and a WebSocket stays one invocation, so a live
// connection reconnects (seamlessly) before reaching this many statements.
export function liveQueryBudget(){const v=Number((env as unknown as {LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET?:string}).LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET);return Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=20?v:800;}
export function database() { const db = (env as unknown as { DB?: D1Database }).DB; if (!db) throw new Error('서버 연결을 준비 중입니다. 잠시 후 다시 시도해 주세요.'); return db.withSession('first-primary'); }
export async function hash(token: string) { const bytes = await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256', new TextEncoder().encode(token)); return Array.from(new Uint8Array(bytes), b => b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join(''); }
export type RoomRow={state:string;version:number;expires:number};
// Plain columns mirrored from the JSON state on every write. Live movement writes are
// guarded by these instead of parsing the whole room JSON inside SQLite each time.
export function roomGate(r:Room){return `${r.phase}|${r.paused?1:0}|${r.seq}|${r.field?.epoch??-1}`;}
export function roomLocks(r:Room){const f=r.field;return '|'+[...Object.keys(f?.venting||{}),...Object.keys(f?.watching||{})].join('|')+'|';}
export const ROOM_WRITE_SQL='UPDATE mafia_rooms SET state = ?, version = version + 1, live_gate = ?, live_locks = ? WHERE code = ? AND version = ?';
export function roomWrite(db:ReturnType<typeof database>,r:Room,code:string,version:number){return db.prepare(ROOM_WRITE_SQL).bind(JSON.stringify(r),roomGate(r),roomLocks(r),code,version);}
export type RoomSeed={row:RoomRow;db:ReturnType<typeof database>};
export async function mutate(code: string, key: string | null, fn?: (r:Room)=>void, seed?:RoomSeed) {
  const db = seed?.db||database();
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 48; attempt++) {
    const row = attempt===0&&seed?seed.row:await db.prepare('SELECT state, version, expires FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code = ?').bind(code).first<RoomRow>();
    if (!row || row.expires < Date.now()) throw new Error('방을 찾을 수 없거나 만료되었습니다. 코드를 확인해 주세요.');
    const r:Room = JSON.parse(row.state); r.revision = row.version; if (key) actor(r,key);
    const changed = tick(r); if (fn) fn(r);
    if (!changed && !fn) return r;
    r.revision = row.version + 1;
    const result = await roomWrite(db,r,code,row.version).run();
    if (result.meta.changes === 1) return r;
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, Math.random() * 45));
  }
  throw new Error('요청이 몰리고 있습니다. 잠시 후 다시 눌러 주세요.');
}

FILE: lib/surveillance.ts

import type {MapContext} from './map.ts';
import {population,mapIdentity,mapFor,sight,distance,type Point} from './map.ts';
export function securityConsole(count:MapContext){const rooms=mapFor(count).locations,r=rooms.find(r=>r.id==='security')||rooms.find(r=>r.id==='control')!;return {x:r.x+r.w*.32,y:r.y+r.h*.7,room:r.name};}
export function communicationsConsole(count:MapContext){const r=mapFor(count).locations.find(r=>r.id==='comms')!;return{x:r.x+r.w*.45,y:r.y+r.h*.76,room:r.name};}
function buildCameras(count:MapContext){const rooms=mapFor(count).locations,ids=['lab','power','comms','medical',...(population(count)>10?['security','loading']:[]),...(population(count)>20?['navigation','hangar']:[])];return ids.map((id,i)=>{const r=rooms.find(r=>r.id===id)!;return{id,number:i+1,name:r.name,x:r.x+r.w/2,y:r.y+r.h*.6,w:640,h:420,mount:{x:r.x+r.w*.64,y:r.y+28}};});}
export function cameraSees(camera:ReturnType<typeof camerasFor>[number],p:Point,count:MapContext){return Math.abs(p.x-camera.x)<=camera.w/2&&Math.abs(p.y-camera.y)<=camera.h/2&&sight(camera,p,count);}
export function atSecurity(p:Point,count:MapContext){const console=securityConsole(count);return distance(p,console)<=100&&sight(p,console,count);}

const cameraCache=new Map<string,ReturnType<typeof buildCameras>>();
export function camerasFor(count:MapContext){const key=mapIdentity(count)+':'+population(count);let cameras=cameraCache.get(key);if(!cameras){cameras=buildCameras(count);cameraCache.set(key,cameras);}return cameras;}

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx";
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge";

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]): string {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs));
}

======================================================================
FILE: scripts/execution-profile.mjs

import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";

export function readExecutionProfile() {
  let settings;
  try {
    settings = JSON.parse(readFileSync(new URL("../.sites-runtime/execution-profile.json", import.meta.url), "utf8"));
  } catch (error) {
    // Clean clones and remote builds have no checkout-local selection.
    if (error.code === "ENOENT") return "portable";
    throw error;
  }
  if (!["managed-linux", "portable"].includes(settings?.executionProfile)) {
    throw new Error("Invalid local execution profile; rerun the Sites plugin's configure-execution-profile.mjs.");
  }
  return settings.executionProfile;
}

import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { accessSync, constants } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { projectRoot } from "./sites-env.mjs";
import { readExecutionProfile } from "./execution-profile.mjs";
import { runNpmInstall } from "./npm-install.mjs";

if (!process.env.npm_execpath) {
  throw new Error("Run this installer with npm run install:ci.");
}

if (![
  "SHARP_IGNORE_GLOBAL_LIBVIPS",
  "SHARP_FORCE_GLOBAL_LIBVIPS",
  "npm_config_build_from_source",
  "NPM_CONFIG_BUILD_FROM_SOURCE",
].some((key) => key in process.env)) {
  process.env.SHARP_IGNORE_GLOBAL_LIBVIPS = "1";
}

if (readExecutionProfile() === "managed-linux") {
  const result = spawnSync("bash", [path.join(projectRoot, "scripts/install-ci.sh")], {
    stdio: "inherit",
  });
  if (result.error) throw result.error;
  process.exit(result.status ?? 1);
}

// Invoke npm's JavaScript entrypoint, avoiding platform-specific shell shims.
const installed = await runNpmInstall([
  process.execPath,
    process.env.npm_execpath, "ci", "--prefix", projectRoot, "--workspaces=false",
    "--include=dev", "--include=optional", "--prefer-offline", "--no-audit", "--no-fund",
]);
if (installed.signal) process.kill(process.pid, installed.signal);
if (installed.code !== 0 || installed.signal) process.exit(installed.code || 1);

try {
  accessSync(
    path.join(
      projectRoot, "node_modules", ".bin",
      process.platform === "win32" ? "vinext.cmd" : "vinext",
    ),
    process.platform === "win32" ? constants.F_OK : constants.X_OK,
  );
} catch {
  console.error("npm ci exited successfully but the local vinext executable is unavailable.");
  process.exitCode = 69;
}


======================================================================
FILE: scripts/npm-install.mjs
======================================================================
import { spawn, spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import {
  closeSync,
  constants,
  fstatSync,
  ftruncateSync,
  openSync,
  readFileSync,
  writeFileSync,
} from "node:fs";
import { constants as osConstants } from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { createInterface } from "node:readline";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const MAX_PACKAGES = 100_000;

// npm reports tarball cache reads separately from registry metadata requests.
// Count unique installed tarball URLs, not image seeds or network bytes. Require
// complete coverage of npm's installed lock so older/partial logs stay unknown.
export class NpmCacheProgress {
  entries = new Map();
  invalid = false;

  constructor(registry) {
    try {
      const value = new URL(registry);
      if (["http:", "https:"].includes(value.protocol)) this.registry = value;
    } catch {}
  }

  accept(line) {
    const match = line.match(
      /^npm http (?:cache |fetch GET 200 )(\S+) \d+ms(?: attempt #\d+)? \(cache (hit|miss|revalidated|updated|skip)\)$/,
    );
    if (!match) return;
    const [, spec, status] = match;
    // Pacote's direct content-cache log uses name@URL; HTTP cache logs use URL.
    const url = spec.replace(/^(?:@[^/]+\/)?[^@/]+@(?=https?:\/\/)/, "");
    if (
      url.length > 4096 ||
      (this.entries.size >= MAX_PACKAGES && !this.entries.has(url))
    ) {
      this.invalid = true;
      return;
    }
    const downloaded = !["hit", "revalidated"].includes(status);
    this.entries.set(url, downloaded || this.entries.get(url) === true);
  }

  counts(lock) {
    if (this.invalid || lock?.lockfileVersion !== 3 || !lock.packages)
      return {};
    const urls = new Set(
      Object.values(lock.packages)
        .map((pkg) => pkg?.resolved)
        .filter((url) => typeof url === "string" && /^https?:\/\//.test(url)),
    );
    if (!urls.size || urls.size > MAX_PACKAGES) return {};
    const observed = new Set();
    let downloaded = 0;
    for (const url of urls) {
      let candidates = [url];
      if (this.registry) {
        const locked = new URL(url);
        if (locked.hostname === "registry.npmjs.org") {
          // Match npm/pacote's host rewrite and registry-fetch's path prefix
          // using npm's effective configuration, never an arbitrary suffix.
          candidates = [
            ...new Set([
              url,
              new URL(locked.pathname, this.registry).href,
              this.registry.href.replace(/\/$/, "") +
                locked.pathname +
                locked.search,
            ]),
          ];
        }
      }
      const matches = candidates.filter((candidate) =>
        this.entries.has(candidate),
      );
      if (
        !matches.length ||
        matches.some((candidate) => observed.has(candidate))
      )
        return {};
      for (const candidate of matches) observed.add(candidate);
      // A corrupt direct-cache hit followed by a mirror fetch is a download.
      if (matches.some((candidate) => this.entries.get(candidate)))
        downloaded++;
    }
    return {
      packages_reused: urls.size - downloaded,
      packages_downloaded: downloaded,
    };
  }
}

export async function runNpmInstall(command, cacheSeed = "not_applicable") {
  let descriptor;
  try {
    if (process.env.SITES_INSTALL_REPORT_PATH && constants.O_NOFOLLOW) {
      descriptor = openSync(
        process.env.SITES_INSTALL_REPORT_PATH,
        constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW | constants.O_NONBLOCK,
      );
      if (!fstatSync(descriptor).isFile()) {
        closeSync(descriptor);
        descriptor = undefined;
      }
    }
  } catch {
    if (descriptor !== undefined) {
      try {
        closeSync(descriptor);
      } catch {}
    }
    descriptor = undefined;
  }
  const env = { ...process.env };
  delete env.SITES_INSTALL_REPORT_PATH;
  const [executable, ...args] = command;
  let registry;
  const installIndex = args.indexOf("ci");
  if (descriptor !== undefined && installIndex >= 0) {
    try {
      const configured = spawnSync(
        executable,
        [...args.slice(0, installIndex), "config", "get", "registry"],
        {
          env,
          encoding: "utf8",
          stdio: ["ignore", "pipe", "ignore"],
          timeout: 3000,
          maxBuffer: 4096,
        },
      );
      if (configured.status === 0) registry = configured.stdout.trim();
    } catch {}
  }
  const progress = new NpmCacheProgress(registry);
  let receivedSignal;
  const handlers = ["SIGINT", "SIGHUP", "SIGTERM"].map((signal) => {
    const handler = () => {
      receivedSignal ??= signal;
    };
    process.on(signal, handler);
    return [signal, handler];
  });
  let result = { code: 1, signal: null };
  try {
    const child = spawn(executable, [...args, "--loglevel=http"], {
      env,
      stdio: ["inherit", "inherit", "pipe"],
    });
    const lines = createInterface({ input: child.stderr, crlfDelay: Infinity });
    lines.on("line", (line) => {
      progress.accept(line);
      // Do not add package URLs to normal helper output just for telemetry.
      if (!line.startsWith("npm http ")) process.stderr.write(`${line}\n`);
    });
    let startError;
    child.once("error", (error) => {
      startError = error;
    });
    result = await new Promise((resolve) =>
      child.once("close", (code, signal) => {
        resolve({
          code: startError ? (startError.code === "ENOENT" ? 127 : 1) : code,
          signal,
        });
      }),
    );
    lines.close();
    if (startError) process.stderr.write("Unable to start npm.\n");
  } finally {
    for (const [signal, handler] of handlers)
      process.removeListener(signal, handler);
    result.signal ??= receivedSignal;
    try {
      let counts = {};
      if (result.code === 0 && !result.signal) {
        try {
          counts = progress.counts(
            JSON.parse(readFileSync("node_modules/.package-lock.json", "utf8")),
          );
        } catch {}
      }
      if (descriptor !== undefined) {
        ftruncateSync(descriptor, 0);
        writeFileSync(
          descriptor,
          `${JSON.stringify({
            version: 1,
            cache_seed:
              result.code === 0 && !result.signal
                ? cacheSeed
                : "decision_unavailable",
            ...counts,
          })}\n`,
        );
      }
    } catch {
      // Telemetry must not change installation behavior.
    } finally {
      if (descriptor !== undefined) {
        try {
          closeSync(descriptor);
        } catch {}
      }
    }
  }
  return result;
}

if (
  process.argv[1] &&
  path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)
) {
  const [cacheSeed, ...command] = process.argv.slice(2);
  const result = await runNpmInstall(command, cacheSeed);
  process.exitCode = result.signal
    ? 128 + (osConstants.signals[result.signal] ?? 0)
    : result.code ?? 1;
  if (result.signal) {
    try {
      process.kill(process.pid, result.signal);
    } catch {}
  }
}


======================================================================
FILE: scripts/pnpm-install.mjs
======================================================================
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import {
  accessSync,
  closeSync,
  constants,
  fstatSync,
  ftruncateSync,
  openSync,
  readFileSync,
  writeFileSync,
  writeSync,
} from "node:fs";
import { constants as osConstants } from "node:os";
import path from "node:path";
import { createInterface } from "node:readline";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

const MAX_PACKAGES = 100_000;
const CACHE_SEEDS = new Set(["seed_used", "seed_unavailable", "seed_lockfile_mismatch", "decision_unavailable", "not_applicable"]);
const STORE_STATES = new Set(["created", "seeded", "reused", "unavailable"]);
// The caller may use operational status only for
// initial-setup fallback; an established project always retains pnpm. Broad
// fetch failures can include TLS/auth errors and stay fatal.
const OPERATIONAL_FAILURE_CODES = new Set([
  "ERR_PNPM_UNEXPECTED_STORE",
  "ERR_PNPM_UNEXPECTED_VIRTUAL_STORE",
  "ERR_PNPM_OUTDATED_LOCKFILE",
  "ERR_PNPM_FROZEN_LOCKFILE_WITH_OUTDATED_LOCKFILE",
  "ERR_PNPM_NO_LOCKFILE",
  "ERR_PNPM_FETCH_408",
  "ERR_PNPM_FETCH_429",
  "ERR_PNPM_FETCH_500",
  "ERR_PNPM_FETCH_502",
  "ERR_PNPM_FETCH_503",
  "ERR_PNPM_FETCH_504",
]);

// These are pnpm-reported package IDs, not an assertion about network bytes or
// image-cache provenance. Do not interpret an installed-tree no-op as 100% reuse.
export class InstallProgress {
  constructor(project) {
    this.project = path.resolve(project);
    this.reused = new Set();
    this.downloaded = new Set();
    this.complete = false;
    this.added = undefined;
    this.invalid = false;
  }

  accept(event) {
    if (event?.name === "pnpm:stats" && event.prefix === this.project &&
        Number.isSafeInteger(event.added) && event.added >= 0) this.added = event.added;
    if (event?.name === "pnpm:stage" && event.stage === "importing_done" &&
        event.prefix === this.project) this.complete = true;
    if (event?.name !== "pnpm:progress" || event.requester !== this.project ||
        !["fetched", "found_in_store"].includes(event.status)) return;
    if (typeof event.packageId !== "string" || !event.packageId || event.packageId.length > 4096 ||
        this.reused.size + this.downloaded.size >= MAX_PACKAGES) {
      this.invalid = true;
      return;
    }
    if (event.status === "fetched") {
      this.reused.delete(event.packageId);
      this.downloaded.add(event.packageId);
    } else if (!this.downloaded.has(event.packageId)) {
      this.reused.add(event.packageId);
    }
  }

  counts(success) {
    if (!success || !this.complete || !(this.added > 0) || this.invalid ||
        this.reused.size + this.downloaded.size === 0) return {};
    return { packages_reused: this.reused.size, packages_downloaded: this.downloaded.size };
  }
}

function openReport() {
  const report = process.env.SITES_INSTALL_REPORT_PATH;
  if (!report || !constants.O_NOFOLLOW) return undefined;
  let fd;
  try {
    fd = openSync(report, constants.O_WRONLY | constants.O_NOFOLLOW | constants.O_NONBLOCK);
    if (fstatSync(fd).isFile()) return fd;
  } catch {}
  if (fd !== undefined) closeSync(fd);
  return undefined;
}

function writeReport(fd, report) {
  if (fd === undefined) return;
  try {
    const body = `${JSON.stringify(report)}\n`;
    writeSync(fd, body, 0, "utf8");
    ftruncateSync(fd, Buffer.byteLength(body));
  } catch {
    // Optional telemetry must not change installation behavior or expose its path.
  }
}

function showLine(line, progress, failure) {
  let event;
  try {
    event = JSON.parse(line);
  } catch {
    process.stdout.write(`${line}\n`);
    return;
  }
  progress.accept(event);
  if (event?.level === "error" && failure.code !== 65) {
    failure.code = event.name === "pnpm" && OPERATIONAL_FAILURE_CODES.has(event.err?.code)
      ? 70 : 65;
  }
  const message = event?.message ?? event?.err?.message;
  if (event?.name === "pnpm:lifecycle" && typeof event.line === "string") {
    process.stdout.write(`${event.line}\n`);
  } else if (typeof message === "string" && ["warn", "error", "info"].includes(event.level)) {
    const output = event.level === "error" ? process.stderr : process.stdout;
    output.write(`${message}\n`);
  }
}

async function openLock(filename, waitSeconds) {
  let fd;
  try {
    if (!filename || !constants.O_NOFOLLOW || !constants.O_NONBLOCK) throw new Error("Unsupported lock");
    fd = openSync(filename, constants.O_CREAT | constants.O_RDWR | constants.O_NOFOLLOW | constants.O_NONBLOCK, 0o600);
    if (!fstatSync(fd).isFile()) throw new Error("Expected a regular lock file");
    const child = spawn("flock", ["-w", waitSeconds, "3"], { stdio: ["ignore", "ignore", "ignore", fd] });
    const code = await new Promise((resolve) => {
      child.once("error", () => resolve(undefined));
      child.once("close", resolve);
    });
    if (code === 0) return { status: "locked", fd };
    closeSync(fd);
    return { status: code === undefined ? "unavailable" : "busy" };
  } catch {
    if (fd !== undefined) closeSync(fd);
    return { status: "unavailable" };
  }
}

async function holdInstallLocks(projectLock, sharedLock, waitSeconds) {
  // The shell holds both leases through stdin. EOF (including caller exit)
  // releases the same open file descriptions that flock locked in its child.
  const requests = createInterface({ input: process.stdin, crlfDelay: Infinity });
  const project = await openLock(projectLock, "0");
  let shared;
  try {
    process.stdout.write(`${project.status}\n`);
    for await (const request of requests) {
      if (request === "shared" && project.fd !== undefined) {
        shared ??= await openLock(sharedLock, waitSeconds);
        process.stdout.write(`${shared.status}\n`);
      } else if (request === "release-shared") {
        if (shared?.fd !== undefined) closeSync(shared.fd);
        shared = undefined;
        process.stdout.write("released\n");
      } else {
        process.stdout.write("unavailable\n");
      }
    }
  } finally {
    if (shared?.fd !== undefined) closeSync(shared.fd);
    if (project.fd !== undefined) closeSync(project.fd);
    requests.close();
  }
}

async function main() {
  const argsIn = process.argv.slice(2);
  if (argsIn[0] === "--hold-install-locks") {
    await holdInstallLocks(argsIn[1], argsIn[2], argsIn[3]);
    return;
  }
  if (argsIn[0] === "--report-store") {
    const [, cacheSeed, storeScope, storeState, stdout] = argsIn;
    if (!CACHE_SEEDS.has(cacheSeed) || !["project", "workspace", "unknown"].includes(storeScope) ||
        !STORE_STATES.has(storeState)) { process.exitCode = 64; return; }
    const report = { version: 1, cache_seed: cacheSeed, store_scope: storeScope, store_state: storeState };
    const fd = openReport();
    writeReport(fd, report);
    if (fd !== undefined) closeSync(fd);
    if (stdout === "1") process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(report)}\n`);
    return;
  }
  const [cacheSeed, storeScope, storeState, store, executable, ...prefix] = argsIn;
  if (!CACHE_SEEDS.has(cacheSeed) || !["project", "workspace"].includes(storeScope) ||
      !STORE_STATES.has(storeState) || storeState === "unavailable" ||
      !store || !path.isAbsolute(store) || !executable) {
    process.stderr.write("Invalid pnpm installation arguments.\n");
    process.exitCode = 64;
    return;
  }
  const fd = openReport();
  const report = { version: 1, cache_seed: cacheSeed, store_scope: storeScope, store_state: storeState };
  writeReport(fd, report);
  const progress = new InstallProgress(process.cwd());
  const failure = {};
  const env = { ...process.env };
  delete env.SITES_INSTALL_REPORT_PATH;
  const args = [...prefix, "install", "--prod=false", "--ignore-scripts=false",
    "--frozen-lockfile", "--prefer-offline", "--store-dir", store,
    "--cache-dir", path.join(store, "policy-cache"), "--fetch-retries=0",
    "--fetch-timeout=30000", "--network-concurrency=1", "--reporter=ndjson",
    "--package-import-method=clone-or-copy"];
  let result = { code: 1, signal: null };
  let receivedSignal;
  try {
    const child = spawn(executable, args, { env, stdio: ["inherit", "pipe", "inherit"] });
    const lines = createInterface({ input: child.stdout, crlfDelay: Infinity });
    lines.on("line", (line) => showLine(line, progress, failure));
    // timeout/exec owns the inherited group. Relaying would deliver signals twice.
    const signalHandlers = ["SIGINT", "SIGHUP", "SIGTERM"].map((signal) => {
      const handler = () => { receivedSignal ??= signal; };
      process.on(signal, handler);
      return [signal, handler];
    });
    let spawnError;
    child.once("error", (error) => { spawnError = error; });
    result = await new Promise((resolve) => child.once("close", (code, signal) => {
      resolve({ code: spawnError ? (spawnError.code === "ENOENT" ? 127 : 1) : code, signal });
    }));
    for (const [signal, handler] of signalHandlers) process.removeListener(signal, handler);
    lines.close();
    result.signal ??= receivedSignal;
    if (spawnError) {
      process.stderr.write("Unable to start pnpm.\n");
      result.code = spawnError.code === "ENOENT" ? 127 : 65;
    } else if (result.code !== 0 && !result.signal) {
      result.code = failure.code ?? 65;
    }
    if (result.code === 0 && !result.signal) {
      accessSync("node_modules/.bin/vinext", constants.X_OK);
      const lock = readFileSync("pnpm-lock.yaml");
      writeFileSync("node_modules/.sites-install.json", `${JSON.stringify({
        package_manager: "pnpm@11.25.0",
        lockfile_sha256: createHash("sha256").update(lock).digest("hex"),
        node: process.version,
        platform: `${process.platform}-${process.arch}`,
      }, null, 2)}\n`);
    }
  } catch {
    process.stderr.write("Dependency setup did not produce a usable Vinext installation.\n");
    result = { code: 65, signal: receivedSignal ?? null };
  } finally {
    Object.assign(report, progress.counts(result.code === 0 && !result.signal));
    writeReport(fd, report);
    if (fd !== undefined) closeSync(fd);
  }
  if (report.packages_reused !== undefined) {
    process.stdout.write(`[sites] pnpm reused ${report.packages_reused} packages and downloaded ${report.packages_downloaded}\n`);
  }
  if (result.code === 0 && !result.signal) process.stdout.write("[sites] dependency setup passed\n");
  process.exitCode = result.signal ? 128 + (osConstants.signals[result.signal] ?? 0) : result.code ?? 1;
  if (result.signal) {
    try { process.kill(process.pid, result.signal); } catch {}
  }
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) await main();


======================================================================
FILE: scripts/run-framework.mjs
======================================================================
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { readExecutionProfile } from "./execution-profile.mjs";

const [command, ...args] = process.argv.slice(2);
if (!["dev", "build"].includes(command)) throw new Error("Expected dev or build.");
const managedLinux = readExecutionProfile() === "managed-linux";

if (managedLinux && command === "build") {
  const result = spawnSync("bash", [
    fileURLToPath(new URL("./build-verified.sh", import.meta.url)), ...args,
  ], { stdio: "inherit" });
  if (result.error) throw result.error;
  process.exit(result.status ?? 1);
}

// Import in this process so the preview owner retains its PID and signals.
const cli = new URL(managedLinux
  ? "../node_modules/vite/bin/vite.js"
  : "../node_modules/vinext/dist/cli.js", import.meta.url);
process.argv = [process.execPath, fileURLToPath(cli), command,
  ...(!managedLinux && command === "dev" ? ["--port", "5173"] : []), ...args];
await import(cli.href);

import { mkdirSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

export const projectRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../", import.meta.url));
const runtimeRoot = process.env.SITES_RUNTIME_ROOT || path.join(projectRoot, ".sites-runtime");

process.env.CLOUDFLARE_CF_FETCH_ENABLED ||= "false";
process.env.WRANGLER_SEND_METRICS ||= "false";
process.env.WRANGLER_WRITE_LOGS ||= "false";
process.env.WRANGLER_LOG_PATH ||= path.join(runtimeRoot, "wrangler/logs");
process.env.WRANGLER_REGISTRY_PATH ||= path.join(runtimeRoot, "wrangler/dev-registry");
process.env.MINIFLARE_REGISTRY_PATH ||= path.join(runtimeRoot, "wrangler/registry");

process.chdir(projectRoot);
for (const directory of [
  path.dirname(process.env.WRANGLER_LOG_PATH),
  process.env.WRANGLER_REGISTRY_PATH,
  process.env.MINIFLARE_REGISTRY_PATH,
]) {
  mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true });
}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {newRoom,configOf} from '../lib/game.ts';
import {initField,resumeField,fieldAct} from '../lib/field.ts';
const now=1000000;
function fixture(){const r=newRoom('ABC234','host',configOf({mode:'field',count:6}),now);r.players=Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>({id:`p${i}`,key:`k${i}`,name:`학생${i}`,avatar:i,role:i?'crewmate':'impostor',alive:true,ready:true,connected:false,used:false,shield:true,publicRole:false,intel:[]}));initField(r,now);resumeField(r,now);r.field.cooldowns={};const poses=r.players.map(p=>({id:p.id,x:p.id==='p1'?1160:1000,y:940,at:now,seq:r.seq,warp:0,ack:0,clock:now,previous:'중앙 홀',place:'중앙 홀'}));poses[1].history=JSON.stringify([{x:1060,y:940,at:now-500,warp:0,seq:r.seq}]);return{r,poses};}
let {r,poses}=fixture();assert.throws(()=>fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1'},poses,now));assert.throws(()=>fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-900},poses,now));fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-400},poses,now);assert.equal(r.players[1].alive,false);
({r,poses}=fixture());r.field.invisible.p1=now+2000;assert.throws(()=>fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-400},poses,now));
({r,poses}=fixture());r.field.venting.p1={vent:'x',until:now+2000};assert.throws(()=>fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-400},poses,now));
({r,poses}=fixture());poses[1].warp=now-100;assert.throws(()=>fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-400},poses,now));
({r,poses}=fixture());r.field.protects.p1=now+1000;fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-400},poses,now);assert.equal(r.players[1].alive,true);assert.equal(r.field.effects.at(-1).kind,'blocked');
// The rewind window is bounded even when history covers the rendered moment.
({r,poses}=fixture());poses[1].history=JSON.stringify([{x:1060,y:940,at:now-1600,warp:0,seq:r.seq},{x:1060,y:940,at:now-1000,warp:0,seq:r.seq}]);fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-1050},poses,now);assert.equal(r.players[1].alive,false,'inside the window');
({r,poses}=fixture());poses[1].history=JSON.stringify([{x:1060,y:940,at:now-1600,warp:0,seq:r.seq},{x:1060,y:940,at:now-1000,warp:0,seq:r.seq}]);assert.throws(()=>fieldAct(r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-1150},poses,now),'beyond the window');
// v22: the attacker's view decides. The target was in range and in sight on the attacker's screen and has
// since stepped round a corner (out of sight now): the press still kills. Aimed at where the target is now
// (out of sight, out of range) it does not.
{const A={x:600,y:1040},H={x:670,y:1040},C={x:705.36,y:1075.36};
 const corner=()=>{const {r,poses}=fixture();poses[0]={...poses[0],...A};poses[1]={...poses[1],...C,at:now,history:JSON.stringify([{...H,at:now-1000,warp:0,seq:r.seq},{...H,at:now-400,warp:0,seq:r.seq}])};return{r,poses};};
 let t=corner();fieldAct(t.r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:t.r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now-450},t.poses,now);assert.equal(t.r.players[1].alive,false,'kill what you saw');
 t=corner();assert.throws(()=>fieldAct(t.r,'k0',{action:'kill',seq:t.r.seq,target:'p1',targetAt:now},t.poses,now),'no kill through the wall');}
console.log('PASS: attack uses validated recent rendered target position (kills what the attacker saw, even round a corner since); expired rewind, invisibility, vents and warp history rejected; current protection still blocks.');

FILE: tests/browser/verify-ui.mjs

// End-to-end UI check against a running local server (pnpm run start), with bot players that
// move over the real WebSocket protocol. Not part of the default test list: it needs
// Playwright + Chromium.  Usage:
//   BASE=http://127.0.0.1:8787 OUT=./verify-out node tests/browser/verify-ui.mjs [step...]
//   (PW_MODULE=/path/to/playwright/index.mjs if playwright is not resolvable; CHROMIUM=path)
// Steps: lobby aurora nereid host solo mobile perf29 hitches   (default: all)
// Headless Chromium renders WebGL in software (SwiftShader): frame times are not device numbers.
import fs from 'node:fs';
const BASE=process.env.BASE||'http://127.0.0.1:8787',OUT=process.env.OUT||'verify-out';fs.mkdirSync(OUT,{recursive:true});
const {chromium}=await import(process.env.PW_MODULE||'playwright');
const token=()=>Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
const pause=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
async function api(key,path,body){const res=await fetch(BASE+path,{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key,'Content-Type':'application/json',Origin:BASE},body:JSON.stringify(body)});const data=await res.json();if(!res.ok)throw Error(path+' '+JSON.stringify(data).slice(0,200));return data;}
const report={};const note=(k,v)=>{report[k]=v;console.log(k,typeof v==='object'?JSON.stringify(v):v);};
async function room({mapId='aurora',count=16,start=true,mode='field'}={}){
 const host=token();let r=await api(host,'/api/game',{action:'create',config:{mode,mapId,count,discussion:300,vote:120}});const code=r.code;
 const players=Array.from({length:count},(_,i)=>({key:token(),name:i?`학생${String(i).padStart(2,'0')}`:'브라우저',avatar:i%16}));
 for(const p of players){const v=await api(p.key,'/api/game',{action:'join',code,name:p.name,avatar:p.avatar});p.id=v.me;}
 if(start){await Promise.all(players.map(p=>api(p.key,'/api/game',{action:'ready',code,ready:true})));r=await api(host,'/api/game',{action:'start',code,seq:r.seq});if(mode==='field')r=await api(host,'/api/game',{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq});}
 return {code,host,players,seq:r.seq};
}
// A bot: authenticates on /api/live and walks around its spawn with held-key style input.
function bot(code,p,seq,{wander=true}={}){
 const url=BASE.replace(/^http/,'ws')+'/api/live';let ws,pending=[],n=0,warp=0,ack=0,dir={x:0,y:0},timer,closed=false,ready=false;const stats={frames:0};
 const connect=()=>{ws=new WebSocket(url);ws.onopen=()=>ws.send(JSON.stringify({type:'auth',code,token:p.key}));
  ws.onmessage=e=>{const m=JSON.parse(e.data);if(m.type==='ready'){ack=m.ack;n=ack;warp=m.warp;pending=[];ready=true;}if(m.type==='frame'){stats.frames++;const own=m.frame.own;if((own.warp||0)!==warp){warp=own.warp||0;pending=[];n=own.ack||0;}ack=own.ack||0;pending=pending.filter(i=>i.n>ack);stats.own=own;}if(m.type==='resend'){pending=pending.filter(i=>i.n>m.after);}if(m.type==='rebase'){pending=[];n=m.own.ack||0;}};
  ws.onclose=()=>{ready=false;if(!closed)setTimeout(connect,500);};};
 connect();let turn=0;
 timer=setInterval(()=>{if(!ready||ws.readyState!==1)return;if(wander&&Date.now()>turn){const a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,still=Math.random()<.2;dir=still?{x:0,y:0}:{x:Math.cos(a),y:Math.sin(a)};turn=Date.now()+800+Math.random()*1600;}
  if(dir.x||dir.y){for(let k=0;k<2;k++)pending.push({n:++n,dx:dir.x,dy:dir.y,ms:20});if(pending.length>120)pending=pending.slice(-120);}
  ws.send(JSON.stringify({type:'input',seq,warp,hold:false,inputs:pending}));},40);
 return {stats,stop(){closed=true;clearInterval(timer);try{ws.close();}catch{}},act(body){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const id=crypto.randomUUID(),t=setTimeout(()=>reject(Error('timeout')),5000);const on=e=>{const m=JSON.parse(e.data);if(m.type==='actResult'&&m.id===id){clearTimeout(t);ws.removeEventListener('message',on);resolve(m);}};ws.addEventListener('message',on);ws.send(JSON.stringify({type:'act',id,body:{...body,protocol:2,code,seq,warp,inputs:pending,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()}}));});},set dir(v){dir=v;wander=false;}};
}
const browser=await chromium.launch({executablePath:process.env.CHROMIUM||'/opt/pw-browsers/chromium',args:['--use-angle=swiftshader','--enable-unsafe-swiftshader','--ignore-gpu-blocklist']});
async function page(key,code,{viewport={width:1280,height:800},mobile=false,query='?netdebug=1',resume=true,init=null}={}){
 const ctx=await browser.newContext({viewport,deviceScaleFactor:1,isMobile:mobile,hasTouch:mobile});if(init)await ctx.addInitScript(init);
 await ctx.addInitScript(([c,k])=>{try{localStorage.setItem('nightfall-session',JSON.stringify({code:c,token:k}));}catch{}},[code,key]);
 const pg=await ctx.newPage();const errors=[];pg.on('pageerror',e=>errors.push(e.message));pg.on('console',m=>{if(m.type()==='error'&&!/favicon|404/.test(m.text()))errors.push(m.text());});
 await pg.goto(BASE+'/'+query,{waitUntil:'domcontentloaded'});
 // The entry screen offers the saved session as "이전 방 … 돌아가기".
 if(resume)await pg.locator('button.resume').click({timeout:30000}).catch(async e=>{await pg.screenshot({path:`${OUT}/resume-missing-${Date.now()}.png`});console.log('resume button missing:',(await pg.evaluate(()=>document.body.innerText)).slice(0,300).replace(/\n/g,' '));throw e;});return {pg,ctx,errors};
}
// Per-frame sampling of what this browser draws for other players (needs ?netdebug=1).
async function sampleMotion(pg,ms){return pg.evaluate(ms=>new Promise(resolve=>{const net=window.__fieldNet;if(!net)return resolve({error:'no __fieldNet'});const last={},jumps=[],dts=[];let vanish=0,frames=0,prevT=performance.now();const seen=new Map();
 const tick=t=>{dts.push(t-prevT);prevT=t;frames++;const ids=new Set((net.snapshot?.people||[]).map(p=>p.id).filter(id=>net.interpolated[id]));for(const id of ids){const p=net.interpolated[id];if(id===net.room.me)continue;if(last[id]){jumps.push(Math.hypot(p.x-last[id].x,p.y-last[id].y));}last[id]={x:p.x,y:p.y};seen.set(id,t);}
  for(const id of Object.keys(last))if(!ids.has(id)){const own=net.visual,q=last[id];if(Math.hypot(q.x-own.x,q.y-own.y)<(net.snapshot?.radius||350)*.7)vanish++;delete last[id];}
  if(t-start<ms)requestAnimationFrame(tick);else{jumps.sort((a,b)=>a-b);dts.sort((a,b)=>a-b);resolve({frames,maxJumpPx:+(jumps.at(-1)||0).toFixed(1),p99JumpPx:+(jumps[Math.floor(jumps.length*.99)]||0).toFixed(1),vanishNearby:vanish,frameDtP50:+dts[Math.floor(dts.length*.5)].toFixed(1),frameDtP95:+dts[Math.floor(dts.length*.95)].toFixed(1),stats:net.stats.summary(),transport:net.healthy()?'live':'http'});}};const start=performance.now();requestAnimationFrame(tick);}),ms);}
const steps=process.argv.slice(2);const want=s=>!steps.length||steps.includes(s);

if(want('lobby')){
 const {code,players}=await room({count:16,start:false});const {pg,ctx,errors}=await page(players[0].key,code,{resume:false});
 await pg.waitForSelector('.doll-avatar img',{timeout:60000});await pause(2500);await pg.screenshot({path:`${OUT}/lobby.png`,fullPage:true});note('lobby.errors',errors);await ctx.close();
}
async function fieldStep(name,mapId,count){
 const R=await room({mapId,count});const bots=R.players.slice(1).map(p=>bot(R.code,p,R.seq));
 const {pg,ctx,errors}=await page(R.players[0].key,R.code);
 await pg.waitForSelector(mapId==='nereid'?'.fps-surface canvas':'.field-viewport canvas',{timeout:90000}).catch(async e=>{await pg.screenshot({path:`${OUT}/${name}-fail.png`});console.log('PAGE TEXT:',(await pg.evaluate(()=>document.body.innerText)).slice(0,600),'ERRORS',errors);throw e;});
 await pg.waitForFunction(()=>window.__fieldNet?.healthy(),null,{timeout:60000}).catch(()=>{});await pause(4000);
 await pg.screenshot({path:`${OUT}/${name}-play.png`});
 // Hold D for a second: own movement must be acknowledged over the socket.
 const before=await pg.evaluate(()=>({...window.__fieldNet.visual,ack:window.__fieldNet.lastAck}));
 await pg.locator(mapId==='nereid'?'.fps-surface canvas':'.field-viewport canvas').first().focus().catch(()=>{});
 await pg.keyboard.down('KeyW');await pause(1000);await pg.keyboard.up('KeyW');await pause(600);
 const after=await pg.evaluate(()=>({...window.__fieldNet.visual,ack:window.__fieldNet.lastAck,pending:window.__fieldNet.pending.length}));
 note(`${name}.ownMove`,{movedPx:+Math.hypot(after.x-before.x,after.y-before.y).toFixed(1),acked:after.ack-before.ack,pendingAfter:after.pending});
 note(`${name}.remoteMotion`,await sampleMotion(pg,8000));
 // Network panel (F3) is on via ?netdebug=1.

 await pg.click('.fullscreen-toggle');await pause(800);

 await pg.keyboard.press('KeyM');await pause(700);

 await pg.keyboard.press('Escape');

 note(`${name}.escClosesMap`,await pg.waitForFunction(()=>!document.querySelector('[role=dialog]'),null,{timeout:4000}).then(()=>true,()=>false));
 note(`${name}.afterEsc`,await pg.evaluate(()=>({fullscreenElement:document.fullscreenElement?.tagName||null,immersive:document.body.classList.contains('immersive-play')})));
 if(mapId==='nereid'){await pg.mouse.click(640,400);await pause(400);note(`${name}.pointerLock`,await pg.evaluate(()=>document.pointerLockElement?.tagName||null));await pg.keyboard.press('Escape');}
 // Meeting transition while fullscreen: the host calls a meeting.
 if(!(await pg.evaluate(()=>!!document.fullscreenElement||document.body.classList.contains('immersive-play'))))await pg.click('.fullscreen-toggle');
 const state=await fetch(`${BASE}/api/game?code=${R.code}`,{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+R.host}}).then(r=>r.json()).catch(()=>null);
 await api(R.host,'/api/game',{action:'next',code:R.code,seq:state?.seq??R.seq}).catch(e=>note(`${name}.meetingError`,e.message));

 note(`${name}.errors`,errors);bots.forEach(b=>b.stop());await ctx.close();return R;
}
if(want('aurora'))await fieldStep('aurora','aurora',16);

// Hitches: main-thread long tasks from page load through the first 12 s of play (a player
// rejoining straight into a running game, 20 players), time until the game screen shows and
// frame intervals after the first 4 s. Software WebGL: compare builds with it, not devices.
if(want('hitches')){
 const watch=()=>{window.__long=[];window.__raf=[];try{new PerformanceObserver(l=>{for(const e of l.getEntries())window.__long.push([e.startTime,e.duration]);}).observe({type:'longtask',buffered:true});}catch{}let last=0;const tick=t=>{if(last)window.__raf.push([t,t-last]);last=t;requestAnimationFrame(tick);};requestAnimationFrame(tick);};
 for(const mapId of ['nereid','aurora']){const R=await room({mapId,count:20});const bots=R.players.slice(1).map(p=>bot(R.code,p,R.seq));const {pg,ctx,errors}=await page(R.players[0].key,R.code,{query:'',init:watch});
  await pg.waitForSelector(mapId==='nereid'?'.fps-surface canvas':'.field-viewport canvas',{timeout:120000});const appear=await pg.evaluate(()=>performance.now());await pause(12000);
  const d=await pg.evaluate(()=>({long:window.__long,raf:window.__raf})),long=d.long.filter(([t])=>t>=appear-500&&t<appear+12000).map(([,v])=>v),raf=d.raf.filter(([t])=>t>appear+4000).map(([,v])=>v).sort((a,b)=>a-b),q=p=>Math.round(raf[Math.min(raf.length-1,Math.floor(p*raf.length))]||0);
  note(`hitches-${mapId}`,{gameScreenMs:Math.round(appear),longTasks:long.length,longTotalMs:Math.round(long.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)),longMaxMs:Math.round(Math.max(0,...long)),frameP50:q(.5),frameP95:q(.95),frameP99:q(.99)});note(`hitches-${mapId}.errors`,errors);bots.forEach(b=>b.stop());await ctx.close();}
}

FILE: tests/checkpoint-multiplayer.mjs

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
const origin='http://terminal.local:4173',token=()=>Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
async function req(key,body,game=false,status=200){const res=await fetch(origin+(game?'/api/game':'/api/field'),{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});const data=await res.json();assert.equal(res.status,status,JSON.stringify(data));return data;}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import * as T from 'three';
import {createDoll} from '../lib/doll-character.ts';
import {configOf} from '../lib/game.ts';
import {readFileSync} from 'node:fs';
let maxMeshes=0,maxTriangles=0;
// v22 figure proportions: 1.6-1.9 tall (bun included), head about half of the height,
// one mesh per joint group and material kind, shared materials, bounded triangle count.
const materials=new Set();
for(let i=0;i<16;i++){const d=createDoll(i);d.root.updateMatrixWorld(true);const bounds=new T.Box3().setFromObject(d.root),head=new T.Box3().setFromObject(d.head);assert.ok(bounds.max.y>1.6&&bounds.max.y<1.9,`height ${bounds.max.y}`);assert.ok((head.max.y-head.min.y)/bounds.max.y>.45,'big figure head');assert.ok(bounds.min.y>-.01&&bounds.min.y<.03,'stands on the floor');assert.ok(bounds.max.x-bounds.min.x>.85);assert.equal(d.arms.length,2);assert.equal(d.legs.length,2);assert.ok(d.arms.every(a=>a.position.y>.7)&&d.legs.every(l=>l.position.y>.3&&l.position.y<.5),'shoulder and hip pivots');let meshes=0,triangles=0;d.root.traverse(o=>{if(o instanceof T.Mesh){meshes++;materials.add(o.material);const p=o.geometry.attributes.position;triangles+=(o.geometry.index?.count||p.count)/3;assert.ok(p.array.every(Number.isFinite));assert.ok(o.geometry.attributes.normal);}});maxMeshes=Math.max(maxMeshes,meshes);maxTriangles=Math.max(maxTriangles,triangles);assert.ok(meshes<=12,'static parts must be merged per joint group');assert.ok(triangles<20000,`triangle budget (${triangles})`);d.dispose();}
assert.ok(materials.size<=4,`all figures share their materials (${materials.size})`);

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { newRoom, defaults, distribution, addPlayer, act, next, view, tick } from '../lib/game.ts';
function setup(){const r=newRoom('ABC234','host',{...defaults,mode:'classic'});for(let i=0;i<16;i++){addPlayer(r,`p${i}`,`학생${i+1}`,i);act(r,`p${i}`,{action:'ready',ready:true});}act(r,'host',{action:'start',seq:r.seq});return r;}
function p(r,role){return r.players.find(x=>x.role===role)}
function doAct(r,x,action,target){act(r,x.key,{action,target,seq:r.seq,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()});}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {movementKey,movementVector,inputSteps} from '../lib/input.ts';
import {mapFor,walkable,spawn,ventsFor,EMERGENCY,REPAIR,distance,sight} from '../lib/map.ts';
import {securityConsole,communicationsConsole} from '../lib/surveillance.ts';
assert.ok(movementKey('KeyW'));assert.equal(movementKey('KeyQ'),false);const held=new Set(['KeyW','ArrowUp']);assert.deepEqual(movementVector(held),{x:0,y:-1});held.add('KeyD');assert.ok(Math.abs(Math.hypot(...Object.values(movementVector(held)))-1)<1e-10);held.delete('KeyW');held.delete('ArrowUp');assert.deepEqual(movementVector(held),{x:1,y:0});
for(const fps of [30,60,120,144,240,360]){let remainder=0,total=0,queued=0,max=0;for(let i=0;i<fps*10;i++){const batch=inputSteps(remainder,1000/fps);remainder=batch.remainder;total+=batch.steps;queued+=batch.steps;max=Math.max(max,queued);if((i+1)%fps===0)queued=0;}assert.equal(total,500,`${fps} Hz`);assert.ok(max<=50);}
// Flood-fill actual walkable floor, including inset room walls, for every active map size.
for(const count of [6,16,29]){const m=mapFor(count),step=20,w=Math.ceil(m.width/step),h=Math.ceil(m.height/step),mask=new Uint8Array(w*h);for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++)mask[y*w+x]=walkable({x:x*step,y:y*step},count)?1:0;const start=spawn(0),idx=Math.round(start.y/step)*w+Math.round(start.x/step),queue=[idx];mask[idx]=2;for(let q=0;q<queue.length;q++){const i=queue[q],x=i%w,y=Math.floor(i/w);for(const [nx,ny] of [[x+1,y],[x-1,y],[x,y+1],[x,y-1]]){if(nx<0||nx>=w||ny<0||ny>=h)continue;const ni=ny*w+nx;if(mask[ni]===1){mask[ni]=2;queue.push(ni);}}}for(const p of [...m.stations,...ventsFor(count),EMERGENCY,REPAIR,securityConsole(count),communicationsConsole(count)]){assert.ok(walkable(p,count),JSON.stringify(p));let reachable=false;for(let dy=-1;dy<=1;dy++)for(let dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++){const q={x:(Math.round(p.x/step)+dx)*step,y:(Math.round(p.y/step)+dy)*step};if(mask[q.y/step*w+q.x/step]===2&&sight(p,q,count))reachable=true;}assert.ok(reachable,`${count} unreachable ${JSON.stringify(p)}`);}assert.ok(queue.length>1000);}
console.log('PASS: identical 50 Hz input across 30–360 Hz displays; held WASD/arrows, diagonal normalization; 12/16/20-room walkable connectivity to every mission, vent, repair and camera terminal.');

class Socket{static OPEN=1;static all=[];readyState=0;bufferedAmount=0;sent=[];constructor(){Socket.all.push(this);}send(value){this.sent.push(JSON.parse(value));}open(){this.readyState=1;this.onopen();}receive(value){this.onmessage({data:JSON.stringify(value)});}close(){this.readyState=3;this.onclose();}}

// teleported by writing field_positions (the owner would overwrite them); the attacker walks
// next to the victim with real inputs instead.

{const me=reconnected.frames.at(-1).frame.own,victim=peers[1].frames.at(-1).frame.own;assert.ok(Math.hypot(victim.x-me.x,victim.y-me.y)<90,'attacker reached the victim');}

const killed=await request(clients[0].key,'/api/field',{action:'kill',protocol:2,code,seq,warp:0,inputs:attackInputs,target:base[1].frame.own.id,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()});assert.ok(killed.frame.bodies.some(p=>p.id===base[1].frame.own.id));assert.equal(killed.frame.own.ack,last+1);
await pause(400);assert.ok(observer.frames.at(-1).frame.bodies.some(p=>p.id===base[1].frame.own.id),'live observers see authoritative kill');
await request(host,'/api/game',{action:'pause',code,seq});await pause(250);const frozen=reconnected.frames.at(-1).frame.own;
reconnected.socket.send(JSON.stringify({type:'input',seq,warp:0,inputs:[{n:last+2,dx:0,dy:1,ms:20}]}));await pause(250);assert.equal(reconnected.frames.at(-1).frame.own.y,frozen.y);

======================================================================
FILE: tests/live-protocol-multiplayer.mjs
======================================================================
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
// Live protocol added in v22: actions over the socket, requestId de-duplication across
// socket/HTTP, the movement lease (HTTP cannot move a live-owned player), input-gap resend,
// and D1 query-budget recycling (set LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET=40 to exercise the last part).
// Runs inside one Miniflare isolate: it checks protocol behaviour, not distributed timing.
const origin='http://terminal.local:4173',total=6;
const token=()=>crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll('-','')+crypto.randomUUID().replaceAll('-','');
const pause=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
async function request(key,path,body,status=200){const res=await fetch(origin+path,{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});const data=await res.json();assert.equal(res.status,status,JSON.stringify(data).slice(0,300));return data;}
const host=token();let r=await request(host,'/api/game',{action:'create',config:{mode:'field',count:total,discussion:300}});const code=r.code;
const clients=Array.from({length:total},(_,i)=>({key:token(),name:'규약'+i,avatar:i%16}));
for(const c of clients)await request(c.key,'/api/game',{action:'join',code,...c});
await Promise.all(clients.map(c=>request(c.key,'/api/game',{action:'ready',code,ready:true})));
r=await request(host,'/api/game',{action:'start',code,seq:r.seq});r=await request(host,'/api/game',{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq});const seq=r.seq;
const base=await Promise.all(clients.map(c=>request(c.key,'/api/field',{action:'frame',protocol:2,inputs:[],warp:0,code,seq})));
const ids=base.map(b=>b.frame.own.id);
// Make player 0 an impostor next to crewmate 1; the live isolate adopts the new version.
const saved=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT state FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=?').bind(code).first(),state=JSON.parse(saved.state);
state.players.forEach((p,i)=>{p.role=i===0?'impostor':'crewmate';});state.field.cooldowns={};
await workerDb.prepare('UPDATE mafia_rooms SET state=?,version=version+1 WHERE code=?').bind(JSON.stringify(state),code).run();
async function connect(key){const res=await fetch(origin+'/api/live',{headers:{Upgrade:'websocket'}});assert.equal(res.status,101);const socket=res.webSocket;socket.accept();const frames=[],messages=[];let ready=null;
 socket.addEventListener('message',e=>{const b=JSON.parse(e.data);messages.push(b);if(b.type==='ready')ready=b;if(b.type==='frame')frames.push(b);});
 socket.send(JSON.stringify({type:'auth',code,token:key}));for(let i=0;i<80&&!frames.length;i++)await pause(25);
 // Like the real client: a ping every 2 s (the server drops connections silent for 12 s).
 const ping=setInterval(()=>{if(socket.readyState===1)socket.send(JSON.stringify({type:'ping',sent:performance.now()}));else clearInterval(ping);},2000);assert.ok(ready&&frames.length,'authenticated frame stream');
 const act=(body,id=crypto.randomUUID())=>new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const started=performance.now(),timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(Error('act timeout')),4000);const on=e=>{const b=JSON.parse(e.data);if(b.type==='actResult'&&b.id===id){clearTimeout(timer);socket.removeEventListener('message',on);resolve({...b,ms:performance.now()-started});}};socket.addEventListener('message',on);socket.send(JSON.stringify({type:'act',id,body}));});
 return {socket,frames,messages,ready,act};}
const a=await connect(clients[0].key),b=await connect(clients[1].key),c=await connect(clients[2].key),watcher=await connect(host);
for(let i=0;i<60&&a.frames.at(-1).room?.myRole!=='impostor'&&!a.frames.some(f=>f.room?.myRole==='impostor');i++)await pause(25);
assert.ok(a.frames.some(f=>f.room?.myRole==='impostor'),'live isolate adopted the role change');
// 1) Attack over the socket: confirmed in the reply, applied once, seen by observers.
const requestId=crypto.randomUUID(),kill={action:'kill',protocol:2,code,seq,warp:0,inputs:[],target:ids[1],requestId};
const result=await a.act(kill);assert.equal(result.ok,true,result.error);assert.ok(result.frame.bodies.some(x=>x.id===ids[1]),'kill confirmed in actResult');
console.log(`live kill request→confirm ${Math.round(result.ms)}ms (single local isolate)`);
// Same requestId over HTTP (client fallback after a lost reply) is accepted without a second effect.
const before=(await workerDb.prepare('SELECT state FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=?').bind(code).first()).state;
const replay=await request(clients[0].key,'/api/field',kill);assert.ok(replay.frame.bodies.filter(x=>x.id===ids[1]).length===1);
const after=JSON.parse((await workerDb.prepare('SELECT state FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=?').bind(code).first()).state);
assert.equal(after.field.bodies.filter(x=>x.id===ids[1]).length,1,'no duplicate body');assert.equal(JSON.parse(before).field.cooldowns[ids[0]],after.field.cooldowns[ids[0]],'cooldown not reset by replay');
// A refused action is reported as an error, not a transport failure.
const refused=await a.act({...kill,requestId:crypto.randomUUID()});assert.equal(refused.ok,false);assert.ok(typeof refused.error==='string'&&refused.error.length>0);
for(let i=0;i<40&&!c.frames.at(-1).frame.bodies.some(x=>x.id===ids[1]);i++)await pause(25);
assert.ok(c.frames.at(-1).frame.bodies.some(x=>x.id===ids[1]),'other live player sees the body');
assert.ok(watcher.frames.at(-1).frame.bodies.some(x=>x.id===ids[1]),'host sees the body');
// 2) Movement lease: HTTP cannot move a player whose socket owns the movement.
const own=c.frames.at(-1).frame.own,owned=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT owner,x,y FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?').bind(code,ids[2]).first();
assert.ok(owned.owner.startsWith('live:'),'live connection holds the lease');
const steps=Array.from({length:10},(_,i)=>({n:(own.ack||0)+i+1,dx:1,dy:0,ms:20}));
const http=await request(clients[2].key,'/api/field',{action:'frame',protocol:2,base:own,inputs:steps,warp:0,code,seq,count:total,mapId:'aurora'});
assert.notEqual(http.transport,'checkpoint','HTTP checkpoint refused while leased');
const row=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT owner,x FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?').bind(code,ids[2]).first();assert.equal(row.x,owned.x);assert.ok(row.owner.startsWith('live:'));
// 3) Input gap: the server asks for the missing inputs instead of skipping them.
c.socket.send(JSON.stringify({type:'input',seq,warp:0,inputs:[{n:(own.ack||0)+5,dx:1,dy:0,ms:20}]}));
for(let i=0;i<40&&!c.messages.some(m=>m.type==='resend');i++)await pause(25);
const resend=c.messages.find(m=>m.type==='resend');assert.ok(resend,'resend requested');assert.equal(resend.after,own.ack||0);
c.socket.send(JSON.stringify({type:'input',seq,warp:0,inputs:steps}));
for(let i=0;i<60&&(c.frames.at(-1).frame.own.ack||0)<steps.at(-1).n;i++)await pause(25);
const moved=c.frames.at(-1).frame.own;assert.equal(moved.ack,steps.at(-1).n);assert.ok(moved.x>own.x+20,'resent inputs applied in order');
// 4) After the socket closes and the lease expires, HTTP movement works again.
c.socket.close();await pause(2700);
const fresh=await request(clients[2].key,'/api/field',{action:'frame',protocol:2,inputs:[],warp:0,code,seq});
const next=Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>({n:(fresh.frame.own.ack||0)+i+1,dx:0,dy:1,ms:20}));
const resumed=await request(clients[2].key,'/api/field',{action:'frame',protocol:2,base:fresh.frame.own,inputs:next,warp:0,code,seq,count:total,mapId:'aurora'});
assert.equal(resumed.transport,'checkpoint','HTTP movement resumes after the lease');assert.equal(resumed.frame.own.ack,next.at(-1).n);
// 5) Query-budget recycling: the server asks for a replacement socket before the budget runs out.
if(Number(process.env.LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET)>=20){
 // ~3 queries/s per connection here (3 connections share ~9/s), so 40 is reached within ~15 s.
 for(let i=0;i<500&&!a.messages.some(m=>m.type==='recycle')&&!b.messages.some(m=>m.type==='recycle')&&!watcher.messages.some(m=>m.type==='recycle');i++)await pause(50);
 const retiring=[a,b,watcher].find(p=>p.messages.some(m=>m.type==='recycle'));assert.ok(retiring,'recycle requested');
 const key=retiring===a?clients[0].key:retiring===b?clients[1].key:host;const replacement=await connect(key);
 assert.ok(replacement.frames.length,'replacement socket streams frames');
 if(retiring!==watcher)assert.equal(replacement.ready.ack,retiring.frames.at(-1).frame.own.ack,'ack carried over');
 for(let i=0;i<40&&retiring.socket.readyState===1;i++)await pause(250);assert.notEqual(retiring.socket.readyState,1,'old socket closed after handover');
 replacement.socket.close();console.log('recycle handover verified');
}
// 6) A room change made through another request (host calls a meeting over HTTP) reaches
// every live connection. Workers forbid writing to a socket from another request's context,
// so each connection's own pump must deliver it (found with a real browser in v22 work).
const live=[a,b,watcher].filter(p=>p.socket.readyState===1);const gameState=await fetch(origin+'/api/game?code='+code,{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+host}}).then(r=>r.json());
await request(host,'/api/game',{action:'next',code,seq:gameState.seq});
for(let i=0;i<60&&!live.every(p=>p.frames.some(f=>f.room&&f.room.phase!=='field'));i++)await pause(50);
assert.ok(live.length>=2&&live.every(p=>p.frames.some(f=>f.room&&f.room.phase!=='field')),'meeting reaches every live connection');
for(const p of [a,b,watcher])if(p.socket.readyState===1)p.socket.close();
console.log('PASS: live actions with confirmation, requestId de-dup across socket and HTTP, refused-action errors, live/host body visibility, movement lease, input-gap resend, lease expiry fallback, room changes reach every connection'+(Number(process.env.LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET)>=20?', query-budget recycle.':'.'));

import {mapFor,walkable,spawn,ventsFor,EMERGENCY,REPAIR} from '../lib/map.ts';
for(const count of [6,16,20,22,29]){
 const map=mapFor(count),step=20,start=spawn(0),key=(x,y)=>`${x},${y}`,seen=new Set([key(start.x,start.y)]),queue=[start];
 for(let i=0;i<queue.length;i++){const p=queue[i];for(const [dx,dy] of [[step,0],[-step,0],[0,step],[0,-step]]){const x=p.x+dx,y=p.y+dy,k=key(x,y);if(!seen.has(k)&&walkable({x,y},count)){seen.add(k);queue.push({x,y});}}}
 for(const p of [...map.stations,...ventsFor(count),EMERGENCY,REPAIR])assert.ok(queue.some(q=>Math.hypot(p.x-q.x,p.y-q.y)<30),`unreachable: ${count} ${p.id||JSON.stringify(p)}`);
 for(const vent of ventsFor(count)){assert.ok(vent.links.length>=1);for(const id of vent.links){const to=ventsFor(count).find(v=>v.id===id);assert.equal(to.network,vent.network);assert.ok(to.links.includes(vent.id));}}
 assert.equal(new Set(map.stations.map(s=>s.kind)).size,12);assert.equal(map.stations.length,map.locations.length*3);
 console.log(`PASS: ${count} players, ${map.locations.length} rooms, ${map.stations.length} consoles, ${map.tasksPerPlayer} tasks each; all consoles/vents connected through walkable corridors (${seen.size} grid points).`);
}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {stepInput,reconcileMotion,consumeInputs} from '../lib/motion.ts';
import {distance,spawn} from '../lib/map.ts';
let clock=100000,server={...spawn(0),ack:0,clock},local=spawn(0),pending=[],n=0,inflight=null,nextSend=clock,corrections=0,replies=0;
for(let tick=0;tick<900;tick++){clock+=20;const elapsed=tick*20;const dir=elapsed<2000?[0,-1]:elapsed<4800?[-1,0]:elapsed<7500?[0,1]:elapsed<10500?[1,0]:[0,0];const input={n:++n,dx:dir[0],dy:dir[1],ms:20};pending.push(input);local=stepInput(local,input,29);
if(inflight?.stage==='request'&&clock>=inflight.arrive){server=consumeInputs(server,inflight.batch,clock,29);inflight={...inflight,stage:'response',snapshot:{...server},arrive:clock+180+(tick%9)*45};}
if(inflight?.stage==='response'&&clock>=inflight.arrive){const before={...local},result=reconcileMotion(inflight.snapshot,pending,29);pending=result.pending;local=result.position;corrections=Math.max(corrections,distance(before,local));replies++;inflight=null;nextSend=clock+150;}
if(!inflight&&clock>=nextSend)inflight={stage:'request',batch:[...pending],arrive:clock+120+(tick%7)*55};}
assert.ok(replies>=12);assert.ok(corrections<.2,`rubber band correction ${corrections}`);
const duplicate=consumeInputs(server,[{n:server.ack,dx:1,dy:0,ms:50}],clock,29);assert.equal(duplicate.ack,server.ack);assert.equal(duplicate.x,server.x);
const speed=consumeInputs({...spawn(0),ack:0,clock},Array.from({length:200},(_,i)=>({n:i+1,dx:1,dy:0,ms:50})),clock,29);assert.ok(speed.x-spawn(0).x<=21);assert.equal(speed.ack,2);
assert.equal(consumeInputs(server,[{n:server.ack+2,dx:1,dy:0,ms:20}],clock,29).ack,server.ack);
console.log(`PASS: ${replies} delayed round trips (300–1000 ms), turns and wall collisions, maximum reconciliation displacement ${corrections.toFixed(3)} px, duplicate/reordered input rejection, speed budget.`);

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {movementKey,movementVector,inputSteps} from '../lib/input.ts';
import {mapFor,walkable,spawn,ventsFor,EMERGENCY,REPAIR,emergencyFor,repairFor,distance,sight} from '../lib/map.ts';
import {securityConsole,communicationsConsole} from '../lib/surveillance.ts';
assert.ok(movementKey('KeyW'));assert.equal(movementKey('KeyQ'),false);const held=new Set(['KeyW','ArrowUp']);assert.deepEqual(movementVector(held),{x:0,y:-1});held.add('KeyD');assert.ok(Math.abs(Math.hypot(...Object.values(movementVector(held)))-1)<1e-10);held.delete('KeyW');held.delete('ArrowUp');assert.deepEqual(movementVector(held),{x:1,y:0});
for(const fps of [30,60,120,144,240,360]){let remainder=0,total=0,queued=0,max=0;for(let i=0;i<fps*10;i++){const batch=inputSteps(remainder,1000/fps);remainder=batch.remainder;total+=batch.steps;queued+=batch.steps;max=Math.max(max,queued);if((i+1)%fps===0)queued=0;}assert.equal(total,500,`${fps} Hz`);assert.ok(max<=50);}
// Flood-fill actual walkable floor, including inset room walls, for every active map size.
for(const count of [6,16,29].map(count=>({count,mapId:"nereid"}))){const m=mapFor(count),step=20,w=Math.ceil(m.width/step),h=Math.ceil(m.height/step),mask=new Uint8Array(w*h);for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++)mask[y*w+x]=walkable({x:x*step,y:y*step},count)?1:0;const start=spawn(0,count),idx=Math.round(start.y/step)*w+Math.round(start.x/step),queue=[idx];mask[idx]=2;for(let q=0;q<queue.length;q++){const i=queue[q],x=i%w,y=Math.floor(i/w);for(const [nx,ny] of [[x+1,y],[x-1,y],[x,y+1],[x,y-1]]){if(nx<0||nx>=w||ny<0||ny>=h)continue;const ni=ny*w+nx;if(mask[ni]===1){mask[ni]=2;queue.push(ni);}}}for(const p of [...m.stations,...ventsFor(count),emergencyFor(count),repairFor(count),securityConsole(count),communicationsConsole(count)]){assert.ok(walkable(p,count),JSON.stringify(p));let reachable=false;for(let dy=-1;dy<=1;dy++)for(let dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++){const q={x:(Math.round(p.x/step)+dx)*step,y:(Math.round(p.y/step)+dy)*step};if(mask[q.y/step*w+q.x/step]===2&&sight(p,q,count))reachable=true;}assert.ok(reachable,`${count} unreachable ${JSON.stringify(p)}`);}assert.ok(queue.length>1000);}
console.log('PASS NEREID: identical 50 Hz input across 30–360 Hz displays; held WASD/arrows, diagonal normalization; 12/16/20-room walkable connectivity to every mission, vent, repair and camera terminal.');

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
const origin='http://terminal.local:4173',token=()=>Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
async function req(key,body,game=false,status=200){const res=await fetch(origin+(game?'/api/game':'/api/field'),{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});const data=await res.json();assert.equal(res.status,status,JSON.stringify(data));return data;}
const host=token();let r=await req(host,{action:'create',config:{mode:'field',count:29,mapId:'nereid'}},true);const code=r.code,users=Array.from({length:29},(_,i)=>({key:token(),name:'이동'+i,avatar:i%16}));
await Promise.all(users.map(u=>req(u.key,{action:'join',code,...u},true)));await Promise.all(users.map(u=>req(u.key,{action:'ready',ready:true,code},true)));r=await req(host,{action:'start',code,seq:r.seq},true);r=await req(host,{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq},true);
const frames=await Promise.all(users.map(u=>req(u.key,{action:'frame',code,seq:r.seq,protocol:2,inputs:[],warp:0})));
await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,130));
const payload=(i,base=frames[i].frame.own)=>({action:'frame',code,seq:r.seq,count:29,mapId:'nereid',protocol:2,warp:0,base,inputs:Array.from({length:5},(_,n)=>({n:base.ack+n+1,dx:1,dy:0,ms:20}))});
const moved=await Promise.all(users.map((u,i)=>req(u.key,payload(i))));assert.ok(moved.every(f=>f.transport==='checkpoint'));moved.forEach((f,i)=>{assert.equal(f.frame.own.ack,5);assert.ok(Math.abs(f.frame.own.x-frames[i].frame.own.x-20.5)<.2);});
assert.ok(moved.every(f=>!Object.hasOwn(f.frame.own,'history')),'movement history must stay server-side');
const stored=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT history FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?').bind(code,moved[0].frame.own.id).first();assert.ok(JSON.parse(stored.history).length>=1);
const wrongMap=await req(users[28].key,{...payload(28,moved[28].frame.own),mapId:'aurora'});assert.equal(wrongMap.transport,'sync','map mismatch cannot use fast checkpoint');
const checkpoint=moved[0].frame.own,rowsBefore=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT * FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?').bind(code,checkpoint.id).first();
for(let i=0;i<5;i++)await req(users[0].key,{...payload(0,checkpoint),inputs:[]});
const rowsAfter=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT * FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?').bind(code,checkpoint.id).first();assert.deepEqual(rowsAfter,rowsBefore,'idle frames must not write positions');
const forged=await req(users[0].key,{...payload(0),base:{...checkpoint,x:4000,y:2000,clock:Date.now()-100000},inputs:[{n:6,dx:0,dy:0,ms:20}]});assert.equal(forged.transport,'sync');assert.ok(forged.frame.own.x<1800);
const victimBefore=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT * FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?').bind(code,moved[1].frame.own.id).first();await req(users[0].key,payload(1,moved[1].frame.own));const victimAfter=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT * FROM field_positions WHERE code=? AND player=?').bind(code,moved[1].frame.own.id).first();assert.deepEqual(victimBefore,victimAfter,'checkpoint must belong to authenticated player');
const f=await req(users[2].key,{...payload(2),inputs:[]});const concurrent=payload(2,f.frame.own);const [a,b]=await Promise.all([req(users[2].key,concurrent),req(users[2].key,concurrent)]);assert.equal(a.frame.own.ack,b.frame.own.ack);assert.equal(a.frame.own.x,b.frame.own.x);
await req(host,{action:'pause',code,seq:r.seq},true);const paused=await req(users[3].key,payload(3,moved[3].frame.own));assert.equal(paused.transport,'sync');assert.equal(paused.frame.own.x,moved[3].frame.own.x);
await req(host,{action:'pause',code,seq:r.seq},true);r=await req(host,{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq},true);const meeting=await req(users[4].key,payload(4,moved[4].frame.own));assert.equal(meeting.transport,'sync');assert.equal(meeting.frame.own.x,moved[4].frame.own.x);
console.log('PASS: 29 clients use one-roundtrip verified movement; idle frames produce zero position writes; forged coordinates/clock and cross-player checkpoints rejected; duplicate input exactly once; pause and meeting gates.');

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {updatePresence,retainedPeers,presenceOpacity} from '../lib/peer-presence.ts';
import {pushRemote,sampleRemote} from '../lib/remote-motion.ts';
import {drawCharacter} from '../lib/characters.ts';
const cache={},peer={id:'p1',x:1120,y:940,alive:true,at:10000,warp:0},own={x:1100,y:940};
updatePresence(cache,[peer],0);let track=pushRemote(undefined,peer,0);sampleRemote(track,0,16);

// walking speed x1.3 for 250 ms (66 px) -- but along the interpolated path, never snapping to the raw packet.

======================================================================
FILE: tests/remote-idle-start.mjs
======================================================================
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {pushRemote,sampleRemote} from '../lib/remote-motion.ts';
import {distance,WORLD} from '../lib/map.ts';
// An actor that stood still for a while is first seen with its old motion timestamp. When it
// starts walking, observers must show the walk with the normal delay, not 1-2 s late
// (found in the 29-isolate simulator: attacks then failed until the delay drained).
const offset=5000; // receive clock = server clock + offset (unknown to the client)
let track,last=null,maxStep=0;
const idleSince=10000-1600; // last move 1.6 s before the observer first sees it
const shown=[];
for(let now=10000;now<14000;now+=1000/60){
 const server=now; // samples arrive ~120 ms after the server stamped them
 if(Math.round(now)%100<17){
  const moving=server>=11000,at=moving?server-120:idleSince,x=moving?1000+(server-120-11000)*WORLD.speed/1000:1000;
  track=pushRemote(track,{x,y:900,at,warp:0,alive:true},now+offset);
 }
 if(!track)continue;
 const p=sampleRemote(track,now+offset,29);
 if(last)maxStep=Math.max(maxStep,distance(last,p));last=p;shown.push({now,delay:now-p.at,x:p.x});
}
const after=shown.filter(s=>s.now>11800);
assert.ok(after.every(s=>s.delay<450),`delay after the actor started walking: max ${Math.round(Math.max(...after.map(s=>s.delay)))} ms`);
assert.ok(maxStep<=WORLD.speed*1.3/60+.5,`no visible jump (max ${maxStep.toFixed(2)} px/frame)`);
console.log(`PASS: idle actor starts walking; delay ${Math.round(Math.min(...after.map(s=>s.delay)))}–${Math.round(Math.max(...after.map(s=>s.delay)))} ms after 0.8 s, max step ${maxStep.toFixed(2)} px/frame.`);

FILE: tests/remote-jitter.mjs

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {pushRemote,sampleRemote} from '../lib/remote-motion.ts';
import {rewindTarget} from '../lib/lag-compensation.ts';
import {distance} from '../lib/map.ts';
const start=100000,delays=[80,180,350,100,450,140],packets=[];
for(let t=0;t<=10000;t+=100)packets.push({arrive:t+delays[t/100%delays.length],sample:{x:1200+140*Math.sin(t/700),y:940+100*Math.cos(t/700),at:start+t,warp:0,alive:true}});
packets.sort((a,b)=>a.arrive-b.arrive);let tr,index=0,prev,maxJump=0,stalled=0,frames=0;
for(let t=0;t<10000;t+=1000/60){while(index<packets.length&&packets[index].arrive<=t){const p=packets[index++];tr=pushRemote(tr,p.sample,p.arrive);}if(!tr)continue;const point=sampleRemote(tr,t,29);if(prev&&t>1000){maxJump=Math.max(maxJump,distance(prev,point));if(distance(prev,point)<.05)stalled++;frames++;}prev=point;}
assert.ok(maxJump<=4.5,`packet teleport ${maxJump}`);assert.ok(stalled/frames<.15,`stalled ${stalled}/${frames}`);
const s={x:1160,y:940,at:start+1000,seq:2,warp:0,history:JSON.stringify([{x:1080,y:940,at:start+500,seq:2,warp:0}])};
assert.equal(Math.round(rewindTarget(s,start+600,start+1100,29).x),1096);
assert.equal(rewindTarget(s,start+300,start+1100,29),null);assert.equal(rewindTarget({...s,warp:1},start+600,start+1100,29),null);assert.equal(rewindTarget(s,start+1300,start+1100,29),null);
console.log(`PASS: observer rendering with 80–450 ms jitter, reordered packets and turns; max frame movement ${maxJump.toFixed(2)}px; stalls ${(stalled/frames*100).toFixed(1)}%; bounded server-only history rewind rejects expired/future/warp history.`);


======================================================================
FILE: tests/remote-low-fps.mjs
======================================================================
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {pushRemote,sampleRemote} from '../lib/remote-motion.ts';
import {distance,WORLD} from '../lib/map.ts';
// A slow device (7.5 fps, measured in headless Chromium) must still show a walking actor at
// walking speed. With a per-frame cap the actor fell behind and the delay kept growing.
// Back and forth across the open hall centre at walking speed (164 px legs).
const xAt=ms=>{const d=(ms*WORLD.speed/1000)%328;return 1128+(d<164?d:328-d);};
let track,last=null;const delays=[],steps=[];
for(let now=0;now<8000;now+=133){
 // Server samples every 50 ms, received 120 ms later; delivered in the frame after they arrive.
 for(let s=Math.ceil((now-133-120)/50)*50;s+120<=now;s+=50){if(s<0||s+120<=now-133)continue;track=pushRemote(track,{x:xAt(s),y:940,at:10000+s,warp:0,alive:true},s+120);}
 if(!track)continue;const p=sampleRemote(track,now,29);if(last&&now>2000){steps.push(distance(last,p));delays.push(10000+now-p.at);}last=p;
}
const avg=steps.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/steps.length,expected=WORLD.speed*.133;
assert.ok(Math.abs(avg-expected)<expected*.15,`average step ${avg.toFixed(1)}px vs ${expected.toFixed(1)}px at 7.5 fps`);
assert.ok(Math.max(...delays)-Math.min(...delays)<120,`render delay stays bounded (${Math.round(Math.min(...delays))}-${Math.round(Math.max(...delays))} ms)`);
console.log(`PASS: 7.5 fps observer shows walking speed (avg ${avg.toFixed(1)} px/frame), delay ${Math.round(Math.min(...delays))}-${Math.round(Math.max(...delays))} ms.`);

// Offline geometry inspection with depth buffering (no browser or WebGL needed).
import * as T from 'three';
import {createDoll} from '../lib/doll-character.ts';
import {writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';
const scene=new T.Scene(),camera=new T.OrthographicCamera(-2.1,2.1,1.4,-1.4,.1,20);
camera.position.set(0,1.35,-6);camera.lookAt(0,.96,0);camera.updateMatrixWorld();
for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const d=createDoll(0);d.root.position.x=(i-1)*1.35;d.root.rotation.y=[0,-.65,-1.57][i];scene.add(d.root);}
scene.updateMatrixWorld(true);const triangles=[];
scene.traverse(o=>{if(!o.isMesh||!o.visible||!o.parent.visible||o.material.transparent)return;const g=o.geometry.index?o.geometry.toNonIndexed():o.geometry,p=g.attributes.position,normal=g.attributes.normal;const nm=new T.Matrix3().getNormalMatrix(o.matrixWorld);
for(let i=0;i<p.count;i+=3){const v=[],n=new T.Vector3();for(let j=0;j<3;j++){const q=new T.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(p,i+j).applyMatrix4(o.matrixWorld).project(camera);v.push([(q.x+1)*420,(1-q.y)*280,q.z]);n.add(new T.Vector3().fromBufferAttribute(normal,i+j).applyMatrix3(nm));}n.normalize();const lit=o.material.isMeshBasicMaterial?1:.62+Math.max(0,n.dot(new T.Vector3(-.3,.6,-.8).normalize()))*.48,c=o.material.color.clone().multiplyScalar(lit).convertLinearToSRGB();triangles.push([v,[c.r*255,c.g*255,c.b*255]]);}});
writeFileSync('/workspace/doll-triangles.json',JSON.stringify(triangles));
