import * as T from 'three';
import {SVGRenderer} from 'three/addons/renderers/SVGRenderer.js';
import {createDoll} from '../lib/doll-character.ts';
import {writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';
class Node{constructor(tag){this.tag=tag;this.attrs={};this.childNodes=[];this.style={};}setAttribute(k,v){this.attrs[k]=v;}appendChild(n){this.childNodes.push(n);}removeChild(n){this.childNodes=this.childNodes.filter(c=>c!==n);}get outerHTML(){return `<${this.tag} ${Object.entries(this.attrs).map(([k,v])=>`${k}="${v}"`).join(' ')}>${this.childNodes.map(n=>n.outerHTML).join('')}</${this.tag}>`;}}
globalThis.document={createElementNS:(_,tag)=>new Node(tag)};
const scene=new T.Scene();scene.add(new T.AmbientLight('#ffffff',.65));const light=new T.DirectionalLight('#fff2df',.85);light.position.set(-3,5,-5);scene.add(light);
for(let n=0;n<4;n++){const doll=createDoll([0,3,10,14][n]);doll.root.position.x=(n-1.5)*1.35;doll.root.rotation.y=-.12;scene.add(doll.root);}
const camera=new T.OrthographicCamera(-3,3,1.5,-1.5,.1,20);camera.position.set(0,1.4,-6);camera.lookAt(0,.95,0);const renderer=new SVGRenderer();renderer.setSize(1400,700);renderer.setPrecision(3);renderer.render(scene,camera);renderer.domElement.setAttribute('xmlns','http://www.w3.org/2000/svg');writeFileSync('/workspace/dolls-check.svg',renderer.domElement.outerHTML);

FILE: tests/role-draw.mjs

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {drawRoles} from '../lib/role-draw.ts';
import {newRoom,defaults,distribution,addPlayer,act,view} from '../lib/game.ts';
const counts=a=>Object.fromEntries([...new Set(a)].sort().map(r=>[r,a.filter(x=>x===r).length]));
for(const mode of ['classic','field'])for(let n=6;n<=29;n++){
 const r=newRoom('ABC234','host',{...defaults,mode,count:n});for(let i=0;i<n;i++)addPlayer(r,'key'+i,'학생'+i,i%16);
 let previous=[];const results=new Set();
 for(let round=0;round<30;round++){
  r.players.forEach(p=>p.ready=true);act(r,'host',{action:'start',seq:r.seq});const roles=r.players.map(p=>p.role);results.add(roles.join(','));const expected=Object.entries(distribution(n,mode)).flatMap(([r,c])=>Array(c).fill(r));assert.deepEqual(counts(roles),counts(expected));
  if(round){const old=counts(previous),fresh=counts(roles),minimum=Math.max(0,...Object.keys(fresh).map(role=>(old[role]||0)+fresh[role]-n));assert.equal(roles.filter((role,i)=>role===previous[i]).length,minimum);}
  assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(view(r,'key0')).includes('previousRole'));
  const stable=r.players.map(p=>p.role);view(r,'key1');assert.deepEqual(r.players.map(p=>p.role),stable);
  previous=roles;r.phase='finished';act(r,'host',{action:'reset',seq:r.seq});assert.deepEqual(r.players.map(p=>p.previousRole),previous);assert.ok(r.players.every(p=>p.role===null));
 }
 // Classic with 6 players is 3 special roles + 3 citizens: the minimum-repeat rule makes the
 // two halves swap every round, so only 2×3! = 12 allocations are reachable and >10 of 12 in
 // 30 rounds failed ~14% of runs. The bound below still rejects a fixed or 2-cycle draw.
 assert.ok(results.size>(mode==='classic'&&n===6?6:10),'fresh random allocation each game');
}
// Changed player count or role composition, and a mathematically unavoidable repeat.
assert.deepEqual(counts(drawRoles(['a','a','a','b'],['a','a','a','b'])),{a:3,b:1});
for(let i=0;i<100;i++){const p=['a','a','a','b'],v=drawRoles(p,p);assert.equal(v.filter((r,j)=>r===p[j]).length,2);assert.equal(drawRoles(['a','b','c'],['a',null,'b']).length,3);}
console.log('PASS: both modes, 6–29 players, 30 consecutive rounds each; exact counts, minimum possible repeats, randomized assignments, reset persistence, unchanged active roles and private history.');

FILE: tests/roles-expanded.mjs

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {newRoom,defaults,addPlayer,act,next,view} from '../lib/game.ts';
import {fieldAct,fieldTick,fieldSnapshot,startMeeting,resumeField} from '../lib/field.ts';
import {fieldDistribution,isImpostor} from '../lib/field-roles.ts';
import {spawn} from '../lib/map.ts';
const now=1000000;
function setup(){const r=newRoom('ABC234','host',{...defaults,count:29},now);for(let i=0;i<29;i++){addPlayer(r,'k'+i,'학생'+i,i%16);act(r,'k'+i,{action:'ready',ready:true},now);}act(r,'host',{action:'start',seq:r.seq},now);next(r,now);return r;}
const use=(r,p,b,ps,at=now+40000)=>fieldAct(r,p.key,{seq:r.seq,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),...b},ps,at);
const poses=r=>r.players.map(p=>({id:p.id,...spawn(0),at:now,seq:r.field.epoch,place:'중앙 홀',previous:'중앙 홀'}));
for(let n=6;n<=29;n++){const d=fieldDistribution(n);assert.equal(Object.values(d).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),n);assert.ok(Object.values(d).every(x=>x>=0));assert.equal(Object.entries(d).filter(([k])=>isImpostor(k)).reduce((a,[,b])=>a+b,0),n<=8?1:n<=12?2:n<=18?3:n<=24?4:5);if(n>=14)for(const role of ['investigator','judge','viper','shapeshifter','phantom'])assert.ok(d[role]>0);}
let r=setup(),v=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='viper'),d=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='investigator'),crew=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='crewmate'),noise=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='noisemaker'),ps=poses(r);
use(r,v,{action:'kill',target:noise.id},ps);assert.equal(noise.alive,false);assert.equal(r.field.bodies[0].dissolveAt,now+52000);assert.equal(view(r,d.key).field.cases.length,0);assert.equal(view(r,crew.key).field.cases.length,0);assert.ok(fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,ps,now+41000).alerts.length);assert.equal(fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,ps,now+52001).bodies.length,0);assert.throws(()=>use(r,crew,{action:'report',target:noise.id},ps,now+52001));
// Pause extends dissolution and all expired bodies disappear authoritatively.
act(r,'host',{action:'pause',seq:r.seq},now+41000);act(r,'host',{action:'pause',seq:r.seq},now+46000);assert.equal(r.field.bodies[0].dissolveAt,now+57000);fieldTick(r,now+57001);assert.equal(r.field.bodies.length,0);
startMeeting(r,'테스트 회의',now+58000);let file=view(r,d.key).field.cases[0];assert.ok(file);assert.ok(!JSON.stringify(view(r,crew.key)).includes('locations'));assert.equal(view(r,crew.key).field.cases.length,0);
act(r,d.key,{action:'caseNote',seq:r.seq,caseId:file.id,memo:'현장 목격과 증언 비교'},now+58001);assert.equal(view(r,d.key).field.cases[0].memo,'현장 목격과 증언 비교');assert.throws(()=>act(r,crew.key,{action:'caseNote',seq:r.seq,caseId:file.id,memo:'침입'},now+58002));
resumeField(r,now+60000);ps=poses(r);assert.throws(()=>use(r,d,{action:'skill',caseId:file.id,target:v.id},ps,now+61000));use(r,d,{action:'skill',caseId:file.id,target:v.id},ps,now+76000);file=view(r,d.key).field.cases[0];assert.equal(file.answers[0].place,'중앙 홀');assert.equal(file.answers[0].name,v.name);assert.equal(Object.keys(file.answers[0]).sort().join(','),'id,name,place');assert.throws(()=>use(r,d,{action:'skill',caseId:file.id,target:v.id},ps,now+97000));
const suspects=r.players.filter(p=>p.alive&&p.id!==d.id&&p.id!==v.id);for(let i=0;i<2;i++)use(r,d,{action:'skill',caseId:file.id,target:suspects[i].id},ps,now+98000+i*21000);assert.throws(()=>use(r,d,{action:'skill',caseId:file.id,target:suspects[2].id},ps,now+150000));
// Judge is task-gated; only the first valid simultaneous judgment spends a charge.
for(const wrong of [false,true]){r=setup();const judge=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='judge'),j2=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='crewmate');j2.role='judge';const target=r.players.find(p=>wrong?p.role==='engineer':isImpostor(p.role));startMeeting(r,'회의',now+40000);next(r,now+41000);assert.throws(()=>act(r,judge.key,{action:'judge',seq:r.seq,target:target.id},now+42000));for(const j of [judge,j2])r.field.tasks[j.id].slice(0,3).forEach(t=>t.done=true);act(r,judge.key,{action:'judge',seq:r.seq,target:target.id},now+42000);act(r,j2.key,{action:'judge',seq:r.seq,target:target.id},now+42001);assert.equal(view(r,j2.key).field.judge.pending,target.id);assert.throws(()=>act(r,judge.key,{action:'judge',seq:r.seq,target:target.id},now+42002));next(r,now+43000);assert.equal(judge.used,true);assert.equal(j2.used,false);assert.equal(j2.alive,true);assert.equal(judge.alive,!wrong);assert.equal(target.alive,wrong);assert.equal(r.candidate,wrong?judge.id:target.id);}
console.log('PASS: 6–29 role balance; Viper decay/report/pause and Noisemaker; private meeting cases, historical interrogation, limit/cooldown; task-gated Judge success/misjudgment, simultaneous refund.');

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
const origin='http://terminal.local:4173',token=()=>Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
async function request(key,body,game=true,status=200){const res=await fetch(origin+(game?'/api/game':'/api/field'),{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});const data=await res.json();assert.equal(res.status,status,JSON.stringify(data));return data;}
const host=token();let r=await request(host,{action:'create',config:{mode:'field',count:6}});const code=r.code,users=Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>({key:token(),name:'직업'+i,avatar:i}));const joins=await Promise.all(users.map(u=>request(u.key,{action:'join',code,...u})));await Promise.all(users.map(u=>request(u.key,{action:'ready',code,ready:true})));r=await request(host,{action:'start',code,seq:r.seq});r=await request(host,{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq});
const update=async fn=>{const saved=await workerDb.prepare('SELECT state FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=?').bind(code).first(),s=JSON.parse(saved.state);fn(s);await workerDb.prepare('UPDATE mafia_rooms SET state=?,version=version+1 WHERE code=?').bind(JSON.stringify(s),code).run();};
await update(s=>{for(let i=0;i<s.players.length;i++)s.players.find(p=>p.id===joins[i].me).role=['viper','investigator','judge','noisemaker','crewmate','crewmate'][i];s.field.cooldowns={};s.field.skillCooldowns={};s.field.warps=Object.fromEntries(s.players.map(p=>[p.id,{x:1000,y:900,at:Date.now()}]));});
const shot=await request(users[0].key,{action:'kill',code,seq:r.seq,target:joins[3].me},false);assert.equal(shot.frame.effects.at(-1).acid,true);assert.ok(shot.frame.bodies[0].dissolveAt);assert.equal(shot.room.field.cases.length,0);
await update(s=>{s.field.bodies[0].dissolveAt=Date.now()-1;});const vanished=await request(users[4].key,{action:'frame',code,seq:r.seq},false);assert.equal(vanished.frame.bodies.length,0);await request(users[4].key,{action:'report',code,seq:r.seq,target:joins[3].me},false,400);
r=await request(host,{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq});const detective=await request(users[1].key,{action:'frame',code,seq:r.seq},false),file=detective.room.field.cases[0];assert.ok(file);const note=await request(users[1].key,{action:'caseNote',code,seq:r.seq,caseId:file.id,memo:'비공개 사건 메모'});assert.equal(note.field.cases[0].memo,'비공개 사건 메모');await request(users[4].key,{action:'caseNote',code,seq:r.seq,caseId:file.id,memo:'위조'},true,400);
r=await request(host,{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq});await request(users[2].key,{action:'judge',code,seq:r.seq,target:joins[0].me},true,400);await update(s=>{s.field.tasks[joins[2].me].slice(0,3).forEach(t=>t.done=true);});const judged=await request(users[2].key,{action:'judge',code,seq:r.seq,target:joins[0].me});assert.equal(judged.field.judge.pending,joins[0].me);r=await request(host,{action:'next',code,seq:r.seq});assert.equal(r.winner,'town');assert.equal(r.players.find(p=>p.id===joins[0].me).alive,false);
console.log('PASS: compiled Worker + D1 acid kill/expiry/report rejection, private detective cases/notes and forged access rejection, task-gated Judge resolution/victory.');


======================================================================
FILE: tests/run-all.mjs
======================================================================
// Runs every automated test: unit tests directly, HTTP/WebSocket tests through the built
// Worker in Miniflare (needs `pnpm run build` first). Cross-platform: `node tests/run-all.mjs`.
// Not included: tests/sim (simulator, see tests/sim/README.md), tests/browser (needs a running
// server and Playwright) and render-doll*.mjs (old offline model renders).
import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {readdirSync,existsSync} from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
const root=path.resolve(import.meta.dirname,'..');
const HTTP=['multiplayer.mjs','field-multiplayer.mjs','security-multiplayer.mjs','latency-multiplayer.mjs','roles-multiplayer.mjs','speech-multiplayer.mjs','checkpoint-multiplayer.mjs','first-person-multiplayer.mjs','nereid-multiplayer.mjs','solo-multiplayer.mjs','live-multiplayer.mjs','live-protocol-multiplayer.mjs'];
const unit=readdirSync(path.join(root,'tests')).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.mjs')&&!HTTP.includes(f)&&!f.startsWith('render-doll')&&f!=='run-all.mjs').sort();
const results=[];
const run=(label,args,env={})=>{const t=Date.now();const r=spawnSync(process.execPath,args,{cwd:root,env:{...process.env,...env},encoding:'utf8',timeout:600000});const ok=r.status===0;results.push({label,ok,ms:Date.now()-t});console.log(`${ok?'PASS':'FAIL'}  ${label}  (${((Date.now()-t)/1000).toFixed(1)}s)`);if(!ok)console.log((r.stdout+r.stderr).split('\n').filter(l=>!/^\s+at /.test(l)).slice(-12).join('\n'));};
for(const f of unit)run(f,['--no-warnings','--experimental-strip-types',path.join('tests',f)]);
if(!existsSync(path.join(root,'dist/server/index.js')))console.log('SKIP  HTTP tests: run `pnpm run build` first');
else{for(const f of HTTP)run(f+' (Miniflare)',['tests/worker.cjs'],{TEST_FILE:f});run('live-protocol-multiplayer.mjs (Miniflare, LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET=40)',['tests/worker.cjs'],{TEST_FILE:'live-protocol-multiplayer.mjs',LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET:'40'});}
const failed=results.filter(r=>!r.ok);console.log(`\n${results.length-failed.length}/${results.length} passed`);process.exit(failed.length?1:0);

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {SCIENCE_KINDS,scienceProblem} from '../lib/science.ts';
import {puzzleCorrect} from '../lib/puzzles.ts';
import {newRoom,defaults,addPlayer,act,next,view} from '../lib/game.ts';
import {fieldAct,progress,fieldWin} from '../lib/field.ts';
import {isCrew,isImpostor} from '../lib/field-roles.ts';
import {mapFor,spawn,walkable} from '../lib/map.ts';
for(const kind of SCIENCE_KINDS)for(let seed=0;seed<4;seed++){const p=scienceProblem(kind,seed);assert.ok(puzzleCorrect(kind,[seed],p.answer));assert.ok(!puzzleCorrect(kind,[seed],[99]));assert.ok(p.hint&&p.explanation);}
const now=1000000;
for(const count of [6,16,29]){
 const r=newRoom('ABC234','host',{...defaults,mode:'field',mapId:'nereid',count},now);
 for(let i=0;i<count;i++){addPlayer(r,'key'+i,'학생'+i,i%16);act(r,'key'+i,{action:'ready',ready:true},now);assert.ok(walkable(spawn(i,r.config),r.config));}
 act(r,'host',{action:'start',seq:r.seq},now);next(r,now);
 const map=mapFor(r.config),crew=r.players.find(p=>isCrew(p.role)),imp=r.players.find(p=>isImpostor(p.role));
 assert.ok(Object.values(r.field.tasks).every(ts=>ts.length===map.tasksPerPlayer&&ts.every(t=>map.stations.some(s=>s.id===t.station&&s.kind.startsWith('science_')))));
 const ps=r.players.map((p,i)=>({id:p.id,...spawn(i,r.config),at:now,seq:r.field.epoch,ack:0,clock:now,warp:0,place:'집결동',previous:'집결동'}));
 const action=(b,at=now+40000)=>fieldAct(r,crew.key,{seq:r.seq,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),...b},ps,at);
 for(const kind of SCIENCE_KINDS){const station=map.stations.find(s=>s.kind===kind);r.field.tasks[crew.id]=[{station:station.id,done:false}];Object.assign(ps.find(p=>p.id===crew.id),{x:station.x,y:station.y});
 action({action:'taskStart',station:station.id});const c=r.field.challenges[crew.id],answer=scienceProblem(kind,c.solution[0]).answer;
 assert.throws(()=>action({action:'taskFinish',station:station.id,nonce:'bad',answer},now+43000));
 assert.throws(()=>action({action:'taskFinish',station:station.id,nonce:c.nonce,answer:[99]},now+43000));
 action({action:'taskFinish',station:station.id,nonce:c.nonce,answer},now+43000);assert.ok(r.field.tasks[crew.id][0].done);
 }
 r.phase='vote';const iv=view(r,imp.key),cv=view(r,crew.key);assert.equal(iv.players.filter(p=>isImpostor(p.role)).length,r.players.filter(p=>isImpostor(p.role)).length);assert.equal(cv.players.filter(p=>isImpostor(p.role)).length,0);
 r.phase='field';for(const p of r.players.filter(p=>isCrew(p.role)))for(const t of r.field.tasks[p.id])t.done=true;
 assert.equal(progress(r).done,progress(r).total);fieldWin(r,now+50000);assert.equal(r.phase,'finished');assert.equal(r.winner,'town');
}
console.log('PASS: 48 science problem variants, wrong answers/nonces rejected, new-map 6/16/29-player science allocation and spawns, impostor teammate privacy, complete crew missions win.');

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {newRoom,defaults,addPlayer,act,next} from '../lib/game.ts';
import {fieldAct,fieldSnapshot,fieldTick,poseFor} from '../lib/field.ts';
import {securityConsole,communicationsConsole,camerasFor} from '../lib/surveillance.ts';
import {walkable} from '../lib/map.ts';
const now=1000000;
function setup(count=29){const r=newRoom('ABC234','host',{...defaults,count},now);for(let i=0;i<count;i++){addPlayer(r,'k'+i,'학생'+i,i%16);act(r,'k'+i,{action:'ready',ready:true},now);}act(r,'host',{action:'start',seq:r.seq},now);next(r,now);return r;}
const action=(r,p,b,poses,at=now+40000)=>fieldAct(r,p.key,{seq:r.seq,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),...b},poses,at);
for(const count of [6,10,16,20,29]){const r=setup(count),console=securityConsole(count);assert.ok(walkable(console,count));assert.ok(walkable(communicationsConsole(count),count));assert.equal(camerasFor(count).length,count<=10?4:count<=20?6:8);}
let r=setup(),crew=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='crewmate'),imp=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='impostor'),shifter=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='shapeshifter'),phantom=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='phantom'),cam=camerasFor(29)[0],terminal=securityConsole(29);
const poses=r.players.map(p=>({id:p.id,x:terminal.x,y:terminal.y,at:now,seq:r.field.epoch,ack:0,clock:now,warp:0,place:'보안실',previous:'중앙 홀'}));
Object.assign(poses.find(p=>p.id===imp.id),{x:cam.x,y:cam.y});
assert.throws(()=>action(r,imp,{action:'cameraStart'},poses));assert.equal(fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,poses,now+40000).cameras,null);
action(r,crew,{action:'cameraStart'},poses);let snap=fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,poses,now+40001);assert.equal(snap.cameras.length,8);assert.ok(snap.cameras[0].people.some(p=>p.id===imp.id));assert.ok(snap.activeCameras);assert.equal(snap.people.length,0);assert.equal(snap.bodies.length,0);assert.equal(fieldSnapshot(r,imp.key,poses,now+40001).cameras,null);assert.ok(fieldSnapshot(r,imp.key,poses,now+40001).activeCameras);
assert.throws(()=>action(r,crew,{action:'emergency'},poses));assert.throws(()=>action(r,crew,{action:'taskStart',station:r.field.tasks[crew.id][0].station},poses));
// Lighting does not affect camera vision, but only camera bounds are exposed.
r.field.lightsUntil=now+60000;snap=fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,poses,now+40002);assert.ok(snap.cameras[0].people.some(p=>p.id===imp.id));assert.ok(!snap.cameras[0].people.some(p=>p.id===crew.id));r.field.lightsUntil=0;
// Concealed players stay concealed; copied names and appearances remain copied.
for(const p of [shifter,phantom])Object.assign(poses.find(x=>x.id===p.id),{x:cam.x+30,y:cam.y});r.field.invisible[phantom.id]=now+50000;r.field.disguises[shifter.id]={target:crew.id,until:now+50000};r.field.venting[imp.id]={vent:'lab',until:now+50000};snap=fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,poses,now+40003);assert.ok(!snap.cameras[0].people.some(p=>[imp.id,phantom.id].includes(p.id)));assert.equal(snap.cameras[0].people.find(p=>p.id===shifter.id).name,crew.name);assert.equal(snap.cameras[0].people.find(p=>p.id===shifter.id).avatar,crew.avatar);delete r.field.venting[imp.id];
// Communications blocks the data on the server, and repair requires physical presence.
action(r,imp,{action:'commsSabotage'},poses,now+40004);snap=fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,poses,now+40005);assert.ok(snap.cameras.every(c=>c.offline&&!c.people.length&&!c.bodies.length&&!c.effects.length));assert.equal(snap.activeCameras,false);assert.throws(()=>action(r,imp,{action:'commsRepair'},poses));const repairer=r.players.find(p=>p.role==='engineer');Object.assign(poses.find(p=>p.id===repairer.id),communicationsConsole(29));action(r,repairer,{action:'commsRepair'},poses,now+40006);assert.ok(fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,poses,now+40007).cameras[0].people.length);
// A watcher can be killed; the feed closes immediately.
Object.assign(poses.find(p=>p.id===imp.id),terminal);r.field.cooldowns[imp.id]=0;action(r,imp,{action:'kill',target:crew.id},poses,now+40008);assert.equal(crew.alive,false);assert.equal(fieldSnapshot(r,crew.key,poses,now+40009).cameras,null);assert.equal(r.field.watching[crew.id],undefined);
// Impostors may watch, but cannot attack, sabotage or advance their kill cooldown.
r.field.cooldowns[imp.id]=now+60000;action(r,imp,{action:'cameraStart'},poses,now+41000);assert.throws(()=>action(r,imp,{action:'commsSabotage'},poses,now+41001));action(r,imp,{action:'cameraStop'},poses,now+43000);assert.equal(r.field.cooldowns[imp.id],now+62000);assert.equal(poseFor(r,imp,poses,now+43001).warp,now+43000);
action(r,imp,{action:'cameraStart'},poses,now+44000);fieldTick(r,now+57000);assert.equal(r.field.watching[imp.id],undefined);assert.equal(fieldSnapshot(r,imp.key,poses,now+57001).cameras,null);assert.equal(fieldSnapshot(r,imp.key,poses,now+57001).activeCameras,false);
console.log('PASS: 4/6/8 reachable cameras; remote access rejected; server-limited live feeds; no nearby view while watching; lights unaffected; ghost/vent/phantom concealment; disguise; comms outage and proximity repair; vulnerable watchers; frozen kill cooldown; disconnect expiry.');

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {pushRemote,sampleRemote} from '../lib/remote-motion.ts';
import {distance} from '../lib/map.ts';
export function scenario(name,delays,outage=false){
 const start=100000,events=[],duration=60000;
 function polls(kind,id){let t=id*7,i=0;while(t<duration){const trip=delays[(i+id)%delays.length],send=t,read=t+trip*.6,receive=t+trip;if(!outage||!(send>20000&&send<21500))events.push({kind,id,send,read,at:kind==='peer'?read:receive});t=receive+12;i++;}}
 for(let i=0;i<29;i++)polls('peer',i);polls('observer',0);events.sort((a,b)=>a.at-b.at);
 const poses=Array.from({length:29},(_,i)=>({x:1200+120*Math.sin(i),y:940+100*Math.cos(i),at:start,warp:0,alive:true})),hist=[],tracks=[],last=[],stall=Array(29).fill(0);let index=0,maxJump=0,longest=0,stalled=0,total=0,lag=0;
 // Snapshot reads and response deliveries are distinct: include the time of the other player's last update.
 const reads=events.filter(e=>e.kind==='observer').map(e=>({...e,kind:'read',at:e.read}));events.push(...reads);events.sort((a,b)=>a.at-b.at);
 const snapshots=new Map();
 for(let t=0;t<duration;t+=1000/60){while(index<events.length&&events[index].at<=t){const e=events[index++];if(e.kind==='peer'){poses[e.id]={x:1200+120*Math.sin(e.send/650+e.id),y:940+100*Math.cos(e.send/650+e.id),at:start+e.read,warp:0,alive:true};}else if(e.kind==='read')snapshots.set(e.send,structuredClone(poses));else{const ps=snapshots.get(e.send);for(let i=0;i<29;i++)tracks[i]=pushRemote(tracks[i],ps[i],e.at);snapshots.delete(e.send);}}
 for(let i=0;i<29;i++){if(!tracks[i])continue;const p=sampleRemote(tracks[i],t,29);if(last[i]&&t>3000){const d=distance(last[i],p);maxJump=Math.max(maxJump,d);if(d<.05){stall[i]+=1000/60;stalled++;longest=Math.max(longest,stall[i]);}else stall[i]=0;total++;lag=Math.max(lag,start+t-p.at);}last[i]=p;}}
 return{name,maxJump:Math.round(maxJump*100)/100,longestStall:Math.round(longest),stalledPercent:Math.round(stalled/total*1000)/10,maxLag:Math.round(lag)};
}
const cases=[['stable',[180]],['varying',[100,220,350,150,450]],['slow',[400,700,500]],['outage',[180],true]];
for(const c of cases){const result=scenario(...c);console.log(result);assert.ok(result.maxJump<=4.6,'remote actors must not teleport between frames');if(c[0]==='stable')assert.equal(result.longestStall,0);if(c[0]==='varying'){assert.ok(result.longestStall<=100);assert.ok(result.stalledPercent<1);}if(c[0]==='outage')assert.ok(result.longestStall>500,'a genuine connection outage cannot be hidden indefinitely');}

// Line-by-line transcription of the v21 FieldGame network/movement logic (React state
// replaced by plain fields) so the simulator measures the shipped client behaviour.
import {LiveClient} from '../../lib/live-client.ts';
import {stepInput,reconcileMotion} from '../../lib/motion.ts';
import {inputSteps,INPUT_STEP_MS} from '../../lib/input.ts';
import {updatePresence,retainedPeers} from '../../lib/peer-presence.ts';
import {pushRemote,sampleRemote} from '../../lib/remote-motion.ts';
import {spawn,moveTo,distance,WORLD} from '../../lib/map.ts';
import {isImpostor} from '../../lib/field-roles.ts';

export function createClient({index,code,token,room,http,metrics,wsUrl}){
 const c={index,code,token,hidden:false,intent:{x:0,y:0},alive:true,dead:false};
 let latest=room,snapshot=null,bootstrapped=false,connected=false,busy=false,live=null;
 let local=spawn(index,room.config),visualLocal={...local},correction={x:0,y:0},pending=[],inputNumber=1,lastAck=0,warp=0,inputElapsed=0,wasMoving=false;
 const remoteTracks={},presence={},interpolated={};let remoteEpoch=-1;
 const sentAt=new Map(),seenBodies=new Set();
 c.me=()=>latest.me;c.view=()=>latest;c.snapshot=()=>snapshot;c.position=()=>visualLocal;c.pendingCount=()=>pending.length;
 c.rendered=new Map();c.trackOf=id=>remoteTracks[id];c.isLiveHealthy=()=>!!live?.healthy();
 function apply(data,source){
  const f=data.frame;if(data.revision<latest.revision||f.own.warp!==undefined&&f.own.warp<warp||snapshot&&data.revision<=latest.revision&&f.serverNow<snapshot.serverNow){metrics.count('frame.dropped');return;}
  metrics.count('frame.'+source);if(process.env.SIM_TRACE&&index===Number(process.env.SIM_TRACE))console.error('[trace]',Math.round(performance.now()),source,'rev',data.revision,'room',!!data.room,'bodies',f.bodies.length,'alive',data.room?.players.find(p=>p.id===data.room.me)?.alive);
  if(remoteEpoch!==f.seq){for(const k of Object.keys(remoteTracks))delete remoteTracks[k];for(const k of Object.keys(presence))delete presence[k];for(const k of Object.keys(interpolated))delete interpolated[k];remoteEpoch=f.seq;}
  const received=performance.now(),removed=new Set();
  for(const body of f.bodies)removed.add(body.id);
  for(const e of f.effects||[]){if(e.kind==='attack'&&e.target)removed.add(e.target);if(e.kind==='vanish'||e.kind==='vent')removed.add(e.actor);}
  updatePresence(presence,f.people,received,removed);
  for(const id of Object.keys(remoteTracks))if(!presence[id]){delete remoteTracks[id];delete interpolated[id];metrics.count('track.deleted');}
  for(const person of f.people){if(person.id!==latest.me)metrics.sample(person.id,person.at||f.serverNow,f.serverNow);remoteTracks[person.id]=pushRemote(remoteTracks[person.id],{...person,at:person.at||f.serverNow,warp:person.warp||0},performance.now());}
  if(data.room){latest=data.room;}connected=true;snapshot=f;
  if(!bootstrapped||warp!==(f.own.warp||0)){pending=[];inputNumber=(f.own.ack||0)+1;lastAck=f.own.ack||0;warp=f.own.warp||0;local={x:f.own.x,y:f.own.y};bootstrapped=true;correction={x:0,y:0};}
  else if((f.own.ack||0)>=lastAck){
   for(const [n,t] of sentAt)if(n<=(f.own.ack||0)){metrics.ack(performance.now()-t);sentAt.delete(n);}
   const result=reconcileMotion(f.own,pending,latest.config);pending=result.pending;const dx=local.x-result.position.x,dy=local.y-result.position.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)>.01)metrics.correction(Math.hypot(dx,dy));correction={x:correction.x+dx,y:correction.y+dy};local=result.position;lastAck=f.own.ack||0;}
  const me=latest.players.find(p=>p.id===latest.me);if(me&&!me.alive&&c.alive){c.alive=false;metrics.event('deathSeen',{who:index});}
  for(const b of f.bodies)if(!seenBodies.has(b.id)){seenBodies.add(b.id);metrics.bodySeen(b.id);}
  c.onFrame?.(f,data);
 }
 c.apply=apply;
 async function frameOnce(){
  if(live?.healthy())return;
  const body={action:'frame',base:snapshot?.own,count:latest.config.count,mapId:latest.config.mapId,revision:latest.revision,camera:false,protocol:2,inputs:busy?[]:pending,warp,code,seq:latest.seq};
  const data=await http(token,'/api/field',body);if(data.error)throw Error(data.error);apply(data,'http');
 }
 let stopped=false,pollTimer;
 const poll=async()=>{const started=performance.now();let failed=false;try{await frameOnce();}catch(e){failed=true;connected=false;metrics.count('http.error');}finally{if(!stopped)pollTimer=setTimeout(poll,c.hidden?1000:failed?350:Math.max(12,100-(performance.now()-started)));}};
 const boot=setInterval(()=>{if(!snapshot||live)return;live=new LiveClient({url:wsUrl,code,token,input:()=>({seq:latest.seq,warp,hold:busy,inputs:busy?[]:pending}),frame:data=>apply(data,'live'),status:()=>{}});c.live=live;live.start();},100);
 c.start=()=>{void poll();};
 c.stop=()=>{stopped=true;clearTimeout(pollTimer);clearInterval(boot);live?.stop();};
 c.act=async(action,extra={})=>{if(busy)return null;busy=true;try{const data=await http(token,'/api/field',{action,protocol:2,inputs:pending,warp,code,seq:latest.seq,requestId:crypto.randomUUID(),...extra});if(data.error)return data;apply(data,'action');return data;}finally{busy=false;}};
 // One rendered frame (FirstPersonViewport/2D draw loop movement + remote sampling).
 c.frame=(time,dt)=>{
  if(c.hidden){inputElapsed=0;return;}
  const halt=!!latest.paused||busy&&false;
  const direction=c.alive?c.intent:{x:0,y:0},moving=Math.hypot(direction.x,direction.y)>.05&&!halt&&connected&&pending.length<180;
  const inputDirection=moving?direction:{x:0,y:0};
  if(bootstrapped&&connected&&!halt&&pending.length<180&&(moving||wasMoving)){
   const batch=moving?inputSteps(inputElapsed,dt*1000):{steps:1,remainder:0};inputElapsed=batch.remainder;
   for(let i=0;i<batch.steps&&pending.length<180;i++){const input={n:inputNumber++,dx:inputDirection.x,dy:inputDirection.y,ms:INPUT_STEP_MS};pending.push(input);sentAt.set(input.n,performance.now());local=stepInput(local,input,latest.config);}
  }else inputElapsed=0;wasMoving=moving;
  if(pending.length>=180)metrics.count('frame.inputBlocked');
  const predicted=stepInput(local,{n:0,dx:inputDirection.x,dy:inputDirection.y,ms:inputElapsed},latest.config);
  const decay=Math.exp(-dt*18);correction.x*=decay;correction.y*=decay;visualLocal=moveTo(predicted,{x:predicted.x+correction.x,y:predicted.y+correction.y},Math.hypot(correction.x,correction.y),latest.config);
  const s=snapshot;c.rendered.clear();if(!s)return;
  const liveIds=new Set(s.people.map(p=>p.id)),held=retainedPeers(presence,time,visualLocal,s.radius||WORLD.vision,latest.config).filter(p=>!liveIds.has(p.id));
  for(const p of [...s.people,...held]){if(p.id===latest.me)continue;const track=remoteTracks[p.id];const pos=track?sampleRemote(track,latest.paused?(track.painted??time):time,latest.config,!!live?.healthy()):p;interpolated[p.id]=pos;if(p.alive&&!p.vented&&!p.invisible)c.rendered.set(p.id,{x:pos.x,y:pos.y,at:pos.at});}
 };
 // FieldGame.kill(): pick the nearest eligible rendered target and send the strike.
 c.tryKill=async(targetId)=>{
  const live=snapshot?.people||[],role=latest.myRole;
  const p=live.find(p=>p.id===targetId&&p.alive&&!p.vented&&!p.invisible);if(!p)return{skipped:'not-visible'};
  const pos={...p,...interpolated[p.id]};if(distance(visualLocal,pos)>=93)return{skipped:'far'};
  if(!isImpostor(role)&&role!=='lonewolf')return{skipped:'role'};
  return c.act('kill',{target:targetId,targetAt:pos.at});
 };
 return c;
}

// Simulator adapter around the shipped client netcode (lib/field-net.ts).
import {FieldNet} from '../../lib/field-net.ts';
import {isImpostor} from '../../lib/field-roles.ts';
import {spawn,distance} from '../../lib/map.ts';
export function createClient({index,code,token,room,http,metrics,wsUrl}){
 const c={index,code,token,hidden:false,intent:{x:0,y:0},alive:true};
 const seenBodies=new Set();
 const net=new FieldNet({code,token,room,spawn:spawn(index,room.config),liveUrl:wsUrl,http:body=>http(token,'/api/field',body),
  onRoom:v=>{const me=v.players.find(p=>p.id===v.me);if(me&&!me.alive&&c.alive){c.alive=false;metrics.event('deathSeen',{who:index});}},
  onFrame:f=>{for(const b of f.bodies)if(!seenBodies.has(b.id)){seenBodies.add(b.id);metrics.bodySeen(b.id);}for(const p of f.people)if(p.id!==net.room.me)metrics.sample(p.id,p.at||f.serverNow,f.serverNow);}});
 net.setHiddenProbe(()=>c.hidden);
 c.net=net;Object.defineProperty(c,'live',{get:()=>net.live});
 c.rendered=new Map();c.trackOf=id=>net.tracks[id];c.position=()=>net.visual;c.view=()=>net.room;
 const tap=(w,fn)=>{const push=w.push.bind(w);w.push=v=>{push(v);fn(v);};};tap(net.stats.ack,v=>metrics.ack(v));tap(net.stats.correction,v=>metrics.correction(v));
 c.start=()=>net.start();c.stop=()=>net.stop();
 c.frame=(time,dt)=>{
  if(c.hidden)return;
  net.step(dt,c.alive?c.intent:{x:0,y:0},!!net.room.paused);
  c.rendered.clear();
  for(const p of net.render(time,{retain:true}))if(!p.mine&&p.alive&&!p.vented&&!p.invisible)c.rendered.set(p.id,{x:p.x,y:p.y,at:p.at});
 };
 c.tryKill=async targetId=>{
  const s=net.snapshot,role=net.room.myRole;const p=s?.people.find(p=>p.id===targetId&&p.alive&&!p.vented&&!p.invisible);if(!p)return{skipped:'not-visible'};
  const pos={...p,...net.interpolated[p.id]};if(distance(net.visual,pos)>=93)return{skipped:'far'};
  if(!isImpostor(role)&&role!=='lonewolf')return{skipped:'role'};
  if(process.env.SIM_KILLDBG){const tr=net.tracks[targetId],last=tr?.samples.at(-1),sn=Date.now()+net.serverOffset;console.error('[kill]',index,'target',Math.round(pos.x),Math.round(pos.y),'renderAge',Math.round(sn-pos.at),'lastSampleAge',last?Math.round(sn-last.at):null,'lastSample',last&&Math.round(last.x),last&&Math.round(last.y),'delay',tr&&Math.round(tr.delay),'tgt',tr&&Math.round(tr.target),'n',tr?.samples.length,'me',Math.round(net.visual.x),Math.round(net.visual.y));}
  try{return await net.act('kill',{target:targetId,targetAt:pos.at});}catch(e){return{error:e.message};}
 };
 return c;
}

FILE: tests/sim/d1.mjs

// A D1-compatible facade over node:sqlite. Every call pays a configurable network
// round trip and executes serially, like the single primary that D1 routes
// `withSession('first-primary')` traffic to. Counters feed the DB-load report.
import {DatabaseSync} from 'node:sqlite';
import {readdirSync,readFileSync} from 'node:fs';
const sleep=ms=>ms>0?new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms)):Promise.resolve();
const plain=row=>row?Object.assign({},row):null;
const writes=/^\s*(insert|update|delete|create|drop|alter|replace)/i;
export class SimD1{
 constructor({rtt=()=>0,commitMs=0}={}){this.sql=new DatabaseSync(':memory:');this.rtt=rtt;this.commitMs=commitMs;this.queue=Promise.resolve();this.reset();}
 reset(){this.stats={roundTrips:0,statements:0,writeStatements:0,rowsRead:0,bytesRead:0,busyMs:0,waitMs:0,errors:0,byTag:{}};}
 migrate(dir){for(const f of readdirSync(dir).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.sql')).sort())this.sql.exec(readFileSync(dir+'/'+f,'utf8').replaceAll('--> statement-breakpoint',''));return this;}
 withSession(){return this;}
 prepare(sql){return new Statement(this,sql,[]);}
 exec(sql){this.sql.exec(sql);}
 execute(stmt){
  const s=this.sql.prepare(stmt.sql);this.stats.statements++;
  if(writes.test(stmt.sql)){this.stats.writeStatements++;const r=s.run(...stmt.params);return{results:[],success:true,meta:{changes:Number(r.changes),last_row_id:Number(r.lastInsertRowid)}};}
  const rows=s.all(...stmt.params).map(plain);this.stats.rowsRead+=rows.length;for(const r of rows)for(const v of Object.values(r))if(typeof v==='string')this.stats.bytesRead+=v.length;
  return{results:rows,success:true,meta:{changes:0}};
 }
 async call(fn,tag){
  this.stats.roundTrips++;this.stats.byTag[tag]=(this.stats.byTag[tag]||0)+1;
  await sleep(this.rtt()/2);
  const queued=performance.now();
  const run=this.queue.then(async()=>{this.stats.waitMs+=performance.now()-queued;const t=performance.now();try{return fn();}finally{const spent=performance.now()-t;this.stats.busyMs+=spent;}});
  this.queue=run.then(r=>r&&r.wrote&&this.commitMs?sleep(this.commitMs):undefined,()=>{});
  try{const r=await run;await sleep(this.rtt()/2);return r.value;}catch(e){this.stats.errors++;await sleep(this.rtt()/2);throw e;}
 }
 // SIM_CAP_ERROR_AT=n: the n-th batch fails like the host's per-connection query cap (exercises cap learning).
 batch(stmts){this.batches=(this.batches||0)+1;if(this.batches===Number(process.env.SIM_CAP_ERROR_AT))return Promise.reject(new Error('D1_ERROR: Too many API requests by single worker invocation.'));return this.call(()=>{this.sql.exec('BEGIN');try{const out=stmts.map(s=>this.execute(s));this.sql.exec('COMMIT');return{value:out,wrote:stmts.some(s=>writes.test(s.sql))};}catch(e){this.sql.exec('ROLLBACK');throw e;}},'batch');}
}
class Statement{
 constructor(db,sql,params){this.db=db;this.sql=sql;this.params=params;}
 bind(...p){return new Statement(this.db,this.sql,p.map(v=>typeof v==='boolean'?Number(v):v));}
 first(col){return this.db.call(()=>{const r=this.db.execute(this).results[0]||null;return{value:col&&r?r[col]:r,wrote:false};},'first');}
 all(){return this.db.call(()=>({value:this.db.execute(this),wrote:false}),'all');}
 run(){return this.db.call(()=>({value:this.db.execute(this),wrote:writes.test(this.sql)}),'run');}
}

// Node ESM hooks so server modules written for Workers can run in a plain Node
// simulation: `cloudflare:workers` is stubbed, `@/` maps to the project root and
// extensionless relative imports resolve to .ts/.tsx files.
import {existsSync,statSync} from 'node:fs';
import {fileURLToPath,pathToFileURL} from 'node:url';
import path from 'node:path';
const root=path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),'../..');
const STUB='data:text/javascript,export const env=new Proxy({},{get:(_,k)=>k==="DB"?globalThis.__SIM_DB__:typeof k==="string"?process.env[k]:undefined});';
function candidate(file){for(const ext of ['','.ts','.tsx','/index.ts'])if(existsSync(file+ext)&&statSync(file+ext).isFile())return file+ext;return null;}
export async function resolve(spec,context,next){
 if(spec==='cloudflare:workers')return{url:STUB,shortCircuit:true};
 let file=null;
 if(spec.startsWith('@/'))file=candidate(path.join(root,spec.slice(2)));
 else if((spec.startsWith('./')||spec.startsWith('../'))&&context.parentURL?.startsWith('file:')){const [bare,query]=spec.split('?');const f=candidate(path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(context.parentURL)),bare));if(f)return{url:pathToFileURL(f).href+(query?'?'+query:''),shortCircuit:true};}
 if(file)return{url:pathToFileURL(file).href,shortCircuit:true};
 return next(spec,context);
}

FILE: tests/sim/net.mjs

// Simulated client <-> edge network. Each direction is a FIFO stream (WebSocket over
// TCP), so a delayed segment (loss + retransmission) also delays everything behind it.
export function rng(seed){let s=seed>>>0||1;return()=>{s^=s<<13;s>>>=0;s^=s>>17;s^=s<<5;s>>>=0;return s/4294967296;};}
export class Link{
 constructor({base=20,jitter=8,loss=0,rto=[200,500],random=Math.random}={}){Object.assign(this,{base,jitter,loss,rto,random});this.last=0;this.throttle=0;this.pending=[];}
 delay(){const r=this.random;let d=this.base+(r()*2-1)*this.jitter;if(this.loss&&r()<this.loss)d+=this.rto[0]+r()*(this.rto[1]-this.rto[0]);return Math.max(1,d);}
 // Returns when the message reaches the far end. FIFO: never before the previous one.
 schedule(fn){const now=performance.now(),at=Math.max(this.last+.01,now+this.delay());this.last=at;setTimeout(fn,at-now);}
}
// Browser-like client socket bound to one server isolate.
export function socketPair({up,down,acceptOn,onServerCreated}){
 const listeners={message:[],close:[],error:[]};let serverOpen=true,clientOpen=false;
 const client={readyState:0,bufferedAmount:0,onopen:null,onmessage:null,onclose:null,onerror:null,hidden:false,_held:null,_heldTimer:null,
  send(data){if(client.readyState!==1)return;
   // A hidden browser tab runs its timers at most once per second; model by coalescing.
   if(client.hidden){client._held=data;if(!client._heldTimer)client._heldTimer=setTimeout(()=>{client._heldTimer=null;const d=client._held;client._held=null;if(d&&client.readyState===1)up.schedule(()=>serverSide.deliver(d));},1000);return;}
   up.schedule(()=>serverSide.deliver(data));},
  close(){if(client.readyState===3)return;client.readyState=3;up.schedule(()=>serverSide.hangup());down.schedule(()=>client.onclose?.({code:1000}));}};
 const serverSide={readyState:1,
  accept(){},addEventListener(type,fn){listeners[type].push(fn);},
  send(data){if(!serverOpen)return;down.schedule(()=>{if(client.readyState===1)client.onmessage?.({data});});},
  close(){if(!serverOpen)return;serverOpen=false;serverSide.readyState=3;for(const fn of listeners.close)fn({});down.schedule(()=>{if(client.readyState!==3){client.readyState=3;client.onclose?.({code:1008});}});},
  deliver(data){if(!serverOpen)return;for(const fn of listeners.message)fn({data});},
  hangup(){if(!serverOpen)return;serverOpen=false;serverSide.readyState=3;for(const fn of listeners.close)fn({});}};
 onServerCreated?.(serverSide);
 // Opening handshake: one round trip.
 up.schedule(()=>{acceptOn(serverSide);down.schedule(()=>{if(client.readyState===0){client.readyState=1;clientOpen=true;client.onopen?.();}});});
 return client;
}

FILE: tests/sim/realtime-sim.mjs

// Multi-isolate realtime simulator.
//   node --import ./tests/sim/register.mjs tests/sim/realtime-sim.mjs [scenario...] [--client baseline|current] [--out file]
// Runs the real route handlers and N independent copies of lib/live-server.ts (one per
// simulated Worker isolate) against a shared SQLite database that is reached through a
// D1-like facade with injected round-trip latency. Clients follow the real client logic.
// This reproduces the cross-isolate data flow; it is NOT a measurement of Cloudflare.
import {monitorEventLoopDelay} from 'node:perf_hooks';
import {writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';
import {pathToFileURL,fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import path from 'node:path';
import {SimD1} from './d1.mjs';
import {Link,rng,socketPair} from './net.mjs';
import {distance,sight,spawn,WORLD,walkable} from '../../lib/map.ts';

const root=path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)),'../..');
const args=process.argv.slice(2),flag=(k,d)=>{const i=args.indexOf(k);return i>=0?args[i+1]:d;};
const clientKind=flag('--client','baseline'),outFile=flag('--out',null),duration=Number(flag('--seconds','20'))*1000;
const SCENARIOS={
 'p6c2-iso1-d25':{room:6,clients:2,isolates:1,d1:[25,8],net:[20,8],loss:0},
 'p16-iso16-d25':{room:16,clients:16,isolates:16,d1:[25,8],net:[20,8],loss:0},
 'p29-iso29-d25':{room:29,clients:29,isolates:29,d1:[25,8],net:[20,8],loss:0},
 'p29-iso1-d170':{room:29,clients:29,isolates:1,d1:[170,40],net:[20,8],loss:0},
 'p29-iso29-d170':{room:29,clients:29,isolates:29,d1:[170,40],net:[20,8],loss:0},
 'p29-iso29-d25-recycle':{room:29,clients:29,isolates:29,d1:[25,8],net:[20,8],loss:0,budget:120},
 'p29-iso29-d170-adverse':{room:29,clients:29,isolates:29,d1:[170,40],net:[30,15],loss:.02,hide:{client:5,from:6000,to:11000},reconnect:{client:7,at:12000}},
};
const chosen=args.filter(a=>SCENARIOS[a]);const list=chosen.length?chosen:Object.keys(SCENARIOS);
const pct=(a,p)=>{if(!a.length)return null;const s=[...a].sort((x,y)=>x-y);return Math.round(s[Math.min(s.length-1,Math.floor(p/100*s.length))]*10)/10;};
const sleep=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
const token=()=>Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(32)),b=>b.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('');
const {createClient}=await import(clientKind==='baseline'?'./baseline-client.mjs':'./current-client.mjs');
const gameRoute=await import(pathToFileURL(path.join(root,'app/api/game/route.ts')).href);
const fieldRoute=await import(pathToFileURL(path.join(root,'app/api/field/route.ts')).href);

async function runScenario(name,S){
 const random=rng(name.length*7919+S.clients);
 process.env.LIVE_D1_QUERY_BUDGET=S.budget?String(S.budget):'';
 const db=new SimD1({rtt:()=>Math.max(2,S.d1[0]+(random()*2-1)*S.d1[1]),commitMs:1.5}).migrate(path.join(root,'drizzle'));globalThis.__SIM_DB__=db;
 const isolates=[];for(let k=0;k<S.isolates;k++)isolates.push(await import(pathToFileURL(path.join(root,'lib/live-server.ts')).href+`?scenario=${encodeURIComponent(name)}&iso=${k}`));
 const metrics=makeMetrics();
 // Edge HTTP: client network latency in both directions around the real route handler.
 const httpLinks=new Map();
 const http=async(key,p,body,index=-1)=>{const link=httpLinks.get(index)||{up:new Link({base:S.net[0],jitter:S.net[1],loss:S.loss,random}),down:new Link({base:S.net[0],jitter:S.net[1],loss:S.loss,random})};httpLinks.set(index,link);
  await new Promise(r=>link.up.schedule(r));const route=p==='/api/game'?gameRoute:fieldRoute;const t=performance.now();
  const res=await route.POST(new Request('http://sim'+p,{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)}));const data=await res.json();metrics.serverMs(p+':'+(body.action),performance.now()-t);
  await new Promise(r=>link.down.schedule(r));return data;};
 // Room setup through the real API.
 const host=token();let r=await gameRoute.POST(new Request('http://sim/api/game',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+host},body:JSON.stringify({action:'create',config:{mode:'field',mapId:'aurora',count:S.room,discussion:300,vote:120}})})).then(x=>x.json());
 const code=r.code,keys=Array.from({length:S.room},token);
 const call=(key,body)=>gameRoute.POST(new Request('http://sim/api/game',{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key},body:JSON.stringify({code,...body})})).then(x=>x.json());
 const joined=[];for(let i=0;i<S.room;i++)joined.push(await call(keys[i],{action:'join',name:'학생'+i,avatar:i%16}));
 for(const k of keys)await call(k,{action:'ready',ready:true});
 r=await call(host,{action:'start',seq:r.seq});r=await call(host,{action:'next',seq:r.seq});
 if(r.phase!=='field')throw Error('field phase expected: '+JSON.stringify(r).slice(0,200));
 // Deterministic attackers and victims; cooldowns cleared so attacks can happen on schedule.
 const kills=S.clients>=6?[[0,1,7000],[2,3,11000],[4,5,15000]]:[[0,1,9000]];
 const row=await db.prepare('SELECT state FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=?').bind(code).first();const state=JSON.parse(row.state);
 const ids=joined.map(j=>j.me);
 state.players.forEach((p,i)=>{p.role=kills.some(k=>k[0]===i)?'impostor':kills.some(k=>k[1]===i)?'crewmate':(['impostor','shapeshifter','phantom','viper'].includes(p.role)?'crewmate':p.role);});
 state.field.cooldowns={};state.field.meetingAt=Date.now()+3600e3;state.field.sabotageAt=Date.now()+3600e3;
 await db.prepare('UPDATE mafia_rooms SET state=?,version=version+1 WHERE code=?').bind(JSON.stringify(state),code).run();
 // Clients.
 globalThis.window=globalThis;
 const routeOf=i=>i%S.isolates;let reconnectShift=0;
 globalThis.WebSocket=class{static OPEN=1;constructor(url){const i=Number(new URL(url).searchParams.get('client'));const iso=(routeOf(i)+(i===S.reconnect?.client?reconnectShift:0))%S.isolates;metrics.count('ws.connect');
  return socketPair({up:new Link({base:S.net[0],jitter:S.net[1],loss:S.loss,random}),down:new Link({base:S.net[0],jitter:S.net[1],loss:S.loss,random}),acceptOn:server=>isolates[iso].acceptLive(server),onServerCreated:server=>{clients[i].serverSocket=server;}});}};
 const clients=[];
 for(let i=0;i<S.clients;i++){const initial=await (await gameRoute.GET(new Request('http://sim/api/game?code='+code,{headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+keys[i]}}))).json();
  const c=createClient({index:i,code,token:keys[i],room:initial,http:(k,p,b)=>http(k,p,b,i),metrics:metrics.scope(i),wsUrl:`ws://sim/api/live?client=${i}`});c.id=ids[i];clients.push(c);}
 // Ground truth: each player's own rendered position over time.
 const truth=clients.map(()=>[]);const truthAt=(i,t)=>{const h=truth[i];for(let k=h.length-1;k>=0;k--)if(h[k].t<=t)return h[k];return h[0];};
 const t0=performance.now(),perfToDate=Date.now()-performance.now();for(const c of clients)c.start();
 const loop=monitorEventLoopDelay({resolution:5});loop.enable();
 // Intents: bounded random walk around the central hall; attackers chase their target.
 const plan=clients.map((_,i)=>({until:0,dir:{x:0,y:0}}));
 const center={x:1210,y:940};
 const killState=kills.map(([a,v,at])=>({a,v,at,attempts:[],done:false}));
 const vis=new Map();let last=performance.now(),frames=0,lastVisCheck=0;
 const hideState={applied:false,cleared:false},reconnectDone={v:false};const killedAt=new Map();
 await new Promise(resolve=>{const tick=async()=>{
  const now=performance.now(),dt=Math.min(.05,(now-last)/1000);last=now;const t=now-t0;frames++;
  if(S.hide&&!hideState.applied&&t>=S.hide.from){hideState.applied=true;clients[S.hide.client].hidden=true;const s=clients[S.hide.client].live?.socket;if(s)s.hidden=true;}
  if(S.hide&&hideState.applied&&!hideState.cleared&&t>=S.hide.to){hideState.cleared=true;for(const key of [...vis.keys()])if(Math.floor(key/1000)===S.hide.client)vis.delete(key);clients[S.hide.client].hidden=false;const s=clients[S.hide.client].live?.socket;if(s)s.hidden=false;metrics.event('tabReturn',{t});}
  if(S.reconnect&&!reconnectDone.v&&t>=S.reconnect.at){reconnectDone.v=true;reconnectShift=1;const c=clients[S.reconnect.client];c.serverSocket?.close(1001,'Reconnect');metrics.event('forcedReconnect',{t});}
  for(let i=0;i<clients.length;i++){const c=clients[i],p=plan[i],me=c.position();
   const k=killState.find(k=>k.a===i&&!k.done&&t>=k.at);
   if(k){const target=c.rendered.get(clients[k.v].id),goal=target||truthAt(k.v,performance.now());const d=distance(me,goal);if(process.env.SIM_SKIPS&&frames%30===0)console.error('[chase]',Math.round(t),'target?',!!target,Math.round(d),'me',Math.round(me.x),Math.round(me.y),'pending',c.net?.pending.length,'conn',c.net?.connected,'boot',c.net?.bootstrapped);c.intent=d>40?{x:(goal.x-me.x)/d,y:(goal.y-me.y)/d}:{x:0,y:0};
    if(target&&distance(me,target)<75&&!k.inflight&&now-(k.lastTry||0)>250){k.inflight=true;k.lastTry=now;const sent=performance.now();if(!k.witnesses){k.witnesses=new Set();clients.forEach((o,j)=>{if(j!==i&&j!==k.v&&o.rendered.has(clients[k.v].id))k.witnesses.add(j);});}const truthGap=distance(truthAt(i,sent),truthAt(k.v,sent));
     c.tryKill(clients[k.v].id).then(res=>{const rec={sent,at:Math.round(sent-t0),ms:performance.now()-sent,truthGap,result:res?.error?'error:'+res.error:res?.skipped?'skip:'+res.skipped:'ok'};if(!res?.skipped)k.attempts.push(rec);else if(process.env.SIM_SKIPS)console.error('[skip]',Math.round(sent-t0),res.skipped,Math.round(distance(me,target)));if(!res?.error&&!res?.skipped&&res?.frame?.bodies?.some(b=>b.id===clients[k.v].id)){k.done=true;k.confirmed=performance.now();k.sent=sent;metrics.event('killConfirmed',{...rec,a:i,v:k.v});killedAt.set(k.v,performance.now());}}).finally(()=>{k.inflight=false;});}
    if(t>k.at+6000&&!k.done){k.done=true;k.timeout=true;}
   }else if(!c.alive){c.intent={x:0,y:0};}
   else{if(now>p.until){const tether=killState.some(k=>k.a===i||k.v===i)?200:420,still=random()<.15,toCenter=distance(me,center)>tether;let a=random()*Math.PI*2;if(toCenter)a=Math.atan2(center.y-me.y,center.x-me.x)+(random()-.5);p.dir=still?{x:0,y:0}:{x:Math.cos(a),y:Math.sin(a)};p.until=now+700+random()*1800;}c.intent=t<1500?{x:0,y:0}:p.dir;}
   if(process.env.SIM_DEBUG_CLIENT===String(i)&&frames%20===0&&c.net){const n=c.net,own=n.snapshot?.own;console.error('[dbg]',Math.round(t),'hidden',c.hidden,'vis',Math.round(n.visual.x),Math.round(n.visual.y),'srv',own&&Math.round(own.x),own&&Math.round(own.y),'ack',own?.ack,'lastAck',n.lastAck,'next',n.inputNumber,'pend',n.pending.length,'healthy',n.healthy(),'counts',JSON.stringify(n.stats.counts));}
   c.frame(now,dt);if(!c.hidden)truth[i].push({t:now,x:c.position().x,y:c.position().y});if(truth[i].length>400)truth[i].splice(0,100);
  }
  // Observer quality.
  const checkVisibility=now-lastVisCheck>100;if(checkVisibility)lastVisCheck=now;
  // Bodies anyone has been shown: such a victim is legitimately gone (both client adapters expose their latest snapshot).
  const bodyIds=new Set();if(checkVisibility)for(const c of clients)for(const b of (c.net?c.net.snapshot:c.snapshot?.())?.bodies||[])bodyIds.add(b.id);
  for(let o=0;o<clients.length;o++){const obs=clients[o];if(obs.hidden||!obs.alive)continue;const O=truthAt(o,now);
   for(let k=0;k<clients.length;k++){if(k===o)continue;const key=o*1000+k,R=truthAt(k,now),shown=obs.rendered.get(clients[k].id),prev=vis.get(key);
    if(shown&&prev?.pos){const jump=distance(prev.pos,shown);metrics.jump(jump);if(jump>40&&process.env.SIM_JUMPS)console.error('[jump]',Math.round(t),'obs',o,'remote',k,Math.round(jump),'track',obs.trackOf?.(clients[k].id)?.samples.length);
     // Stall = the remote's true motion at the displayed moment was moving but the display did not move.
     if(Number.isFinite(shown.at)){const tp=shown.at-perfToDate,moving=distance(truthAt(k,tp-120),truthAt(k,tp))>205*.12*.5;if(moving)metrics.motionFrame(jump<.05);}}
    if(process.env.SIM_TRACK===`${o},${k}`&&frames%6===0){const tr=obs.trackOf?.(clients[k].id);console.error('[track]',Math.round(t),'delay',shown?Date.now()-shown.at:null,'err',shown?Math.round(distance(shown,R)):null,'last',tr?Date.now()-tr.samples.at(-1).at:null,'n',tr?.samples.length,'off',tr?Math.round(tr.offset):null,'int',tr?Math.round(tr.interval):null,'excess',tr?Math.round(tr.delay-tr.target):null,'need',tr?Math.round(tr.target-Math.min(...tr.lags)):null,'gaps',tr?JSON.stringify(tr.gaps.slice(-6).map(Math.round)):null,'lagsMinusBase',tr?JSON.stringify(tr.lags.slice(-4).map(v=>Math.round(v-Math.min(...tr.lags)))):null,'moving',Math.round(distance(truthAt(k,now-100),R)));}
    if(shown){metrics.displayError(distance(shown,R));if(Number.isFinite(shown.at)){const tp=shown.at-perfToDate;metrics.displayDelay(Date.now()-shown.at);metrics.trajError(distance(shown,truthAt(k,tp)));}}
    if(checkVisibility){const should=clients[k].alive&&!killedAt.has(k)&&!bodyIds.has(clients[k].id)&&!clients[k].hidden&&[0,200,400].every(back=>{const a=truthAt(o,now-back),b=truthAt(k,now-back);return distance(a,b)<=WORLD.vision*.8&&sight(a,b,{count:S.room,mapId:'aurora'});});
     if(should&&t>3000){metrics.visCheck(!!shown);if(!shown&&prev?.shownWhenShould&&prev?.should&&prev?.wasShown){metrics.count('vanish.event');if(process.env.SIM_VANISH&&obs.net){const n=obs.net,id=clients[k].id,snap=n.snapshot,inSnap=snap?.people.some(p=>p.id===id),pr=n.presence[id],own=snap?.own,known=pr?.person;console.error('[vanish]',Math.round(t),'obs',o,'remote',k,'truthDist',Math.round(distance(O,R)),'inSnap',inSnap,'snapAge',snap?Math.round(Date.now()+n.serverOffset-snap.serverNow):null,'missingFor',pr?.missingAt!==undefined?Math.round(performance.now()-pr.missingAt):null,'srvDist',own&&known?Math.round(distance(own,known)):null,'srvSight',own&&known?sight(own,known,{count:S.room,mapId:'aurora'}):null,'ownErr',own?Math.round(distance(own,O)):null,'knownErr',known?Math.round(distance(known,R)):null,'knownAge',known?Math.round(Date.now()-known.at):null);}}}
     // A vanish is: shown and in clear view at the previous check, still in clear view, now gone.
     vis.set(key,{pos:shown?{...shown}:null,shownWhenShould:should?!!shown:prev?.shownWhenShould,should,wasShown:!!shown});}
    else vis.set(key,{...prev,pos:shown?{...shown}:null});
   }}
  if(t<duration)setTimeout(tick,16);else resolve();
 };tick();});
 loop.disable();
 // Client-side counters (FieldNet): HTTP frames/actions, live frames, resends, rebases, reconnects.
 for(const c of clients){for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(c.net?.stats.counts||{}))for(let n=0;n<v;n++)metrics.count('client.'+k);if(c.net?.live)for(let n=0;n<c.net.live.reconnects;n++)metrics.count('client.reconnects');}
 for(const c of clients)c.stop();
 // Kill outcomes: when did the victim and observers learn about it?
 const killReport=killState.map(k=>{const conf=metrics.events.find(e=>e.type==='killConfirmed'&&e.v===k.v);const death=metrics.events.find(e=>e.type==='deathSeen'&&e.who===k.v);const bodySeen=metrics.events.filter(e=>e.type==='bodySeen'&&e.victim===clients[k.v].id&&k.witnesses?.has(e.observer)).map(e=>e.t);
  const first=k.attempts[0];return{attacker:k.a,victim:k.v,attempts:k.attempts.length,errors:k.attempts.filter(a=>a.result!=='ok').map(a=>a.result).slice(0,4),confirmMs:conf?Math.round(conf.ms):null,pressToConfirmMs:conf&&first?Math.round(conf.sent+conf.ms-first.sent):null,firstPressAt:first?first.at:null,victimSeesDeathMs:death&&first?Math.round(death.t-first.sent):null,observersSeeBodyP50:first&&bodySeen.length?Math.round(pct(bodySeen.map(t=>t-first.sent),50)):null,observersSeeBodyMax:first&&bodySeen.length?Math.round(Math.max(...bodySeen.map(t=>t-first.sent))):null,truthGapAtPress:first?Math.round(first.truthGap):null,witnesses:k.witnesses?.size??0,timeout:!!k.timeout};});
 const seconds=duration/1000;
 const summary={scenario:name,client:clientKind,...S,
  ackMs:{p50:pct(metrics.acks,50),p95:pct(metrics.acks,95),max:pct(metrics.acks,100)},
  correctionPx:{events:metrics.corrections.length,p95:pct(metrics.corrections,95),max:pct(metrics.corrections,100)},
  remoteSampleAgeMs:{p50:pct(metrics.ages,50),p95:pct(metrics.ages,95),max:pct(metrics.ages,100)},
  observer:{frameJumpPxMax:pct(metrics.jumps,100),frameJumpPxP99:pct(metrics.jumps,99),stalledMovingFramesPct:metrics.motion.total?Math.round(metrics.motion.stalled/metrics.motion.total*1000)/10:null,displayErrorPx:{p50:pct(metrics.errors,50),p95:pct(metrics.errors,95)},displayDelayMs:{p50:pct(metrics.delays,50),p95:pct(metrics.delays,95),max:pct(metrics.delays,100)},trajectoryErrorPx:{p50:pct(metrics.traj,50),p95:pct(metrics.traj,95),max:pct(metrics.traj,100)},missingWhileVisiblePct:metrics.vis.total?Math.round(metrics.vis.missing/metrics.vis.total*1000)/10:null,vanishEvents:metrics.counts['vanish.event']||0},
  kills:killReport,
  db:{roundTripsPerSec:Math.round(db.stats.roundTrips/seconds),statementsPerSec:Math.round(db.stats.statements/seconds),writeStatementsPerSec:Math.round(db.stats.writeStatements/seconds),kbReadPerSec:Math.round(db.stats.bytesRead/1024/seconds),primaryBusyPct:Math.round(db.stats.busyMs/duration*1000)/10,avgQueueWaitMs:Math.round(db.stats.waitMs/Math.max(1,db.stats.roundTrips)*10)/10,stateBytes:(await db.prepare('SELECT length(state) n FROM mafia_rooms WHERE code=?').bind(code).first()).n},
  counts:metrics.counts,
  simFidelity:{eventLoopP99Ms:Math.round(loop.percentile(99)/1e6),framesPerSec:Math.round(frames/seconds)},
 };
 delete globalThis.__SIM_DB__;await sleep(1500);
 return summary;
}
function makeMetrics(){
 const m={lastAt:new Map(),delays:[],traj:[],counts:{},acks:[],corrections:[],ages:[],jumps:[],errors:[],motion:{total:0,stalled:0},vis:{total:0,missing:0},events:[],server:{}};
 m.count=k=>{m.counts[k]=(m.counts[k]||0)+1;};
 m.event=(type,data)=>m.events.push({type,t:performance.now(),...data});
 m.jump=d=>{if(m.jumps.length<400000)m.jumps.push(d);};m.motionFrame=stalled=>{m.motion.total++;if(stalled)m.motion.stalled++;};
 m.displayDelay=d=>{if(m.delays.length<400000)m.delays.push(d);};m.trajError=d=>{if(m.traj.length<400000)m.traj.push(d);};
 m.displayError=d=>{if(m.errors.length<400000)m.errors.push(d);};m.visCheck=shown=>{m.vis.total++;if(!shown)m.vis.missing++;};
 m.serverMs=(k,ms)=>{(m.server[k]||=[]).push(ms);};
 m.scope=i=>({count:m.count,event:(t,d)=>m.event(t,{...d,observer:i}),ack:ms=>m.acks.push(ms),correction:px=>m.corrections.push(px),sampleAge:ms=>{if(m.ages.length<400000)m.ages.push(ms);},sample:(id,at,serverNow)=>{const k=i+':'+id;if((m.lastAt.get(k)??-1)>=at)return;m.lastAt.set(k,at);if(m.ages.length<400000)m.ages.push(Date.now()-at);},bodySeen:victim=>m.event('bodySeen',{victim,observer:i})});
 return m;
}
const results=[];
for(const name of list){process.stderr.write(`▶ ${name} (${clientKind})\n`);const s=await runScenario(name,SCENARIOS[name]);results.push(s);console.log(JSON.stringify(s));}
if(outFile)writeFileSync(outFile,JSON.stringify(results,null,1));
process.exit(0);

const key=token();async function req(body,field=false){const response=await fetch(origin+(field?'/api/field':'/api/game'),{method:'POST',headers:{Authorization:'Bearer '+key,'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});const data=await response.json();assert.equal(response.status,200,JSON.stringify(data));return data;}
for(const mapId of ['aurora','nereid']){let r=await req({action:'create',config:{mode:'field',mapId,count:29}});const code=r.code;r=await req({action:'soloStart',code,seq:r.seq});assert.equal(r.host,false);assert.equal(r.solo,true);assert.equal(r.players.length,1);
 const frame=await req({action:'frame',code,seq:r.seq,protocol:2,inputs:[],warp:0},true);await new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,130));const base=frame.frame.own;
 const moved=await req({action:'frame',code,seq:r.seq,count:29,mapId,protocol:2,warp:0,base,inputs:Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>({n:i+1,dx:1,dy:0,ms:20}))},true);assert.equal(moved.transport,'checkpoint');assert.equal(moved.frame.own.ack,5);assert.ok(moved.frame.own.x>base.x);
 r=await req({action:'soloStop',code,seq:r.seq});assert.equal(r.phase,'lobby');assert.equal(r.host,true);assert.equal(r.config.count,29);assert.equal(r.players.length,0);
}

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {newRoom,defaults,act,view,addPlayer} from '../lib/game.ts';
import {fieldWin,fieldAct,poseFor} from '../lib/field.ts';
import {mapFor,ventsFor} from '../lib/map.ts';
import {scienceProblem} from '../lib/science.ts';
for(const mapId of ['aurora','nereid'])for(const count of [6,16,29]){
 const r=newRoom('ABC234','host',{...defaults,mode:'field',mapId,count},1000000);
 assert.throws(()=>act(r,'stranger',{action:'soloStart',seq:r.seq},1000000));
 act(r,'host',{action:'soloStart',seq:r.seq},1000000);assert.equal(r.phase,'field');assert.equal(r.field.epoch,r.seq);assert.equal(view(r,'host').host,false);assert.equal(view(r,'host').solo,true);assert.equal(r.config.count,count);assert.equal(fieldWin(r,1000000),false);assert.throws(()=>addPlayer(r,'student','학생',1));
 const p=r.players[0],station=mapFor(r.config).stations.find(s=>s.id===r.field.tasks[p.id][0].station),pos={...poseFor(r,p,[],1000000),x:station.x,y:station.y};
 fieldAct(r,'host',{action:'taskStart',seq:r.seq,station:station.id},[pos],1000000);
 const c=r.field.challenges[p.id],answer=station.kind.startsWith('science_')?scienceProblem(station.kind,c.solution[0]).answer:station.kind==='calibrate'?[50,50,50]:c.solution;
 fieldAct(r,'host',{action:'taskFinish',seq:r.seq,station:station.id,nonce:c.nonce,answer},[pos],1010000);assert.equal(r.field.tasks[p.id][0].done,true);

import vinext from "vinext";
import { defineConfig } from "vite";
import hostingConfig from "./.openai/hosting.json";
import { readExecutionProfile } from "./scripts/execution-profile.mjs";
import { sites } from "./build/sites-vite-plugin";

const SITE_CREATOR_PLACEHOLDER_DATABASE_ID =
  "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000000";

const { d1, r2 } = hostingConfig;

// macOS Seatbelt blocks FSEvents, so Codex previews need polling for HMR.
const isCodexSeatbeltSandbox = process.env.CODEX_SANDBOX === "seatbelt";
const managedLinux = readExecutionProfile() === "managed-linux";

const localBindingConfig = {
  main: "vinext/server/fetch-handler",
  compatibility_flags: ["nodejs_compat"],
  d1_databases: d1
    ? [
        {
          binding: d1,
          database_name: "site-creator-d1",
          database_id: SITE_CREATOR_PLACEHOLDER_DATABASE_ID,
        },
      ]
    : [],
  r2_buckets: r2
    ? [
        {
          binding: r2,
          bucket_name: "site-creator-r2",
        },
      ]
    : [],
};

export default defineConfig(async () => {
  // Use Miniflare's local Request.cf placeholder unless fetching is requested.
  process.env.CLOUDFLARE_CF_FETCH_ENABLED ??= "false";
  process.env.WRANGLER_SEND_METRICS ??= "false";

  // Keep Wrangler and Miniflare state project-local. These are non-secret tool
  // settings; application environment belongs in ignored `.env*` files.
  process.env.WRANGLER_WRITE_LOGS ??= "false";
  process.env.WRANGLER_LOG_PATH ??= ".wrangler/logs";
  process.env.WRANGLER_REGISTRY_PATH ??= ".wrangler/dev-registry";
  process.env.MINIFLARE_REGISTRY_PATH ??= ".wrangler/registry";

  // Wrangler snapshots its log path while the Cloudflare plugin is imported.
  const { cloudflare } = await import("@cloudflare/vite-plugin");

  return {
    server: {
      ...(managedLinux ? { host: "0.0.0.0", allowedHosts: ["terminal.local"] } : {}),
      ...(isCodexSeatbeltSandbox ? { watch: { useFsEvents: false, usePolling: true } } : {}),
    },
    plugins: [
      vinext(),
      sites({ mockAuth: !managedLinux }),
      cloudflare({
        viteEnvironment: { name: "rsc", childEnvironments: ["ssr"] },
        inspectorPort: false,
        config: localBindingConfig,
      }),
    ],
  };
});


======================================================================
FILE: lib/frame-pacer.ts
======================================================================
// Frame pacing for the game loops (2D field and 3D first person).
//
// Drawing on every display refresh up to the target rate, and on every 2nd/3rd/... refresh
// above it, keeps every frame the same length. The old test "at least 15.7 ms since the last
// frame" alternated 1- and 2-refresh frames on 75/90/144 Hz screens (75 Hz -> 37 fps), which
// reads as judder even when the average rate looks fine.
export type Pacer={due(time:number):boolean;refresh():number;interval():number;target:number};
export function framePacer(target=60):Pacer{
 const deltas:number[]=[];let lastTick=0,lastFrame=0,refresh=1000/60,every=1;
 const pacer:Pacer={target,
  due(time){
   // The refresh interval is the shortest recent rAF delta: slow frames (2+ refreshes) do not
   // move it, so a device slower than its screen draws on every callback and never skips a
   // frame that is ready (a quantile was inflated by slow frames and skipped the fast ones).
   if(lastTick){const d=time-lastTick;if(d>4&&d<100){deltas.push(d);if(deltas.length>30)deltas.shift();if(deltas.length>=4)refresh=Math.min(...deltas);}}
   lastTick=time;every=Math.max(1,Math.floor(1000/pacer.target/refresh+.25));
   if(lastFrame&&time-lastFrame<refresh*(every-.5))return false;
   lastFrame=time;return true;},
  refresh:()=>refresh,interval:()=>refresh*every};
 return pacer;
}

// Keeps the 3D view smooth on slow GPUs: when frames keep missing their slot, render fewer
// pixels (down to 60 %); if that is still not enough, hold a steady 30 fps instead of an
// uneven 40-50. Recovers quality when there is headroom again. Changes are rate-limited so
// the picture never flickers between sizes.
export type Governor={frame(time:number,interval:number):boolean;scale:number;target:number};
export function qualityGovernor(start=1,{min=.6,step=.9,grow=1.05}={}):Governor{
 // ceiling: the last scale that missed frames; growth stays just under it and the ceiling
 // relaxes by 4 % every 30 s. A failed probe returns straight to the last scale that held
 // (good), and retries of 60 fps after a 30 fps lock back off (20 s, 40 s, ... 160 s).
 let window:number[]=[],lastDrawn=0,changedAt=0,lockedAt=0,retry=20000,ceiling=1,ceilingAt=0,streak=0,good=0;
 const g:Governor={scale:start,target:60,
  // Called for every drawn frame with the interval the pacer is aiming for; returns true when
  // scale or target changed (the caller resizes the drawing buffer).
  frame(time,interval){
   if(lastDrawn)window.push((time-lastDrawn)/interval);lastDrawn=time;
   if(window.length<40||time-changedAt<1200)return false;
   const sorted=[...window].sort((a,b)=>a-b),missed=window.filter(r=>r>1.45).length/window.length,median=sorted[sorted.length>>1];window=[];
   if(missed>.2){changedAt=time;streak=0;
    if(g.target===60&&lockedAt){g.target=30;lockedAt=time;retry=Math.min(160000,retry*2);return true;}
    // Far over budget (most frames take 3+ slots): go straight to the smallest size, one resize
    // instead of five (each resize reallocates the drawing buffer).
    if(g.scale>min+.001){ceiling=g.scale;ceilingAt=time;g.scale=median>2.5?min:good&&good<g.scale?good:Math.max(min,g.scale*step);if(good>=ceiling)good=0;return true;}
    if(g.target>30){g.target=30;lockedAt=time;return true;}
    return false;}
   if(missed>=.03){streak=0;return false;}
   if(++streak>=2&&g.target===60)good=g.scale;
   if(ceiling<1&&time-ceilingAt>30000){ceiling=Math.min(1,ceiling*1.04);ceilingAt=time;}
   if(g.target<60&&time-lockedAt>retry){g.target=60;changedAt=time;return true;}
   if(g.target===60&&streak>=2&&g.scale<Math.min(1,ceiling*.97)-.001){g.scale=Math.min(ceiling*.97,g.scale*grow,1);changedAt=time;streak=0;return true;}
   if(g.target===60&&lockedAt&&streak>=2)lockedAt=0;
   return false;}};
 return g;
}


======================================================================
FILE: tests/frame-pacer.mjs
======================================================================
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {framePacer,qualityGovernor} from '../lib/frame-pacer.ts';
// Simulated displays: rAF fires every refresh (with a little timestamp jitter). Every drawn
// frame must be the same length (a whole number of refreshes), at the highest rate <= ~target.
const run=(hz,target,seconds=3,jitter=.3)=>{const pacer=framePacer(target),period=1000/hz,drawn=[];let seed=7;const rand=()=>((seed=seed*16807%2147483647)/2147483647-.5)*2*jitter;
 for(let i=1;i<=hz*seconds;i++){const t=i*period+rand();if(pacer.due(t))drawn.push(t);}
 const gaps=drawn.slice(30).map((t,i,a)=>i?t-a[i-1]:null).filter(Boolean),fps=gaps.length/((drawn.at(-1)-drawn[30])/1000);
 return{fps:Math.round(fps),even:Math.max(...gaps)-Math.min(...gaps)<period*.5};};
// The v22 test (draw when at least 1000/60-1 ms passed) for comparison.
const old=(hz)=>{const period=1000/hz;let last=0,n=0,first=0,end=0;for(let i=1;i<=hz*3;i++){const t=i*period;if(t-last>=1000/60-1){last=t;if(!first)first=t;end=t;n++;}}return Math.floor((n-1)/((end-first)/1000)+.01);};
const expect={60:60,75:75,90:90,120:60,144:72,165:55,240:60};
for(const [hz,fps] of Object.entries(expect)){const r=run(+hz,60);assert.ok(r.even,`${hz} Hz frames must be even`);assert.ok(Math.abs(r.fps-fps)<=1,`${hz} Hz: ${r.fps} fps, expected ${fps}`);}
for(const hz of [60,90,120,144]){const r=run(hz,30);assert.ok(r.even);assert.ok(Math.abs(r.fps-(hz===144?29:30))<=1,`${hz} Hz at 30: ${r.fps}`);}
assert.equal(old(75),37);assert.equal(old(90),45);assert.equal(old(144),48);
// A device slower than the screen draws on every callback (never skips a ready frame).
{const pacer=framePacer(60);let drawn=0;for(let i=1;i<=120;i++)if(pacer.due(i*33.4))drawn++;assert.ok(drawn>=118);}
// ... also when its frame times vary (mostly 67-83 ms, sometimes one or two refreshes): no
// callback is skipped (a quantile-based refresh estimate skipped the quick ones).
{const pacer=framePacer(60),gaps=[66.7,83.3,66.7,33.3,83.3,66.7,16.7,83.3];let t=0,skipped=0;for(let i=0;i<400;i++){t+=gaps[i%gaps.length];if(!pacer.due(t)&&i>8)skipped++;}assert.equal(skipped,0,'slow, uneven device skipped frames');}
// Governor: a GPU that needs 26 ms per frame at full resolution (cost ~ pixels = scale^2) on a
// 60 Hz screen. It must settle at a resolution that fits the 16.7 ms slot.
const slots=(g,ms)=>Math.max(g.target===30?2:1,Math.ceil(ms*g.scale*g.scale/16.67-1e-9));
{const g=qualityGovernor(1);let t=0,changes=0;for(let i=0;i<1500;i++){t+=slots(g,26)*16.67;if(g.frame(t,g.target===30?33.33:16.67))changes++;}
 assert.equal(g.target,60);assert.ok(26*g.scale*g.scale<=16.7,`settled scale ${g.scale.toFixed(2)}`);assert.ok(changes<=6,`few changes (${changes})`);}
// A GPU that cannot make 60 fps even at the lowest resolution holds a steady 30 instead.
{const g=qualityGovernor(1);let t=0,changes=0;for(let i=0;i<1500;i++){t+=slots(g,60)*16.67;if(g.frame(t,g.target===30?33.33:16.67)&&t<20000)changes++;}
 assert.equal(g.target,30);assert.ok(g.scale<=.61);assert.ok(changes<=2,`far over budget: straight to the smallest size, then 30 fps (${changes} changes)`);}
// A fast GPU never loses resolution.
{const g=qualityGovernor(1);let t=0;for(let i=0;i<1500;i++){t+=16.67;g.frame(t,16.67);}assert.equal(g.scale,1);assert.equal(g.target,60);}
console.log(`PASS: even frame pacing at 60/75/90/120/144/165/240 Hz (v22 cap: 75 Hz -> ${old(75)} fps, 90 Hz -> ${old(90)} fps, 144 Hz -> ${old(144)} fps), 30 fps lock pacing, slow devices never skip, quality governor settles / locks 30 / keeps full resolution.`);

# 밤의 시민 v24 작업 보고서 — 귀여운 캐릭터 모델 + 끊김·지연 개선

기준: v23(이 브랜치의 이전 커밋 `597e581`). **로컬 변경이며 배포하지 않았습니다.**

표기: **[실측]** 이 환경에서 실행해 확인 · **[추정]** 코드 근거의 판단 · **[미검증]** 확인하지 못함

---

## 1. 요약

| 영역 | 무엇을 했나 | 확인 |
|---|---|---|
| 캐릭터 모델 | 처음부터 다시 만듦: 둥근 머리, 단순한 점 눈(하이라이트 2개), 낮게 놓인 이목구비, 볼터치, 끝이 둥근 큼직한 머리카락, 콩 모양 몸, 벙어리장갑 손, 둥근 신발 | [실측] 렌더·실제 앱 |
| 모델 비용 | 인형당 삼각형 평균 17,325 → 12,119(−30 %). 아바타마다 한 번 만든 뒤 복사(복사 0.22 ms) | [실측] |
| 프레임 간격 | 75/90/144 Hz 화면에서 들쭉날쭉하던 프레임을 고르게(예: 75 Hz 37 fps → 75 fps) | [실측] 단위 테스트 |
| 3D 화질 자동 조절 | GPU가 느리면 해상도를 낮추고(최저 60 %), 그래도 부족하면 고른 30 fps로 고정. 터치 기기도 60 fps를 목표로(전엔 무조건 30) | [실측] 단위 테스트 |
| 게임 중 멈춤 | 오프스크린 렌더러 강제 해제(120–150 ms 멈춤) 제거, 필요 없는 초상화·스프라이트 작업 취소, 3D에서는 2D용 스프라이트를 만들지 않음, 로딩 중에 인형·셰이더 미리 준비 | [실측] 아래 4 |
| 반응 속도 | 3D에서 장치를 누를 때 "손 뻗기 260 ms → 그 다음 요청"을 동시에 진행 | [추정] 코드 |
| 회귀 | 자동 테스트 38/38(새 프레임 간격 테스트 포함), 타입 검사, 브라우저 점검 전 단계 통과, 콘솔 오류 0 | [실측] |

## 2. 캐릭터 모델: 무엇이 이질적이었고 어떻게 바꿨나

v23 인형은 3D 공에 애니메이션식 큰 눈(흰자·홍채·속눈썹·쌍꺼풀)을 그려 붙이고 머리카락을 뾰족한 가닥으로 만든 형태라, 가까이서 보거나 각도가 바뀌면 "그림을 붙인 공"처럼 어색해 보였습니다. 이번에는 장난감 피규어처럼 **큰 덩어리와 단순한 표정**으로 다시 만들었습니다.

- **머리**: 거의 둥근 공(턱만 아주 살짝 좁아짐).
- **눈**: 흰자 없는 세로로 긴 점 눈. 아래쪽에 눈 색이 은은하게 비치고, 큰 하이라이트와 작은 하이라이트를 양쪽 눈 같은 방향에 둠. 여자 캐릭터는 눈꼬리에 작은 속눈썹 두 개.
- **배치**: 눈을 얼굴 가운데보다 조금 아래에, 입을 눈 바로 아래에 작게(아기 얼굴 비율). 짧고 부드러운 눈썹.
- **볼**: 피부에 칠한 장밋빛 볼터치(조명을 받음). 어두운 피부는 붉게 뜨지 않도록 약하게.
- **머리카락**: 앞머리까지 하나로 이어진 도톰한 머리 껍데기. 가장자리는 둥근 물결 모양이고 피부 쪽으로 말려 들어가 **두께가 보이는 둥근 테**로 끝납니다(가늘고 뾰족한 가닥 없음). 옆으로 넘긴 머리는 큰 앞머리 덩어리 하나로 방향만 줌. 단발·긴 머리·트윈테일·포니테일·똥머리·바보털·머리핀·동그란 안경은 그대로 유지하되 모두 끝이 둥근 큼직한 형태로.
- **몸**: 콩 모양 몸통, 둥근 목깃, 작은 V 칼라와 단추 2개, 소매 끝 색 띠, 벙어리장갑 손, 둥근 신발과 밑창.
- **캐릭터별 스타일**은 `lib/doll-character.ts`의 `LOOKS` 표(머리 길이, 앞머리 종류, 꼬리, 입 모양, 신발 색) 한 곳에서 바꿀 수 있습니다.

1인칭 내 팔도 새 인형과 같은 소매·장갑·피부색입니다(v23에서 맞춘 구조 유지).

비교: `docs/v24/model/compare-*.jpg`(위/왼쪽 v23, 아래/오른쪽 v24), 실제 앱: `docs/v24/browser/`.

## 3. 끊김·지연: 원인과 수정

### 3.1 프레임 간격이 들쭉날쭉하던 문제
두 게임 화면(2D 오로라, 3D 네레이드)이 "직전 프레임에서 15.7 ms가 지났으면 그린다"는 방식으로 60 fps를 맞추고 있었습니다. 60 Hz 화면에서는 문제없지만, 다른 주사율에서는 1칸·2칸 프레임이 섞여 끊겨 보입니다 [실측: 시뮬레이션].

| 화면 주사율 | v23 | v24 |
|---|---|---|
| 60 Hz | 60 fps | 60 fps |
| 75 Hz | 37 fps(들쭉날쭉) | 75 fps |
| 90 Hz | 45 fps | 90 fps |
| 120 Hz | 60 fps | 60 fps(2칸마다 고르게) |
| 144 Hz | 48 fps | 72 fps(고르게) |

`lib/frame-pacer.ts`가 화면 주사율을 재서 "매 칸" 또는 "정확히 N칸마다" 그립니다. 화면보다 느린 기기에서는 준비된 프레임을 절대 건너뛰지 않습니다(`tests/frame-pacer.mjs`).

### 3.2 느린 GPU에서 요동치던 3D 화면
3D 화면에 **화질 자동 조절**을 넣었습니다. 프레임이 제 시간을 자주 넘기면 그리는 해상도를 낮추고(10 %씩, 많이 느리면 한 번에 60 %까지), 최저 해상도로도 부족하면 들쭉날쭉한 40–50 fps 대신 **고른 30 fps**로 고정합니다. 여유가 생기면 천천히 되돌리고, 직전에 실패한 크기 바로 아래에서 멈춰 크기가 왔다갔다하지 않게 했습니다. 30 fps로 고정된 경우 60 fps 재시도는 20 s, 40 s, … 160 s 간격으로 늘어납니다.
- 전에는 터치 기기(휴대전화·태블릿)는 성능과 상관없이 3D를 **30 fps로 고정**했습니다. 이제 60 fps를 목표로 하고, 기기가 못 따라오면 위 조절이 알아서 낮춥니다.
- F3 진단 패널에 **"3D 그리기 · 해상도"**(프레임당 처리 시간 p95, 현재 해상도 %, 목표 fps) 줄을 추가했습니다.

### 3.3 게임 중 갑자기 멈추던 문제
헤드리스 브라우저 CPU 프로파일로 찾은 원인과 수정입니다 [실측].
1. **오프스크린 렌더러 강제 해제**: 초상화·탑뷰 스프라이트를 만든 뒤 1.2초 쉬면 WebGL 컨텍스트를 강제로 버렸는데(`forceContextLoss`), 이것이 게임 시작 직후 **120–150 ms 멈춤**을 만들었습니다. 이제 버리지 않고 30초 넘게 쉬거나 탭이 숨겨질 때만 정리하며, 같은 캔버스를 다시 씁니다.
2. **매번 캔버스 크기 변경**: 초상화(384×448)와 스프라이트(170×190)를 번갈아 만들 때마다 캔버스 크기를 바꿔 버퍼를 새로 잡았습니다. 이제 크기를 고정하고 스프라이트는 한쪽 모서리에 그립니다. 초상화 인코딩은 메인 스레드를 막는 `toDataURL` 대신 `toBlob`.
3. **보이지 않는 초상화를 게임 중에 그리던 문제**: 첫 화면의 캐릭터 선택 칸(16명)이 만들던 초상화가, 플레이어가 곧장 게임으로 들어간 뒤에도 게임 중에 계속 만들어지고 있었습니다. 이제 그 카드가 사라지면 남은 작업은 취소됩니다. 게임 화면이 열려 있는 동안에는 "미리 만들어 두기" 스프라이트 작업도 건너뜁니다(2D 화면이 실제로 필요한 것은 그대로 만듦).
4. **3D 화면에서 2D용 스프라이트 생성**: 3D 지도(네레이드)는 탑뷰 스프라이트를 쓰지 않는데도 만들고 있었습니다. 3D에서는 만들지 않습니다.
5. **처음 보는 플레이어에서 멈춤**: 플레이어가 처음 시야에 들어올 때마다 인형을 새로 만들었습니다(약 20 ms). 이제 아바타마다 한 번 만든 "원본"을 복사(0.22 ms)하고, 3D 로딩 화면 동안 방 안 모든 아바타의 원본과 셰이더를 미리 준비합니다. 셰이더의 동기식 오류 검사도 껐습니다.

### 3.4 장치 반응이 늦던 문제
3D에서 미션 장치·긴급 버튼·CCTV를 누르면 손 뻗는 애니메이션(260 ms)을 **끝낸 뒤에야** 서버에 요청을 보내, 화면이 열리기까지 260 ms + 왕복 시간이 걸렸습니다. 이제 요청을 애니메이션 시작과 동시에 보내고, 손이 닿고 응답이 오면(둘 중 늦은 쪽) 바로 엽니다 [추정: 코드상 대기 시간이 "합"에서 "최댓값"으로 바뀜. 실제 앱에서 장치를 눌러 보는 자동 확인은 하지 못함].

## 4. 측정 [실측: 헤드리스 Chromium, 소프트웨어 WebGL]

같은 측정 스크립트(`tests/browser/verify-ui.mjs hitches`, 새로 추가)로 v23과 v24 빌드를 나란히 띄워 3번씩 쟀습니다. 20명 방, 플레이어가 **게임 진행 중인 방에 곧장 다시 들어가는** 상황이며, 숫자는 "페이지 로드 → 게임 화면 표시 후 12초"의 메인 스레드 긴 작업(50 ms 이상)입니다.

| 지도 | 항목 | v23 | v24 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 네레이드(3D) | 게임 화면이 뜨기까지 | 7.2–7.8 s | **2.8–4.5 s** |
| | 긴 작업 개수 / 합계 | 4–5개 / 0.90–1.17 s | **2개 / 0.58–0.84 s** |
| | 프레임 간격 중앙값 | 200–217 ms | **150–183 ms** |
| 오로라(2D) | 게임 화면이 뜨기까지 | 5.5–6.1 s | **1.1–1.4 s** |
| | 긴 작업 개수 / 합계 | 4–5개 / 0.60–0.66 s | **0–1개 / 0–0.08 s** |

- 소프트웨어 WebGL이라 프레임 시간 자체(150–200 ms)는 실제 기기 값이 아닙니다. **두 빌드를 같은 조건에서 비교한 상대값**으로 봐 주세요.
- 남은 점 ①: 네레이드에서 가장 긴 단일 작업은 v24가 0.5–0.6 s로 v23(0.3–0.5 s)보다 깁니다. 이 환경은 프레임이 매우 느려서 화질 조절이 해상도를 한 번에 최저로 낮추는데, 소프트웨어 WebGL에서는 그 캔버스 크기 변경 한 번이 느립니다. 실제 GPU에서는 짧고, 빠른 기기에서는 일어나지 않습니다 [추정].
- 남은 점 ②: 오로라에서 게임에 들어온 직후 몇 초는 v24 프레임 p95가 조금 높습니다(167–200 ms vs 100–117 ms). v24가 2–4초 일찍 게임 화면에 들어가서, v23은 로딩 중에 만들던 방 인원 스프라이트를 v24는 게임 첫 몇 초에 만들기 때문입니다. 15초 뒤 다시 재면 같습니다(프레임 92–109 vs 106–111). 로비를 거쳐 들어오는 보통 흐름에서는 로비에서 미리 만들어집니다.

## 5. 검증
- `node tests/run-all.mjs`: **38/38 통과** [실측]. `doll-character` 테스트(비율·머리 크기·접지·관절·메시 수·공유 재질·삼각형 한도)는 조건을 바꾸지 않고 통과. 새 `frame-pacer` 테스트: 60–240 Hz 고른 간격, 느리고 들쭉날쭉한 기기에서 프레임 건너뛰지 않음, 화질 조절의 수렴·30 fps 고정·빠른 기기 유지.
- `tsc --noEmit` 통과. eslint: 새로 만든 파일과 라이브러리 파일은 경고·오류 0. 기존에 React Compiler 규칙 오류가 있던 컴포넌트(`FieldGame`, `FirstPersonViewport`, `DollAvatar`)는 **v23과 같은 개수**(새로 늘지 않음).
- 실제 앱(`pnpm run build` → 로컬 D1 → `pnpm run start`) + `verify-ui.mjs lobby nereid aurora solo mobile`: 로비 카드, 두 지도 이동, 전체화면·지도, 회의 전환, 포인터 잠금, 진행자 단독, 모바일 세로·가로 모두 통과, **콘솔 오류 0** [실측]. 스크린샷 `docs/v24/browser/`.

## 6. 남은 점·미검증
- **실기기** [미검증]: 휴대전화·태블릿·크롬북에서의 실제 프레임, 화질 자동 조절이 실제로 어느 해상도에 자리 잡는지, 발열. 배포 후 F3 패널의 "3D 그리기 · 해상도" 줄로 확인할 수 있습니다.
- **장치 반응 시간**: 3.4는 코드 변경에 따른 판단이며 실제 앱에서 장치를 눌러 시간을 잰 것은 아닙니다 [추정].
- **디자인 취향**: 얼굴·머리 스타일은 주관적인 선택입니다. 특정 캐릭터의 머리 모양·입 모양은 `LOOKS` 표만 바꾸면 됩니다.
- 네트워크(실시간 서버·보간) 구조는 v22에서 시뮬레이터로 맞춘 그대로 두었습니다. 느린 기기에서 프레임이 빨라지면 다른 플레이어 표시 지연도 함께 줄어듭니다(메시지 처리가 프레임 사이에 일어나므로) [추정].

- 화면·성능: `lib/frame-pacer.ts`(신규), `components/FirstPersonViewport.tsx`, `components/FieldGame.tsx`, `lib/scene3d.ts`, `lib/field-net.ts`(진단 패널 값)
- 테스트·도구: `tests/frame-pacer.mjs`(신규), `tests/browser/verify-ui.mjs`(`hitches` 단계 추가)
- 문서: 이 파일, `START_HERE_KO.md`, `SOURCE_FOR_CLAUDE.txt`, `docs/v24/`
